 1,1
svargāpavargavisphārau bhuvanasyodaye yathā  |
bhavarogaharau vande caṇḍikācandraśekharau  || 1,1.1 ||
rasoparasalohānāṃ tailamūlaphalaiḥ saha  |
asādhyaṃ pratyayopetaṃ kathyate rasasādhanam  || 1,1.2 ||
vaidyānāṃ yaśase'rthāya vyādhitānāṃ hitāya ca  |
vādināṃ kautukārthāya vṛddhānāṃ dehasiddhaye  || 1,1.3 ||
mantriṇāṃ mantrasiddhyarthaṃ vividhāścaryakāraṇam  |
pañcakhaṇḍamidaṃ śāstraṃ sādhakānāṃ hitapradam  || 1,1.4 ||
rasakhaṇḍe tu vaidyānāṃ vyādhitānāṃ rasendrake  |
vādināṃ vādakhaṇḍe ca vṛddhānāṃ ca rasāyane  || 1,1.5 ||
mantriṇāṃ mantrakhaṇḍe ca rasasiddhiḥ prajāyate  |
sutarāṃ nāsti saṃdehaḥ tattatkhaṇḍavilokinām  || 1,1.6 ||
hato hanti jarāmṛtyuṃ mūrchito vyādhighātakaḥ  |
dhatte ca khegatiṃ baddhaḥ ko'nyaḥ sūtātkṛpākaraḥ  || 1,1.7 ||
jarāmaraṇadāridryaroganāśakaromataḥ  |
mūrchito harate vyādhīnnaso dehe carannapi  || 1,1.8 ||
mohayedyaḥ parānbaddho jīvayecca mṛtaḥ parān  |
mūrchito bodhayedanyāṃstaṃ sūtaṃ ko na sevate  || 1,1.9 ||
āyurdraviṇamārogyaṃ vahnirmedhā mahadbalam  |
rūpayauvanalāvaṇyaṃ rasopāsanayā bhavet || 1,1.10 ||
mārayejjāritaṃ sūtaṃ gandhakenaiva mūrchayet |
baddhaḥ syāddrutisatvābhyāṃ rasasyaivaṃ tridhā gatiḥ  || 1,1.11 ||
doṣahīno raso brahmā mūrchitastu janārdanaḥ  |
mārito rudrarūpaḥ syādbaddhaḥ sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ  || 1,1.12 ||
vedhako dehalohābhyāṃ sūto devi sadāśivaḥ  |
darśanādrasarājasya brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 1,1.13 ||
sparśanānnāśayeddevi gohatyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
kiṃ punarbhakṣaṇāddevi prāpyate paramaṃ padam  || 1,1.14 ||
alpamātropayogitvādaruceraprapsaṅgataḥ  |
kṣipramārogyadāyitvādbheṣajebhyo raso'dhikaḥ  || 1,1.15 ||
yaduktaṃ śambhunā pūrvaṃ rasakhaṇḍe rasāyane  |
rasasya vandanārthe ca dīpikā rasamaṅgale  || 1,1.16 ||
vyādhitānāṃ hitārthāya proktaṃ nāgārjunena yat |
uktaṃ carpaṭisiddhena svargavaidyakapālike  || 1,1.17 ||
anekarasaśāstreṣu saṃhitāsvāgameṣu ca  |
yaduktaṃ vāgbhaṭe tantre suśrute vaidyasāgare  || 1,1.18 ||
anyaiśca bahubhiḥ siddhairyaduktaṃ ca vilokya tat |
tatra yadyadasādhyaṃ syādyadyaddurlabhamauṣadham  || 1,1.19 ||
tattatsarvaṃ parityajya sārabhūtaṃ samuddhṛtam  |
kvacicchāstre kriyā nāsti kramasaṃkhyā na ca kvacit || 1,1.20 ||
mātrā yuktiḥ kvacinnāsti sampradāyo na ca kvacit |
tena siddhirna tatrāsti rase vātha rasāyane  || 1,1.21 ||
vaidye vāde prayoge ca yasmādyatno mayā kṛtaḥ  |
yadyadgurumukhājjñātaṃ svānubhūtaṃ ca yanmayā  |
tattallokahitārthāya prakaṭīkriyate'dhunā  || 1,1.22 ||
śrīmān sūtanṛpo dadāti vilasaṃllakṣmīṃ vapuḥ śāśvataṃsvānāṃ prītikarīmacañcalamano māteva puṃsāṃ yathā  |
anyo nāsti śarīranāśakagadapradhvaṃsakārī tataḥ kāryaṃ nityamahotsavaiḥ prathamataḥ sūtādvapuḥsādhanam  || 1,1.23 ||
sākṣādakṣayadāyako bhuvi nṛṇāṃ pañcatvamuccaiḥ kuto mūrcchāṃ mūrchitavigraho gadabhṛtāṃ hantyuñcakaiḥ prāṇinām  |
baddhaṃ prāpya surāsurendracaritāṃ tāṃ tāṃ gatiṃ prāpayet |
so'yaṃ pātu paropakāracaturaḥ śrīsūtarājo jagat || 1,1.24 ||
yadanyatra tadatrāsti yadatrāsti na tatkvacit |
rasaratnākaraḥ so'yaṃ nityanāthena nirmitaḥ  |
tataḥ kuryātprayatnena rasasaṃskāramuttamam  || 1,1.25 ||
avijñātyā ca śāstrārthaṃ prayogakuśalo bhiṣak |
yama eva sa vijñeyaḥ martyānāṃ mṛtyurūpadhṛk || 1,1.26 ||
{rasasya mahādoṣāḥ}
nāgo vaṅgo malo vahniścāṃcalyaṃ ca viṣaṃ giriḥ  |
asahyāgnirmahādoṣā niṣiddhāḥ pārade sthitāḥ  || 1,1.27 ||
jāyaṃ gaṇḍastanau nāgātkuṣṭhaṃ vaṅgādrujā malāt |
vahnerdāho bījanāśaścāñcalyānmaraṇaṃ viṣāt || 1,1.28 ||
gireḥ sphoṭo hyasahyāgnerdoṣānmoha upajāyate  |
{nirdoṣapāradasya guṇāḥ}
doṣahīno yadā sūtastadā mṛtyujarāpahaḥ  || 1,1.29 ||
sākṣādamṛtamapyeṣa doṣayukto raso viṣam  |
tasmāddoṣaviśuddhyarthaṃ rasaśuddhirvidhīyate  || 1,1.30 ||
raso grāhyaḥ sunakṣatre palānāṃ śatamātrakam  |
pañcāśataṃ pañcaviṃśadvā dvādaśaṃ caikameva vā  || 1,1.31 ||
palādūnaṃ na kartavyaṃ rasasaṃskāramuttamam  |
aghoreṇa ca mantreṇa rasasaṃskārapūjanam  || 1,1.32 ||
aghorebhyo'tha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ  |
sarvebhyaḥ sarvasarvebhyo namaste'stu rudrarūpebhyaḥ  || 1,1.33 ||


 1, 2
niḥsāraṃ vīkṣya viśvaṃ gadavikalavapurvyāptamevātitaptam  |
bhūyaḥ kāruṇyasindhoḥ sakalaguṇanidheḥ sūtarājasya yuktim  || 1,2.1 ||
dṛṣṭvā sūtasya śāstrāṇyavahitamanasā prāṇināmiṣṭasiddhyai  |
śṛṇvantūccairmayoktaṃ suvipulamatayo bhogikendrāḥ sarendrāḥ  |
yāvatsūtaṃ na śuddhaṃ na ca mṛtamatha no mūrchitaṃ gandhabandhaṃ  |
no vajraṃ māritaṃ vā na ca gaganavadho nāpasūtāśca śuddhāḥ  |
svarṇādyaṃ sarvalohaṃ viṣamapi na mṛtaṃ tailapāto na yāvat |
tāvadvaidyaḥ kva siddho bhavati vasubhujāṃ maṇḍale ślāghyayogyaḥ  || 1,2.2 ||
{aṣṭadoṣanivāraṇa}
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi doṣāṣṭakanivāraṇam  |
iṣṭakārajanīcūrṇaiḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśaiḥ rasasya tu  || 1,2.3 ||
mardayettaptakhalve taṃ jambīrotthadravairdinam  |
khalvaṃ lohamayaṃ vātha pāṣāṇāśmamathāpi vā  || 1,2.4 ||
kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayetsūtaṃ nāgadoṣasya śāntaye  |
viśālāṅkolacūrṇena vaṅgadoṣaṃ vināśayet || 1,2.5 ||
rājavṛkṣo malaṃ hanti citrakaṃ hanti vahnijam  |
cāñcalyaṃ kṛṣṇadhattūraistraiphalairviṣanāśanam  || 1,2.6 ||
kaṭutrayaṃ giriṃ hanti asahyāgniṃ trikaṇṭakaḥ  |
{(2)}
pratidoṣaṃ kalāṃśena tatra cūrṇaṃ sakanyakam  || 1,2.7 ||
suvastragālitaṃ sūtaṃ khalve kṣiptvā yathākramam  |
pratyekaṃ pratyahaṃ yatnātsaptarātraṃ vimardayet || 1,2.8 ||
uddhṛtyoṣṇāranālena mṛdbhāṇḍe kṣālayetsudhīḥ  |
sarvadoṣavinirmuktaḥ saptakañcukavarjitaḥ  || 1,2.9 ||
jāyate śuddhaḥ sūto'yaṃ yujyate vaidyakarmaṇi  |
{pāradaśodhana}
ajāśakṛttuṣāgniṃ ca jvālayitvā bhuvi kṣipet |
tasyopari sthitaṃ khalvaṃ tatroktaṃ mardayedrasam  || 1,2.10 ||
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ devadāru ca kākatuṇḍīṃ jayādravaiḥ  |
karkoṭīmūṣalīkanyādrave dattvā vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ pātanāyantre śuddhaṃ ca viniyojayet || 1,2.11 ||
kumāryāśca niśācūrṇe dinaṃ sūtaṃ vimardayet |
pātayetpātanāyantre samyakśuddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 1,2.12 ||
{hiṃgulotthapāradaśodhana}
pāribhadrarasaiḥ peṣyaṃ hiṃgulaṃ yāmamātrakam  |
jambīrāṇāṃ dravairvātha pātyaṃ pātālayantrake  || 1,2.13 ||
taṃ sūtaṃ yojayedyoge saptakañcukavarjitam  |
ityevaṃ śuddhayaḥ khyātā yatheṣṭaikā prakārayet || 1,2.14 ||
{śuddhasūtamāraṇauṣadhayaḥ}
athaitāṃ mūlikāṃ vakṣye śuddhasūtasya māraṇe  |
brahmadaṇḍī meghanādā citrakaṃ tṛṇamustikā  || 1,2.15 ||
vajravallī balā śuṇṭhī kaṭutumbyardhacandrikā  |
viṣamuṣṭyarkalākṣāśca gokṣuraḥ kākatuṇḍikā  || 1,2.16 ||
kanyā caṇḍālinīkandaṃ sarpākṣī sarapuṅkhikā  |
vastā raktāgranirguṇḍī lajjālī devadālikā  || 1,2.17 ||
jātī jayantī vārāhī bhūkadambaṃ kuraṇṭakam  |
koṣātakī nīrakaṇā lāṅgalī sahadevikāḥ  || 1,2.18 ||
cakramardo'mṛtākandaṃ kākamācī ravipriyā  |
viṣṇukrāntā hastiśuṇḍī snukpayo bhṛṅgarāṭpaṭuḥ  || 1,2.19 ||
ityetāḥ mūlikā ākhyātā yojyā pāradamārikāḥ  |
etāḥ samastā vyastā vā deyā hyaṣṭādaśādhikāḥ  || 1,2.20 ||
{pāradabhasmanirmāṇa}
aprasūtagavāṃ mūtre peṣayedraktamūlikām  |
taddravaiḥ śodhitaṃ sūtaṃ tulyaṃ gandhakasaṃyutam  || 1,2.21 ||
taptakhalve caturyāmamavicchinnaṃ vimardayet |
tatpiṇḍaṃ pātayedyantre triṃśaddhaṭṭamahāpuṭe  || 1,2.22 ||
evaṃ daśapuṭairmāryaṃ mardyaṃ pātyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
taduddhṛtya punarmardyaṃ vajramūṣāntare kṣipet || 1,2.23 ||
bhūdharākhye puṭe pacyāddaśadhā bhasmatāṃ vrajet |
dravairdravaiḥ punarmardyaṃ siddho'yaṃ vajrasūtakaḥ  || 1,2.24 ||
mūlikāmāritaḥ sūto jāraṇakramavarjitaḥ  |
na krameddehalohābhyāṃ rogahartā bhaveddhruvam  || 1,2.25 ||
{pāradabhasmanirmāṇa (2)}
rasaṃ gandhakatailena dviguṇena vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ vātha sarpākṣīviṣṇukrāntāhvabhṛṅgajaiḥ  || 1,2.26 ||
tryahaṃ vimardayeddrāvaistriṃśaddhaṭṭamahāpuṭe  |
ityevamaṣṭadhā pācyaṃ raso bhasmībhaveddhruvam  || 1,2.27 ||
{rasabhasmanirmāṇa (3)}
śvetāṅkoṭajaṭāvāri sūtaṃ mardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre mūṣāyāṃ bhasmatāṃ vrajet || 1,2.28 ||
devadālīṃ harikrāntāmāranālena peṣayet |
saptadhā sūtakaṃ tena kuryāddhamanamutthitam  || 1,2.29 ||
taṃ sūtaṃ kharpare kuryāddattvā dattvā caturdravam  |
cullyopari pacedvahnau bhasma syādaruṇopamam  || 1,2.30 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (4)}
kāṣṭhodumbarajaiḥ kṣīraiḥ sitāṃ hiṃgu vibhāvayet |
saptavāraṃ prayatnena śodhyaṃ peṣyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,2.31 ||
kāṣṭhodumbarapañcāṅgaiḥ kaṣāyaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśakam  |
dattvā tena punarmardyaṃ hiṃgu vaṅgaraseśvaram  || 1,2.32 ||
kṣiptvā nirudhya mūṣāyāṃ bhūdharākhye puṭe pacet |
aṣṭadhā mriyate sūto deyaṃ hiṃgu puṭe puṭe  || 1,2.33 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (5)}
apāmārgasya bījāni tathairaṇḍasya cūrṇayet |
taccūrṇaṃ pārade deyaṃ mūṣāyāmeva rodhayet || 1,2.34 ||
ruddhvā laghupuṭe pacyāccaturbhirbhasmatāṃ vrajet |
{pāradamāraṇa (6)}
kaṭutumbyudbhave kande garbhe nārīpayaḥsu vai  || 1,2.35 ||
saptadhā mriyate sūtaḥ svedayedgomayāgninā  |
{pāradamāraṇa (7)}
aṅkolasya jaṭātoyaiḥ piṣṭvā khalve vimardayet || 1,2.36 ||
sūtaṃ gandhakasaṃyuktaṃ dinānte taṃ nirodhayet |
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre dinānte taṃ samuddharet || 1,2.37 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (8)}
dhānyābhraṃ sūtakaṃ tulyaṃ mardayenmārakadravaiḥ  |
dinaikaṃ tena kalkena vastre liptvā ca vartikām  || 1,2.38 ||
vilipya tailairvartiṃ tāmeraṇḍotthaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
prajvālya taddhṛtaṃ bhāṇḍe grāhayetpatitāmadhaḥ  || 1,2.39 ||
kṛṣṇabhasma bhavettacca punarmardyaṃ triyāmakaiḥ  |
dinaikaṃ tatpacedyantre kacchapākhye na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,2.40 ||
mṛtaḥ sūto bhavetsadyastattadyogeṣu yojayet |
{pāradamāraṇa (9)}
dvipalaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ tu sūtārdhaśuddhagandhakam  || 1,2.41 ||
mardayenmārakadrāvairdinamekaṃ nirantaram  |
baddhvā tu bhūdhare yantre dinaikaṃ mārayetpuṭāt || 1,2.42 ||
{vajramūṣā}
tuṣadagdhasya bhāgau dvāveko valmīkamṛttikā  |
lohakiṭṭasya bhāgaikaṃ śvetapāṣāṇabhāgakam  || 1,2.43 ||
narakeśasamaṃ kiṃcicchāgīkṣīreṇa peṣayet |
yāmadvayaṃ dṛḍhaṃ mardyaṃ tena mūṣāndhasaṃpuṭām  || 1,2.44 ||
śoṣayitvātha saṃlipya tatkalkaiḥ saṃnirudhya ca  |
vajramūṣā samākhyātā samyakpāradamārikā  || 1,2.45 ||
{bhasmasūtavarṇāḥ}
śvetaṃ pītaṃ tathā raktaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ceti caturvidham  |
lakṣaṇaṃ bhasmasūtasya śreṣṭhaṃ syāduttarottaram  || 1,2.46 ||


 1, 3
{jāraṇapūrvaṃ māraṇam}
athāto jāraṇāpūrvaṃ bījaṃ māraṇamucyate  |
ajīrṇaṃ cāpyabījaṃ ca sūtakaṃ yastu mārayet || 1,3.1 ||
brahmahā sa durācāro mama drohī maheśvari  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena jāritaṃ mārayedrasam  || 1,3.2 ||
{rasasya samukhakaraṇam}
saṃsthāpya gomayaṃ bhūmau pakvamūṣāṃ tataḥ param  |
tanmadhye kaṭutumbyutthaṃ tailaṃ dattvā rasaṃ kṣipet || 1,3.3 ||
kākamācīrasaṃ deyaṃ tailaṃ tulyaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  |
gandhakaṃ vrīhimātraṃ ca kṣiptvā taṃ ca nirodhayet || 1,3.4 ||
tatpṛṣṭhe pāvako deyaḥ pūrṇaṃ vā vahnikharparam  |
svāṅgaśītalatāṃ jñātvā jīrṇe taile ca gandhakam  || 1,3.5 ||
kākamācīdravaṃ cāgnau dattvā dattvā ca jārayet |
mūṣādho gomayaṃ cātra dattvā codadharvaṃ ca pāvakam  || 1,3.6 ||
ṣaḍguṇaṃ gandhakaṃ jāryaṃ sūtasyaivaṃ mukhaṃ bhavet |
{samukhapāradasya jāraṇāpūrvako māraṇavidhiḥ}
taṃ sūtaṃ mardayennīrairjambīrotthaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,3.7 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaiḥ pūrvairdvātriṃśāṃśaṃ tataḥ punaḥ  |
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ śuddhahemapatraṃ sūteṣu nikṣipet || 1,3.8 ||
śikhipittena sampiṣṭaṃ tailaiśca sarṣapodbhavaiḥ  |
liptvā hemaṃ kṣipetsūtaṃ yāmaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ  || 1,3.9 ||
mardyaṃ taṃ pūrvavatpacyānmūṣāyāṃ jambīradravaiḥ  |
pūrayedrodhayeccāgniṃ dattvā yatnena jārayet || 1,3.10 ||
grāse grāse ca tanmardyaṃ jambīrāṇāṃ dravaiḥ dṛḍham  |
mūlikā lavaṇaṃ gandhamabhāve pittatailayoḥ  || 1,3.11 ||
piṣṭvā jambīranīreṇa hemapatraṃ pralepayet |
ityevaṃ jāraṇā kāryā tataḥ sūtaṃ ca mārayet || 1,3.12 ||
athavā nirmukhaṃ sūtaṃ viḍayogena mārayet |
{viḍanirmāṇavidhi}
viḍamatra pravakṣyāmi sādhayedbhiṣajāṃ varaḥ  || 1,3.13 ||
śaṃkhacūrṇaṃ ravikṣīraiścātape bhāvayeddinam  |
tadvajjambīrajairdrāvairdinaikaṃ dhūmasārakam  || 1,3.14 ||
suvarcalamajāmūtraiḥ kvāthyaṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam  |
kaṇṭakārīṃ ca saṃkvāthyaṃ dinaikaṃ naramūtrakaiḥ  || 1,3.15 ||
sajjīkṣāraṃ tintiḍīkaṃ kāśīśaṃ ca śilājatum  |
jambīrotthairdravairbhāvyaṃ pṛthagyāmaṃ catuṣṭayam  || 1,3.16 ||
jayapālabījaṃ tvagghīnaṃ mūlakānāṃ dravairdinam  |
saindhavaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ guñjāṃ śigrumūladravairdinam  || 1,3.17 ||
etatsarvaṃ samāṃśaṃ tu mardyaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ  |
tadgolaṃ rakṣayedyatnādviḍo'yaṃ vāḍavānalaḥ  |
anena mardayetsūtaṃ grasate taptakhalvake  || 1,3.18 ||
{viḍayogena pāradādimāraṇaprakāraḥ}
svarṇābhrakasarvalohāni yatheṣṭāni ca jārayet |
mārayetpūrvayogena māraṇaṃ cātra kathyate  || 1,3.19 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (1)}
kumbhīṃ samūlāmuddhṛtya gomūtreṇa supeṣayet |
taddravairmardayetsūtaṃ dinaikaṃ kāntasampuṭe  || 1,3.20 ||
liptvā niyāmakā deyā ūrdhvaścādhastadanvayet |
mṛdvagninā dinaikaṃ tu pacecculyāṃ mṛto bhavet || 1,3.21 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (2)}
goghṛtaṃ gandhakaṃ sūtaṃ piṣṭvā piṇḍīṃ prakalpayet |
kumārīdalamadhyasthaṃ kṛtvā sūtreṇa veṣṭayet || 1,3.22 ||
taṃ kāntasampuṭe ruddhvā tribhirlaghupuṭaiḥ pacet |
tato dhmāte bhavedbhasma cāndhamūṣe kṣayaṃ dhruvam  || 1,3.23 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (3)}
śākavṛkṣasya pakvāni phalānyādāya śodhayet |
peṣayedravidugdhena tena mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 1,3.24 ||
ādiprasūtagorjātajarāyoścūrṇapūritaḥ  |
tanmadhye sūtakaṃ ruddhvā dhmāto bhasmatvamāpnuyāt || 1,3.25 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (4)}
karkoṭakīṃ kākamācīṃ ca kañcukīṃ kaṭutumbikām  |
kākajaṅghāṃ kākatuṇḍīṃ kākinīṃ kākamañjarīḥ  || 1,3.26 ||
piṣṭvaitān vajramūṣāstairlepaṃ kṛtvā rasaṃ kṣipet |
marditaṃ dinamekaṃ tu tairevārdrotthitai rasaiḥ  || 1,3.27 ||
ruddhvātha bhūdhare pacyādaṣṭavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
mardayelliptamūṣāstā ruddhvā dhmāto mṛto bhavet || 1,3.28 ||
{pāradamāraṇa (5)}
rasairniyāmakairmardyo dṛḍhaṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam  |
dviguṇairgandhatailaiśca pacenmṛdvagninā śanaiḥ  || 1,3.29 ||
yāvatkhoṭo bhavettattadrodhayellauhasampuṭe  |
harītakīṃ jale piṣṭvā lauhakiṭṭena mūṣikām  || 1,3.30 ||
kṛtvā tanmadhyataḥ kṣiptvā sampuṭaṃ cāndhayetpunaḥ  |
tasyordhvaṃ srāvakākāraṃ hṛtvā nāgadrutaṃ kṣipet || 1,3.31 ||
kaṭhinena dhamettāvadyāvannāgo druto bhavet |
na dhamecca punastāvadyāvatkaṭhinatāṃ vrajet || 1,3.32 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punardhmātastriyāmairmriyate rasaḥ  |
{niyāmakauṣadhyaḥ}
niyāmakāstato vakṣye sūtasya mārakarmaṇi  || 1,3.33 ||
sarpākṣī kṣīriṇī vandhyā matsyākṣī śarapuṅkhikā  |
kākajaṅghā śikhiśikhā brahmadaṇḍyākhuparṇikā  || 1,3.34 ||
varṣābhūḥ kañcukī mūrvā padmakotpalaciñcikā  |
śatāvarī vajralatā vajrakandā triparṇikā  || 1,3.35 ||
maṇḍūkaparṇī pāṭalī citrako grīṣmasundaraḥ  |
kākamācī mahārāṣṭrī haridrā tilakarṇikā  || 1,3.36 ||
śvetārkaśigrudhattūramṛgadūrvāharītakī  |
guḍūcī mūṣalī puṅkhā bhṛṅgarāḍraktacitrakam  || 1,3.37 ||
tagaraṃ śūraṇaṃ muṇḍī mayaṅkā potakokilam  |
saindhavaṃ śvetavarṣābhūḥ sāmbharaṃ hiṃgu mākṣikam  || 1,3.38 ||
viṣṇukāntā somavallī vraṇaghnī yakṣalocanam  |
vyāghrapādī haṃsapadī vṛścikālī kuraṇṭakam  || 1,3.39 ||
svayambhūkusumaṃ kuñcī hastiśuṇḍīndravāruṇī  |
bījānyahaskarasyāpi sarvatraite niyāmakāḥ  || 1,3.40 ||
etāḥ samastā vyastā vā deyā hyaṣṭādaśādhikāḥ  |
māraṇe mūrcchane bandhe rasasyaitāni yojayet || 1,3.41 ||
{rasasya jāraṇamāraṇavidhiḥ}
aprasūtagavāṃ mūtraiḥ piṣṭvā pūrvaniyāmikāḥ  |
taddravairmardayetsūtaṃ yathā pūrvoditaṃ kramāt || 1,3.42 ||
ityevaṃ jāraṇaṃ proktaṃ māraṇaṃ parikīrtitam  |
{rasabhasmaparīkṣā}
parīkṣā mārite sūte kartavyā ca yathoditā  || 1,3.43 ||
adhastuṣāgninā tapto hyakṣīṇastiṣṭhate yadā  |
tadā bhasma vijānīyāccullyāṃ yāmaṃ nirīkṣayet || 1,3.44 ||
mūlikāmāritaṃ sūtaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
jārito yāti sūto'sau jarādāridryaroganut || 1,3.45 ||
mūrchito vyādhināśāya baddhaḥ sarvatra yojayet || 1,3.46 ||


 1, 4
{mūrchana}
athātaḥ śuddhasūtasya mūrcchanā vidhirucyate  |
meghanādo vacā hiṃgu śūraṇairmardayedrasam  || 1,4.1 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ tu tadgolaṃ hiṅgunā veṣṭayedbahiḥ  |
pacellavaṇayantrasthaṃ dinaikaṃ caṇḍavahninā  || 1,4.2 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ samāruhya dṛḍhaṃ vastreṇa bandhayet |
ūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ dattvā saumyānale pacet || 1,4.3 ||
jīrṇe gandhe punardeyaṃ ṣaḍbhirvāraiḥ samaṃ samam  |
ṣaḍguṇe gandhake jīrṇe mūrchito rogahā bhavet || 1,4.4 ||
{mercury:: mūrchana}
gandhakaṃ madhusāraṃ ca śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ samam  |
yāmaikaṃ pācayetkhalve kācakupyāṃ niveśayet || 1,4.5 ||
ruddhvā dvādaśayāmaṃ taṃ vālukāyantragaṃ pacet |
sphoṭayetsvāṃgaśītaṃ taṃ tadūrdhvaṃ gandhakaṃ tyajet || 1,4.6 ||
adhaḥsthaṃ rasamādāya sarvarogeṣu yojayet |
{mercury:: formulation}
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ sūtārddhaṃ saindhavaṃ kṣipet || 1,4.7 ||
dravaiḥ sitajayantyāśca mardayeddivasatrayam  |
kṛtvā golaṃ ca saṃśoṣya kṣiptvā mūṣāṃ nirundhayet || 1,4.8 ||
śoṣayitvā dhametkiṃcitsutaptāṃ tāṃ jale kṣipet |
tasmādrasaṃ samuddhṛtya trikaṇṭarasabhāvitam  || 1,4.9 ||
yojayetsarvarogeṣu dhamedvā bhūdhare pacet |
{mercury:: formulation}
rasārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ ghṛtairyuktaṃ tu golakam  || 1,4.10 ||
kṛtvā taṃ bandhayedvastre dolāyantragataṃ pacet |
gomūtrāntaḥ kṛtaṃ yāmaṃ naramūtrairdinatrayam  || 1,4.11 ||
śoṣayecca punarvastre baddhvāveṣṭya sadā dṛḍham  |
śuṣkaṃ nirudhya mūṣāyāṃ tatastuṣāgninā pacet || 1,4.12 ||
ūrdhvabhāgamadhaḥ kṛtvā adhobhāgaṃ ca ūrdhvagam  |
ityādiparivartena svedayeddivasatrayam  || 1,4.13 ||
paścāduddhṛtya taṃ sūtaṃ yogavāhaṃ rujāpaham  || 1,4.14 ||
{mercury:: formulation}
sadyojātasya bālasya viṣṭhāṃ pālāśabījakam  |
cāṇḍālī rudhiraṃ sūtaṃ sūtapādaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam  || 1,4.15 ||
jayantyā mardayeddrāvairdinaikaṃ tattu golakam  |
peṣayetsahadevyātha lepayettāmrasaṃpuṭam  || 1,4.16 ||
tanmadhye golakaṃ kṣiptvā dviyāmaṃ svedayellaghu  |
vālukāyantramadhye tu samuddhṛtya tataḥ punaḥ  || 1,4.17 ||
citrakaiḥ sahadevyā ca gandhakairlepayedbahiḥ  |
sampuṭaṃ bandhayedvastre mṛdālepya ca śoṣayet || 1,4.18 ||
taṃ ruddhvāndhamūṣāyāṃ dhmāte sampuṭamāharet |
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ haredrogān yogavāho mahārasaḥ  || 1,4.19 ||
sampuṭaṃ sūtatulyaṃ syācchāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā  || 1,4.20 ||
{mercury:: formulation}
dhattūrakadravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ gandhaṃ sasūtakam  |
andhamūṣe dinaṃ svedyaṃ bhūdhare mūrchito bhavet || 1,4.21 ||
kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgulāṃ mūṣāṃ supakvāṃ mṛnmayīṃ dṛḍhām  |
mūṣāgarbhaṃ vilepyātha mūlairbahulapatrakaiḥ  || 1,4.22 ||
tanmadhye sūtakaṃ kṣiptvā mūṣāṃ pūryāttu taddravaiḥ  |
ruddhvā salavaṇairyantraiśculyāṃ dīptāgninā pacet || 1,4.23 ||
saptāhānte samuddhṛtya yavamānaṃ jvarāpaham  |
{mercury:: mūrchana}
kāśīśaṃ saindhavaṃ sūtaṃ tulyaṃ tulyaṃ vimardayet || 1,4.24 ||
kāśīśasyāsya bhāgena dātavyā phullatūrikā  |
stokastokaṃ kṣipetkhalve triyāmaṃ caiva mūrchayet || 1,4.25 ||
pratyekaṃ śataniṣkaṃ syādūnaṃ naivādhikaṃ bhavet |
sthālīsampuṭayantreṇa dinaṃ caṇḍāgninā pacet || 1,4.26 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ tataścullyāṃ mūrchitaṃ cāharetsūtam  |
{mercury:: mūrchana}
kuraṇṭakarasairbhāvyamātape mardayedrasam  || 1,4.27 ||
latākarañjapatrairvāṅguṣṭhāgrena vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ mūrchitaṃ samyaksarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,4.28 ||
{hairaṇyagarbhaka}
atha sūtasya śuddhasya mūrchitasyāpyayaṃ vidhiḥ  |
sūtatulyaṃ ghṛtaṃ jīrṇaṃ dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ ca gandhakam  || 1,4.29 ||
ravikṣīrairdinaṃ mardyamandhayitvā ca bhūdhare  |
puṭakena bhavetsiddho raso hairaṇyagarbhakaḥ  || 1,4.30 ||
{vaikrāntabaddharasa}
kaṭutumbyudbhave kande vandhyāyāḥ kṣīrakandake  |
apakvaikaṃ samādāya tadgarbhe piṇḍikā tataḥ  || 1,4.31 ||
daśaniṣkaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ niṣkaikaṃ śuddhagandhakam  |
stokaṃ stokaṃ kṣipedgandhaṃ pāṣāṇe taṃ tu kuṭṭayet || 1,4.32 ||
yāmamātre bhavetpiṇḍī rasaṃ kande vinikṣipet |
adha ūrdhvaṃ bhasma vaikrāntaṃ dattvā niṣkārddhamātrakam  || 1,4.33 ||
tataḥ kandasya majjābhirmukhaṃ baddhvā mṛdā dṛḍham  |
liptamaṅgulamānena sarvataḥ śoṣya golakam  || 1,4.34 ||
pācayedbhūdhare yantre tata uddhṛtya punaḥ pacet |
ūrdhvabhāgamadhaḥ kuryādityevaṃ parivartayet || 1,4.35 ||
krameṇa cālayedūrdhvaṃ bahiryugmopalaiḥ pacet |
tato bhinnastu saṃgrāhyo baddhaḥ syāddāḍimopamam  |
nāmnā vaikrāntabaddho'yaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,4.36 ||
{gandhabaddhapārada}
athavā gandhapīṭhīnāṃ vastre baddhvā tu gandhakam  |
tulyaṃ dattvā nirundhyātha sampuṭe lohaje dṛḍhe  || 1,4.37 ||
puṭayedbhūdhare tāvadyāvajjīryati gandhakam  |
evaṃ punaḥ punardeyaṃ yāvadgandhastu ṣaḍguṇam  || 1,4.38 ||
ityevaṃ gandhake baddhaḥ sūtaḥ syātsarvarogahṛt |
{gandhabaddhapārada (2)}
mūṣā jambīravistārā dairghyeṇa ṣoḍaśāṅgulā  |
apakvā sudṛḍhā kāryā sikatābhāṇḍamadhyagā  || 1,4.39 ||
tribhāgaṃ vālukā lagnā pādāṃśena bahiḥ sthitāḥ  |
palaikaṃ cūrṇitaṃ gandhaṃ mūṣāmadhye vinikṣipet || 1,4.40 ||
śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ paścātkṣipedgandhapalaṃ tataḥ  |
bhāṇḍamāropayeccullyāṃ mūṣāmācchādya yatnataḥ  || 1,4.41 ||
mandāgninā pacettāvadyāvannirdhūmatāṃ vrajet |
gandhadhūme gate pūryā kākamācīdravaistu sā  || 1,4.42 ||
drave jīrṇe punaḥ pūryā nāgavallīdaladravaiḥ  |
jīrṇe dhustūrakadrāvaiḥ pūrayitvā punaḥ pacet || 1,4.43 ||
yāvajjīryati tadgandhaṃ kākamācyādibhiḥ punaḥ  |
dattvā dattvā pacettadvaddhusturādikramādrasam  || 1,4.44 ||
bhittvā mūṣāṃ samādāya jarāvyādhiharo rasaḥ  |
yojayedgandhabaddho'yaṃ yogavāheṣu sarvataḥ  || 1,4.45 ||
{mercury:: mūrchana:: test}
kajjalābho yadā sūto vihāya ghanacāpalam  |
mūrchitaḥ sa tadā jñeyo nānārasagataḥ kvacit || 1,4.46 ||
mādhuryagauravopetaḥ tejasā bhāskaropamaḥ  |
vahnimadhye yadā tiṣṭhettadā vṛkṣasya lakṣaṇam  || 1,4.47 ||
sa jayati rasarājo mṛtyuśaṅkāpahārī sakalaguṇanidhānaḥ kāyakalpādhikārī  |
valipalitavināśaṃ sevanādvīryavṛddhiṃ sthiramapi kurute yaḥ kāminīnāṃ prasaṅge  || 1,4.48 ||
ityetā māritāḥ sūtā mūrchitā baddhamāgatāḥ  |
pratyekaṃ yogavāhaḥ syāttattadyogeṣu yojayet || 1,4.49 ||
māritaṃ dehasiddhyarthaṃ mūrchitaṃ vyādhināśanam  |
rasabhasma kvacidroge dehārthe mūrchitaṃ kvacit || 1,4.50 ||
baddhaṃ dvābhyāṃ prayuñjīta śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā  |
{mercury:: storage}
dante śṛṅge'thavā vaṃśe rakṣayetsādhitaṃ rasam  || 1,4.51 ||
{mercury:: medic. properties}
pāradaṃ krimikuṣṭhaghnaṃ balyamāyuṣyadṛṣṭidam  |
sevanātsarvarogaghnaṃ rucyaṃ gurukaṣāyakam  || 1,4.52 ||
sūte guṇānāṃ śatakoṭirvajre cābhre sahasraṃ kanake śataikam  |
tāre guṇāśītistadardhaṃ kānte tīkṣṇe catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ ravau tadardham  || 1,4.53 ||
rasādibhiryā kriyate cikitsā daivīti sadbhiḥ parikīrtitā sā  |
sā mānuṣī mantrakṛtā śiphādyaiḥ sā rākṣasī śastrakṛtādibhiryā  || 1,4.54 ||


 1, 5
{uparasanāmāni}
gandhakaṃ vajravaikrāntaṃ vajrābhraṃ tālakaṃ śilā  |
kharparaṃ śikhitutthaṃ ca vimalāṃ hemamākṣikam  || 1,5.1 ||
kāsīsaṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ varāṭīmatha hiṃgulam  |
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ śaṃkhabhūnāgaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaśca śilājatu  || 1,5.2 ||
ete uparasāḥ śodhyāḥ māryā drāvyāḥ puṭe kvacit || 1,5.3 ||
{gandha:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
apakvagandhaṃ kurute'ti kuṣṭhaṃ tāpaṃ bhramaṃ pittarujāṃ karoti  |
rūpaṃ sukhaṃ vīryabalaṃ ca hanti tasmātsaṃśuddhaṃ vidhiyojanīyam  || 1,5.4 ||
{gandhakaśodhana}
sājyaṃ bhāṇḍe payaḥ kṣiptvā mukhaṃ vastreṇa bandhayet |
tatpṛṣṭhe cūrṇitaṃ gandhaṃ kṣiptvā srāveṇa rodhayet || 1,5.5 ||
bhāṇḍaṃ nikṣipya bhūmyante ūrdhve deyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu  |
tataḥ kṣīre drutaṃ gandhaṃ śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 1,5.6 ||
{gandhakaśodhana (2)}
athavārkasnuhīkṣīrairvastraṃ lepyaṃ ca saptadhā  |
gandhakaṃ navanītena piṣṭvā vastraṃ pralepayet || 1,5.7 ||
tadvahnijvalitā deśe hṛtvā dhāryā hyadhomukhā  |
tailaṃ patedadhobhāṇḍe grāhyaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 1,5.8 ||
{sulfur:: śuddha:: medic. properties}
śuddho gandho haredrogān kuṣṭhamṛtyujvarādikān  |
agnikārī mahānuṣṇo vīryavṛddhiṃ karoti ca  || 1,5.9 ||
{vajra:: śodhana}
vyāghrīkandayutaṃ vajraṃ dolāyantreṇa pācitam  |
saptāhātkaudrave kvāthe kaulatthe vimalaṃ bhavet || 1,5.10 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
trisaptakṛtvastattaptaṃ kharamūtreṇa secayet |
ṣaḍguṇaistālakaṃ piṣṭvā tadgole kuliśaṃ kṣipet || 1,5.11 ||
pradhmātaṃ vājimūtreṇa siktaṃ pūrvoditakramaiḥ  |
bhasmībhavati tadvajraṃ vajravatkurute tanum  || 1,5.12 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
ūrṇāśṛṅgaṃ paripiṣya piṇḍametasya madhye tu nidhāya vajram  |
piṇḍe'thavādhāya ca vajravallyāḥ puṭatrayaṃ tasya rase vidadhyāt || 1,5.13 ||
mṛtyureva bhavedasya vajrākhyasya na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,5.14 ||
{vajra:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
aśuddhavajramāyurghnaṃ pīḍāṃ kuṣṭhaṃ karoti ca  |
pāṇḍutāpagurutvaṃ ca tasmācchuddhaṃ tu kārayet || 1,5.15 ||
{vajra:: subtypes:: colour, caste}
śvetaraktapītakṛṣṇā dvijādyāḥ vajrajātayaḥ  |
rasāyane bhavedvipraḥ śvetaḥ siddhipradāyakaḥ  || 1,5.16 ||
kṣatriyo mṛtyujidrakto valīpalitarogahā  |
dravakārī bhavedvaiśyaḥ pīto dehasya dārḍhyakṛt || 1,5.17 ||
kṛṣṇaḥ śūdro rujāṃ hanti vayaḥsthairyaṃ karoti ca  |
puṃstrīnapuṃsakāścaite lakṣaṇena tu lakṣayet || 1,5.18 ||
{vajra:: puṃvajra:: parīkṣā}
vṛttāḥ phalakasampūrṇāstejasvanto bṛhadbhavāḥ  |
puruṣāste samākhyātā rekhābinduvivarjitāḥ  || 1,5.19 ||
{vajra:: stīvajra:: parīkṣā}
rekhābindusamāyuktāḥ ṣaṭkoṇāstāḥ striyaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
{vajra:: napuṃsaka:: parīkṣā}
trikoṇāyattā dīrghā vijñeyāstā napuṃsakāḥ  || 1,5.20 ||
pūrvapūrvamime śastāḥ puruṣāḥ balavattarāḥ  |
śarīrakāntijanakā bhogadā vajrayoṣitaḥ  || 1,5.21 ||
napuṃsakāstvalpavīryāḥ kāmukāḥ sattvavarjitāḥ  |
strī tu strīṇāṃ pradātavyā klībaṃ klībe tathaiva ca  || 1,5.22 ||
sarveṣāṃ sarvadā yojyāḥ puruṣāḥ balavattarāḥ  || 1,5.23 ||
{vajra:: śodhana}
gṛhītvā tu śubhaṃ vajraṃ vyāghrīkandodare kṣipet |
mahiṣīviṣṭhayā lepyaṃ karīṣāgnau vipācayet || 1,5.24 ||
niśāyāṃ tu caturyāmaṃ niśānte vāśvamūtrake  |
secayettāni pratyekaṃ saptarātreṇa śudhyati  || 1,5.25 ||
{vajra:: śodhana}
meghanādā śamī śyāmā śṛṅgī madanakodbhavam  |
kulatthaṃ vetasaṃ cātha agastyaṃ sindhuvārakāḥ  || 1,5.26 ||
eteṣāṃ sajalaiḥ kvāthairvajraṃ jambīramadhyagam  |
dolāyantre tryahaṃ pācyamevaṃ vajraṃ viśuddhayet || 1,5.27 ||
{vajra:: śodhana}
kulatthakodravakvāthe dolāyantre vipācayet |
vyāghrīkandagataṃ vajraṃ saptāhācchuddhimṛcchati  || 1,5.28 ||
{vajra:: śodhana}
vyāghrīṃ kandagataṃ vajraṃ mṛdā liptaṃ puṭe pacet |
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya hayamūtreṇa secayet || 1,5.29 ||
vajrīkṣīreṇa vā siñcedevaṃ śuddhaṃ ca mārayet || 1,5.30 ||
{vajra:: brāhmaṇa:: māraṇa}
viprajātyādivajrāṇāṃ māraṇaṃ kathyate punaḥ  |
aśvatthabadarījhiṇṭīmākṣikaṃ karkaṭāsthi ca  || 1,5.31 ||
tulyaṃ snuhīpayaḥ piṣṭvā vajraṃ tadgolake kṣipet |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyādviprajātirmṛto bhavet || 1,5.32 ||
{vajra:: kṣatriya:: māraṇa}
karavīraṃ meṣaśṛṅgaṃ ca badaraṃ ca udumbaram  |
arkadugdhasamaṃ piṣṭvā vipravanmārayennṛpam  || 1,5.33 ||
{vajra:: vaiśya:: māraṇa}
balāṃ cātibalāṃ gandhaṃ peṣayetkacchapāsthi ca  |
etairvā vāruṇīdugdhaiḥ mriyedvaiśyo'pi vipravat || 1,5.34 ||
{vajra:: śūdra:: māraṇa}
sūraṇaṃ lasunaṃ śaṅkhaṃ samaṃ peṣyaṃ manaḥśilām  |
vaṭakṣīreṇa mūṣāntarvipravacchūdramāraṇam  || 1,5.35 ||
{vajra:: strī, napuṃsaka:: māraṇa}
striyasteṣāṃ mriyante ca tattadauṣadhayogataḥ  |
napuṃsakamṛtisteṣāṃ caturṇāmauṣadhaiḥ samam  || 1,5.36 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
dvivarṣarūḍhakārpāsairmūlaṃ kāntamukhaiḥ saha  |
nārīstanyena sampiṣya piṣṭvā dhmātaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 1,5.37 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
meṣaśṛṅgabhujaṅgāsthikūrmapṛṣṭhāmlavetasaiḥ  |
gajadantasamaṃ piṣṭvā vajrīdugdhena golakam  || 1,5.38 ||
kṛtvā tanmadhyagaṃ vajraṃ mriyate dhamanena tu  |
{vajra:: māraṇa}
trivarṣanāgavandhyāstu kārpāsasyātha mūlikā  |
piṣṭvā tanmadhyagaṃ vajraṃ kṛtvā mūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 1,5.39 ||
pacedgajapuṭe taṃ ca mriyate saptadhā puṭaiḥ  || 1,5.40 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
matkuṇānāṃ tu raktena saptadhātapaśoṣitam  |
kuliśaṃ bhāvitaṃ tadakdhrūrṇitāpi manaḥśilā  || 1,5.41 ||
liptvā ca badarīpatraiḥ veṣṭayitvā pure pacet |
punarlepyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ saptadhā mriyate'pi ca  || 1,5.42 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
vajraṃ mahānadīśuktau kṣiptaṃ bhāvyaṃ muhurmuhuḥ  |
snuhyarkonmattakanyānāṃ dvayeṇaikena cāhnikam  || 1,5.43 ||
kṛṣṇakarkaṭamāṃsena piṣṭitaṃ veṣṭayedbahiḥ  |
bhūnāgasya mṛdā samyagdhmāte bhasmatvamāpnuyāt || 1,5.44 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
raktotpalasya mūlaiśca meghanādasya kuḍmalaiḥ  |
piṇḍitairveṣṭitaṃ dhmātaṃ vajraṃ bhasma bhavatyalam  || 1,5.45 ||
{vajra:: mṛta:: medic. properties}
vajramāyurbalaṃ rūpaṃ dehasaukhyaṃ karoti ca  |
sevito hanti rogāṃśca mṛto vajro na saṃśayaḥ  |
{vaikrānta:: śodhana}
vaikrāntaṃ vajravacchodhyaṃ nīlaṃ vā lohitaṃ ca vā  |
{vaikrānta:: māraṇa}
hayamūtre tatsecyaṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ trisaptadhā  || 1,5.46 ||
tataścottaravāruṇyāḥ pañcāṅge golake kṣipet |
ruddhvā mūṣāpuṭe pacyāduddhṛtya golake punaḥ  || 1,5.47 ||
kṣiptvā ruddhvā pacedevaṃ saptadhā bhasmatāṃ vrajet |
{vaikrānta:: substitute forvajra}
bhasmībhūtaṃ ca vaikrāntaṃ vajrasthāne niyojayet || 1,5.48 ||


 1, 6
{abhra:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
aśuddhābhraṃ nihantyāyurvardhayenmārutaṃ kapham  |
ahataṃ chedayeddehaṃ mandāgnikrimidāyakam  || 1,6.1 ||
{abhra:: subtypes}
kṛṣṇaṃ pītaṃ sitaṃ raktaṃ yojyaṃ yoge rasāyane  |
pinākaṃ darduraṃ nāgaṃ vajraṃ ceti caturvidham  || 1,6.2 ||
pinākādyāstrayo varjyā vajraṃ yatnātsamāharet |
{pināka:: parīkṣā}
muñcatyagnau vinikṣipte pināko dalasaṃcayam  || 1,6.3 ||
ajñānādbhakṣaṇaṃ tasya mahākuṣṭhapradāyakam  |
{dardura}
darduro nihito hyagnau kurute darduradhvanim  || 1,6.4 ||
{nāga}
nāgaścāgnigataḥ śabdaṃ phūtkāraṃ ca vimuñcati  |
sa ca dehagato nityaṃ vyādhiṃ kuryādbhagandaram  || 1,6.5 ||
{vajrābhra}
vajrābhrakaṃ vahnisaṃsthaṃ na kiṃcidvikṛtiṃ vrajet |
tasmādvajrābhrakaṃ yojyaṃ vyādhivārddhakyamṛtyujit || 1,6.6 ||
{dhānyābhraka:: production}
dhamedvajrābhrakaṃ vahnau tataḥ kṣīre niṣecayet |
bhinnapatraṃ tu taṃ jñātvā meghanādadravāmlayoḥ  || 1,6.7 ||
bhāvayedaṣṭayāmaṃ taddhānyābhraṃ kārayetsudhīḥ  |
{dhānyābhraka:: production}
athavābhrasya bhāgau dvau ṭaṅkaścaikaṃ jalaiḥ saha  || 1,6.8 ||
dvidinaṃ sthāpayetpātre sūkṣmaṃ kṛtvā prapeṣayet |
baddhvā dhānyayutaṃ vastre mardayetkāñjikaiḥ saha  || 1,6.9 ||
adho yadgālitaṃ sūkṣmaṃ śuddhaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ bhavet || 1,6.10 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
punarnavāmeghanādadravairdhānyābhrakaṃ dinam  |
mardyaṃ gajapuṭe pacyātpunaściñcātha sūraṇaiḥ  || 1,6.11 ||
dravairmustabhavairmardyaṃ pṛthagdeyaṃ puṭatrayam  |
evaṃ markadalairveṣṭyaṃ deyaṃ vā mocasampuṭe  || 1,6.12 ||
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ yathā doṣeṣu yojayet |
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
goghṛtaistriphalāṃ kvāthaiḥ paktvā ca pūrvavatpacet || 1,6.13 ||
pañcaviṃśatpuṭaireva kāsamardyāḥ dravaiḥ pacet |
deyaṃ puṭatrayaṃ kṣīrairmardayecca puṭe puṭe  || 1,6.14 ||
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ jarāmṛtyurujāpaham  |
{abhra:: māraṇa}
dhānyābhrakasya bhāgaikaṃ dvau bhāgau ṭaṃkaṇasya ca  || 1,6.15 ||
piṣṭvā tadandhamūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā pacet |
svabhāvaśītalaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,6.16 ||
{dhānyābhraka:: māraṇa}
dhānyābhrakamamlaṃpiṣṭaṃ puṭe tapte'mlasecanam  |
tatpiṣṭvā dhārayetkhalve bhāvyamamlāranālakaiḥ  || 1,6.17 ||
taptaṃ taptaṃ cāranālaiḥ pācyaṃ śodhyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
puṭe vā dhamane pācyaṃ viṃśadvāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,6.18 ||
taptaṃ taptaṃ kṣipeddugdhe piṣṭvātha śoṣayetpunaḥ  |
dugdhataptaṃ puṭaṃ pacyāttaptaṃ dugdhena secayet || 1,6.19 ||
evaṃ trisaptavārāṇi śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ puṭe pacet |
peṣayitvā pacetsthālyāṃ lauhadarvyā vicālayet || 1,6.20 ||
dugdhasthaṃ ca tato dugdhaiḥ puṭe pacyātpunaḥ punaḥ  |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ pacyāddivā caikaṃ puṭe niśi  || 1,6.21 ||
taṇḍulī vajravallī ca tālamūlī punarnavā  |
cāṅgerī maricaṃ caiva balāyāḥ payasā saha  || 1,6.22 ||
ebhiśca peṣayeccābhraṃ pratyekaṃ taṃ tryahaṃ tryaham  |
sthitvā tapte puṭe paścātpratyekena punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,6.23 ||
piṣṭvā punaḥ puṭe ghṛṣṭaṃ kajjalābhaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 1,6.24 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
dhānyābhrakasya śuddhasya daśāṃśaṃ maricaṃ kṣipet |
peṣayedamlavargeṇa cāmle bhāvyaṃ dinatrayam  || 1,6.25 ||
taṃ śuṣkaṃ sampuṭe dhāmyaṃ khadirāṅgārakairdṛḍham  |
ūrdhvapātraṃ nirūpyātha secayedamlakena tat || 1,6.26 ||
agastyaśigruvarṣābhūmūlaistaṃ patrajai rasaiḥ  |
piṣṭvābhraṃ secayettena yadvānyāmlarasena ca  || 1,6.27 ||
sitāmadhvājyagokṣīrairdadhnāmlaṃ peṣyamabhrakam  |
matsyākṣyāḥ karavīrāyāḥ dravaiḥ piṣṭvā tridhā pacet || 1,6.28 ||
tato gajapure pācyaṃ niścandraṃ jāyate'bhrakam  |
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
dhānyābhrakaṃ dravairmardyaṃ matsyākṣītulasīdravaiḥ  || 1,6.29 ||
mūlajaiḥ kokilākṣasya kumārīśvetadūrvayoḥ  |
vyāghrīkandapunarnavayā dinametairvimardayet || 1,6.30 ||
kuñjarākhyaiḥ puṭaiḥ sapta piṣṭvā piṣṭvā pacetpunaḥ  |
tadvatpañcāmṛtaiḥ pācyaṃ piṣṭvā piṣṭvā tu saptadhā  || 1,6.31 ||
evaṃ niścandratāṃ yāti sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,6.32 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
dhānyābhraṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ tulyaṃ gomūtraistulasīdalaiḥ  |
vākucyāḥ sūraṇairalpairdinaṃ piṣṭvā puṭe pacet || 1,6.33 ||
jayantyāśca dravaiḥ paścānmardyaṃ mardya tridhā puṭet |
caturgajapuṭenaivaṃ niścandraṃ sarvarogajit || 1,6.34 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa}
dhānyābhrakaṃ ravikṣīraiḥ ravimūladravaiśca vā  |
mardyaṃ mardyaṃ puṭe pacyātsaptadhā mriyate dhruvam  || 1,6.35 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa}
dhānyābhrakaṃ tuṣāmlāmlairātape sthāpayeddinam  |
yāmaṃ mardyaṃ caturgolaṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 1,6.36 ||
evaṃ gokṣīramadhyasthaṃ sthāpyaṃ mardya puṭe pacet |
evaṃ kārpāsatoyena sthāpyaṃ peṣyaṃ puṭe pacet || 1,6.37 ||
tato'mlaiścaiva kārpāsairgavāṃ kṣīraiḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
gharmapākaṃ mardanaṃ ca puṭaṃ caivamanukramāt || 1,6.38 ||
evaṃ viṃśatpuṭe prāpte mṛto bhavati niścitam  || 1,6.39 ||
{abhra:: mṛta:: amṛtīkaraṇa}
sarveṣāṃ ghātitābhrāṇāmamṛtīkaraṇaṃ śṛṇu  |
triphalotthakaṣāyasya palānyādāya ṣoḍaśa  || 1,6.40 ||
gomūtrasya palānyaṣṭau mṛtābhrasya palāndaśa  |
ekīkṛtya lauhapātre pācayenmṛduvahninā  || 1,6.41 ||
drave jīrṇe samādāya sarvaṃ rogeṣu yojayet |
{abhra:: medic. properties}
anupānaṃ vinā hyabhraṃ jarāmṛtyurujāpaham  || 1,6.42 ||
yojayedanupānairvā tattadrogaharaṃ kṣaṇāt |
mṛtaṃ cābhraṃ haredrogān jarāmṛtyumanekadhā  || 1,6.43 ||
sevitaṃ dehadārḍhyaṃ ca rūpavīryaṃ vivardhayet || 1,6.44 ||


 1, 7
{haritāla:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
aśuddhatālamāyurghnaṃ kakamārutamehakṛt |
tāpaśophāṅgasaṃkocaṃ kurute tena śodhayet || 1,7.1 ||
{haritāla:: śodhana}
tālakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā daśāṃśena ca ṭaṅkaṇam  |
jambīrāṇāṃ dravaiḥ kṣālyaṃ kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayetpunaḥ  || 1,7.2 ||
vastraiścaturguṇairbaddhvā dolāyantre dinaṃ pacet |
saṃyuktaṃ cāranālena dinaṃ kuṣmāṇḍajaiḥ rasaiḥ  || 1,7.3 ||
tilatailaiḥ pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ ca triphalājalaiḥ  |
trivāraṃ tālakaṃ bhāvyaṃ piṣṭvā mūtraiśca kāñjikaiḥ  || 1,7.4 ||
tatphalairdaśabhirdeyaṃ ruddhvā puṭaṃ ca peṣayet |
evaṃ dvādaśadhā pācyaṃ śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 1,7.5 ||
{haritāla:: śodhana}
tālakaṃ poṭalībaddhaṃ saptāhaṃ kāñjike pacet |
dolāyantreṇa yāmaikaṃ tataḥ kuṣmāṇḍajaiḥ rasaiḥ  || 1,7.6 ||
tilataile pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ ca triphalājalaiḥ  |
evaṃ yantre caturyāmaṃ pācyaṃ śudhyati tālakaḥ  || 1,7.7 ||
{haritāla:: śuddha:: medic. properties}
tālako harate rogān kuṣṭhamṛtyujvarāpahaḥ  |
śodhitaḥ śītavīrye ca kurute vāyuvardhanam  || 1,7.8 ||
{manaḥśilā:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
aśmarīṃ mūtrahṛdrogamaśuddhā kurute śilā  |
mandāgniṃ malabandhaṃ ca śuddhā sarvarujāpahā  || 1,7.9 ||
{manaḥśilā:: śodhana}
ajāmūtre tryahaṃ pācyā dolāyantre manaḥśilā  |
saptadhā tairajāpittairgharme bhāvyaṃ viśuddhaye  || 1,7.10 ||
{manaḥśilā:: śodhana}
jayantībhṛṅgarājottharaktāgastyarasaiḥ śilā  |
dolāyantre dinaṃ pācyā yāmaṃ chāgasya mūtrake  || 1,7.11 ||
kṣālayedāranālena sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,7.12 ||
{rasaka:: śodhana}
naramūtraiśca gomūtraiḥ saptāhaṃ rasakaṃ pacet |
dolāyantreṇa samyaktacchuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 1,7.13 ||
{tuttha:: śodhana}
viṣṭhayā mardayetkhalve mārjārakapotayoḥ  |
daśāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dadyātpācyaṃ mṛdvagninā tataḥ  || 1,7.14 ||
puṭaṃ dadhnā puṭaṃ kṣaudrairdeyaṃ tutthaviśuddhaye  || 1,7.15 ||
{vimalā:: śodhana}
vimalā trividhaṃ pācyā rambhātoyena saṃyutā  |
amlavetasadhānyāmlameṣīmūtreṇa peṣayet || 1,7.16 ||
dolāyantre caturyāmaṃ śuddhireṣā mahottamā  |
{vimala:: śodhana}
karkaṭīmeṣaśṛṅgyutthadravairjambīrajairdravaiḥ  || 1,7.17 ||
bhāvayedātape tīvre vimalā śudhyati dhruvam  || 1,7.18 ||
{mākṣika:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
mandāgniṃ balahāniṃ ca vraṇaviṣṭambhanetraruk |
kurute mākṣiko mṛtyumaśuddho nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 1,7.19 ||
{mākṣika:: śodhana}
mākṣikaṃ naramūtreṇa kvāthayetkodravairdravaiḥ  |
vetasenāmlavargeṇa ṭaṃkaṇena kaṭutrikaiḥ  || 1,7.20 ||
dolāyantre dinaṃ pācyaṃ sūraṇasyaiva madhyagam  |
dinaṃ rambhādravaiḥ pacyāttaddhṛtvā peṣayedghṛtaiḥ  || 1,7.21 ||
eraṇḍatailasaṃyuktaṃ puṭe pacyādviśuddhaye  |
{mākṣika:: śodhana}
mākṣikasya trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ saindhavasya ca  || 1,7.22 ||
mātuluṅgadravairvātha jambīrotthadravaiḥ pacet |
lohapātre pacettāvadyāvatpātraṃ sulohitam  || 1,7.23 ||
tāmravarṇamayo yāti tāvacchudhyati mākṣikam  |
{mākṣika:: śodhana}
agastyapuṣpaniryāsaiḥ śigrumūlaṃ vigharṣayet || 1,7.24 ||
dravaiḥ pāṣāṇabhedyāśca pacyādebhiśca mākṣikam  |
tadvaṭīṃ cāndhamūṣāyāṃ viṃśadbhirupalaiḥ pacet || 1,7.25 ||
punaḥ piṣṭvātha rundhyācca puṭaiḥṣaḍbhirviśudhyati  || 1,7.26 ||
{mākṣika:: śodhana}
meghanādapāṣāṇabhedī piṣṭvā tatpiṇḍamadhye mākṣikaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā nikṣipet |
tadgolakaṃ vastre baddhvā dolāyantre kulatthakvāthe dinamekaṃ pacet |
etacchuddhalohānāṃ yuktasthāne māraṇe yojyam  || 1,7.27 ||
{mākṣika:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality}
bhaṅge suvarṇasaṃkāśo'ntaḥ kṛṣṇo bahiśchaviḥ  |
bṛhadvarṇamiti khyāto mākṣikaḥ śreṣṭha ucyate  || 1,7.28 ||
vyāpayatyaṅgamaṅgāni tailabindurivāmbhasi  |
na vinā śodhanaṃ sarve dhātavaḥ prabalādayaḥ  || 1,7.29 ||
rogopaśamakartāraḥ śodhanaṃ tena vakṣyate  || 1,7.30 ||
{coral:: māraṇa}
pravālānāṃ strīdugdhena bhāvanā paścāddhaṇḍikāmadhye sthāpayitvā nirudhyopari śarāvakaṃ dattvā lepayet |
vahnisaṃdīpanaṃ kṛtvā praharadvayena vidrumaṃ mriyate  || 1,7.31 ||
{uparasa (?):: māraṇa}
kulatthasya paceddroṇe vāridroṇena buddhimān  |
tena pādāvaśeṣeṇa kvāthe'ṣṭau maṇayaḥ śilā  || 1,7.32 ||
ātape tridinaṃ śuṣkaṃ kvāthasiktaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
muktācūrṇaṃ samādāya karakāmbuvibhāvitam  || 1,7.33 ||
ātape tridinaṃ bhāvyaṃ cūrṇitaṃ mṛtyumāpnuyāt || 1,7.34 ||
{śilājatu:: śodhana}
śaṅkhaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ caiva pūrvavacchodhayeddinam  |
gomūtraistriphalākvāthairbhṛṅgarājadravairjatum  || 1,7.35 ||
mardayedāyase pātre dinācchuddhiḥ śilājatoḥ  |
{darada:: śodhana}
meṣīkṣīreṇa daradamamlavargaiśca bhāvitam  || 1,7.36 ||
saptavāraṃ prayatnena śuddhimāyāti niścitam  |
{uparasa:: śodhana}
sūryāvartaṃ vajrakandaṃ kadalī devadālikā  || 1,7.37 ||
śigru kośātakī vandhyā kākamācī ca vāyasī  |
āsāmekarasenaiva trikṣārairlavaṇairyutam  || 1,7.38 ||
bhāvayedamlavargeṇa dinamekaṃ prayatnataḥ  |
sauvīraṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ śuddhabhūnāgamṛttikā  || 1,7.39 ||
śaṅkhaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ caiva sarvoparasāśca ye  |
pṛthagbhāvyaṃ vidhānena śuddhiṃ yānti dine dine  || 1,7.40 ||
tataḥ paścāttu taddrāvairdolāyantre dinaṃ sudhīḥ  |
śudhyante nātra sandehaḥ sarveṣu paramā amī  || 1,7.41 ||
muñcanti drutasattvāṃśca mataṃ sādhāraṇaṃ smṛtam  || 1,7.42 ||
{?:: sattvapātana}
gugguluṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ lākṣā majjā sarjarasaṃ punaḥ  |
ūrṇā guñjā kṣetramīnamasthīni śaśakasya ca  || 1,7.43 ||
guḍamadhvājyapiṇyākaṃ tutthaṃ peṣyamajājalaiḥ  |
sarvaṃ tulyaṃ ca dhānyābhraṃ bhūnāgaṃ mṛttikāpi ca  || 1,7.44 ||
kāntapāṣāṇatulyaṃ ca kaṭhinyuparasāśca ye  |
melayenmāhiṣaiḥ pacyāddadhyādigomayāntikaiḥ  || 1,7.45 ||
dṛḍhaṃ mardyaṃ vaṭīṃ kuryātkarṣamātraṃ tu śoṣayet |
koṣṭhīyantre dhamedgāḍhamaṅgāraiśca cirodbhavaiḥ  || 1,7.46 ||

pūrvadrāvaiḥ sahāloḍya ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ viṃśatpuṭe paktvā mṛtaṃ bhavati bhasmasāt || 1,8.95 ||
{tin:: māraṇa}
vaṅgapādena sūtena vaṅgapatrāṇi lepayet |
ciñcāvṛkṣasya saṃgṛhya cāntaśchannaṃ ca taṇḍulaiḥ  || 1,8.96 ||
piṣṭvā tatpiṇḍamadhye tu vaṅgapatrāṇi lepayet |
śirīṣarajanīcūrṇaiḥ kumāryāḥ śubhagolakam  || 1,8.97 ||
sūtaliptaṃ vaṅgapatraṃ golake samalepitam  |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pakvaṃ pūrvasaṃkhyā mṛto bhavet || 1,8.98 ||
{tin:: māraṇa}
akṣabhallātakaṃ toyaiḥ piṣṭvā tāni vilepayet |
tatastilakhalī madhye kṣiptvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet || 1,8.99 ||
gajākhye jāyate bhasma catvāriṃśativaṅgakam  |
{tin:: mṛta:: medic. properties}
satiktalavaṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ pāṇḍughnaṃ krimimehajit || 1,8.100 ||
lekhinaṃ pittalaṃ kiṃcitsarvadehāmayāpaham  || 1,8.101 ||


 1, 9
{iron:: aśuddha:: medic. properties}
aśuddhamamṛtaṃ lauhamāyurhānirujākaram  |
hṛtpīḍāṃ ca tṛṣāṃ jāḍyaṃ tasmācchuddhaṃ ca mārayet || 1,9.1 ||
{kāntaloha:: parīkṣā}
pātre yasminprasarati na cettailabindurvisṛṣṭaḥ  |
hiṅgurgandhaṃ prasarati nijaṃ tiktatāṃ nimbukaśca  |
pāke dagdhaṃ bhavati śikharākāratā naiva bhūmau  |
kāntaṃ lauhaṃ tadidamuditaṃ lakṣaṇoktaṃ na cānyat || 1,9.2 ||
kāntaṃ mṛdutaraṃ tāraṃ rukmābhaṃ timiraṃ karam  |
svāduryato bhavennimbakalko rātriniveśitaḥ  || 1,9.3 ||
kāntaṃ taduttamaṃ yacca rūpyenāvartitaṃ milet |
sarvarogaharametatsarvakuṣṭhaharaṃ param  || 1,9.4 ||
{iron:: subtypes:: śodhana}
śaśachāgaraktasaṃliptaṃ trivāraṃ cāgnitāpitam  |
kāntādimuṇḍaparyantaṃ sarvarogaharaṃ param  || 1,9.5 ||
{iron:: śodhana:: adri}
triphalāṣṭaguṇaistoyaistriphalāṣoḍaśaṃ palam  |
tatkvāthe pādaśeṣe tu lauhasya patrapañcakam  || 1,9.6 ||
kṛtvā patrāṇi taptāni saptavārāṇi secayet |
evaṃ pralīyate doṣo girijo lauhasambhavaḥ  || 1,9.7 ||
{iron:: śodhana}
trividhaṃ lauhacūrṇaṃ vā gomūtraiḥ ṣaḍguṇaiḥ pacet |
prakṣālayedāranāle śoṣyaṃ śuddhimavāpnuyāt || 1,9.8 ||
{iron:: subtypes:: śodhana}
raktamālā haṃsapādo gojihvā triphalāmṛtā  |
gopālī tumbururdantī tulyagomūtrapeṣitam  || 1,9.9 ||
asminmadhye lauhapattraṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ dvisaptadhā  |
secayetkāntamuṇḍāntaṃ sarvadoṣāpanuttaye  || 1,9.10 ||
sarveṣvauṣadhakalpeṣu lauhakalpaṃ praśasyate  |
tasmātsarvaṃ prayatnena lauhamādau vimārayet || 1,9.11 ||
ayaḥ pañcapalādūrdhvaṃ yāvatpalatrayodaśāt |
ādau mantrastataḥ karma yathākartavyamucyate  || 1,9.12 ||
{iron:: māraṇa}
hiṅgulasya palān pañca nārīstanyena peṣayet |
tena lauhasya patrāṇi lepayetpalapañcakam  || 1,9.13 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyātkaṣāyaistraiphalaiḥ punaḥ  |
jambīrairāranālairvā viṃśatyaṃśena hiṅgulam  || 1,9.14 ||
piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭellohaṃ tathaivaṃ pācayetpunaḥ  |
catvāriṃśatpuṭairevaṃ kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam  || 1,9.15 ||
ho mriyate trālasande dattvā dattvā ca hiṅgulam  |
{iron:: māraṇa}
arjunasya tvacā peṣyā kāñjikenātilohitā  || 1,9.16 ||
tanmadhye lohacūrṇaṃ ca kāṃsyapātre vinikṣipet |
dinaikaṃ bhāvayedgharme dravaiḥ pūryaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,9.17 ||
arjunaiḥ sāranālairvā trividhaṃ mārayedayaḥ  |
{iron:: māraṇa}
dantīpatraṃ dravaṃ yacca lauhacūrṇaṃ viloḍayet || 1,9.18 ||
dinaikaṃ bhāvayedgharme dravaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
ruddhvā rātrau puṭaiḥ pacyādebhirdrāvaiśca bhāvayet || 1,9.19 ||
evamaṣṭadinaṃ kuryāttrividhaṃ mriyate hyayaḥ  |
{iron:: māraṇa}
ciñcāpatranibhāṃ kuryāttrividhaṃ lohapatrakam  || 1,9.20 ||
mṛtpātrasthaṃ kṣipedgharme dantyā drāvaiḥ prapūrayet |
patraṃ punaḥ punastāvadyāvajjvarati vai tvayaḥ  || 1,9.21 ||
mriyate tīvragharmeṇa cūrṇīkṛtya niyojayet |
{iron:: māraṇa}
kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ tathā muṇḍacūrṇaṃ matsyākṣajairdravaiḥ  || 1,9.22 ||
ātape tridinaṃ bhāvyaṃ dvidinaṃ citrakadravaiḥ  |
trikaṇṭakadravaistryahaṃ sahadevyā dravaistryaham  || 1,9.23 ||
gomūtraistriphalākvāthe bhāvayecca tryahaṃ tryaham  |
dhātakyāśca tato mardyaṃ kramāddeyaṃ puṭaṃ puṭam  || 1,9.24 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭenaivaṃ mṛtaṃ yogeṣu yojayet |
{iron:: māraṇa}
dravaiḥ kuraṇṭapatrotthaiḥ lauhacūrṇaṃ vimardayet || 1,9.25 ||
dinaikamātape tīvre dravairmardyaṃ trikaṇṭakaiḥ  |
vandhyābhṛṅgīpunarnavayorgomūtraiśca dinaṃ punaḥ  || 1,9.26 ||
gomūtraistriphalā kvāthyā tatkaṣāyeṇa bhāvayet |
trisaptāhaṃ prayatnena dinaikaṃ mardayettataḥ  || 1,9.27 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pañcādiṃ kvāthena mardayet |
divā mardyaṃ puṭaṃ rātrāvekaviṃśatidināni vai  || 1,9.28 ||
ekaviṃśaddinenaiva mriyate trividhaṃ hyayaḥ  |
{iron:: māraṇa}
mākṣikaṃ ca śilā hyamlairharidrā maricāni ca  || 1,9.29 ||
piṣṭvā mardyaṃ lohapatraṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ niṣecayet |
saptadhā triphalākvāthe jalena kṣālayetpunaḥ  || 1,9.30 ||
kuṭṭayellohadaṇḍena peṣayettriphalājalaiḥ  |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena lohasya dātavyaṃ mākṣikaṃ śilā  || 1,9.31 ||
amlena loḍitaṃ ruddhvā gajāndhakapuṭe pacet |
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma kāntaṃ tīkṣṇādimuṇḍakam  || 1,9.32 ||
{iron:: māraṇa}
tindūphalasya majjābhirliptvā sthāpyātape khare  |
dhārayetkāṃsyapātrasthaṃ dinaikena puṭatyalam  || 1,9.33 ||
lepyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryātdināntāntaṃ pralepayet |
triphalākvāthasaṃyuktaṃ dinaikena mṛtaṃ bhavet || 1,9.34 ||
{iron:: māraṇa}
sthālyāṃ vā lohapātre vā lauhadarvyā viloḍayet |
pācayettriphalākvāthe dinaikaṃ lohacūrṇakam  || 1,9.35 ||
tatpiṇḍaṃ triphalātoyaiḥ piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena mūṣāyāṃ nirvāte'harniśaṃ pacet || 1,9.36 ||
evaṃ tridhā prakartavyaṃ sthālīpākaṃ puṭāntaram  |
bhṛṅgyā drāvaṃ tālamūlī hastikarṇasya mūlakam  || 1,9.37 ||
śatāvarī vidāryāśca mūlakvāthe ca traiphale  |
piṣṭvā tatpūrvavatsthālyāṃ pācyaṃ peṣyaṃ puṭe tridhā  || 1,9.38 ||
tataḥ punarnavātoyairdaśamūlakaṣāyakaiḥ  |
bṛhatyāśca kaṣāyairvā bījapūrasya toyataḥ  || 1,9.39 ||
brahmabījastathāśigrukvāthe gopayasāpi vā  |
pratyekena prapeṣyādau pūrvagarbhapuṭe pacet || 1,9.40 ||
bhāvayettu dravenaiva puṭānte yāmamātrakam  |
pratyekena kramādevaṃ piṣṭvā puṭaiśca bhāvayet || 1,9.41 ||
mriyate nātra saṃdehaḥ kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam  |
sarvametanmṛtaṃ lauhaṃ dhmātavyaṃ mitrapañcakaiḥ  |
yadyevaṃ syānnirutthānaṃ sevyaṃ vāritaraṃ bhavet || 1,9.42 ||
{iron:: māraṇa}
madhvājyaṃ mṛtaṃ lauhaṃ ca sarūpyaṃ saṃpuṭe kṣipet |
ruddhvā dabhāyaṃ tu saṃgrāhyaṃ rūpyaṃ ca pūrvamānakam  || 1,9.43 ||
tadā lauhaṃ mṛtaṃ vidyādamṛtaṃ mārayetpunaḥ  || 1,9.44 ||
{iron:: māraṇa:: niruttha}
gandhakaṃ tu mṛtaṃ lauhaṃ tulyaṃ khalve vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ kanyakādrāvai ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 1,9.45 ||
ityevaṃ sarvalohānāṃ kartavyo'yaṃ nirutthitaḥ  || 1,9.46 ||
{iron:: māraṇa:: vāritara}
śuddhasūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ kṛtvā khalve tu kajjalīm  |
dvayoḥ samaṃ lauhacūrṇaṃ mardayetkanyakādravaiḥ  || 1,9.47 ||
yāmadvayātsamuddhṛtya tadgolaṃ tāmrapātrake  |
ācchādyairaṇḍapatraiśca yāmārddheṇoṣṇatāṃ vrajet || 1,9.48 ||
dhānyarāśau nyasetpaścāttridinānte samuddharet |
sampiṣya gālayedvastre sadyo vāritaraṃ bhavet || 1,9.49 ||
kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ tathā muṇḍaṃ nirutthaṃ jāyate mṛtam  |
svarṇādīnmārayedevaṃ cūrṇīkṛtya tu lohavat || 1,9.50 ||
siddhayogamidaṃ khyātaṃ siddhānāṃ sammukhāgatam  |
{iron:: mṛta:: medic. use}
annabhūtamāyasādyaṃ sarvarogajvarāpaham  || 1,9.51 ||
triphalārasasaṃyuktaṃ sarvarogeṣu yojayet || 1,9.52 ||
mṛtāni lauhāni vaśībhavanti  |
nighnanti yuktyā hyakhilāmayāni  |
abhyāsayogāddṛḍhayogasiddham  |
kurvanti ruṅmṛtyujarāvināśam  || 1,9.53 ||
{iron:: mṛta:: amṛtīkaraṇa}
toyāṣṭabhāgaśeṣena triphalāpalapañcakam  |
ghṛtaṃ kvāthasya tulyaṃ syāccūrṇaṃ tulyaṃ mṛtāyasam  || 1,9.54 ||
pācayettāmrapātre ca lauhadarvyā vicālayet |
mṛdvagninā pacettāvadyāvajjīryati gandhakam  || 1,9.55 ||
lauhatulyā śivā yojyā supakvenaivāvatārayet |
yogavāhamidaṃ khyātaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ mahāmṛtam  || 1,9.56 ||
evaṃ kāntasya tīkṣṇasya muṇḍasyāpi vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ  |
{iron:: mṛta:: amṛtīkaraṇa}
guḍasya kuḍave pakvaṃ lauhabhasma palānvitam  || 1,9.57 ||
kolapramāṇaṃ rogeṣu tacca yogena yojayet |
ghṛtaṃ tulyaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ lohapātragataṃ pacet || 1,9.58 ||
jīrṇe ghṛtaṃ samādāya yogavāheṣu yojayet || 1,9.59 ||
omamṛtena bhakṣyāya namaḥ anena manunā lauhaṃ bhakṣayet |
{iron:: mṛta:: medic. properties}
āyurvīryaṃ balaṃ datte pāṇḍumehādikuṣṭhanut |
āmavātaharaṃ lauhaṃ valīpalitanāśanam  || 1,9.60 ||
{upaloha:: śodhana}
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ saptadhāmlena mardayet |
kāṃsyāraghoṣapatrāṇi tilakalkena lepayet || 1,9.61 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyācchuddhimāyāti nānyathā  |
{upaloha:: māraṇa}
tāmravanmārayetteṣāṃ kṛtvā sarvatra yojayet || 1,9.62 ||
{bronśe:: medic. properties}
kāṃsyaṃ kaṣāyamuṣṇaṃ ca laghu rūkṣaṃ ca tiktakam  |
kaphapittarujaṃ hanti hṛddehāyuṣyavardhanam  || 1,9.63 ||
{brass:: medic. properties}
rītikā ca galaṃ rūkṣamatiktalavaṇaṃ saram  |
śodhanaṃ sarvarogaghnaṃ balavīryāyuṣyavarddhanam  || 1,9.64 ||
{maṇḍūra:: production}
alpāṅgāre dhametkiṭṭaṃ lauhajaṃ ca gavāṃ jalaiḥ  |
secayedakṣapatraiśca saptavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1,9.65 ||
maṇḍūro'yaṃ samākhyātaḥ śuddhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ niyojayet |
kiṭṭācchataguṇaṃ muṇḍaṃ muṇḍāttīkṣṇaṃ śatādhikam  || 1,9.66 ||
tīkṣṇāllakṣaguṇaṃ kāntaṃ bhakṣaṇātkurute guṇam  |
tasmātkāntaṃ sadā sevyaṃ jarāmṛtyuharaṃ param  || 1,9.67 ||


 1, 10
tailānāṃ pātanaṃ vakṣye sūryapāke'thavānale  |
yantrayogena yattailaṃ grāhyaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 1,10.1 ||
{dhattūra:: oil}
dhattūrabījacūrṇāni vastrapūtāni kārayet |
ālipya kāṃsyapātraṃ tu dhārayedātape khare  || 1,10.2 ||
sutaptaṃ vastrapūtaṃ ca pātayettailamāharet |
{śigru:: oil}
śigrupuṣkarabījānāṃ bījasya mārkavasya ca  || 1,10.3 ||
grāhyaṃ dhattūravattailamekaikasya pṛthakpṛthak |
yathā dhattūrajaṃ tailaṃ kvāthādgharme samuddhṛtam  || 1,10.4 ||
tathā sarvatra tailāni saṃgrāhyānyauṣadhāntaraiḥ  |
{aṅkoṭa:: oil}
aṅkoṭasyāpi tailaṃ syātkākatuṇḍayā samūlayā  || 1,10.5 ||
vākucīdevadālyāśca karkoṭīmūlataḥ bhavet |
apāmārgakaṣāyeṇa tailaṃ syādviṣatuṇḍajam  || 1,10.6 ||
mūlakvāthaiḥ kumāryāstu tailaṃ jaipālajaṃ bhavet |
kvāthena raktamārgasya vākucītailamāharet || 1,10.7 ||
kvāthena cendravāruṇyāstailamāragvadhaṃ bhavet |
kākatuṇḍyapāmārgotthakvāthāttailaṃ samāharet || 1,10.8 ||
{kaṭutumbī:: oil}
bījāni kaṭutumbyāśca gomayena viloḍayet |
śuṣkaṃ dhānyatuṣaiḥ sārddhaṃ kuṭṭayecca ulūkhale  || 1,10.9 ||
nistuṣaṃ taṃ vicūrṇyātha bhṛṅgarājarasaiḥ saha  |
mardayitvātape tailaṃ gṛhṇīyātpīḍane sati  || 1,10.10 ||
{oilfroṃ kṛṣṇā andkākatuṇḍī}
kṛṣṇāyāḥ kākatuṇḍyāśca bījaṃ cūrṇāni kārayet |
kāntapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ caikīkṛtya nirodhayet || 1,10.11 ||
dhānyarāśigataṃ paktvā uddhṛte tailamāharet |
dhātrīphalarasairbhāvyaṃ cūrṇaṃ pāṣāṇabījakam  || 1,10.12 ||
dinaikaṃ ca tato yantre tailaṃ grāhyaṃ ca tailake  |
{oil:: froṃ guñjā andkarañja}
guñjākarañjaphalaṃ ca naramūtreṇa bhāvayet || 1,10.13 ||
saptavāraṃ tato gharme lepayetkāṃsyabhājanam  |
uddhṛtya dhārayedgharme tailaṃ patati pīḍanāt || 1,10.14 ||
{oil:: jyotiṣmatī}
vardhamānāranālena piṣṭvā cūrṇaṃ vibhāvayet |
jyotiṣmatyutthabījānāmātape tailamāharet || 1,10.15 ||
{oil:: putrajīva}
putrajīvasya bījānāṃ cūrṇamagastyabījajam  |
āmrātavatprakartavyaṃ tatastailaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,10.16 ||
{oil:: bilva}
nārikelāmbunā bhāvyaṃ bilvabījasya cūrṇakam  |
dinaikaṃ tailayantreṇa tailamākṛṣya yojayet || 1,10.17 ||
{oil:: aṅkola}
nistuṣāṅkolabījānāṃ sukhaṃ kiṃcidvigharṣayet |
pralepayetkāṃsyapātre piṣṭvā caṇakalepane  || 1,10.18 ||
tanmukhe ṭaṃkaṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ kiṃcitkiṃcitpralepayet |
dhārayedātape tīvre mukhāttailaṃ samāharet || 1,10.19 ||
{keśataila}
śamīcūrṇasamaṃ piṣṭvā chidramāṇḍe niveśayet |
chidrādhaḥ sthāpayedbhāṇḍaṃ chidre keśaṃ ca dāpayet || 1,10.20 ||
jalena secayeddravyaṃ chidrādho grāhayecca tam  |
tanmadhye dhṛtakeśasya kṣipedūrdhvaṃ puṭaṃ śanaiḥ  || 1,10.21 ||
tatkṣaṇācchravarūpaṃ ca keśatailamidaṃ bhavet |
{oil:: extraction (gen.)}
apakvabhānupatrāṇāṃ rasamādāya bhāvayet || 1,10.22 ||
samastaṃ bījacūrṇaṃ coktānuktaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
ātape mucyate tailaṃ sādhyāsādhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 1,10.23 ||
{oil}
tathaivottaravāruṇyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa samāharet |
tailaṃ samastabījānāṃ grāhayedātape khare  || 1,10.24 ||
{oil}
sarvabījāsthimāṃsānāṃ śuṣkaṃ piṣṭvā hyanekadhā  |
sarvabījeṣu vā tailaṃ grāhyaṃ pātālayantrake  || 1,10.25 ||
{oil}
vaṃśādisarvakāṣṭhānāṃ nārikelakapālakam  |
tuṣadhānyādibījānāṃ garbhayantreṇa tailakam  || 1,10.26 ||
grāhayetsarvabījānāṃ taṃ ca yogeṣu yojayet || 1,10.27 ||
{vatsanābha}
vatsanābhaṃ viṣaṃ svādu dīpanaṃ kaphavātajit |
tridoṣaśamanaṃ yogayuktaṃ sudhāmayaṃ bhavet || 1,10.28 ||
bṛṃhaṇaṃ balavīryasya vāḍabāgniśatopamam  |
sannipāte pratīkāre prabhavaḥ prabhavo'sya hi  || 1,10.29 ||
uddhṛtaṃ phalapākānte navaṃ snigdhaṃ ghanaṃ guru  |
avyāpakaṃ viṣaharaṃ vātātapaviśoṣitam  || 1,10.30 ||
raktasarṣapatailena liptaṃ vāsasi dhārayet |
athavāpi yathā prāptaṃ viṣaṃ gomūtrasaṃyutam  || 1,10.31 ||
ātape tridinaṃ śuṣkaṃ nihitaṃ vīryadhṛkbhavet |
mṛtaṃ sūtābhrakaṃ lauhaṃ viṣaṃ ca tulyavīryakam  || 1,10.32 ||
tasmādviṣaṃ yogavāhe yojyaṃ yoge rasāyane  |
tāni caiva tu mānāni aṣṭau ṣaḍvā caturthakam  || 1,10.33 ||
mātrātrayaṃ samākhyātamuttamādhamamadhyam  |
dātavyaṃ sarvarogeṣu ghṛtāśine hitāśine  || 1,10.34 ||
kṣīrāśine pradātavyaṃ rasāyanavate nare  |
na krodhine na pittāḍhye na klībe rājayakṣmaṇi  || 1,10.35 ||
kṣuttṛṣṇāśramakarmādhvaśoṣiṇe kṣayarogiṇe  |
garbhiṇībālavṛddheṣu na viṣaṃ rājamandire  || 1,10.36 ||
na dātavyaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ visaṃvāde kadācana  |
ācāryeṇa tu bhoktavyaṃ śiṣyapratyayakārakam  || 1,10.37 ||
anena mantreṇa mardayedbhūmau na sthāpayet |
amṛtamiti vadediti kramo'yam  |
oṃ siddhagurubhyo namaḥ  |
paramagurubhyo namaḥ  |
parātparagurubhyo namaḥ  |
parameṣṭhigurubhyo namaḥ  |
{vegetable poisons}
saktukaṃ mustakaṃ śṛṅgī vālakaṃ sarṣapāhvayam  |
vatsanābhaṃ ca kūrmaśca śvetaśṛṅgī tathāṣṭamam  || 1,10.38 ||
ityaṣṭau yojayedyoge kālakūṭādi varjayet |
{poisons}
kālakūṭaṃ meṣaśṛṅgī hālāhalaṃ ca darduram  || 1,10.39 ||
karkaṭaṃ markaṭaṃ granthi hāridraṃ raktaśṛṅgakam  |
keśavaṃ daśamaṃ ceti varjanīyaṃ bhiṣagvaraiḥ  || 1,10.40 ||
saktukādyān prayuñjīta sarvaroge rasāyane  |
etadviṣaṃ jāticatuṣṭayaṃ ca vicārya yojyaṃ bhiṣaguttamena  || 1,10.41 ||
vipro rakṣati yauvanaṃ narapatistadbhūtale pālatāṃ vaiśyaḥ kuṣṭhavināśane ca kuśalaḥ śūdro harejjīvanam  |
tasmāccāpi bhiṣagvareṇa nipuṇaistadvedinā bhāvayetkuryādeva tato viṣaṃ nṛpavaro mṛtyuṃjayāya kṣitau  || 1,10.42 ||
śvetā vā yadi vā piṅgā madhurā ūṣarāpi vā  |
lomaśā brahmajātiḥ syātkṣatrajātistu lohitā  || 1,10.43 ||
pītā vā madhurā kiṃcidvaiśyajātistu dhūsarā  |
kṛṣṇā śūdrasya dṛśyeta eteṣāṃ ca bhiṣagvaraiḥ  || 1,10.44 ||
kṣīraṃ sampūrṇabhāṇḍe'pi viṣaṃ dattvā vicintayet |
jāyate'pi yadā varṇaṃ tadā jātiṃ vinirdiśet || 1,10.45 ||
śuklaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ceti caturvidham  |
brahmakṣatraviṭśūdrāṇāṃ jñātavyo jātinirṇayaḥ  || 1,10.46 ||
kṣiptaṃ dugdhe viṣaṃ vaidyo jānīyātkramaśo yadi  |
śvetaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ coṣṇalameva ca  || 1,10.47 ||
tutthena ṭaṅkaṇenaiva mriyate peṣaṇādviṣam  |
viṣeṣu jaṅgamākhyeṣu viṣaṃ nāgabhavaṃ hitam  || 1,10.48 ||
idameva mahāśreṣṭhaṃ tridoṣakṣapaṇaṃ kṣaṇāt |
dīpanaṃ kurute sadyo vaḍavāgniśatopamam  || 1,10.49 ||
saṃnipātapratikāre prabhāvaḥ prabhavo hi saḥ  || 1,10.50 ||
nāgodbhavaṃ yathāprāptaṃ viṣaṃ gomūtrasaṃyutam  |
ātape tridinaṃ śuṣkaṃ nihitaṃ vīryadhṛgbhavet || 1,10.51 ||
atimātraṃ yadā bhuṅkte tadārjya ṭaṅkaṇaṃ pibet |
rajanī meghanādā vā sarpākṣī vā ghṛtānvitā  || 1,10.52 ||
lihedvā madhusarpirbhyāṃ cūrṇitāmarjunatvacam  |
putrajīvakamajjāṃ vā pibedvā nimbakadvayam  || 1,10.53 ||
evaṃ viṣavidhiḥ khyātaḥ prayogaṃ ca vadāmyaham  |
viṣaṃ trikaṭukaṃ mustaṃ haridrānimbapatrakam  || 1,10.54 ||
viḍaṅgamaṣṭamaṃ cūrṇaṃ chāgamūtraiḥ samaṃ samam  |
caṇakābhā vaṭī khyātā syājjayā yogavāhikā  || 1,10.55 ||
viṣaṃ pāṭhāśvagandhāśca balā tālīsapattrakam  |
maricaṃ pippalī nimbamajāmūtreṇa tulyakam  || 1,10.56 ||
vaṭikā pūrvavatkāryā vaṭikā yogavāhikā  |
nidrāṃ tandrāṃ klamaṃ dāhaṃ saphenaṃ lomaharṣaṇam  || 1,10.57 ||
śoṣaṃ caivātisāraṃ ca kurute jaṅgamaṃ viṣam  |
sthāvaraṃ tu jvaraṃ hikkāṃ dantaharṣaṃ galagraham  || 1,10.58 ||
phenachardyaruciśvāsaṃ mūrcchāṃ ca kurute viṣam  |
na jānāti yadā mantrī viṣaṃ bhakṣeccikitsitam  || 1,10.59 ||
viṣameva tadādāya majjatyambunidhāviva  |
tasmādyatnena saṃrakṣedrājā viṣacikitsakāt || 1,10.60 ||
prathamaṃ vahnikharparikāyāṃ manāgbhṛṣṭvā vakṣyamāṇamantreṇa nirviṣaṃ vidhāya gṛhṇīyāditi  |
nimbudrave pittaṃ vāratrayaṃ vibhāvya prakṣālya saṃśoṣya gṛhṇīyāditi  |
{śilājatu:: origin}
hemādyāḥ sūryasaṃtaptāḥ sravanti giridhātavaḥ  |
jatvābhaṃ mṛdu mṛtsnābhaṃ yanmalaṃ tacchilājatu  || 1,10.61 ||
{śilājatu:: medic. properties}
anaghaṃ cālpakaṣāyaṃ ca kaṭu pāke śilājatu  |
nātyuṣṇaśītaṃ dhātubhyaścaturbhyastasya sambhavaḥ  || 1,10.62 ||
{śilājatu:: froṃ gold:: medic. properties}
hemno'tha rajatāttāmrādvaraṃ kālāyasādapi  |
madhuraṃ ca satiktaṃ ca japāpuṣpanibhaṃ ca yat || 1,10.63 ||
vipāke kaṭu śītaṃ ca tatsuvarṇasya nisṛtam  |
{śilājatu:: froṃ silver:: medic. properties}
rājataṃ kaṭukaṃ śvetaṃ śītaṃ svādu vipacyate  || 1,10.64 ||
{śilājatu:: froṃ copper:: medic. properties}
tāmrādbarhiṇakaṇṭhābhaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ pacyate kaṭu  |
{śilājatu:: fromiron:: medic. properties}
yacca guggulusaṃkāśaṃ satiktaṃ lavaṇānvitam  || 1,10.65 ||
vipāke kaṭu śītaṃ ca sarvaśreṣṭhaṃ tadāyasam  |
gomūtragandhaḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvakarmasu yaugikāḥ  || 1,10.66 ||
rasāyanaprayogeṣu paścimaṃ tu viśiṣyate  |
yathākramaṃ vātapitte śleṣmapitte kaphe triṣu  || 1,10.67 ||
viśeṣeṇa praśasyante malā hemādidhātujāḥ  |
{śilājatu:: phys. properties}
lauhaḥ kiṭṭāyate vahnau vidhūmaṃ dahyate'mbhasi  || 1,10.68 ||
tṛṇādyagre kṛtaṃ śreṣṭhamadho galati tantuvat |
daṃśadaṃṣṭrauṣadhādidoṣaharaṇārthaṃ meṣaśṛṅgaṃ bhūrjapatreṇa dhūpayet |
kvāthyadravyaṃ śilājatusamaṃ caturguṇena jalaṃ dattvā caturbhāgāvaśeṣeṇa bhāvayedityekaḥ pakṣaḥ  |
vāgbhaṭṭastu aṣṭaguṇajaladānenāṣṭāvaśeṣe pūrvavadubhayathaiva vyavahāraḥ  |
bhadraśilājatu triphalādaśamūloṣṇakvāthe nikṣipya  |
kevaloṣṇodake vā sthite ūrdhvaṃ vībhūte paddhapatravatsarvaṃ grāhyam  |
tataḥ śivāguḍikoktakrameṇa bhāvanāṃ dattvā viśodhya sālasārādinā bhāvayedyathā  |
sālayugmau karañjau dvau khadiraṃ candanadvayam  |
gardabhāṇḍo'rjunaśceha lodhrayugmadhavāsanāḥ  || 1,10.69 ||
śirīṣāgurukālīyapūgapūtikakarkaṭāḥ  |
sālasārādirapyeṣa gaṇaḥ śleṣmagadāpahaḥ  || 1,10.70 ||
mehagulmārśaḥkuṣṭhārimedaḥpāṇḍurujāpahaḥ  |
ebhirdivātape śoṣyaṃ rātrau rātrau ca bhāvayet || 1,10.71 ||
draveṇa yāvatā dravyamekībhūyārdratāṃ vrajet |
bhavetpramāṇaṃ nirdiṣṭaṃ bhiṣagbhirbhāvanāvidhau  || 1,10.72 ||
bhaveddravyaṃ samaṃ kvāthyaṃ kvāthaṃ cāṣṭāvaśeṣitam  |
tenārdrasamaṃ dravyaṃ śoṣayetprabalātape  || 1,10.73 ||
dagdhahīrakaṃ yojyaṃ nikṣipyāgnau dhmāpayitvā nirguṇḍīrasena saptavārānnirvāpya prakṣālya ca gṛhṇīyāt || 1,10.74 ||
jāyante vāmarukvāthe janapade grīṣme'rkatāpārditāḥ  |
śītoṣaṇe śiśire ca guggulurasaṃ muñcanti te pañcadhā  || 1,10.75 ||
hemābhaṃ mahiṣākṣatulyamaparaṃ tatpadmarāgopamaṃ bhṛṅgābhaṃ kumudadyutiṃ ca vidhinā grāhyā parīkṣyā tataḥ  || 1,10.76 ||
vahnau jvalanti tapane vilayaṃ prayānti klidyanti koṣṇasalile payasā samānāḥ  || 1,10.77 ||
grāhyāḥ śubhāḥ parihareccirakālajātānaṅgātsphuṭaṃ kharparagandhikatulyavarṇān  || 1,10.78 ||
{guggulu:: medic. properties}
svāde svāduḥ kaṣāyatiktakaṭuko vīrye vipāke kaṭuḥ  |
vṛṣyo mārgaviśodhane'tiviśadastīkṣṇo vikāśī saraḥ  || 1,10.79 ||
sāyuṣyaḥ svaradastridoṣaśamano medhāsmṛtiśrīkaraḥ  |
dhanyaḥ pāpanisūdano'gnijanano hṛtkaṇṭhaśodhīpunaḥ  || 1,10.80 ||
{guggulu:: śodhana}
daśamūlakvāthe uṣṇe pūte gugguluṃ parikṣipyāloḍya ca vastrapūtaṃ vidhāya caṇḍātape viśoṣya ghṛtaṃ dattvā piṇḍitavyam  || 1,10.81 ||
{conch:: śodhana}
śaṃkhanābhiṃ tathāmlena saptavāraṃ hi bhāvayet |
raudre malādikaṃ tyaktvā prakṣālya grāhayediti  || 1,10.82 ||
{moneycowrie:: śodhana}
varāṭīṃ takracāṅgerījambīrāṇāṃ rase śubhe  |
prakṣipya bhāvayettāvadyāvacchuklā na paśyati  || 1,10.83 ||
paścāduddhṛtya gṛhṇīyādvarāṭīṃ śuddhimāgatām  || 1,10.84 ||
{pearl:: śodhana}
bhaumikaṃ jalamādāya muktāṃ ca vyuṣitāmapi  |
tyaktvā malādikāṃ tāṃ ca prakṣālya grāhayediti  || 1,10.85 ||


 2


 2, 1
jayati sa rasarājo mṛtyuśaṅkāpahārī sakalaguṇanidhānaṃ kāyakalpādhikārī  |
valipalitavināśaṃ sevito vīryavṛddhiṃ sthiramapi kurute yaḥ kāminīnāṃ prasaṅgam  || 2,1.1 ||
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi dehasiddhiṃ suśobhanām  |
yasyāḥ siddhau manuṣyāṇāṃ jāyante sarvasiddhayaḥ  || 2,1.2 ||
na dehena vinā kiṃcidiṣṭamasti jagattraye  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tasmin yatno vidhīyatām  || 2,1.3 ||
śubhanakṣatradivase vamane recane kṛte  |
tato viśeṣaśuddhyarthaṃ ketakīstanajaṃ dravam  || 2,1.4 ||
tridinaṃ kuḍavaikaikaṃ loṇadoṣaharaṃ pibet |
viḍaṅgaṃ ca vacā kuṣṭhaṃ ketakīstanasaṃyutam  || 2,1.5 ||
kvathitaṃ tridinaṃ pītamamladoṣaharaṃ param  |
śyāmāvahniviḍaṅgāni tryūṣaṇaṃ triphalā vṛṣaḥ  || 2,1.6 ||
saindhavaṃ devadāruśca mustā caitatsamaṃ samam  |
ghṛtairlehyaṃ tu karṣaikaṃ saptāhātsarvadoṣajit || 2,1.7 ||
evaṃ viśuddhadehastu pūjayeddevatāṃ gurum  |
kumārīṃ yoginīcakraṃ tataḥ kuryādrasāyanam  || 2,1.8 ||
{rasāyana}
nirvāte bhūgṛhe vātha bāhyacintāvivarjitaḥ  |
jitendriyo jitakrodhaḥ kṣīraśālyannabhugbhavet || 2,1.9 ||
ṣaṣṭyodanaṃ yavānnaṃ ca godhūmaṃ mudgayūṣakam  |
jāṅgalaṃ bhakṣayenmāṃsaṃ kevalaṃ kṣīrameva vā  || 2,1.10 ||
balānnaṃ vātha bhuñjīta śākaloṇavivarjitam  |
abhyaṅgaṃ mastunā kuryātsnānaṃ caiva sukhāmbunā  || 2,1.11 ||
kākinīṃ strīṃ bhajennityaṃ svānukūlāṃ suyauvanām  |
rasendre bhakṣyamāṇe tu kāmāndho jāyate naraḥ  || 2,1.12 ||
maithunena vinā tasya hyajīrṇo jāyate rasaḥ  |
ajīrṇe kampadāhārtī hikkā mūrchā jvaro'ratiḥ  || 2,1.13 ||
kāsaśvāsārucicchardibhramamohā bhavanti hi  |
seveta subhagāṃ tasmāddurbhagāṃ parivarjayet || 2,1.14 ||
abhyaṅgaṃ kaṭutailena kāñjikaṃ madirāṃ dadhi  |
kaliṅgakāravallyamlatailakūṣmāṇḍarājikāḥ  || 2,1.15 ||
bilvacchatrākavārtākavidalaṃ kākamācikām  |
mūlakaṃ laśunaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śītamuṣṇaṃ ca varjayet || 2,1.16 ||
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ tyājyaṃ divāsvāpaṃ ca maithunam  |
kalahodvegacintāśca śokaṃ caiva vivarjayet || 2,1.17 ||
āvarjanādbhavecchūlaṃ nidrālasyaṃ jvaro'ratiḥ  |
tryahaṃ pibettatpraśāntyai vāriṇā karkaṭīphalam  || 2,1.18 ||
śuṇṭhīsaindhavacūrṇaṃ vā mātuluṅgāmlakairlihet |
sauvarcalaṃ gavāṃ mūtraiḥ pibedvā tatpraśāntaye  || 2,1.19 ||
vandhyākarkoṭakīṃ puṅkhāṃ pātālagaruḍīṃ jalaiḥ  |
kvāthayedaṣṭaguṇitaistadaṣṭāṃśaṃ sasaindhavam  || 2,1.20 ||
pibetsarvavikāraghnaṃ tridinaṃ śivabhāṣitam  |
mūlaṃ vā kāravallyutthaṃ saindhavaṃ vā gavāṃ jalaiḥ  || 2,1.21 ||
tridinaṃ karṣamātraṃ tu pibetsarvavikārajit |
apathyaśīlināmetatkathitaṃ rasasevinām  || 2,1.22 ||
amumeva vidhiṃ kuryādrasāyanavidhau kila  |
amṛtaṃ vidhisaṃyuktaṃ vidhihīnaṃ tu tadviṣam  || 2,1.23 ||
cāturjātakakarpūrakaṅkolaṃ kaṭukīphalam  |
khādettāmbūlasaṃyuktaṃ rasasaṅkrāmaṇe hitam  || 2,1.24 ||
athātra vakṣyate samyagādau pāradamāraṇam  |
samukhasya rasendrasya vāsanāmukhitasya vā  || 2,1.25 ||
krameṇa jārayetsvarṇaṃ samāṃśaṃ pūrvavattataḥ  |
tattulyaṃ gandhakaṃ tasmin dattvā divyauṣadhidravaiḥ  || 2,1.26 ||
mardayettridinaṃ khalve mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ tataḥ  |
karīṣāgnau divārātraṃ trirātraṃ vā tuṣāgninā  || 2,1.27 ||
sveditaṃ mardayedbhūyo bījairdivyauṣadhodbhavaiḥ  |
tulyaṃ khalve caturyāmaṃ vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhamet || 2,1.28 ||
bhasmasūtaṃ bhavettadvai yojyaṃ sarvarasāyane  |
śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ svarṇaṃ yāmamamlairvimardayet || 2,1.29 ||
prakṣālya grāhayetpiṣṭīṃ piṣṭyardhaṃ śuddhagandhakam  |
gandhārdhaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā sarvatulyāṃ haridrikām  || 2,1.30 ||
strīpuṣpeṇa tu tatsarvaṃ mardyaṃ rambhādravānvitam  |
dinānte golakaṃ kṛtvā vālukāyantragaṃ pacet || 2,1.31 ||
dinaṃ mandāgninā taṃ vai samuddhṛtya vicūrṇayet |
cūrṇāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā garbhayantre tryahaṃ pacet || 2,1.32 ||
tuṣāgninā laghutvena jāyate bhasmasūtakaḥ  |
śuddhasūtastribhāgaḥ syādbhāgaikaṃ tāmracūrṇakam  || 2,1.33 ||
dinaikaṃ mardayedamlaiḥ kṣālitaṃ piṣṭimāharet |
mākṣikāddhautasattvaṃ ca piṣṭitulyaṃ prakalpayet || 2,1.34 ||
tatsarvaṃ tridinaṃ mardyaṃ cakramardadaladravaiḥ  |
tadgolaṃ garbhayantrasthaṃ tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  || 2,1.35 ||
karīṣāgnau divārātraṃ pacedvā bhasmatāṃ vrajet |
śuddhasūtaṃ vyomasattvaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,1.36 ||
sarvatulyaṃ viḍaṃ dattvā mardyaṃ rambhādravairdinam  |
bījairdivyauṣadhīnāṃ ca tulyairmardyaṃ dinadvayam  || 2,1.37 ||
garbhayantragataṃ pacyānmriyate pūrvavatpuṭe  |
caturaṅguladīrghaṃ syādvistāre cāṅgulatrayam  || 2,1.38 ||
mṛnmayaṃ sampuṭaṃ kṛtvā chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca kārayet |
lavaṇaṃ viṃśabhāgaṃ syādbhāgamekaṃ tu guggulum  || 2,1.39 ||
sarvaṃ toyaiḥ prapiṣyātha tenaiva sampuṭodaram  |
liptvā tatra rasaṃ rundhyādgarbhayantramidaṃ bhavet || 2,1.40 ||
vimalā pāradaṃ śuddhaṃ tulyaṃ nirguṇḍikādravaiḥ  |
mardayettridinaṃ taṃ vai kācakūpyāṃ niveśayet || 2,1.41 ||
kācakūpyā hyabhāve tu nirundhyāccharāvasampuṭe  |
pācayedvālukāyantre caturyāmānmṛto bhavet || 2,1.42 ||
mākṣikāddhautasattvaṃ tu tatsamaṃ śuddhagandhakam  |
dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ śuddharasaṃ dinaṃ nirguṇḍikādravaiḥ  || 2,1.43 ||
tatsarvaṃ marditaṃ golaṃ vajramūṣāndhitaṃ pacet |
dinaikaṃ vālukāyantre mṛtaṃ syādraktavarṇakam  || 2,1.44 ||
ūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ dātavyaṃ śuddhapārade  |
udare pakvamūṣāyāḥ kākamācīdravaṃ punaḥ  || 2,1.45 ||
dvābhyāṃ caturguṇaṃ dattvā tāmācchādya pacecchanaiḥ  |
kramāgnau vālukāyantre caturyāmānmṛto bhavet || 2,1.46 ||
snuhyā vā hemavallyā vā kṣīraiḥ śuddharasaṃ dinam  |
mardayedgandhakaṃ tulyaṃ garbhayantragataṃ puṭet || 2,1.47 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena mṛtaṃ yogeṣu yojayet |
śuddhasūtasamaṃ guñjālākṣorṇāmadhuṭaṅkaṇam  || 2,1.48 ||
tatsarvaṃ bhṛṅgajairdrāvairdinamekaṃ vimardayet |
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhmātaṃ mriyate śaśisannibham  || 2,1.49 ||
dravaistu kīṭamāriṇyā hyajamodādravaiśca vā  |
ahimāryā dravairvātha kiṃvā śvetāṅkuladravaiḥ  || 2,1.50 ||
mardayetpāradaṃ śuddhaṃ samagandhaṃ dinatrayam  |
sampuṭe mṛnmaye ruddhvā karīṣāgnau divāniśi  || 2,1.51 ||
pacettuṣāgninā vātha tridinānmriyate dhruvam  |
śuddhasūtaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ samāṃśaṃ taptakhalvake  || 2,1.52 ||
haṃsapādyā dravairmardyaṃ tridinānte samuddharet |
bījairdivyauṣadhīnāṃ ca vajramūṣāṃ pralepayet || 2,1.53 ||
tatra pūrvarasaṃ ruddhvā tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  |
pācayitvā samuddhṛtya tatsamaṃ śuddhapāradam  || 2,1.54 ||
ekīkṛtya tryahaṃ mardyaṃ haṃsapādyā dravairdṛḍham  |
tadgolaṃ pūrvavatpacyānmṛtaṃ bhavati śobhanam  || 2,1.55 ||
vajrābhrādyaṣṭalohānāṃ rasakhaṇḍe yathoditam  |
māraṇaṃ vādikhaṇḍe vā tathā jñeyaṃ rasāyane  || 2,1.56 ||
evaṃ mṛto rasavaraḥ paramāmūtaḥ syāttatsevakāḥ satatamasya dṛḍhaṃ tu teṣām  |
dehaṃ karoti sahasā surasundarīṇāṃ krīḍākṣamaṃ paramasundarameva nityam  || 2,1.57 ||


 2, 2
dharmajñaiḥ śivavatsalairnirjarairbhūpairmahāsādhakaiḥ samyagdivyarasāyanena satataṃ kalpāntasīmāvadhi  |
rakṣyaṃ gātramanantapuṇyanicaye muktiśca yasmādbhavettadvakṣye paramādbhutaṃ sukhakaraṃ sāmrājyadaṃ dhīmatām  || 2,2.1 ||
abhrakaṃ bhakṣayedādau māritaṃ cāmṛtīkṛtam  |
māsaikaṃ niṣkaniṣkaṃ vai kṣetrīkaraṇahetave  || 2,2.2 ||
yasmādabhraṃ rasakṣetraṃ tataḥ kuryādrasāyanam  |
akṣetrīkaraṇe sūto hyamṛto viṣatāṃ vrajet || 2,2.3 ||
phalasiddhiḥ kutastasya subījasyoṣare yathā  |
vajrapāradayorbhasma samabhāgaṃ prakalpayet || 2,2.4 ||
sūtapādaṃ mṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ sarvaṃ mardyaṃ dināvadhi  |
haṃsapādyā dravaireva tadgolaṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet || 2,2.5 ||
arkakṣīraiḥ punarmardyaṃ tadvadgajapuṭe pacet |
bhakṣayetsarṣapavṛddhaṃ yāvanmāṣaṃ vivardhayet || 2,2.6 ||
śaraṇyaḥ sādhakānāṃ tu raso'yaṃ vajrapañjaraḥ  |
citrakārdrakasindhūtthamṛtatīkṣṇasuvarcalam  || 2,2.7 ||
samaṃ sarvaṃ sadā cānu bhakṣyaṃ syātkrāmaṇe hitam  |
māsaṣaṭkaprayogeṇa jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,2.8 ||
valīpalitanirmukto divyakāyo mahābalaḥ  |
mṛtasūtāddvādaśāṃśaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ prakalpayet || 2,2.9 ||
dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ kāntatulyaṃ mṛtābhrakam  |
tatsarvaṃ bhṛṅgajairdrāvairmarditaṃ bhāvayettryaham  || 2,2.10 ||
tryahaṃ gokṣurakadrāvaiḥ kṣaudrairmāṣaṃ tato lihet |
raso vajreśvaro nāma vajrakāyakaro nṛṇām  || 2,2.11 ||
caturmāsairjarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinaṃ kila  |
bhṛṅgarājasya pañcāṅgaṃ cūrṇayettriphalāsamam  || 2,2.12 ||
palaikaṃ madhunā lehyaṃ krāmakaṃ paramaṃ rase  |
vajrasūtābhrahemnāṃ tu bhasma śuddhaṃ tu mākṣikam  || 2,2.13 ||
tulyaṃ saptadinaṃ mardyaṃ divyauṣadhirasairdṛḍham  |
ruddhvā taṃ tridinaṃ pacyādvālukāyantragaṃ punaḥ  || 2,2.14 ||
uddhṛtya tridinaṃ bhāvyaṃ bhṛṅgasarpākṣijairdravaiḥ  |
māṣaikaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ vajradhārārasaṃ lihet || 2,2.15 ||
māsaṣaṭkaprayogeṇa rudratulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
valīpalitanirmukto vāyuvego mahābalaḥ  || 2,2.16 ||
punarnavābhṛṅgatilavājigandhāḥ samāṃśakāḥ  |
sarvatulyā sitā yojyā cūrṇitaṃ bhakṣayetpalam  || 2,2.17 ||
suvarṇaṃ pāradaṃ kāntaṃ mṛtaṃ sarvaṃ samaṃ bhavet |
śatāvaryāḥ śiphādrāvairbhāvayeddivasatrayam  || 2,2.18 ||
tridinaṃ triphalākvāthairbhṛṅgadrāvairdinatrayam  |
bhāvitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ bhakṣayedbhairavaṃ rasam  || 2,2.19 ||
māṣaikaikaṃ varṣamātraṃ jīveccandrārkatārakam  |
mūlacūrṇaṃ śatāvaryāḥ kṛṣṇājapayasā yutam  || 2,2.20 ||
palaikaikaṃ pibeccānu krāmakaṃ paramaṃ hitam  |
rasabhasma samaṃ gandhaṃ śilājatvamlavetasam  || 2,2.21 ||
yāmaikaṃ mardayetsarvaṃ madhusarpiryutaṃ lihet |
niṣkaikaikaṃ varṣamātraṃ śilāvīro mahārasaḥ  || 2,2.22 ||
jarākālaṃ nihantyāśu jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  |
palārdhaṃ musalīcūrṇaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasaiḥ pibet || 2,2.23 ||
dhātrīphalarasairvātha krāmakaṃ hyanupānakam  |
meghanādadravairmardyaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ dinatrayam  || 2,2.24 ||
viḍaṅgaṃ dviniśaṃ vyoṣaṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ prakalpayet |
āragvadhasya mūlaṃ tu cūrṇasya dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 2,2.25 ||
cūrṇasya dviguṇaṃ cājyaṃ kṣaudraṃ caiva caturguṇam  |
sarvaṃ pūrvarase kṣiptvā mṛdvagnau cālayatpacet || 2,2.26 ||
tatpiṇḍaṃ karṣamekaikaṃ bhakṣayedamṛtārṇavaḥ  |
varṣamātrāñjarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  || 2,2.27 ||
vākucīcūrṇakarṣaikaṃ dhātrīphalarasaiḥ pibet |
pāradāddviguṇaṃ gandhaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvaṃ vimardayet || 2,2.28 ||
muṇḍyārdrakarasaiḥ khalve trisaptāhaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
etattulyaṃ śuddhatāmraṃ sampuṭe tannirodhayet || 2,2.29 ||
veṣṭayedvastrakhaṇḍena vajramṛttikayā bahiḥ  |
liptvā viśoṣayettaṃ vai samyaggajapuṭe pacet || 2,2.30 ||
uddhṛtya sampuṭaṃ cūrṇyaṃ devadālyā dravaistryaham  |
bhaṅgīpunarnavādrāvaiḥ pṛthagbhāvyaṃ tryahaṃ tryaham  || 2,2.31 ||
tattulyaṃ nāgarāccūrṇaṃ kṣiptvā madhvājyasaṃyutam  |
lihenmāṣadvayaṃ nityaṃ yāvatsaṃvatsarāvadhi  || 2,2.32 ||
raso'yamudayādityo jarāmṛtyuharaḥ paraḥ  |
punarnavādevadālībhṛṅgacūrṇaṃ samaṃ samam  || 2,2.33 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣamanu syātkrāmaṇaṃ param  |
pārado gaganaṃ kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca māritaṃ samam  || 2,2.34 ||
bhṛṅgadhātrīphaladrāvaiśchāyāyāṃ bhāvayettryaham  |
sitāmadhvājyakaistulyaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṇḍe nirodhayet || 2,2.35 ||
dhānyarāśau sthitaṃ māsaṃ tato niṣkatrayaṃ samam  |
bhakṣayecca pibetkṣīraṃ karṣaikaṃ triphalāmanu  || 2,2.36 ||
rātrau śuṇṭhīṃ kaṇāṃ khādedvarṣaikādamaro bhavet |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ vīraḥ syādraso gaganeśvaraḥ  || 2,2.37 ||
vaṭakṣīraistryahaṃ mardyaṃ gandhaṃ śuddharasaṃ samam  |
vaṭakāṣṭhāgninā pacyānmṛtpātre yāmapañcakam  || 2,2.38 ||
kṣipan kṣipan vaṭakṣīraṃ tatkāṣṭhenaiva cālayet |
samuddhṛtya tryahaṃ bhāvyaṃ devadālīdaladravaiḥ  || 2,2.39 ||
uṣṇakāle tu guñjaikaṃ tāmbūlapatrasaṃyutam  |
candravṛddhyā sadā bhakṣyaṃ yāvatṣoḍaśaguñjakam  || 2,2.40 ||
cūrṇamuttaravāruṇyā vākucyā devadālijam  |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ krāmakaṃ hyanupānakam  || 2,2.41 ||
varṣamātrājjarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  |
raso vaṭeśvaro nāma vajrakāyakaro nṛṇām  || 2,2.42 ||
mṛtaṃ sūtaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ triphalāṃ gugguluṃ samam  |
sarvaṃ vātāritailena miśraṃ karṣaṃ lihetsadā  || 2,2.43 ||
ṣaṇmāsena jarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣeṇa śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 2,2.44 ||
ajasya vṛṣaṇaṃ pācyaṃ gavāṃ kṣīreṇa taṃ niśi  |
sitāyuktaṃ pibeccānu raso'yamacaleśvaraḥ  || 2,2.45 ||
rasaṃ vajraṃ svarṇakānte muṇḍaṃ ca māritaṃ samam  |
mākṣikaṃ gandhakaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ  || 2,2.46 ||
saptāhaṃ mardayetkhalve tadgolaṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet |
bhūdhare dinamekaṃ tu khyātaḥ siddharasaḥ paraḥ  || 2,2.47 ||
māṣaikaṃ madhunā lehyaṃ varṣānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
divyakāyo naraḥ siddho bhavedviṣṇuparākramaḥ  || 2,2.48 ||
śvetapaunarnavaṃ mūlaṃ kṣīrapiṣṭaṃ sadā pibet |
bhakṣayedvā sitā sārdhaṃ krāmakaṃ parame rase  || 2,2.49 ||
mṛtasūtasya dviguṇaṃ śuddhaṃ gandhaṃ vimiśrayet |
dinaikaṃ kanyakādrāvairmardayitvā nirodhayet || 2,2.50 ||
dinaikaṃ madhunā pacyānniṣkaikaṃ madhunā lihet |
gandhāmṛto raso nāma vatsarānmṛtyujidbhavet || 2,2.51 ||
samūlaṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ tu chāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet |
tatsamaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ sarvatulyā sitā bhavet || 2,2.52 ||
ekīkṛtya palaikaikaṃ bhakṣayedanupānakam  |
pāradābhraṃ mṛtaṃ tulyaṃ dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ tu gandhakam  || 2,2.53 ||
tatsarvaṃ bhṛṅgajairdrāvairmardayeddinasaptakam  |
ṣaḍvāraṃ cāṅkulītailairbhāvayitvātha bhakṣayet || 2,2.54 ||
māṣamātraṃ tu varṣaikaṃ raso'yaṃ kālakaṇṭakaḥ  |
piṣṭvā karañjapatrāṇi gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibedanu  || 2,2.55 ||
jarāmṛtyuvinirmukto jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  |
mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ kāntaṃ viṣaṃ tāpyaṃ śilājatu  || 2,2.56 ||
tulyāṃśaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ lihedguñjātrayaṃ sadā  |
ṣaṇmāsena jarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  || 2,2.57 ||
aśvagandhāmūlacūrṇaṃ saptabhāgaghṛtaiḥ samam  |
bhāgāṣṭakaṃ guḍaṃ tasmin kṣipedbhāgaṃ ca pippalīm  || 2,2.58 ||
mṛdvagninā ca tatsarvaṃ piṇḍitaṃ bhakṣayetpalam  |
krāmakaṃ hyamṛteśasya rasarājasya siddhaye  || 2,2.59 ||
triguṇaṃ śuddhasūtasya yojayecchuddhagandhakam  |
lohaparpaṭikācūrṇaṃ sūtatulyaṃ vinikṣipet || 2,2.60 ||
snuhyarkapayasā mardyaṃ tatsarvaṃ divasatrayam  |
tacchuṣkaṃ cāndhitaṃ pacyātkarīṣāgnau divāniśam  || 2,2.61 ||
tataśca ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kācaṃ dattvā ruddhvā dhameddṛḍham  |
guñjaikaṃ madhunā khādedrasavīro mahārasaḥ  || 2,2.62 ||
abdaikena jarāṃ hanti jīvedācandratārakam  |
musalīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu guñjāpatradravaiḥ pibet || 2,2.63 ||
chāgīmūtreṇa vā taṃ vai karṣaikaṃ krāmakaṃ param  |
mṛtasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ kākamācyā dravairdinam  || 2,2.64 ||
marditaṃ cāndhitaṃ pacyātkarīṣāgnau divāniśam  |
divyauṣadhadaladrāvairdinaṃ mardyaṃ tamandhayet || 2,2.65 ||
dhmātaṃ tasmātsamuddhṛtya tattulyaṃ hāṭakaṃ mṛtam  |
ekīkṛtya ghṛtairlehyaṃ māṣaikaṃ vatsarāvadhi  || 2,2.66 ||
jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ nihantyāśu satyaṃ kāñcāyano rasaḥ  |
kākamācīdravairbhāvyaṃ cūrṇaṃ dhātrīphalodbhavam  || 2,2.67 ||
madhunā bhakṣayetkarṣamanu syātkrāmakaṃ param  |
mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ gandhaṃ tulyaṃ saptadināvadhi  || 2,2.68 ||
śigrumūladravairmardyaṃ tadgolaṃ bhāṇḍamadhyagam  |
ruddhvā pacyāllaghutvena śākakāṣṭhairdināvadhi  || 2,2.69 ||
parānando raso nāma ghṛtairniṣkaṃ sadā lihet |
dinaikaṃ triphalākvāthaiḥ kuṣṭhaṃ samyagvipācayet || 2,2.70 ||
tacchuṣkaṃ cūrṇitaṃ karṣaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu  |
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,2.71 ||
mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ tulyaṃ mṛtalohaṃ tayoḥ samam  |
lohāṃśaṃ śodhitaṃ gandhaṃ bhāvayeddinasaptakam  || 2,2.72 ||
tatsarvaṃ triphalākvāthairbhṛṅgaśigrukacitrakaiḥ  |
dravaiḥ pṛthakpṛthagbhāvyaṃ saptadhā saptadhā kramāt || 2,2.73 ||
saptadhā kaṭukīkvāthairbhāvitaṃ cūrṇayetpunaḥ  |
cūrṇatulyaṃ kaṇācūrṇaṃ purātanaguḍaiḥ samaiḥ  || 2,2.74 ||
sarvamekīkṛtaṃ khādenniṣkaikaṃ vatsarāvadhi  |
mahākālo raso nāma jarākālabhayaṃkaraḥ  || 2,2.75 ||
tilakoraṇṭapattrāṇi guḍena bhakṣayedanu  |
mṛtapāradasaṃtulyaṃ lohaparpaṭakaṃ bhavet || 2,2.76 ||
triguṇaṃ gandhakaṃ sūtātsarvaṃ divyauṣadhadravaiḥ  |
marditaṃ taddinaṃ ruddhvā dhmāto baddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 2,2.77 ||
tasmin pādaṃ mṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ kṣiptvā vahnyārdrakadravaiḥ  |
mardyaṃ yāmaṃ vicūrṇyātha vyoṣajīrakasaindhavaiḥ  || 2,2.78 ||
tulyaṃ pūrvarasaṃ tulyaṃ niṣkaikaikaṃ ca bhakṣayet |
jarāmṛtyuṃ nihantyāśu hemaparpaṭako rasaḥ  || 2,2.79 ||
aśvagandhāsamāṃ yaṣṭiṃ dhātrīphalarasairdinam  |
bhāvitāṃ lehayetkṣaudraiḥ palaikāṃ krāmakaṃ param  || 2,2.80 ||
svarṇatārārkakāntaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ vā māritaṃ samam  |
kṛṣṇābhrasattvamākṣīkaṃ pratyekaṃ svarṇatulyakam  || 2,2.81 ||
tatsarvaṃ cāndhitaṃ dhāmyaṃ tatkhoṭaṃ mṛtapāradam  |
samaṃ sūtānmṛtaṃ vajraṃ pādāṃśaṃ tatra yojayet || 2,2.82 ||
sarvaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaistaptakhalve vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ taṃ nirudhyātha bhūdhare pāvayeddinam  || 2,2.83 ||
uddhṛtya gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ dattvā ruddhvā dhameddrutam  |
taccūrṇaṃ madhunājyena māṣamātraṃ lihetsadā  || 2,2.84 ||
rasaḥ śrīkaṇṭhanāmāyaṃ khecaratvaṃ prayacchati  |
saṃvatsaraprayogena jīvetkalpāntameva ca  || 2,2.85 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ sarvalohāni kāñcanam  |
palaikaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣīraiḥ krāmakaṃ cānu pāyayet || 2,2.86 ||
śuddhatāmrasya bhāgaikaṃ dviṣaṭśuddharasasya ca  |
trayaṃ bhūnāgasattvasya bhāgamekatra vārayet || 2,2.87 ||
sarvaṃ mardyaṃ taptakhalve jambīrāṇāṃ dravairdinam  |
tatsarvaṃ kacchape yantre kṣiptvā tatraiva gandhakam  || 2,2.88 ||
kāsamardarasaiḥ piṣṭaṃ tulyaṃ dattvā nirudhya ca  |
yāvajjīrṇaṃ puṭe pacyādevaṃ ṣaḍguṇagandhakam  || 2,2.89 ||
jārayetkramayogena samuddhṛtyātha mardayet |
yāmaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaistato niścandramabhrakam  || 2,2.90 ||
amlavetasasaṃtulyaṃ marditaṃ dāpayedrase  |
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ taptakhalve caṇakāmlaṃ ca tālakam  || 2,2.91 ||
kāsīsaṃ ca daśāṃśena dattvā mardyaṃ dināvadhi  |
tatsarvaṃ pakvamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā vastreṇa bandhayet || 2,2.92 ||
dolāyantre sāranāle tryahaṃ laghvagninā pacet |
uddhṛtya kṣālayeduṣṇaiḥ kāñjikairjīryate yadi  || 2,2.93 ||
ajīrṇaṃ cetpacedyantre kacchapākhye viḍānvitam  |
eva punaḥ punarjāryaṃ gaganaṃ sūtatulyakam  || 2,2.94 ||
śikhipittapraliptāni svarṇapattrāṇi tasya vai  |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśayogena dattvā khalve vimardayet || 2,2.95 ||
svedayetpūrvavadyantre jīrṇe svarṇaṃ ca dāpayet |
ityevaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ tu svarṇaṃ jāryaṃ rasasya vai  || 2,2.96 ||
tato jāryaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ ca hemavat |
tālakāsīsajambīrayuktaṃ mardyaṃ ca tatparam  || 2,2.97 ||
tato divyauṣadhadrāvaistaṃ sūtaṃ mardayettryaham  |
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhāmyaṃ baddhaṃ syāccūrṇayetpunaḥ  || 2,2.98 ||
madhuśarkarayā sārdhaṃ guñjāmātraṃ ca bhakṣayet |
rasaḥ khecarabaddho'yaṃ ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyujidbhavet || 2,2.99 ||
valīpalitanirmukto mahābalaparākramaḥ  |
saptāhaṃ bhṛṅgajairdrāvairnīlamuṇḍīphalatrayam  || 2,2.100 ||
bhāvayenmadhusarpirbhyāṃ karṣamātraṃ lihedanu  |
śuddhasūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ kuryātkhalvena kañjalam  || 2,2.101 ||
tayostulyaṃ kāntacūrṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ vā muṇḍameva vā  |
sarvamekīkṛtaṃ khalve mardayetkanyakādravaiḥ  || 2,2.102 ||
dinaikaṃ golakaṃ kṛtvā tāmrapātre niveśayet |
ācchādyairaṇḍapattraistu yāmārdhe'tyuṣṇatāṃ vrajet || 2,2.103 ||
dhānyarāśau nyasettaṃ tu dvidinānte samuddharet |
kanyābhṛṅgīkākamācīmuṇḍīnirguṇḍīcitrakam  || 2,2.104 ||
koraṇṭavākucībrāhmīsahadevīpunarnavāḥ  |
śālmalīvijayādhūrtā dravaireṣāṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 2,2.105 ||
saptadhā saptadhā bhāvyaṃ saptadhā triphalodbhavaiḥ  |
kaṣāyairbhāvitaṃ cūrṇyaṃ jātīphalalavaṃgakam  || 2,2.106 ||
trikaṭu triphalā cailā cūrṇayennavakaṃ samam  |
taccūrṇaṃ pūrvacūrṇaṃ ca samaṃ kṣaudreṇa karṣakam  || 2,2.107 ||
varṣaikaṃ lehayennityaṃ jarākālapraśāntaye  |
svayamagniraso nāma siddhānāṃ sumukhāgataḥ  || 2,2.108 ||
tilāśvagandhayoścūrṇaṃ palārdhaṃ madhunā lihet |
nirguṇḍī nīlikā vajrī brahmadaṇḍī tridaṇḍikā  || 2,2.109 ||
śatapuṣpā mudgaparṇī śvetārko vānarī jayā  |
peṭārīkṛṣṇadhattūravijayākṣīrakandakam  || 2,2.110 ||
etaiḥ samastairvyastairvā dravyairmardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
śuddhasūtaṃ taptakhalve tatkalkaṃ kṣīrakandake  || 2,2.111 ||
vajrakande'thavā ruddhvā tanmajjābhirmṛdā punaḥ  |
taṃ kandaṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā laghupuṭe pacet || 2,2.112 ||
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pācyamityevaṃ saptadhā kramāt |
rasaḥ kakṣapuṭo nāma guñjaikaṃ madhunā lihet || 2,2.113 ||
jīvedbrahmadinaikaṃ tu valīpalitavarjitaḥ  |
varṣaikena na saṃdeho rasakāyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,2.114 ||
bhallātabījacūrṇaṃ ca hayagandhāmṛtāghṛtaiḥ  |
palaikaṃ bhakṣayeccānu krāmakaṃ paramaṃ hitam  || 2,2.115 ||
mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ vajraṃ kāntatārārkahāṭakam  |
tīkṣṇaṃ ca tulyatulyāṃśaṃ sarveṣāṃ gandhakaṃ samam  || 2,2.116 ||
sarvaṃ pālāśatailena mardayeddinasaptakam  |
mahāśaktiraso nāma kṣaudrairmāṣaṃ lihetsadā  || 2,2.117 ||
ṣaṇmāsena jarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
vatsarātsaptakalpāni jīvatyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,2.118 ||
icchāvegī mahāsiddhaḥ parāśaktisamo bhavet |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 2,2.119 ||
pālāśabījajaṃ tailaṃ kṣaudrairlehyaṃ palāṣṭakam  |
krāmakaṃ hyanupānaṃ syātsamyakchaktyā prakāśitam  || 2,2.120 ||
lohitaṃ vātha vā kṛṣṇaṃ vaikrāntaṃ māritaṃ palam  |
svarṇacūrṇapalaikaṃ ca dvipalaṃ śuddhapāradam  || 2,2.121 ||
bālaraṇḍājamūtrābhyāṃ tatsarvaṃ mardayeddinam  |
śarapuṅkhā meṣaśṛṅgī sarpākṣīkaṭutumbikā  || 2,2.122 ||
indravāruṇikā caiṣāṃ dravairmardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
tadgolaṃ garbhayantre tu ruddhvā pacyāddinatrayam  || 2,2.123 ||
tuṣāgninā laghutvena samuddhṛtya vicūrṇayet |
saptadhā bhṛṅgajairdrāvairbhāvitaṃ cūrṇayetpunaḥ  || 2,2.124 ||
triphalātryūṣamadhvājyaiḥ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ vimiśrayet |
māṣaikaikaṃ sadā khādedraso'yaṃ nāṭakeśvaraḥ  || 2,2.125 ||
sarvarogajarāmṛtyūn vatsarānnāśayatyalam  |
divyatejā mahākāyo jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,2.126 ||
mūlatvacaṃ brahmavṛkṣācchāyāśuṣkāṃ vicūrṇitām  |
pibenniṣkadvayāṃ takraiḥ krāmakaṃ paramaṃ śubham  || 2,2.127 ||
suśuddhaṃ śvetavaikrāntaṃ saptāhaṃ bhāvyamātape  |
amlavetasasampiṣṭaṃ tenaiva drutimāpnuyāt || 2,2.128 ||
etaddrutiṃ śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ kṣaudrairdinatrayam  |
marditaṃ lehayenmāṣaṃ māsādbālo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,2.129 ||
vatsarādbrahmatulyaḥ syādraso'yaṃ bālasundaraḥ  |
vākucībījakarṣaikaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu  || 2,2.130 ||
catuḥpalaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ palaikaṃ mṛtahāṭakam  |
palāśakuḍmaladrāvaistattailaiśca dinatrayam  || 2,2.131 ||
mardayettaptakhalve tu svarṇatulyaṃ ca gandhakam  |
śodhitaṃ nikṣipettasmin pūrvoktairmardayeddinam  || 2,2.132 ||
māṣamātrāṃ vaṭīṃ khādedvatsarānmṛtyujidbhavet |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ vīro raso'yaṃ brahmapañjaraḥ  || 2,2.133 ||
vānarīkākatuṇḍyutthabījacūrṇaṃ samaṃ samam  |
śālmalītvagdaladrāvairbhāvayeddivasatrayam  || 2,2.134 ||
tryahaṃ ca bhṛṅgajairdrāvairbhāvitaṃ cūrṇayettataḥ  |
purātanaguḍaistulyaṃ karṣaikamanu bhakṣayet || 2,2.135 ||
raktabhūmau tu bhūnāgān grāhayitvā parīkṣayet |
chede niryāti raktaṃ cettān svīkuryātprayatnataḥ  || 2,2.136 ||
kṛṣṇavarṇāgavājyena samena saha tān pacet |
lohaje cālayan pātre yāvatsindūravarṇakam  || 2,2.137 ||
tatsarvaṃ jāyate bhasma tattulyaṃ mṛtapāradam  |
madhunāloḍitaṃ sarvaṃ guñjārdhārdhaṃ vivardhayan  || 2,2.138 ||
paścādguñjāṃ sadā khādedyāvatsaṃvatsarāvadhi  |
śivāmṛto raso nāma jarāmṛtyuharo nṛṇām  |
āyurbrahmadinaṃ datte śivāmbu pāyayedanu  || 2,2.139 ||
evaṃ divyarasāyanaiḥ samucitaiḥ sārātisāraiḥ śubhaiḥ siddhaṃ dehamanekasādhanabalādyeṣāṃ tu dṛṣṭaṃ mayā  |
tānārādhya ca teṣu sāramakhilaṃ saṃgṛhya śāstrādapi bhūpānāṃ viduṣāṃ mahāmatimatāṃ proktaṃ hitārthāya vai  || 2,2.140 ||


 2, 3
divyayogaguṭikārasāyanaṃ krāmaṇena rahitaṃ na sidhyati  |
śīghrasiddhikarameva sevyatāṃ krāmaṇārthamanupānamatra vai  || 2,3.1 ||
kākatumbī kākamācī nirguṇḍī ca kumārikā  |
gojihvā saindhavaṃ guñjā hyārdrakaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,3.2 ||
piṣṭvā tena praleptavyā mūṣā sarvāṅgulāvadhi  |
pāradaṃ vyomasattvaṃ ca kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam  || 2,3.3 ||
tāpyasattvaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ sarvaṃ saṃcūrṇya mardayet |
dinaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaistanmūṣāyāṃ vinikṣipet || 2,3.4 ||
ācchādyālepya kalkena cāndhayitvā viśoṣayet |
karīṣāgnau divārātraṃ puṭe paktvā samuddharet || 2,3.5 ||
punaḥ praliptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā puṭettataḥ  |
ityevaṃ daśamūṣāsu praliptāsu vipācayet || 2,3.6 ||
jāyate guṭikā divyā mṛtasaṃjīvanī parā  |
vaktre śirasi kaṇṭhe vā karṇe vā dhāritā kare  || 2,3.7 ||
hemnā suveṣṭitā samyagvayaḥstambhakarī parā  |
valīpalitakālāgnimṛtyuśaṅkāvināśanī  || 2,3.8 ||
varṣamātrānna saṃdeho jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  |
śuddhapalaikaṃ tu gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibetsadā  || 2,3.9 ||
anena tvanupānena dehe saṃkramate rasaḥ  |
samukhaṃ pāradaṃ kāntaṃ muṇḍalohābhrasattvakam  || 2,3.10 ||
tattvaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ sarvaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ  |
saptāhaṃ mardayettulyaṃ kṛtvā golaṃ samuddharet || 2,3.11 ||
