nirguṇḍī saindhavaṃ kṣaudraṃ gojihvā kākatuṇḍikā  |
piṣṭvā tu lepayedgolaṃ sarvato'ṅgulamātrakam  || 2,3.12 ||
taṃ ruddhvā vajramūṣāyāṃ pacedyāmaṃ tu bhūdhare  |
liptvā ruddhvā punaḥ pācyamityevaṃ pakṣamātrakam  || 2,3.13 ||
yavaciñcāpalāśottharājīkārpāsabījakaiḥ  |
supiṣṭairlepayenmūṣāṃ tanmadhye pūrvagolakam  || 2,3.14 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śvetakācaṃ ca dattvā pṛṣṭhe nirudhya ca  |
khadirāṅgārayogena dhamedyāvaddrutaṃ bhavet || 2,3.15 ||
tatastaṃ viḍaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ ca niveśayet |
tattulyaṃ dāpayetsvarṇaṃ jayettaṃ dhaman dhaman  || 2,3.16 ||
jīrṇe svarṇe samuddhṛtya taptakhalve vimardayet |
tryahaṃ divyauṣadhidrāvairvajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhamet || 2,3.17 ||
jāyate guṭikā divyā nāmnā vajreśvarī parā  |
vaktrasthā sā jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ hanti saṃvatsarātkila  || 2,3.18 ||
śastrastambhaṃ ca kurute brahmāyuryacchati dhruvam  |
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ samādāya sahadevīṃ su cūrṇayet || 2,3.19 ||
karṣaikāṃ bhakṣayedājyairanu syātkrāmeṇa hitam  |
āraktaṃ meghanādaṃ tu tathā pāṣāṇabhedakam  || 2,3.20 ||
strīstanyasahitaṃ piṣṭvā tena mūṣāṃ pralepayet |
bhagaikaṃ mṛtavajrasya svarṇacūrṇasya ṣoḍaśa  || 2,3.21 ||
kṣiptvā tasyāṃ nirudhyātha yāmamātraṃ dṛḍhaṃ dhamet |
uddhṛtya nikṣipetkhalve śuddhasūtaṃ ca tatsamam  || 2,3.22 ||
mardayeccārdrakadrāvairyāvadbhavati golakaḥ  |
caṇḍālīkandamādāya strīstanyena su peṣayet || 2,3.23 ||
anena golakaṃ liptvā vajramūṣāyāṃ nirodhayet |
paktvā gajapuṭe grāhyā guṭikā vajrasundarī  || 2,3.24 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
brahmavṛkṣasya tvakcūrṇaṃ kṣīrairnityaṃ palaṃ pibet || 2,3.25 ||
krāmaṇaṃ hyanupānaṃ syātsādhakasyātisiddhidam  |
tadudbhavamalairliptaṃ tāmraṃ tu dhamanena hi  || 2,3.26 ||
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ satyaṃ śaṃkarabhāṣitam  |
samukhasya rasendrasya pūrvavatkāñcanaṃ samam  || 2,3.27 ||
jārayedviḍayogena tato mardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
divyauṣadhaiḥ sagomūtrairvajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhamet || 2,3.28 ||
uddhṛtya dhārayedvaktre guṭikā hemasundarī  |
palārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ cājyairdviguṇairlehayedanu  || 2,3.29 ||
varṣaikena jarāṃ hanti jīvedācandratārakam  |
śuddhasūtaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ vyomasattvaṃ sahāṭakam  || 2,3.30 ||
amlavarge samaṃ sarvaṃ mardayeddivasatrayam  |
tadgolakaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kṛtvā chāyāyāṃ śoṣayettataḥ  || 2,3.31 ||
brahmakārpāsabījāni rājikā yavaciñcikā  |
vandhyā sarvaṃ samaṃ piṣṭvā pūrvagolaṃ pralepayet || 2,3.32 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyātsamuddhṛtyātha lepayet |
ruddhvā mūṣāyāṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ guṭikā vajrakhecarī  || 2,3.33 ||
jāyate dhāritā vaktre vatsarānmṛtyunāśinī  |
bhūtāravaṭacūrṇaṃ tu palaikaṃ sitayā yutam  || 2,3.34 ||
bhakṣayetkrāmaṇārthaṃ tu brahmāyurjāyate naraḥ  |
kārpāsyāḥ kākamācyāśca kanyāyāśca daladravaiḥ  || 2,3.35 ||
śuddhasūtaṃ dinaṃ mardyaṃ kṣālyamamlaiḥ samuddharet |
tadrasaṃ niṣkacatvāri niṣkārdhaṃ tāmracūrṇakam  || 2,3.36 ||
pādonaniṣkamabhrotthaṃ sattvaṃ pādaṃ ca hāṭakam  |
hematulyaṃ muṇḍacūrṇaṃ sarvamamlairvimardayet || 2,3.37 ||
dinānte golakaṃ kṛtvā jambīrasyodare kṣipet |
tridinaṃ dolakāyantre pācayetsāranālake  || 2,3.38 ||
uddhṛtya dhārayedvaktre guṭikā vyomasundarī  |
varṣamātrājjarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  || 2,3.39 ||
citramūlasya cūrṇaṃ tu sakṣaudraṃ kāntapātrake  |
āloḍya bhakṣayetkarṣamanu syātkrāmaṇe hitam  || 2,3.40 ||
niṣkamekaṃ svarṇapattraṃ triniṣkaṃ śuddhapāradam  |
jambīraśarapuṅkhotthadravairmardyaṃ dināvadhi  || 2,3.41 ||
tadgolaṃ bandhayedvastre pacedgokṣīrapūrite  |
dolāyantre divārātraṃ guṭikā hāṭakeśvarī  || 2,3.42 ||
jāyate dhāritā vaktre jarāmṛtyuvināśinī  |
varṣamātrānna saṃdeho jīvedvarṣāyutaṃ naraḥ  || 2,3.43 ||
dinaikaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ kvāthaiḥ khadirabījakaiḥ  |
bhāvitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ palaikaṃ krāmakaṃ lihet || 2,3.44 ||
rasaniṣkatrayaṃ śuddhaṃ niṣkaikaṃ tāmracūrṇakam  |
ciñcāphalāmlatakrābhyāṃ khalve mardyaṃ dināvadhi  || 2,3.45 ||
tadgolaṃ bandhayedvasraistakraciñcāmlapūrite  |
dolāyantre dinaṃ pacyādguṭikārkaprabhāvatī  || 2,3.46 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre sūryatulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
pālāśabījajaṃ tailaṃ gokṣīraiḥ karṣamātrakam  || 2,3.47 ||
krāmakaṃ hyanupānaṃ syājjarāmṛtyuharaṃ param  |
svarṇamekaṃ kāntamekaṃ pañcatāraṃ dvipāradam  || 2,3.48 ||
tribhāgaṃ vyomasattvaṃ syātṣaḍbhāgaṃ śulbacūrṇakam  |
sarvametatkṛtaṃ sūkṣmaṃ taptakhalve dinatrayam  || 2,3.49 ||
mardayedamlavargeṇa dolāyantre sakāñjike  |
tadgolaṃ tridinaṃ pacyādguṭikā surasundarī  || 2,3.50 ||
jāyate dhāritā vaktre varṣānmṛtyujarāpahā  |
bhūtāravaṭamūlaṃ ca karṣaṃ kṣīraiḥ pibedanu  || 2,3.51 ||
svarṇatārārkamuṇḍaṃ ca vaṅgasīsābhrasattvakam  |
etatsarvaṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ cūrṇāṃśaṃ mṛtavajrakam  || 2,3.52 ||
sarvatulyaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ sarvaṃ divyauṣadhidravaiḥ  |
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhamet || 2,3.53 ||
guṭikā vajratuṇḍeyaṃ jāyate dhāritā mukhe  |
jarāmṛtyuṃ śastrasaṃghaṃ nāśayedvatsarātkila  || 2,3.54 ||
vajrakāyo mahāvīro jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  |
kumāryāḥ svarasaṃ grāhyaṃ guḍena saha loḍayet || 2,3.55 ||
palaikaṃ krāmakaṃ lehyamanupānaṃ sadaiva hi  |
svarṇacūrṇaṃ tu bhāgaikaṃ tribhāgaṃ śuddhapāradam  || 2,3.56 ||
pādabhāgaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ tatsarvaṃ kṣīrakandajaiḥ  |
dravaiśca devadālyutthaistaptakhalve dināvadhi  || 2,3.57 ||
mardayetkāntacūrṇaṃ ca vajratulyaṃ kṣipecca vai  |
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhāmyaṃ guṭikā kāmasundarī  || 2,3.58 ||
jāyate dhāritā vaktre varṣānmṛtyujarāpahā  |
niṣkatrayaṃ brahmatailaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraṃ paladvayam  || 2,3.59 ||
miśritaṃ pāyayeccānu lakṣāyurjāyate naraḥ  |
lohapātre drute gandhe pādāṃśaṃ pāradaṃ kṣipet || 2,3.60 ||
kiṃciccālyaṃ tu kāṣṭhena muhūrtādavatārayet |
śatāvarī kṣīrakando vajravallīndravāruṇī  || 2,3.61 ||
pāṭhā punarnavā ciñcā lāṅgalī suradālikā  |
etāsāṃ vastrapūtaiśca dravairmardyaṃ dinatrayam  || 2,3.62 ||
tasmin pātre lohamuṣṭyā chāyāśuṣkaṃ vaṭīkṛtam  |
lohasampuṭagaṃ ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ mṛllavaṇairdṛḍham  || 2,3.63 ||
taṃ dhametkhadirāṅgāre yāvadāraktamuddharet |
utkhanyotkhanya tanmadhyāduddharettadrasaṃ punaḥ  || 2,3.64 ||
kācaṭaṅkaṇasaṃyuktaṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ pacet |
gāndhārīguṭikā siddhā varṣaṃ vaktre sthitā sadā  || 2,3.65 ||
kākatuṇḍībījatailaṃ karṣaikaṃ nasyamācaret |
krāmakaṃ hyanupānaṃ syājjīvedvarṣasahasrakam  || 2,3.66 ||
śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ mardanātkajjalīkṛtam  |
tattāmrasampuṭe ruddhvā lavaṇena mṛdā dṛḍham  || 2,3.67 ||
śuṣkaṃ dīpāgninā pacyādyāmaikaṃ bhasmayantrake  || 2,3.68 ||
sampuṭasyordhvalagnaṃ tatsamuddhṛtyātha mardayet |
tulyapāradasaṃyuktaṃ pūrvavatsampuṭe pacet || 2,3.69 ||
uddhṛtya tulyasūtena saṃyuktaṃ marditaṃ pacet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryātpunaḥ pāradaṭaṅkaṇam  || 2,3.70 ||
tulyaṃ tulyaṃ kṣipettasmin dinaṃ sarvaṃ vimardayet |
vajramūṣāgataṃ ruddhvā dhmāte khoṭo bhavedrasaḥ  || 2,3.71 ||
mārtaṇḍī guṭikā hyeṣā varṣaikaṃ yasya vaktragā  |
valīpalitamukto'sau jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,3.72 ||
palāśabījajaṃ tailaṃ palaikaṃ kṣīratulyakam  |
krāmaṇaṃ prapibennityaṃ tatkṣaṇānmūrchito bhavet || 2,3.73 ||
tasya vaktre gavāṃ kṣīraṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ niṣecayet |
prabuddhe kṣīramannaṃ syādbhojane paramaṃ hitam  || 2,3.74 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam  |
vāyuvego mahāsiddhaśchidrāṃ paśyati medinīm  || 2,3.75 ||
kākamācyamṛtādrāvaiḥ pāradaṃ tālakaṃ samam  |
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu kṛtvā golaṃ viśoṣayet || 2,3.76 ||
nikṣipedvajramūṣāyāmācchādya lohaparpaṭaiḥ  |
ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ khoṭabaddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 2,3.77 ||
lohaparpaṭakaṃ dattvā tadvaddhāmyaṃ tridhā punaḥ  |
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre guṭikā tārakeśvarī  || 2,3.78 ||
vākucībījakarṣaikaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibedanu  |
vyomasattvaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ svarṇatārārkamuṇḍakam  || 2,3.79 ||
tīkṣṇaṃ kāntaṃ tālakaṃ ca śuddhaṃ kṛtvā vimiśrayet |
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ samaṃ sarvaṃ cūrṇāṃśaṃ śuddhapāradam  || 2,3.80 ||
tridinaṃ cāmlavargeṇa marditaṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
vidyāvāgīśvarī khyātā guṭikā vatsarāvadhi  || 2,3.81 ||
yasya vaktre sthitā tasya jarā mṛtyurna vidyate  |
karṣaṃ jyotiṣmatītailaṃ krāmaṇārthaṃ pibetsadā  || 2,3.82 ||
vākpatirjāyate dhīro jīveccandrārkatārakam  |
kāntapāṣāṇamākṣīkaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ karkaṭāsthi ca  || 2,3.83 ||
snuhyarkakṣīrabhūnāgaṃ sarvametatsamaṃ bhavet |
strīstanyena dinaṃ mardyaṃ tena mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 2,3.84 ||
tanmadhye drutasūtaṃ tu vajrabhasma samaṃ samam  |
kṣiptvā ruddhvā puṭe pacyādgajākhye yāmamātrakam  || 2,3.85 ||
tataḥ praliptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā dhameddhaṭhāt |
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kāryaṃ vajrasūtaṃ milatyalam  || 2,3.86 ||
tatastasyaiva dātavyaṃ samaṃ kācaṃ saṭaṅkaṇam  |
evaṃ mūṣāśate deyaṃ tulyaṃ tulyaṃ dhaman dhaman  || 2,3.87 ||
tejaḥpuñjo rasendro'sau bhavenmārtaṇḍasaṃnibhaḥ  |
guṭikā vajratuṇḍeyaṃ vaktrasthā mṛtyunāśinī  || 2,3.88 ||
varṣamātrānna saṃdeho rudratulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ pūrvavatkāñcanaṃ bhavet || 2,3.89 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ bhakṣayetkarṣaṃ rudantyā madhusarpiṣā  |
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ karkaṭāsthīni ūrṇā caiva śilājatu  || 2,3.90 ||
mahiṣīkarṇanetrotthaṃ malaṃ strīstanyakaiḥ samam  |
piṣṭvā talliptamūṣāyāmabhrasattvaṃ kṣipeddhamet || 2,3.91 ||
sattvatulyaṃ kṣipettatra pūrvavaddrutapāradam  |
dravaṃ divyauṣadhīnāṃ ca dattvā tatraiva taddhamet || 2,3.92 ||
milito jāyate baddhaḥ pūrvavatkācaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ  |
dhmāto mūṣāśatenāyaṃ tejaḥpuñjo bhavedrasaḥ  || 2,3.93 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre śivatulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
ajarāmarakārīyaṃ guṭikā gaganeśvarī  || 2,3.94 ||
bilvabījotthitaṃ tailaṃ niṣkamātraṃ pibedanu  |
udare jāyate vahniḥ pibetkṣīraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,3.95 ||
sākṣājjātismaratvaṃ ca kavitvaṃ śrutadhāraṇam  |
khecaratvamadṛśyatvaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,3.96 ||
agnimantho vajravallī sūraṇaṃ vanaśūraṇam  |
citrakaśca dravaireṣāṃ śuddhasūtaṃ dināvadhi  || 2,3.97 ||
mardayettaptakhalve tu taṃ rasaṃ palamātrakam  |
palaṃ palaṃ tālavaṅgau tatsarvaṃ cāmlavetasaiḥ  || 2,3.98 ||
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu kṛtvā taṃ golakaṃ punaḥ  |
catuḥpalāṃ nāgamūṣāṃ kṛtvā tasyāṃ tu tatkṣipet || 2,3.99 ||
catuḥpale śuddhatāmrasampuṭe tāṃ nirodhayet |
mṛṇmūṣāyāṃ tu tāṃ ruddhvā āraṇyotpalakaiḥ puṭet || 2,3.100 ||
śatadvayapramāṇaistu svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet |
sarvaṃ divyauṣadhadrāvairmardayeddivasatrayam  || 2,3.101 ||
caturniṣkamitā kāryā vaṭikā śoṣayettataḥ  |
ekaikāṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet || 2,3.102 ||
ānandaguṭikā hyeṣā vaktrasthā mṛtyunāśinī  |
vatsarānnātra saṃdeho jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,3.103 ||
tailaṃ vātāribījotthaṃ gokṣīrairniṣkamātrakam  |
krāmaṇārthaṃ pibennityaṃ śīghrasiddhikaraṃ param  || 2,3.104 ||
vajrabhasmasamaṃ sūtaṃ haṃsapādyā dravaistryaham  |
marditaṃ dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet || 2,3.105 ||
bhūdharākhye divārātrau samuddhṛtyātha tasya vai  |
pūrvāṃśaṃ pāradaṃ dattvā haṃsapādyā dravaistryaham  || 2,3.106 ||
marditaṃ dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
tatkhoṭaṃ dhamanācchodhyaṃ kācaṭaṅkaṇayogataḥ  || 2,3.107 ||
nakṣatrābhaṃ bhavedyāvattāvaddhāmyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
tadrasaṃ vyomasattvaṃ ca kāñcanaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,3.108 ||
samāvartya tataḥ kāryā guṭikā vaktramadhyagā  |
vajrakhecarikā nāma vatsarānmṛtyunāśinī  || 2,3.109 ||
valīpalitanirmukto divyakāyo bhavennaraḥ  |
nirguṇḍīmunicūrṇaṃ tu karṣamājyaiḥ pibedanu  || 2,3.110 ||
kāntaṃ śulbaṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhamet |
tatkhoṭasiddhacūrṇaṃ tu gandhakāmlena mardayet || 2,3.111 ||
ruddhvā samyakpuṭe pacyātsamuddhṛtyātha mardayet |
pūrvavatkramayogena puṭedvārāṃścaturdaśa  || 2,3.112 ||
vajreṇa dvaṃdvitaṃ svarṇamanenaiva tu rañjayet |
mūṣāmadhye dhamannevaṃ saptavāraṃ samaṃ kṣipet || 2,3.113 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇitaṃ bhāvyaṃ strīpuṣpeṇa dināvadhi  |
tattulyaṃ drutasūtaṃ tu sarvaṃ yāmaṃ vimardayet || 2,3.114 ||
veṣṭayedbhūrjapattreṇa vastre baddhvā pacettryaham  |
dolāyantre sāranāle jātaṃ golaṃ samuddharet || 2,3.115 ||
gāndhārī jīvanī caiva lāṅgalī cendravāruṇī  |
etāsāṃ piṇḍakalkena veṣṭayetpūrvagolakam  || 2,3.116 ||
andhayitvā dinaṃ pacyādbhūdhare taṃ samuddharet |
punarlepyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ caturdaśadināvadhi  || 2,3.117 ||
guṭikā jāyate divyā nāmnā ratneśvarī tathā  |
vaktrasthā varṣamātraṃ tu nanditulyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,3.118 ||
jīvedvarṣasahasrāṇi divyatejā mahābalaḥ  |
varṣadvādaśaparyantaṃ yasya vaktre sthitā tu sā  || 2,3.119 ||
tasya prasvedasamparkādaṣṭalohāni kāñcanam  |
jāyante nātra saṃdehaḥ satyamīśvarabhāṣitam  || 2,3.120 ||
pañcāṅgacūrṇaṃ madhvājyai rudantyutthaṃ lihedanu  |
svarṇaṃ kṛṣṇābhrasattvaṃ ca tāraṃ tāmraṃ sucūrṇitam  || 2,3.121 ||
samāṃśaṃ dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
tatkhoṭaṃ bhāgacatvāri bhāgaikaṃ mṛtavajrakam  || 2,3.122 ||
mākṣikaṃ tīkṣṇakāntaṃ ca bhāgaikaikaṃ sucūrṇitam  |
samastaṃ dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet || 2,3.123 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu cūrṇāṃśaṃ drutasūtakam  |
tridinaṃ taptakhalve tu mardyaṃ divyauṣadhidravaiḥ  || 2,3.124 ||
ruddhvātha bhūdhare pacyādahorātrātsamuddharet |
drutasūtaṃ punastulyaṃ dattvā mardyaṃ puṭettathā  || 2,3.125 ||
ityevaṃ saptavārāṃstu drutaṃ sūtaṃ samaṃ samam  |
dattvā mardyaṃ puṭe pacyājjāyate bhasmasūtakaḥ  || 2,3.126 ||
bhasmasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā ruddhvā dhameddṛḍham  |
jāyate guṭikā divyā vikhyātā divyakhecarī  || 2,3.127 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre jīvetkalpasahasrakam  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ sarvalohasya lepanāt || 2,3.128 ||
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ samāvarte na saṃśayaḥ  |
paladvayaṃ bhṛṅgarājadravyaṃ cānu pibetsadā  || 2,3.129 ||
pūrvoktaṃ bhasmasūtaṃ vā guñjāmātraṃ sadā lihet |
varṣaikaṃ madhunājyena lakṣāyurjāyate naraḥ  || 2,3.130 ||
valīpalitanirmukto mahābalaparākramaḥ  |
svarṇaṃ vaikrāntasattvaṃ ca dvaṃdvitaṃ jārayedrase  || 2,3.131 ||
samāṃśaṃ tu bhavedyāvattatastenaiva sārayet |
samena jāyate baddho dhārayettaṃ mukhe sadā  || 2,3.132 ||
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa jarākālāpamṛtyujit |
kumāryā dalajaṃ drāvaṃ sitāyuktaṃ pibedanu  || 2,3.133 ||
svarṇavaikrāntabaddho'yaṃ brahmāyuryacchate nṛṇām  |
vaikrāntasattvatulyāṃśaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ vimardayet || 2,3.134 ||
dinaṃ divyauṣadhadrāvaistadgolaṃ nigaḍena vai  |
liptvā lavaṇagarbhāyāṃ vajramūṣyāṃ nirodhayet || 2,3.135 ||
chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetsaṃdhiṃ tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  |
svedayedvā karīṣāgnau divārātramathoddharet || 2,3.136 ||
tadgolaṃ nigaḍenaiva liptvā tadvannirudhya ca  |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ bandhamāyāti niścitam  || 2,3.137 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedvaktre jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
vaikrāntaguṭikā hyeṣā sarvakāmaphalapradā  || 2,3.138 ||
karṣaikaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu  |
hemnā yaddvaṃdvitaṃ vajraṃ kuryāttatsūkṣmacūrṇitam  || 2,3.139 ||
etaddeyaṃ guhyasūte mūṣāyāmadharottaram  |
pādamātraṃ prayatnena ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ viśoṣayet || 2,3.140 ||
bhūdharākhye dinaṃ pacyātsamuddhṛtyātha mardayet |
divyauṣadhaphaladrāvaistaptakhalve dināvadhi  || 2,3.141 ||
ruddhvātha bhūdhare pacyāddinaṃ laghupuṭaiḥ puṭet |
samuddhṛtya punastadvanmardyaṃ ruddhvā dinatrayam  || 2,3.142 ||
tuṣāgninā śanaiḥ svedyamūrdhvādhaḥ parivartayan  |
jāyate bhasmasūto'yaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,3.143 ||
drutasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgaikaṃ pūrvabhasmakam  |
śuddhanāgasya bhāgaikaṃ sarvamamlena mardayet || 2,3.144 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhāmyaṃ khoṭo bhavati tadrasaḥ  |
dhametprakaṭamūṣāyāṃ yāvannāgakṣayo bhavet || 2,3.145 ||
drutasūtaprakāreṇa drāvayitvā tvimaṃ rasam  |
nikṣipetkacchape yantre viḍaṃ dattvā daśāṃśataḥ  || 2,3.146 ||
svarṇādisarvalohāni krameṇaiva ca jārayet |
pratyekaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ paścādvajradvaṃdvaṃ ca jārayet || 2,3.147 ||
triguṇaṃ tu bhavedyāvattato ratnāni vai kramāt |
jārayeddrāvitānyeva pratyekaṃ triguṇaṃ śanaiḥ  || 2,3.148 ||
tato yantrātsamuddhṛtya divyauṣadhadravairdinam  |
mardyaṃ ruddhvā dhamedgāḍhaṃ jāyate guṭikā śubhā  || 2,3.149 ||
pūjayedaṅkuśīmantrairnāmneyaṃ divyakhecarī  |
yasya vaktre sthitā hyeṣā sa bhavedbhairavopamaḥ  || 2,3.150 ||
divyatejā mahākāyaḥ khecaratvena gacchati  |
yatrecchā tatra tatraiva krīḍate hyaṅganādibhiḥ  || 2,3.151 ||
mahākalpāntaparyantaṃ tiṣṭhatyeva na saṃśayaḥ  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 2,3.152 ||
palāśapuṣpacūrṇaṃ tu tilāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ saśarkarāḥ  |
sarvaṃ palatrayaṃ khādennityaṃ syātkrāmaṇe hitam  || 2,3.153 ||
cūrṇamaśvakhurasyaiva guhyasūte samaṃ kṣipet |
tridinaṃ mātuluṅgāmlaistatsarvaṃ mardayeddṛḍham  || 2,3.154 ||
sūtatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ tasmin kṣiptvātha mardayet |
taptakhalve dinaṃ cāmlaistadgolaṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet || 2,3.155 ||
dinaikaṃ bhūdhare yantre bhāgaikaṃ pūrvapāradam  |
kṣiptvā tasmin dṛḍhaṃ mardyaṃ mātuluṅgadravairdinam  || 2,3.156 ||
ruddhvātha pūrvavatpacyātpunardeyaśca pāradaḥ  |
mardyaṃ pācyaṃ yathāpūrvamevaṃ kuryācca saptadhā  || 2,3.157 ||
rasaṃ punaḥ punardattvā syādevaṃ bhasmasūtakaḥ  |
yojayetsarvayogeṣu jarāmṛtyuharo bhavet || 2,3.158 ||
bhāgaikaṃ nāgacūrṇasya bhāgaikaṃ pūrvabhasmanaḥ  |
drutasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ khoṭaṃ kuryācca pūrvavat || 2,3.159 ||
tadvaddhāmyaṃ gate nāge drāvitaṃ jārayetpunaḥ  |
pūrvavalloharatnāntaṃ jīrṇe baddhā sthitā mukhe  || 2,3.160 ||
pracaṇḍakhecarīnāmnī guṭikā khe gatipradā  |
pūrvavallabhate vīraḥ phalamatyantadurlabham  || 2,3.161 ||
nirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu karṣaṃ takraiḥ pibedanu  |
śuddhasūtasya dātavyaṃ kalāṃśaṃ mṛtavajrakam  || 2,3.162 ||
tatsarvamamlavargeṇa taptakhalve dinatrayam  |
mardayitvā tatastena lepayetsamabhāgataḥ  || 2,3.163 ||
pakvabījasya pattrāṇi tāni bhānudalaiḥ punaḥ  |
veṣṭitāni nirudhyātha nikhanecculligarbhataḥ  || 2,3.164 ||
ācchādya jvālayettatra kāṣṭhāgniṃ divasatrayam  |
uddhṛtya dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ taṃ nirodhayet || 2,3.165 ||
karīṣāgnau puṭe pacyādahorātrātsamuddharet |
vāsanāmukhite sūte tulyametadvinikṣipet || 2,3.166 ||
amlena mardayedyāmaṃ jātaṃ golaṃ samuddharet |
kṣipejjambīragarbhe taṃ vastre baddhvā tryahaṃ pacet || 2,3.167 ||
dolāyantre sāranāle jāyate guṭikā śubhā  |
kaṅkālakhecarī nāmnā vaktrasthā mṛtyunāśinī  || 2,3.168 ||
varṣaikaṃ dhārayedyastu sa jīvedbrahmaṇo dinam  |
gandhakaṃ gugguluṃ tulyamājyaiḥ karṣaṃ lihedanu  || 2,3.169 ||
kṛṣṇābhrakasya sattvaṃ tu kāntamākṣīkakāñcanam  |
tīkṣṇaṃ sauvīracūrṇaṃ ca tulyaṃ ruddhvā dhameddṛḍham  || 2,3.170 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu drutasūtasamaṃ bhavet |
sūtārdhaṃ māritaṃ vajraṃ sarvamamlena mardayet || 2,3.171 ||
dinaikaṃ taptakhalve tu taṃ ruddhvā bhūdhare pacet |
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya tatsamaṃ pūrvasūtakam  || 2,3.172 ||
dattvā divyauṣadhadrāvairmardyaṃ sarvaṃ dināvadhi  |
pūrvavadbhūdhare pacyāddrutasūtaṃ punaḥ samam  || 2,3.173 ||
dattvā mardyaṃ punaḥ pacyādityevaṃ saptavārakam  |
etadbhasmasamaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā cāndhyaṃ dhameddṛḍham  || 2,3.174 ||
jāyate guṭikā divyā kālavidhvaṃsikā parā  |
yasya vaktre sthitā hyeṣā tasya kālaḥ karoti kim  || 2,3.175 ||
varṣaṣaṭkaprayogeṇa jīvetkalpasahasrakam  |
tadgātrasvedamātreṇa sarvalohāni kāñcanam  || 2,3.176 ||
jāyante nātra saṃdehaḥ śivāmbu krāmakaṃ pibet |
svarṇatārārkakāntaṃ ca tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ samaṃ samam  || 2,3.177 ||
dvaṃdvamelāpaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā cābhiṣiktaṃ tu pūrvavat || 2,3.178 ||
samukhe jārayetsūte yāvatpañcaguṇaṃ kramāt |
divyauṣadhadravaistaṃ vai mardayeddivasatrayam  || 2,3.179 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ jāyate guṭikā śubhā  |
nāmnā pañcānanā dhāryā vaktre saṃvatsarāvadhi  || 2,3.180 ||
valīpalitanirmukto jīveccandrārkatārakam  |
hastikarṇī samūlā tu cūrṇyā madhvājyasaṃyutā  || 2,3.181 ||
snigdhabhāṇḍe tu tāṃ ruddhvā dhānyarāśau niveśayet |
trisaptāhātsamuddhṛtya palaikaṃ bhakṣayedanu  || 2,3.182 ||
ṛddhikhaṇḍe tu yatproktaṃ vividhaṃ rasabandhanam  |
atra tasyaiva vakṣyāmi dehavedhakramaṃ yathā  || 2,3.183 ||
jāritairbandhitaistaistai rasarājaiḥ pṛthakpṛthak |
kārayedguṭikāṃ divyāṃ badarāṇḍapramāṇakām  || 2,3.184 ||
sā dhāryā vatsaraṃ vaktre svānurūpaphalapradā  |
guṭikā śatavedhī syādyugāyuṣyakarī nṛṇām  || 2,3.185 ||
sahasravedhī guṭikā aṣṭakalpāntarakṣikā  |
lakṣavedhakarī yā tu sā datte viṣṇuvadbalam  || 2,3.186 ||
vedhikā daśalakṣe yā sā rudrapadadāyinī  |
koṭivedhakarī yā sā īśvaratvakarī nṛṇām  || 2,3.187 ||
vedhikā daśakoṭīnāṃ sā sadākhyapadapradā  |
guṭikārbudavedhī yā sā śrīkaṇṭhapadapradā  || 2,3.188 ||
samyagbhavapadaṃ datte guṭikā śaṅkhavedhikā  |
dhūmravedhī tu yā siddhā sā śaktipadadāyinī  || 2,3.189 ||
parāśaktipadaṃ datte sparśavedhakarī tu yā  |
śabdavedhakarā yā tu sā yasya vatsarāvadhi  || 2,3.190 ||
vaktre sthitā sa vai siddho nityaṃ nityapadaṃ labhet |
svecchācārī vajrakāyo vajrapātairna bhidyate  || 2,3.191 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ sarvalohāni kāñcanam  |
jāyante svedasamparkādgātrasaṃsparśanādapi  || 2,3.192 ||
sarveṣāmuktayogānāmanu syācchuddhagandhakam  |
palārdhaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ rasasaṃkrāmaṇe hitam  || 2,3.193 ||
kāli kāli mahākāli māṃsaśoṇitabhojini  |
raktakṛṣṇamukhe devi rasasiddhiṃ dadasva me  || 2,3.194 ||
svāhā anena siddhamantreṇa śakticakraṃ prapūjayet |
kālikāṃ bhairavaṃ siddhān kumārīṃ sādhitaṃ rasam  || 2,3.195 ||
tato rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ sevayetsiddhimāpnuyāt |
guṭikāṃ dhārayedvaktre pūrvamantraṃ japetsadā  || 2,3.196 ||
rasamantraprayogeṇa śīghraṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
kālāntarasasiddhiryā proktā manthānabhairave  || 2,3.197 ||
tadarthaṃ pañca tattvāni ṣaṣṭhaṃ jīvaṃ ca sādhayet |
kākinyāḥ puṣpakāle tu saṅgaṃ kṛtvā samāharet || 2,3.198 ||
tadyonisthaṃ rajobījaṃ gaganaṃ taṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
kākinyutpannaputrasya sadyoviḍvāyurucyate  || 2,3.199 ||
tejastu kākinīpuṣpaṃ jalaṃ tatputraśoṇitam  |
kākinīputrasarvāṅgaṃ pṛthivītattvamucyate  || 2,3.200 ||
rasasevakadehotthavīryaṃ jīvastu kathyate  |
tatpratyekaṃ koṭivedhaṃ karṣaikaṃ rasasaṃyutam  || 2,3.201 ||
kṛtvā saṃrakṣayedbhinnaṃ supiṣṭaṃ golakīkṛtam  |
unnataṃ pauruṣaṃ yāvadvistāreṇa tadardhakam  || 2,3.202 ||
kuryāttāmrakaṭāhaṃ tu sthūlaṃ yāvatṣaḍaṅgulam  |
caturmukhasya koṣṭhasya pṛṣṭhe dhāryaṃ dṛḍhaṃ yathā  || 2,3.203 ||
goghṛtaṃ naratailaṃ ca samabhāgena melayet |
tenāpūrya kaṭāhaṃ taṃ siddhacakraṃ tato'rcayet || 2,3.204 ||
kumārīgurudevāgnīn bhairavaṃ bhairavīyutam  |
tarpayedbalimāṃsena kṣetrapālaṃ ca pūjayet || 2,3.205 ||
dhamanaṃ tatra kurvīta caturdikṣu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  |
caturbhirvaṅkanālaiśca khadirāṅgārayogataḥ  || 2,3.206 ||
sutaptaṃ phenanirmuktaṃ nirdhūmaṃ ca yadā bhavet |
candrārkagrahanakṣatradevatābhuvanāni ca  || 2,3.207 ||
namaskṛtya guruṃ devaṃ dehaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
sudrutaṃ taṃ vijānīyānnikṣipetpārthivaṃ rasam  || 2,3.208 ||
dhamannatraiva yatnena yāvattatkalkatāṃ vrajet |
aptattvākhyaṃ rasaṃ tasmin kṣipedraktaṃ bhavettu tat || 2,3.209 ||
vāyuyuktaṃ rasaṃ kṣiptvā śubhravarṇaṃ prajāyate  |
tejoyuktaṃ rasaṃ kṣipyādghanībhūtaṃ bhavettu tat || 2,3.210 ||
tata ākāśasaṃyuktaṃ rasaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
āvartitaṃ suvarṇābhaṃ jāyate tatra nikṣipet || 2,3.211 ||
jīvayuktaṃ rasaṃ divyaṃ tato huṃkāramuccaret |
kṛtvā tatra mahārāvaṃ huṃkāratrayasaṃyutam  || 2,3.212 ||
tataścottiṣṭhate siddhaḥ pūrvāhṇe bhāskaro yathā  |
divyatejā mahākāyo mahābalaparākramaḥ  || 2,3.213 ||
navanāgasahasrāṇāṃ balaṃ tasyādhikaṃ bhavet |
jīvate vajradehaḥ san satyaṃ satyaṃ śivoditam  || 2,3.214 ||
jarājarjaritāṅgānāmandhānāṃ paṅgukuṣṭhinām  |
naṣṭavāgjaḍaṣaṇḍānāṃ kubjānāṃ kuṣṭhadehinām  || 2,3.215 ||
anekavyādhiyuktānāṃ bhrāntonmattapiśācinām  |
kiṃ punaḥ svacchadehānāṃ bhūpānāṃ rasasevinām  || 2,3.216 ||
vīrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ ca divyasiddhiprado bhavet |
tasmādvīrataro yo'tra bhairavo'sau na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,3.217 ||
vajrakāyo bhavetsiddho medhāvī divyarūpavān  |
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ vimānaṃ cāpsaroyutam  || 2,3.218 ||
āyāti nātra saṃdehastasya siddhasya sammukham  |
tatrārūḍho rudraloke krīḍate bhairavo yathā  || 2,3.219 ||
kṣutpipāsāvinirmukto jagannāśe na naśyati  |
bhuñjānaḥ sarvabhogāṃśca yogināṃ sa priyo bhavet |
icchāsiddho mahāvīro nityānandamayo bhavet || 2,3.220 ||
dṛṣṭvā samastamanubhūya rasāyaneṣu sārātisārasukhasādhyataraṃ narāṇām  |
dehasya dārḍhyakaraṇe guṭikāprayogāḥ proktāḥ paraṃ śivakarāḥ satataṃ susiddhyai  || 2,3.221 ||


 2, 4
sūtagandhagaganāyasaśulbaṃ māritaṃ ca paramāmṛtīkṛtam  |
iṣṭamekamapi mūlikāgaṇaṃ dehasiddhikaramāśu sevitam  || 2,4.1 ||
mṛtakāntābhrakaṃ sūtaṃ gandhaṃ bhṛṅgaviḍaṅgakam  |
tvagvarjaṃ bilvabījaṃ ca pratyekaṃ palaṣoḍaśa  || 2,4.2 ||
triṃśatpalaṃ tryūṣaṇaṃ ca triṃśattriṃśadghṛtaṃ madhu  |
citramūlaṃ daśapalaṃ sarvaṃ cūrṇaṃ viloḍayet || 2,4.3 ||
palāni triphalāyāstu viṃśapūrvaṃ catuḥśatam  |
kvāthyamaṣṭaguṇaistoyairgrāhyamaṣṭāvaśeṣitam  || 2,4.4 ||
kaṣāyaṃ bhāvayettena māsaikaṃ pūrvaloḍitam  |
lohapātre khare gharme tatpalārdhaṃ sadā pibet || 2,4.5 ||
kṣīraiḥ śayanakāle tu varṣānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
bālo nibiḍasaṃdhiśca jīveccandrārkatārakam  || 2,4.6 ||
mahārasāyanaṃ divyaṃ kāminīśatatoṣakam  |
agnivarṇaṃ kṣipetkṣīre kṛṣṇābhraṃ vahnitāpitam  || 2,4.7 ||
bhinnapattraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā jalamadhye vinikṣipet |
triṃśatpalāni yatnena maricaṃ palapañcakam  || 2,4.8 ||
cūrṇitaṃ nikṣipettasmiṃstridinānte samuddharet |
tatsarvaṃ peṣayecchlakṣṇaṃ sitavastreṇa bandhayet || 2,4.9 ||
jalapūrṇe ghaṭe gharme dolāyantreṇa dhārayet |
śuṣke toye punastoyaṃ dadyādyāvajjale gatam  || 2,4.10 ||
tadabhrakaṃ tato vastraṃ saṃtyajedrakṣayejjalam  |
triṃśadbhāgaṃ tataḥ kuryāttajjalaṃ sābhrakaṃ sudhīḥ  || 2,4.11 ||
tadbhāgaikena saṃloḍya palaikaṃ śvetataṇḍulāt |
triṃśatpale gavāṃ kṣīre tatpaceccātha śītalam  || 2,4.12 ||
madhvājyairdvipalairyuktaṃ niṣkaikaiśca marīcakaiḥ  |
sādhako bhakṣayennityaṃ māsānmṛtyujarāpaham  || 2,4.13 ||
keśā dantā nakhāstasya patanti hyudbhavanti ca  |
vajrakāyo bhavetsiddho vāyuvego mahābalaḥ  || 2,4.14 ||
amṛtābhrakayogo'yaṃ śambhunā gaditaḥ purā  |
mṛtābhraṃ gandhakaṃ śuddhaṃ kaṇā sarvaṃ samaṃ ghṛtaiḥ  || 2,4.15 ||
karṣaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ varṣānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
koraṇṭakasya pattrāṇi mṛtābhraṃ gandhakaṃ samam  || 2,4.16 ||
tatsarvaṃ nīlikādrāvaiḥ saptāhaṃ bhāvyamātape  |
tatkarṣaikaṃ pibetkṣīrairabdānmṛtyujarāpaham  || 2,4.17 ||
mṛtābhrakasya karṣaikaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraṃ palaṃ tathā  |
samūlapattrāṃ sarpākṣīṃ sārdrāṃ piṣṭvā ca gandhakam  || 2,4.18 ||
ekīkṛtya pibetsarvaṃ varṣaikena jarāṃ jayet |
vajrakāyaḥ khecaraśca jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.19 ||
mṛtābhraṃ kāntalohaṃ ca triphalā māgadhī samam  |
pañcāṅgaṃ badarīcūrṇamabhratulyaṃ niyojayet || 2,4.20 ||
sitāmadhvājyasaṃyuktaṃ palārdhaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  |
hanti varṣājjarāṃ mṛtyumāyuḥ syādbrahmaṇo dinam  || 2,4.21 ||
amṛtakrīḍe viṣṇusaṃvaraṇi svāhā  |
anena mantreṇa sarve abhrakayogā abhimantrya bhakṣaṇīyāḥ  |
mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ tilāḥ kṛṣṇā badarīphalacūrṇakam  |
kākatuṇḍībījacūrṇaṃ sarvaṃ tulyaṃ prakalpayet || 2,4.22 ||
śālmalīmallipattrāṇāṃ dravairbhāvyaṃ dinatrayam  |
tridinaṃ bhṛṅgajairdrāvairbhāvitaṃ śoṣayetpunaḥ  || 2,4.23 ||
tasmin tulyaṃ guḍaṃ kṣiptvā vaṭikāḥ karṣamātrakāḥ  |
rudantyutthadravaiḥ kṣīrairmadhvājyābhyāṃ pibetsadā  || 2,4.24 ||
varṣaikena jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ hanti satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ kṛṣṇatilāṃstriphalāṃ cūrṇayetsamam  || 2,4.25 ||
śālmalīketakīdrāvairloḍitaṃ kāntapātrake  |
sthitaṃ rātrau pibetprātaḥ palārdhaṃ mṛtyunāśanam  || 2,4.26 ||
vatsaraikājjarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
kāntabhasma kaṇācūrṇaṃ nimbaniryāsameva ca  || 2,4.27 ||
triphalātulyatulyāṃśaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ palārdhakam  |
lihenmāsāṣṭakaṃ nityaṃ jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  || 2,4.28 ||
kuṣṭhakhaṇḍāni saṃpācya kaṣāye traiphale same  |
śoṣayitvā vicūrṇyātha tāni kāntaṃ mṛtaṃ samam  || 2,4.29 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ varṣānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
koraṇṭapattracūrṇaṃ tu kāntabhasma tilā guḍam  || 2,4.30 ||
tulyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ palārdhaṃ tadvarṣānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
kāntabhasma samaṃ gandhaṃ tailairjyotiṣmatībhavaiḥ  || 2,4.31 ||
lihennityaṃ caturniṣkaṃ brahmāyurjāyate naraḥ  |
bṛhaspatisamo vācā vatsarādbhavati dhruvam  || 2,4.32 ||
mṛtatīkṣṇaṃ trayo bhāgāḥ śuddhagandhāṣṭabhāgakam  |
gharme bhāvyaṃ trisaptāhaṃ tatsarvaṃ kanyakādravaiḥ  || 2,4.33 ||
gokṣīraistatpibetkarṣaṃ jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
varṣamātrānna saṃdeho divyatejā mahābalaḥ  || 2,4.34 ||
mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ śilā śuddhā tulyaṃ madhvājyakairlihet |
niṣkaṃ niṣkaṃ tu varṣaikaṃ jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.35 ||
triniṣkaṃ mṛtatīkṣṇaṃ tu muṇḍaṃ vā kāntameva vā  |
pibeddhāroṣṇapayasā vayaḥstambhakaraṃ nṛṇām  || 2,4.36 ||
varṣadvayaprayogeṇa jīvedācandratārakam  |
samyakkāntamaye pātre dhātrīcūrṇaṃ śivāmbunā  || 2,4.37 ||
rātrau palaikaṃ saṃlipya prātarutthāya bhakṣayet |
valīpalitanirmukto vatsarānmṛtyujidbhavet || 2,4.38 ||
lepayetkāntapātrāntaḥ palaikaṃ triphalāmadhu  |
divārātraṃ sthitaṃ peyaṃ tannityaṃ tu śivāmbunā  |
varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  || 2,4.39 ||
oṃ haḥ amṛte amṛtaśakti amṛtagandhopajīvi niṣpannaṃ candrāmṛtamājñāpitaṃkuru kurusvāhā he he haṃ haḥ gamiti gandhakalohayorbhakṣaṇamantraḥ  |
sarveṣāṃ lohayogānāmanu syātkṣīrapānakam  |
āsvādayetsvādumustānāṃ svarasaṃ dantapīḍitam  || 2,4.40 ||
mūlāni bhakṣayettāsāmāsyavairasyaśāntaye  |
baddhe koṣṭhe tu dīptāgnau taptaṃ kṣīraṃ pibetsadā  || 2,4.41 ||
snānamardanatīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ viṣṭambhe sati varjayet |
tāmbūlaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ sakarpūraṃ muhurmuhuḥ  || 2,4.42 ||
samyagjīrṇe tu dīptāgnau pibetpaścādbubhukṣitaḥ  |
śṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ tato'nnaṃ ca sevyaṃ lauharasāyane  || 2,4.43 ||
brahmavṛkṣasya pañcāṅgaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ sucūrṇitam  |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ varṣaikena jarāṃ jayet || 2,4.44 ||
jīvedvarṣasahasraikaṃ divyatejā mahābalaḥ  |
brahmavṛkṣasya puṣpāṇi chāyāśuṣkāṇi kārayet || 2,4.45 ||
triṃśatpalaṃ tu taccūrṇaṃ caturviṃśatpalaṃ ghṛtam  |
ekīkṛtya kṣipedbhāṇḍe taṃ ruddhvā dhānyarāśigam  || 2,4.46 ||
kṛtvā māsātsamuddhṛtya bhāgān kuryāccaturdaśa  |
bhāgaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ bhuñjīta kāntabhājane  || 2,4.47 ||
evaṃ māsatrayaṃ kuryādvajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmramāyāti kāñcanam  || 2,4.48 ||
brahmavṛkṣasya bījāni cūrṇitāni ghṛtaiḥ saha  |
pūrvavaddhānyamadhye tu kṣiptvā māsātsamuddharet || 2,4.49 ||
palaikaikaṃ sadā khādedvatsarānmṛtyujidbhavet |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.50 ||
brahmabījotthitaṃ tailaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ paladvayam  |
tulyaiḥ pibedbhavenmūrchā siñcettasya mukhe payaḥ  || 2,4.51 ||
bodhe kṣīraudanaṃ dadyānmāsājjñānī bhavennaraḥ  |
dvitīye śukratulyaḥ syāttṛtīye vajravadbhavet || 2,4.52 ||
dūraśrāvī caturthe tu pañcame khegatirbhavet |
māsaṣaṭke svayaṃ kartā śivatulyaparākramaḥ  || 2,4.53 ||
mahākalpāntaparyantaṃ jīvedvarṣaikasevanāt |
brahmavṛkṣamatisthūlaṃ chedayedūrdhvabhāgataḥ  || 2,4.54 ||
adho rakṣyaṃ trihastaṃ syāttasya mūrdhni bilaṃ kṛtam  |
pakvadhātrīphalaiḥ pūryaṃ tatkāṣṭhena nirudhya ca  || 2,4.55 ||
kuśaistu veṣṭayetsarvaṃ lepyaṃ mṛdgomayaiḥ punaḥ  |
āveṣṭya vastrakhaṇḍena limpenmṛdgomayaistataḥ  || 2,4.56 ||
śuṣke gajapuṭaṃ deyaṃ parito'raṇyakotpalaiḥ  |
svāṅgaśītalamuddhṛtya sadravāṇi phalāni ca  || 2,4.57 ||
kṣipenmadhvājyasaṃyukte bhāṇḍe tānyeva bhakṣayet |
yatheṣṭaṃ bhūgṛhāntasthaḥ kṣīrāhārī jarāṃ jayet || 2,4.58 ||
māsadvayena vasudhāṃ chidrāṃ paśyati niścitam  |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ yāvatsarpavatkañcukaṃ tyajet || 2,4.59 ||
śvetapālāśapañcāṅgaṃ cūrṇitaṃ madhunā saha  |
karṣaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ māsānmṛtyujarāpaham  || 2,4.60 ||
brahmāyurjāyate siddho varṣamātrānna saṃśayaḥ  |
ajāghṛtena tadbījamekaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  || 2,4.61 ||
śarīraṃ bhasmanā mardyaṃ māsānmṛtyujarāṃ jayet |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ yāvaddivyakāyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,4.62 ||
anye yogā yathā rakte brahmavṛkṣe ca ye guṇāḥ  |
tathaiva śvetapālāśe bhaveyuḥ sādhakasya vai  || 2,4.63 ||
amṛtaṃ kuru kuru amṛtamālinyai namaḥ  |
anena mantreṇa sarvayogāḥ saptābhimantritā bhakṣaṇīyāḥ  |
śuklapakṣe'tha pūrṇāyāṃ puṣye vā śravaṇe tathā  |
revatyāṃ vātha sampūjya muṇḍīpañcāṅgamuddharet || 2,4.64 ||
chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu taccūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ gopayasā saha  |
varṣaikena jarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  || 2,4.65 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmraṃ sauvarṇatāṃ vrajet |
taccūrṇaṃ tu ghṛtairlehyaṃ tadvatsyādbalamadbhutam  || 2,4.66 ||
oṃ namo'mṛtodbhavāya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā oṃ hrāṃ saḥ  |
iti auṣadhabhakṣaṇamantraḥ  |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ devadālīpañcāṅgaṃ cūrṇayettataḥ  |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ varṣānmṛtyujarāṃ jayet || 2,4.67 ||
jīvetkalpasahasraṃ tu rudratulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
taccūrṇaṃ karṣamātraṃ tu nityaṃ peyaṃ śivāmbunā  || 2,4.68 ||
pūrvavajjāyate siddhirvatsarānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
taccūrṇaṃ vākucīvahnisarpākṣībhṛṅgarāṭsamam  || 2,4.69 ||
cūrṇitaṃ karṣamātraṃ tu nityaṃ peyaṃ śivāmbunā  |
varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ hanti chidrāṃ paśyati medinīm  || 2,4.70 ||
punarnavādevadālyornīrairnityaṃ pibennaraḥ  |
devadālyāśca sarpākṣyāḥ palaikaṃ vā śivāmbunā  || 2,4.71 ||
pibetsyātpūrvavatsiddhirvatsarānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
devadālīṃ ca nirguṇḍīṃ pibetkarṣaṃ śivāmbunā  |
varṣaikena jarāṃ hanti jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,4.72 ||
omamṛtagaṇa rudragaṇāmbhaḥ svāhā  |
ayaṃ ca grahaṇamantraḥ  |
namo bhagavate rudrāya huṃ phaṭsvāhā  |
ayaṃ sādhakasya śikhābandhanamantraḥ  |
oṃ cara ra ra  |
ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ  || 2,4.73 ||
puṣye śvetārkamūlaṃ tu grāhyaṃ chāyāviśoṣitam  |
cūrṇakarṣaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ paladvaṃdvairyutaṃ pibet || 2,4.74 ||
māsaṣaṭkājjarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
dravaṃ śvetārkapattrāṇāṃ bhṛṅgarājadravaiḥ samam  || 2,4.75 ||
ekīkṛtyātape śuṣkaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣīraiścaturguṇaiḥ  |
mṛdvagninā pacettāvadyāvatpiṇḍatvamāgatam  |
tatkarṣaikaṃ ghṛtairlehyaṃ varṣātsyātpūrvavatphalam  || 2,4.76 ||
omāṃ haṃ vāsaramāline svāhā  |
ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ  |
grāhyaṃ somatrayodaśyāṃ hastikarṇasya pattrakam  |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu taccūrṇaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibetpalam  || 2,4.77 ||
varṣamātrājjarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  |
hastikarṇasya pañcāṅgaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇitam  || 2,4.78 ||
karṣamātraṃ pibennityaṃ māsaikamudakaiḥ saha  |
āranālaistatastakrairdadhikṣīrājyakṣaudrakaiḥ  || 2,4.79 ||
pratyekena kramātsevyaṃ māsaikena jarāpaham  |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ sārdhaṃ vajrakāyo mahābalaḥ  || 2,4.80 ||
oṃ garaviṣaṃ dṛṣṭau gṛhṇāmi svāhā  |
hastikarṇagrahaṇamantraḥ  |
omamṛtakuṭījātānāmamṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā  |
anena pūjayet |
omamṛtodbhavāya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru nityaṃ namo namaḥ  |
bhakṣaṇamantraḥ  || 2,4.81 ||
rudantyāścaiva pañcāṅgaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet |
tadardhaṃ musalīcūrṇaṃ musalyardhaṃ phalatrayam  || 2,4.82 ||
mūlānāṃ katakotthānāṃ tailaṃ pātālayantrake  |
grāhayedgarbhayantre vā tattailaṃ kṣālayejjalaiḥ  || 2,4.83 ||
nālikerajalairvātha kṣālyaṃ pañcāṃśavārakam  |
etattailena saṃyuktaṃ pūrvacūrṇaṃ lihetkramāt || 2,4.84 ||
karṣādivardhanaṃ kāryaṃ palāntaṃ cātha vardhayet |
evamabdājjarāṃ hanti āyuḥ syādbrahmaṇo dinam  || 2,4.85 ||
siddhayogo hyayaṃ khyāto vajrakāyakaro nṛṇām  |
puṣyārke grāhayetprātarnirguṇḍīmūlajāṃ tvacam  || 2,4.86 ||
chāyāśuṣkāṃ vicūrṇyātha karṣamekaṃ pibetsadā  |
ajāmūtrapalaikena ṣaṇmāsādamaro bhavet || 2,4.87 ||
varṣamātraprayogeṇa śivatulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
taccūrṇaṃ kṣīramadhvājyairloḍitaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍake  || 2,4.88 ||
ruddhvā kṣipeddhānyarāśau māsāduddhṛtya bhakṣayet |
dvipalaṃ varṣaparyantaṃ jīveccandrārkatārakam  || 2,4.89 ||
taccūrṇārdhapalaṃ cājyairlihetsyātpūrvavatphalam  |
taccūrṇaṃ triphalā muṇḍī bhṛṅgī nimbo guḍūcikā  || 2,4.90 ||
vacā caiṣāṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetpalam  |
varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.91 ||
nirguṇḍīpattrajaṃ drāvaṃ bhāṇḍe mṛdvagninā pacet |
guḍavatpākamāpannaṃ pītaṃ vāntivirekakṛt || 2,4.92 ||
niryānti kṛmayastasya mukhanāsākṣikarṇataḥ  |
rājayakṣmādirogāṃśca saptāhena vināśayet |
māsatrayājjarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  || 2,4.93 ||
oṃ namo māya gaṇapataye bhūpataye kuberāya svāhā iti bhakṣaṇamantraḥ  |
bhallātako'bhayā vīrā kākatuṇḍyāḥ phalaṃ vacā  |
lāṅgalī nimbapattrāṇi sahadevī samaṃ samam  || 2,4.94 ||
eṣāṃ pātālayantreṇa tailaṃ grāhyaṃ prayatnataḥ  |
tattailaṃ nīlikāmūlayuktamardhapalaṃ pibet || 2,4.95 ||
vatsarātpalitaṃ hanti āyuḥ syādbrahmaṇo dinam  |
tailārdhaniṣke tannasye kṛte syātpūrvavatphalam  || 2,4.96 ||
kṛṣṇajīrakaprasthaikaṃ tattulyaṃ bhṛṅgajadravam  |
yaṣṭī nīlotpalaṃ caiva prati prasthārdhamāharet || 2,4.97 ||
pādaprasthaṃ tilāttailaṃ sarvamekatra pācayet |
grāhyaṃ tailāvaśeṣaṃ tannasyaṃ tenaiva kārayet || 2,4.98 ||
nasyaṃ cāṅkollatailena kuryānmṛtyujarāpaham  |
niṣkārdhaniṣkaṃ varṣaikaṃ jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam  || 2,4.99 ||
kākamācīphalaṃ piṣṭvā karṣaikamudakaiḥ pibet |
vatsarātpalitaṃ hanti āyuḥ syādbrahmaṇo dinam  || 2,4.100 ||
guḍūcī musalī muṇḍī nirguṇḍī ca śatāvarī  |
vijayā ca samaṃ cūrṇaṃ sitāmadhvājyasaṃyutam  || 2,4.101 ||
khādetkarṣadvayaṃ nityaṃ vatsarātpalitaṃ jayet |
uktaṃ gorakṣanāthena jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.102 ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇasūtrairvṛkṣaṃ śunakaśālmaleḥ  |
āveṣṭyāghoramantreṇa rātrau kṛṣṇājakaṃ balim  || 2,4.103 ||
dattvāghoraṃ japettatra yāvadaṣṭasahasrakam  |
tasya mūlatvacaṃ grāhyaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet || 2,4.104 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ sadā khādetpalaikaṃ vatsarāvadhi  |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ  || 2,4.105 ||
tasya puṣpāṇi saṃgṛhya gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ sadā pacet |
puṣpavarjaṃ pibetkṣīraṃ māsānmṛtyujarāpaham  || 2,4.106 ||
phalaikaṃ tasya vṛkṣasya gavāṃ kṣīreṇa pācayet |
phalavarjaṃ pibetkṣīraṃ kṣīramevaṃ pibedbudhaḥ  || 2,4.107 ||
caturmāsaprayogeṇa vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ  |
jīvetkalpāntaparyantaṃ vāyuvego mahābalaḥ  || 2,4.108 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmraṃ bhavati kāñcanam  |
kākamācī bhṛṅgarājaḥ sarpākṣī sahadevikā  || 2,4.109 ||
samūlā devadālī ca nimbavākucībījakam  |
phalāni kākatuṇḍyāśca mūlaṃ brahmāśvagandhayoḥ  || 2,4.110 ||
nīlakoraṇṭapattrāṇi triphalā ca samaṃ samam  |
cūrṇaṃ tatkanyakādrāvairbhāvayetsaptavāsaram  || 2,4.111 ||
chāyāyāṃ śoṣitaṃ kuryātsitāmadhvājyasaṃyutam  |
bhakṣetkarṣadvayaṃ nityaṃ varṣamātrājjarāṃ jayet |
jīveccandrārkanakṣatraṃ mahākāyo mahābalaḥ  || 2,4.112 ||
oṃ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ saḥ saḥ saḥ amṛte amṛtavarṣiṇi amṛtasaṃjīvani sarvakāmaprade bhagavān somarāja ājñāpayati svāhā iti bhakṣaṇamantraḥ  |
triphalā vākucībījaṃ pippalī cāśvagandhikā  |
sarvaṃ tulyaṃ kṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetpalam  |
varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ hanti jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,4.113 ||
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ saḥ svāhā anena mantreṇa bhakṣayet |
mūlikākalpayogeṣu guñjaikaṃ mṛtapāradam  |
pratiyogayutaṃ khādetphalaṃ śataguṇaṃ bhavet |
rasendrasya prabhāveṇa śīghraṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt || 2,4.114 ||
asthimudrādharo mantrī lakṣamekaṃ śmaśānataḥ  |
japenmahābhayaṃ hanti siddhiṃ datte rasāyanam  || 2,4.115 ||
tena bhakṣitamātreṇa jīvedācandratārakam  |
ajeyo devadaityānāṃ parvatānapi cālayet || 2,4.116 ||
oṃ hrīṃ mahābhayerum  |
prātaḥ puṣyārkamukhye vividhaśubhadine mantrapūjāvidhānairgrāhyaṃ divyauṣadhīnāṃ phaladalakusumaṃ mūlapattraṃ rasaṃ vā  |
sarvāṅgaṃ vātha siddhyai sakalamabhinavaṃ sevayedbrahmacārī kṣīrānnaṃ codakānnaṃ hitamaśanamidaṃ sarvamanyadvivarjyam  || 2,4.117 ||


 2, 5
udvartanaṃ palitahāri paraṃ narāṇāṃ śobhāvahaṃ sukhakaraṃ kacarañjanaṃ ca  |
vṛddhopayogisukhasādhyamanekayuktyā vakṣye susiddhamanubhūtipathena dṛṣṭam  || 2,5.1 ||
pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ nārīstanyena mardayet |
viṣṇukrāntā meghanādā sarpākṣī munimuṇḍikā  || 2,5.2 ||
āsāṃ dravairdinaṃ khalve mardayettatsamuddharet |
yavacūrṇaṃ tilāścaiva pratyekaṃ rasatulyakam  || 2,5.3 ||
kṣipettasmin ghṛtaiḥ kṣaudraiḥ sarvamāloḍya rakṣayet |
anenodvartanaṃ samyagvalīpalitanāśanam  || 2,5.4 ||
vatsarāddivyadehaḥ syājjīvedvarṣasahasrakam  |
vajrakāpālinīmūlaṃ pāradaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,5.5 ||
śivāmbunā tryahaṃ mardyamudvartātpūrvavatphalam  |
utpalāni samūlāni pāradaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,5.6 ||
saptāhaṃ mardayetkhalve svakīyenāśivāmbunā  |
tenaiva mardayedgātraṃ jāyate pūrvavatphalam  || 2,5.7 ||
pāradasya samāṃśena brahmadaṇḍīyamūlakam  |
kṣiptvā saptadinaṃ mardyaṃ svakīyenāśivāmbunā  || 2,5.8 ||
anenodvartayedgātraṃ jāyate pūrvavatphalam  |
gandhakaṃ kaṭutailena gharme bhāvyaṃ dināvadhi  || 2,5.9 ||
tatpalārdhaṃ sadā khādeddivyakāyakaraṃ nṛṇām  |
jāyate svarṇavaddeho vatsarādvalivarjitaḥ  || 2,5.10 ||
kuṣṭhacūrṇaṃ samadhvājyaṃ nityaṃ karṣaṃ lihettu yaḥ  |
vatsarāddivyadehaḥ syādgandhena śatapuṣpavat || 2,5.11 ||
kāntapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ tu tailamadhvājyasaṃyutam  |
kākatuṇḍīphalaṃ sarvaṃ samametattu kalpayet || 2,5.12 ||
bhāṇḍe ruddhvā kṣipenmāsaṃ dhānyarāśāvathoddharet |
anena lepayecchīrṣaṃ nasyaṃ kuryādanena vai  || 2,5.13 ||
tryahādbhramarasaṃkāśāḥ keśāḥ syurvatsarārdhakam  |
nāgacūrṇapalaikaṃ tu śaṅkhacūrṇapaladvayam  || 2,5.14 ||
pathyācūrṇaṃ niṣkamekaṃ sarvaṃ peṣyaṃ dināvadhi  |
amladadhnā yutaṃ yatnātsnātvādau śirasi kṣipet || 2,5.15 ||
mardayedghaṭikārdhaṃ tu veṣṭyameraṇḍapattrakaiḥ  |
śiraḥ saṃveṣṭya vastreṇa prātaḥ snānaṃ samācaret || 2,5.16 ||
ityevaṃ tridinaṃ yatnātkṛtvā keśāṃśca rañjayet |
pañcāṅgaṃ nīlikābhṛṅgatriphalālohacūrṇakam  || 2,5.17 ||
tulyaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtaṃ sūkṣmamiḍāmūtreṇa mardayet |
dinārdhaṃ tena kalkena pūrvavatkeśarañjanam  || 2,5.18 ||
guñjābījaṃ tu tvagvarjyaṃ kuṣṭhailādevadārukam  |
tulyaṃ cūrṇaṃ dinaṃ bhāvyaṃ bhṛṅgarājabhavairdravaiḥ  || 2,5.19 ||
sarvaṃ caturguṇe taile pācayenmṛduvahninā  |
tenābhyaṅgena keśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhramaropamam  || 2,5.20 ||
hastidantasya dagdhasya samaṃ yojyaṃ rasāñjanam  |
ajākṣīreṇa tatpiṣṭvā lepanātkeśarañjanam  || 2,5.21 ||
triphalā lohacūrṇaṃ tu kṛṣṇamṛdbhṛṅgajadravam  |
ikṣudaṇḍadravaṃ caiva māsaṃ bhāṇḍe nirodhayet || 2,5.22 ||
tallepādrañjayetkeśān syādyāvanmāsapañcakam  |
lohakiṭṭaṃ japāpuṣpaṃ piṣṭvā dhātrīphalaṃ samam  || 2,5.23 ||
tridinaṃ lepitāstena kacāḥ syurbhramaropamāḥ  |
bhṛṅgarājarasaprasthaṃ tailaṃ kṛṣṇatilodbhavam  || 2,5.24 ||
tulyaṃ ca nīlikādrāvaṃ sarvaṃ yāmaṃ vimardayet |
tallepastridinaṃ kāryaḥ keśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhavet || 2,5.25 ||
sindūrasya samaṃ cūrṇaṃ sābuṇaṃ ca tayoḥ samam  |
tajjale peṣitaṃ lepyaṃ tatkṣaṇātkacarañjanam  || 2,5.26 ||
śatapuṣpā kākamācī tilāḥ kṛṣṇāśca rocanam  |
dinaṃ śivāmbunā sarvaṃ mardayellohapātrake  || 2,5.27 ||
tallepaṃ tridinaṃ kuryātkeśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhavet |
cūrṇaṃ sarjī yavakṣāraṃ siddhārthaṃ kāñjikaiḥ saha  || 2,5.28 ||
nāgapuṣpādravairmardyaṃ tallepādrañjanaṃ bhavet |
nīlīpattrāṇi kāsīsaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasaṃ dadhi  || 2,5.29 ||
lohacūrṇaṃ samaṃ piṣṭvā tallepaṃ keśarañjanam  |
cūrṇaṃ sindūramaṅgāraṃ kadalīkandasaṃyutam  || 2,5.30 ||
lohapātre lohamuṣṭyā mardyaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ  |
dinaikaṃ ca tato lepyaṃ keśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhavet || 2,5.31 ||
kuraṇṭakasya pattrāṇi nāgamuṣṭyā vimardayet |
tallepaṃ tridinaṃ kuryājjāyate keśarañjanam  || 2,5.32 ||
āmrāsthi triphalā bhṛṅgī priyaṅgurmātuluṅgakam  |
niśā nīlī mṛṇālāni nāgaṃ lohaṃ ca cūrṇitam  || 2,5.33 ||
samaṃ kalkaṃ kāntapātre nimbatailena bhāvayet |
māsamātraṃ tatastena lepādbhavati rañjanam  || 2,5.34 ||
kākamācīyabījāni samāḥ kṛṣṇatilāstathā  |
tattailaṃ grāhayedyantre tannasyaṃ keśarañjanam  || 2,5.35 ||
goghṛtaṃ bhṛṅgajaṃ drāvaṃ mayūraśikhayā saha  |
mṛdvagninā pacettena syānnasyaṃ keśarañjanam  || 2,5.36 ||
japāpuṣpadravaṃ kṣaudraṃ karṣaikaṃ nasyamācaret |
saptāhādrañjayetkeśān sarvanasyeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ  || 2,5.37 ||
triphalā lohacūrṇaṃ tu vāriṇā peṣayetsamam  |
tattulyena ca tailena bhṛṅgarājarasena ca  || 2,5.38 ||
pacettailāvaśeṣaṃ tatsnigdhabhāṇḍe nirodhayet |
māsaikaṃ bhūgataṃ kuryāttena śīrṣaṃ pralepayet || 2,5.39 ||
kāravallyā dalairveṣṭya tato vastreṇa bandhayet |
nirvāte kṣīrabhojī syātkṣālayettriphalājalaiḥ  || 2,5.40 ||
nityamevaṃ prakartavyaṃ lepanaṃ dinasaptakam  |
kapālarañjanaṃ khyātaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,5.41 ||
bījāni kākatuṇḍyāśca siryālībījasaṃyutam  |
taccūrṇaṃ dinacatvāri bhāvyaṃ nirguṇḍījairdravaiḥ  || 2,5.42 ||
japāpuṣpadravaistāvattataḥ pātālayantrake  |
tailaṃ prāhyaṃ tu tallepātkeśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhavet || 2,5.43 ||
veṣṭyameraṇḍapattraiśca nirvāte kṣīrabhojanam  |
kuryātsnānaṃ kāñjikaiśca nityaṃ saptadine kṛte  || 2,5.44 ||
yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃdehaḥ keśāḥ syurbhramaropamāḥ  |
bhṛṅgarājaṃ kācamācīṃ samāṃśaṃ jalamaṇḍavīm  || 2,5.45 ||
saṃpiṣṭyāpūpikāṃ kṛtvā tailamadhye vipācayet |
pakvāṃ tāṃ peṣayettailairlepaḥ syātkeśarañjanam  || 2,5.46 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena saptāhāttatphalaṃ bhavet |
ayaskāntamaye pātre rātrau lepyaṃ phalatrayam  || 2,5.47 ||
bhṛṅgarājadravaiḥ sārdhaṃ prātaḥ keśān pralepayet |
evaṃ kuryāttrisaptāhaṃ jāyate pūrvavatphalam  || 2,5.48 ||
prakṣipenmāhiṣe śṛṅge kṛṣṇajīraṃ tadandhayet |
gṛhāgre kardame kṣiptvā ṣaṇmāsāttatsamuddharet || 2,5.49 ||
taddrutaṃ jāyate kṛṣṇaṃ karṣaikaṃ śirasi kṣipet |
veṣṭayetpūrvayogena kapālarañjanaṃ bhavet || 2,5.50 ||
bhramarāñjanasaṃkāśaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
nīlīpattraṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ triphalā kṛṣṇamāyasam  || 2,5.51 ||
madanasya ca bījāni puṣpaṃ koraṇṭakasya ca  |
arjunasya tvacaṃ cūrṇaṃ nalinīmūlakardamam  || 2,5.52 ||
sarvaṃ tulyaṃ kṣipedbhāṇḍe lohaje tannirodhayet |
pakṣamekaṃ kṣipedbhūmau bhāṇḍātkalkaṃ samuddharet || 2,5.53 ||
kalkāccaturguṇaṃ tailaṃ tailāccaturguṇaṃ dravam  |
bhṛṅgatriphalajaṃ yojyaṃ pacettailāvaśeṣakam  || 2,5.54 ||
parīkṣārthaṃ kṣipetpakṣaṃ balākāyā yadā bhavet |
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tadā siddhaṃ pātre kṛṣṇāyase kṣipet || 2,5.55 ||
māsaikaṃ dhārayettasmiṃstataḥ keśānvilepayet |
tiṣṭhanti māsaṣaṭkaṃ tu bhramarastomasaṃnibhāḥ  || 2,5.56 ||
vāsāpalāśaciñcotthairdaṇḍairvāśvatthajairdṛḍham  |
nāgaṃ pātragataṃ cālyaṃ yāvadbhavati mūrchitam  || 2,5.57 ||
palaikaṃ tatsamādāya lohacūrṇaṃ paladvayam  |
tripalaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ dāḍimasya phalatvacaḥ  || 2,5.58 ||
śuṣkaṃ cūrṇaṃ palaikaṃ tatsarveṣāṃ kāñjikaṃ samam  |
bhāṇḍe sarvaṃ pacetkiṃcittaṃ kṣipellohabhājane  || 2,5.59 ||
bhṛṅgarājakuraṇṭotthadravaṃ dattvātape kṣipet |
trisaptāhaṃ prayatnena dravo deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,5.60 ||
tatastaṃ rakṣayettena lepātsyātkeśarañjanam  |
uktānukteṣu lepeṣu veṣṭyameraṇḍapattrakaiḥ  || 2,5.61 ||
śiro rātrau divā snānaṃ yuktireṣā praśasyate  |
vajrīkṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ bhāvayedabhayāphalam  |
taccūrṇayuktatailasya lepācchuklā bhavanti hi  || 2,5.62 ||
keśāśca sarvaromāṇi śaṅkhavarṇā bhavanti vai  |
saptāhaṃ vajradugdhena suśvetān bhāvayettilān  || 2,5.63 ||
tebhyastailaṃ gṛhītvā tallepācchuklā bhavanti vai  |
gauryāmalakacūrṇaṃ tu vajrīkṣīreṇa saptadhā  || 2,5.64 ||
bhāvayettena lepena śuklatāṃ yānti mūrdhajāḥ  |
sindūraṃ sphaṭikāṃ śvetāṃ jalena saha lepayet || 2,5.65 ||
tallepena tu romāṇi suśuklāni bhavanti hi  |
māsaikaṃ māgadhīcūrṇaṃ vajrīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet || 2,5.66 ||
narāśvagajavājināṃ śuklīkaraṇamuttamam  |
indragopaṃ tailinī ca tālakaṃ rajanīdvayam  || 2,5.67 ||
manaḥśilā ca tulyāṃśaṃ cūrṇaṃ snukpayasā tryaham  |
bhāvayedarkajaiḥ kṣīraistridinaṃ cātape khare  || 2,5.68 ||
tataḥ kuṣmāṇḍajairdrāvairbhāvayeddinasaptakam  |
kūṣmāṇḍasya tato garbhe kṣiptvā māsātsamuddharet || 2,5.69 ||
tailena sarvaromāṇi keśān saṃlepayettryaham  |
veṣṭayedarkajaiḥ pattraiḥ śuklavarṇā bhavanti ca  || 2,5.70 ||
snāne kṛte śuṣkakaceṣu rātrau lepe kṛte pūrvadalaistu veṣṭyam  |
dadhnā tilaiḥ snānamataḥ prabhāte kuryāttryahaṃ lepanamitthameva  || 2,5.71 ||


 2, 6
yeṣāṃ rāmā ramaṇakuśalā rāgasaktāḥ pragalbhāḥ kāmāsaktā hariṇanayanāścandrabimbānanāśca  |
teṣāṃ vakṣye madanasukhadāṃ vīryavṛddhiṃ prabhūtāṃ mattāḥ siddhāḥ śatamapi dṛḍhāstādṛśāstoṣayanti  || 2,6.1 ||
vajrahemārkasūtābhralohabhasma kramottaram  |
sarvaṃ kanyādravairmardyaṃ śālmalyāśca dravaistryaham  || 2,6.2 ||
tadruddhvā kācakūpyantarvālukāyāṃ tryahaṃ pacet |
tatkalkaṃ musalīkvāthairvajrārkakṣīrasaṃyutaiḥ  || 2,6.3 ||
dinaikaṃ mardayetkhalve ruddhvāntarbhūdhare puṭet |
yāmāduddhṛtya saṃcūrṇyaṃ sitākṛṣṇātrijātakaiḥ  || 2,6.4 ||
samaiḥ samaṃ vimiśryātha māṣaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  |
māgadhī musalī yaṣṭī vānarībījakaṃ samam  || 2,6.5 ||
cūrṇaṃ sitājyagokṣīraiḥ palārdhaṃ pāyayedanu  |
kāminīnāṃ sahasraikaṃ ramamāṇo na muhyati  |
sevanāddṛḍhakāyaḥ syādraso'yaṃ makaradhvajaḥ  || 2,6.6 ||
śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ gandhaṃ raktotpaladaladravaiḥ  |
yāmaṃ mardyaṃ punargandhaṃ sārdhaṃ tatra vinikṣipet || 2,6.7 ||
pūrvadrāvairdinaṃ mardyaṃ rasārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ punaḥ  |
dattvā tadvaddinaṃ mardyaṃ kācakūpyāṃ nirodhayet || 2,6.8 ||
dinaikaṃ vālukāyantre pakvamuddhṛtya cūrṇayet |
bhūkuṣmāṇḍīkaṣāyeṇa bhāvayeddinasaptakam  || 2,6.9 ||
chāyāyāṃ tatsitātulyaṃ niṣkaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  |
śaṇamūlaṃ sabījaṃ ca musalī śarkarā samam  || 2,6.10 ||
gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ palārdhaṃ tu anu rātrau sadā pibet |
anantaṃ vardhate vīryaṃ raso'yaṃ madanodayaḥ  || 2,6.11 ||
śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ badarīcitrakadravaiḥ  |
mardyaṃ cāṅkollajairdrāvaistaptakhalve dinatrayam  || 2,6.12 ||
sadyo hatājamāṃsasya piṇḍe nyastaṃ ca sīvayet |
tatpiṇḍaṃ tilatailena lohapātre śanaiḥ pacet || 2,6.13 ||
yāvanmāṃsaṃ raktavarṇaṃ tataḥ sūtaṃ samuddharet |
tridinaṃ musalīkvāthairbhāvitaṃ sitayā yutam  || 2,6.14 ||
niṣkaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ raso'yaṃ madaneśvaraḥ  |
vidārīkandacūrṇaṃ tu kṣīrājyena palaṃ pibet || 2,6.15 ||
ramayetstrīśataṃ nityaṃ tattyāgādandhatāṃ vrajet |
mṛtābhraṃ pāradaṃ svarṇaṃ tulyaṃ mardyaṃ dinatrayam  || 2,6.16 ||
musalītriphalākvāthairvājigandhākaṣāyakaiḥ  |
kadalīkandajairdrāvaistadgolaṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet || 2,6.17 ||
bhūdhare dinamātraṃ tu samuddhṛtyātha mardayet |
dinaikaṃ pūrvajairdrāvaistadvadruddhvā puṭe pacet || 2,6.18 ||
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pācyamevamaṣṭapuṭaiḥ pacet |
śālmalījātaniryāsaistulyaṃ śarkarayā saha  || 2,6.19 ||
khādenniṣkadvayaṃ nityaṃ drāvayedvanitāśatam  |
gokṣīrairmarkaṭībījaṃ palārdhaṃ pāyayedanu  || 2,6.20 ||
sarvāṅgodvartanaṃ kuryātsayavaiḥ śālmalīdravaiḥ  |
rasaḥ kāmakalākhyo'yaṃ mahāvīryakaro nṛṇām  || 2,6.21 ||
mṛtasūtaṃ trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ hāṭakaṃ mṛtam  |
kadalīkandajairdrāvaiḥ śālmalījadravairdinam  || 2,6.22 ||
gokṣīraiśca dinaṃ mardyaṃ kṣaṇaikaṃ pācayedghṛtaiḥ  |
tanmadhye śarkarāṃ drākṣāṃ dhātrīṃ rambhāphalaṃ madhu  || 2,6.23 ||
gokṣīraṃ musalīṃ māṣān kokilākṣasya bījakam  |
sūtāccaturguṇaṃ kṣiptvā mardyaṃ śālmalijairdravaiḥ  || 2,6.24 ||
tatsarvaṃ dinamekaṃ tu kāmadevo raso bhavet |
niṣkamātraṃ sadā bhakṣyaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraṃ pibedanu  || 2,6.25 ||
maithune dṛḍhaliṅgaḥ syāddrāvayedvanitākulam  |
prārambharajasā strīṇāṃ mardayedbhasmasūtakam  || 2,6.26 ||
mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ ca tāraṃ ca gandhakaṃ ca samaṃ dinam  |
sitāmadhvājyasaṃyuktaṃ niṣkaṃ bhuktvā pibetpayaḥ  || 2,6.27 ||
ratikāmaraso nāma kāminīramaṇe hitaḥ  |
vānarīmūlagodhūmaṃ kokilākṣasya bījakam  || 2,6.28 ||
māṣāścekṣurasaiḥ sarvaṃ loḍitaṃ pācayedghṛtaiḥ  |
tenaiva vaṭakāḥ kāryā nityaṃ khādeddvayaṃ dvayam  || 2,6.29 ||
anupānamidaṃ siddhaṃ sevanādramayecchatam  |
śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ tryahaṃ kahlārajairdravaiḥ  || 2,6.30 ||
marditaṃ cāndhitaṃ pacyādyāmaṃ vālukayantrake  |
raktāgastyadravairbhāvyaṃ dinaikaṃ tu sitāyutam  || 2,6.31 ||
niṣkamātraṃ sadā khādedraso'yaṃ madavardhanaḥ  |
ajasya vṛṣaṇaṃ kṣīre pakvaṃ tilasitāyutam  || 2,6.32 ||
yatheṣṭaṃ bhakṣayeccānu ramayetkāminīśatam  |
paladvayaṃ dvayaṃ śuddhaṃ pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ śubham  || 2,6.33 ||
karṣaikaṃ māritaṃ svarṇaṃ palaikaṃ mṛtatāmrakam  |
raupyabhasma caturniṣkaṃ sarvaṃ pañcāmṛtairdinam  || 2,6.34 ||
mardyaṃ ruddhvā dinaṃ pacyādbhūdhare taṃ samuddharet |
piṣṭvā pañcāmṛtaiḥ khādedvaṭikāṃ badarākṛtim  || 2,6.35 ||
anaṅgasundarī khyātā rāmāṇāṃ ramate śatam  |
śālmalīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu madhuśarkarayānvitam  || 2,6.36 ||
palaikaṃ bhakṣayeccānu sitākṣīraṃ tu pāyayet |
śālmalyutthairdravairmardyaḥ pakṣaikaṃ śuddhapāradaḥ  || 2,6.37 ||
śuddhagandhaṃ trisaptāhaṃ taddravairmardayetpṛthak |
samāvetau punarmardyau ghṛtairyāmacatuṣṭayam  || 2,6.38 ||
tadgolaṃ bandhayedvastre ghṛtairyāmadvayaṃ pacet |
tatastaṃ śālmalīdrāvairmardayeddivasatrayam  || 2,6.39 ||
nikṣipetkācakūpyantarvālukāyantragaṃ pacet |
kṣipecchālmalijaṃ drāvaṃ kūpyā garbhe dināvadhi  || 2,6.40 ||
sārdrameva samuddhṛtya miśryaṃ tatsitayā samam  |
niṣkamātraṃ sadā khādedraso'yaṃ kāmanāyakaḥ  || 2,6.41 ||
musalīṃ sasitāṃ kṣīraiḥ palaikāṃ pāyayedanu  |
kāminīnāṃ sahasraṃ tu kṣobhayennimiṣāntare  || 2,6.42 ||
śuddhasūtatrayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ tāmracūrṇakam  |
kṛtvā piṣṭīṃ nirudhyātha rambhākandodare punaḥ  || 2,6.43 ||
mṛlliptaṃ śoṣitaṃ pacyāddinaikaṃ karīṣāgninā  |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ pacyātkande kande dinaṃ dinam  || 2,6.44 ||
uddhṛtya bandhayedvastre dṛḍhe caiva caturguṇe  |
kṣudraśambūkamāṃsāktachāgīraktagataṃ pacet || 2,6.45 ||
dolāyantre tryahaṃ yāvaddeyaṃ raktaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
guḍūcyā gajapippalyā kadalyā kokilākṣakaiḥ  || 2,6.46 ||
gokṣurīvānarīmūlajātīmūlasya ca dravaiḥ  |
pācayettatkaṣāyairvā dolāyantre dinatrayam  || 2,6.47 ||
tataḥ kṣīre sitāyukte tadvatpacyāddināvadhi  |
uddhṛtya musalīkvāthairmardyaṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam  || 2,6.48 ||
rasaḥ pūrṇendunāmāyaṃ khādenmāṃsaṃ sitāyutam  |
gokṣuro vānarībījaṃ guḍūcī gajapippalī  || 2,6.49 ||
kokilākṣasya bījāni majjā kārpāsabījajā  |
śatāvarī ca rambhāyāḥ phalaṃ sarvaṃ samaṃ bhavet || 2,6.50 ||
sarvatulyā sitā yojyā madhunā loḍitaṃ lihet |
palārdhamanupānaṃ syāttataḥ peyaṃ gavāṃ payaḥ  || 2,6.51 ||
kāminīnāṃ sahasraikaṃ ramate kāmadevavat |
padmabījaṃ kaseruṃ ca kandaṃ nālaṃ ca karṇikām  || 2,6.52 ||
musalī bhṛṅgarāṭdrākṣā pakvaṃ śleṣmātakaṃ phalam  |
vijayā markaṭī māṣāḥ śaṇabījāni vai tilāḥ  || 2,6.53 ||
kokilākṣasya bījāni bhūkuṣmāṇḍī śatāvarī  |
śṛṅgāṭaṃ cirbhiṭaṃ phañjībījāni cāśvagandhikā  || 2,6.54 ||
etatsarvaṃ samaṃ cūrṇya pādāṃśaṃ cāharetpṛthak |
pādāṃśasyāṣṭamāṃśena śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ vimiśrayet || 2,6.55 ||
pāradādaṣṭamāṃśaṃ ca karpūraṃ tatra nikṣipet |
cāturjātakamekaikaṃ karpūrāddviguṇaṃ bhavet || 2,6.56 ||
sūtatulyā sitā yojyā mardyaṃ rambhādravairdinam  |
tadgolaṃ ḍāmare yantre kramavṛddhāgninā pacet || 2,6.57 ||
dinānte cordhvalagnaṃ tadgrāhyaṃ rambhādravairdṛḍham  |
marditaṃ sitayā tulyaṃ māṣaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  || 2,6.58 ||
raso madanakāmo'yaṃ balavīryavivardhanaḥ  |
divyarūpā bhajedrāmāḥ kāmākulakalānvitāḥ  || 2,6.59 ||
bhāgatrayaṃ tu yatpūrvaṃ pṛthakcūrṇaṃ surakṣitam  |
kulīramāṃsacchāgāṇḍacaṭakāṇḍāni vai pṛthak || 2,6.60 ||
pratyekaṃ cūrṇayettulyaṃ sarvatulyaṃ gavāṃ payaḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ cālayan pacyādyāvatpiṇḍatvamāgatam  || 2,6.61 ||
prasārya kāṣṭhapātrāntaśchāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet |
asya cūrṇasya karpūraṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ kṣipet || 2,6.62 ||
cāturjātakacūrṇaṃ tu kṣipeddvātriṃśadaṃśataḥ  |
sarvatulyā sitā yojyā rakṣayennūtane ghaṭe  || 2,6.63 ||
karṣadvayaṃ gavāṃ kṣīrairanupānaiḥ sadā pibet |
niṣekaṃ māritaṃ cābhraṃ khādeccharkarayā samam  || 2,6.64 ||
śālmalīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu bhṛṅgarājasya mūlakam  |
palaikaṃ sitayā cānu seveta kāminīśatam  || 2,6.65 ||
vānarīkokilākṣasya bījāni tilamāṣakāḥ  |
vāsāgokṣurayormūlaṃ sarvaṃ cūrṇaṃ samaṃ bhavet || 2,6.66 ||
cūrṇatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ cābhraṃ sarvatulyā tu śarkarā  |
etatkarṣaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibetkāmāṅganāyakaḥ  || 2,6.67 ||
tailena pakvaṃ caṭakaṃ khādetpūrvaṃ tu bhojanāt |
bhojanānte pibetkṣīraṃ rāmāṇāṃ ramayecchatam  || 2,6.68 ||
aśvagandhāśatāvaryoḥ śālmalyāścitrakasya ca  |
mūlaṃ musalījaṃ kandaṃ kokilākṣasya bījakam  || 2,6.69 ||
vidārīpadminīkandaṃ vānarībījakaṃ samam  |
etaccūrṇaṃ mṛtābhraṃ tu tulyaṃ śarkarayā samam  || 2,6.70 ||
palārdhaṃ pāyayetkṣīraiḥ khādetkukkuṭamāṃsakam  |
kṣīrapānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ramayetkāminīśatam  || 2,6.71 ||
dhātrīphalasya cūrṇaṃ tu bhāvayettatphaladravaiḥ  |
ekaviṃśativārān vai śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,6.72 ||
tatpādāṃśaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ madhvājyaśarkarānvitam  |
palaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ sitākṣīraṃ pibedanu  || 2,6.73 ||
dhātrīlohaprabhāvena ramayetkāminīśatam  |
punarnavā nāgabalā vājigandhā śatāvarī  || 2,6.74 ||
gokṣuraṃ musalīkandaṃ mṛtaṃ sūtaṃ samaṃ samam  |
cūrṇaṃ madhvājyasaṃyuktaṃ niṣkaṃ bhuktvā pibetpayaḥ  || 2,6.75 ||
taṇḍulaṃ vānarībījaṃ cūrṇayetsitayā samam  |
āloḍayedgavāṃ kṣīraistena pacyādapūpikām  || 2,6.76 ||
tāṃ ghṛtairbhakṣayeccānu ramayetkāminīkulam  |
vānarībījacūrṇaṃ tu tvagvarjyaṃ māṣacūrṇakam  || 2,6.77 ||
nālikerodakairbhāvyaṃ yāmānte peṣayettataḥ  |
viṃśatyaṃśena piṣṭasya mṛtamabhraṃ vimiśrayet || 2,6.78 ||
ghṛtaistadvaṭakaṃ paktvā madhvājyābhyāṃ tu bhakṣayet |
kṣīraṃ sitāṃ cānupibedrāmāṇāṃ ramate śatam  || 2,6.79 ||
vālukāsambhavaṃ matsyaṃ supakvaṃ bhakṣayedghṛtaiḥ  |
ṣaṇḍho'pi jāyate kāmī vīryastambhaḥ prajāyate  || 2,6.80 ||
ūrṇanābhiṃ samaṃ kṣaudraiḥ piṣṭvā nābhiṃ pralepayet |
mucyate baddhaṣaṇḍho'pi kṣīrairvahniṃ pibedanu  || 2,6.81 ||
svayamagnirasaṃ cātra triniṣkaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  |
ghṛtāktā dalitā māṣāḥ kṣīreṇa saha pācitāḥ  || 2,6.82 ||
sitājyasaṃyutā bhakṣyā vīryavṛddhikarā hyalam  |
kṣaṇe kṣaṇe bhajedrāmāṃ yathā pārāvato dhruvam  || 2,6.83 ||
samyaṅmāritamabhrakaṃ kaṭuphalaṃ kuṣṭhāśvagandhāmṛtā methī mocaraso vidārimusalīgokaṇṭakekṣurakām  |
rambhākandaśatāvarī hyajamodā māṣāstilā dhānyakaṃ yaṣṭī nāgabalā kacoramadanaṃ jātīphalaṃ saindhavam  || 2,6.84 ||
bhārgīkarkaṭaśuṅgibhṛṅgatrikaṭu dvau jīrakau citrakaṃ cāturjātapunarnavāgajakaṇādrākṣāśaṇaṃ vāsakaḥ  |
śālmalyaṅghri phalatrikaṃ kapibhavaṃ bījaṃ samaṃ cūrṇayeccūrṇāṃśā vijayāsitādviguṇitā madhvājyamiśraṃtu tat || 2,6.85 ||
karṣārdhāṃ gulikāṃ vilehyamathavā kṛtvā sadā sevayetpeyā kṣīrasitānu vīryakaraṇe stambhe'pyalaṃ kāminām  |
śyāmāvaśyakaraḥ samādhisukhadaḥ saṅge'ṅganādrāvakaḥ kṣīṇe puṣṭikaraḥ kṣayakṣayakaro nānāmayadhvaṃsakaḥ  || 2,6.86 ||
kāsaśvāsamahātisāraśamano mandāgnisaṃdīpano hyarśāṃsi grahaṇīpramehanicayaśleṣmātisārapraṇut |
nityānandakaverviśeṣakavitāvācāvilāsodbhavaṃ datte sarvaṃ mahāsthiradaśāṃ dhyānāvasāne bhṛśam  || 2,6.87 ||
abhyāsena nihanti mṛtyupalitaṃ kāmeśvaro vatsarātsarveṣāṃ hitakārako nigaditaḥ śrīnityanāthena vai vṛddhānāmapi kāmavardhanakaraḥ prauḍhāṅganāsaṃgame siddho'yaṃ dhanavastvamoghasukhado bhūpaiḥ sadā sevyatām  || 2,6.88 ||
ityetaduktaṃ bahuvīryavardhanaṃ rātrau sadā kṣīrasitāsamanvitam  |
bhuktottaraṃ sevitamāśu kāmināṃ vidagdharāmākulavaśyakārakam  || 2,6.89 ||


 2, 7
vīryaṃ sthiraṃ yonimukheṣu yeṣāṃ sthūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ dīrghatamaṃ ca liṅgam  |
teṣāṃ pragalbhāḥ pramadāśca sarvā bhavanti tṛptāḥ surataprasaṅge  || 2,7.1 ||
nāgavallīdaladrāvaiḥ saptāhaṃ śuddhapāradam  |
mardayettaptakhalve tu kṣālayetkāñjikaistataḥ  || 2,7.2 ||
tatkṣipedviṣakandasya garbhe niṣkacatuṣṭayam  |
viṣeṇa tanmukhaṃ ruddhvā sthūlavārāhamāṃsake  || 2,7.3 ||
piṇḍaṃ garbhe nirudhyātha mukhaṃ sūtreṇa sīvayet |
saṃdhyākāle baliṃ dattvā kukkuṭaṃ madirāyutam  || 2,7.4 ||
tataścullyāṃ lohapātre taile dhattūrasambhave  |
taṃ viṃśatipale pacyātsapiṇḍaṃ mandavahninā  || 2,7.5 ||
saṃdhyāmārabhya yatnena yāvatsūryodayaṃ tathā  |
haṭhājjāgaraṇaṃ kuryādanyathā tanna sidhyati  || 2,7.6 ||
prātaruddhṛtya guṭikāṃ kṣīrabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
tatkṣīraṃ śuṣyati kṣiprametatpratyayamadbhutam  || 2,7.7 ||
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ dhārayedvaktre vīryastambhakarīṃ ratau  |
kṣīraṃ pītvā ramedrāmāḥ kāmākulakalānvitāḥ  || 2,7.8 ||
mukhāddhastaṃ yadā prāptā tadā vīryaṃ patatyalam  |
brahmāṇḍaguṭikā nāma śoṣayantī mahodadhim  || 2,7.9 ||
dagdhamaṅkollamūlaṃ tu karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā  |
rocanā sahadevī ca samaṃ sarvaṃ prapeṣayet || 2,7.10 ||
viṣamuṣṭikatailena liptaliṅgo hyanena vai  |
naro nārīsahasraikaṃ gacchanvīryaṃ na muñcati  || 2,7.11 ||
śvetārkatūlajāṃ vartiṃ kṛtvā sūkaramedasā  |
yāvajjvalati dīpo'yaṃ tāvadvīryaṃ sthiraṃ nṛṇām  || 2,7.12 ||
indravāruṇikāmūlaṃ puṣye nagnaḥ samuddharet |
tryūṣaṇaiśca gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā kuryādvaṭīṃ dṛḍhām  || 2,7.13 ||
chāyāśuṣkā sthitā vaktre vīryastambhakarī nṛṇām  |
varamaṅkolatailena nābhilepo'pi vīryadhṛk || 2,7.14 ||
raktāpāmārgāmūlaṃ tu somavāre'bhimantrayet |
bhaume prāptaḥ samuddhṛtya bandhetkaṭyāṃ ca vīryadhṛk || 2,7.15 ||
caṭakānaṅkulītailaiḥ pādādhaḥ saṃpralepayet |
na muñcati naro vīryaṃ śayyāṃ pādena na spṛśet || 2,7.16 ||
ḍuṇḍubho nāma yaḥ sarpaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇastamāharet |
tasyāsthi dhārayetkaṭyāṃ naro vīryaṃ na muñcati  || 2,7.17 ||
tanmukte muñcate vīryaṃ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ  |
sahadevīyamūlaṃ tu tatsamaṃ padmakesaram  || 2,7.18 ||
piṣṭvā madhvājyasaṃyukto lepo nābhau tu vīryadhṛk |
śvetasya kokilākṣasya bījaṃ mūlaṃ samāharet || 2,7.19 ||
piṣṭaṃ taṇḍulasambhūtaṃ badarīṇāṃ phalaṃ samam  |
jalaiḥ piṣṭvā vaṭī dhāryā vīryastambhakarī mukhe  || 2,7.20 ||
nāgavallīpayaḥpiṣṭaṃ lajjāmūlaṃ pralepayet |
tannābhau vīryadhṛkpuṃsāṃ mūlaṃ vā tulasībhavam  || 2,7.21 ||
mūlena śvetaguñjāyā vartiṃ kṛtvā pradīpayet |
dīpaṃ sūkaratailena vīryastambhakaraṃ nṛṇām  || 2,7.22 ||
bījamīśvaraliṅgyāstu sūtaṃ vṛścikakaṇṭakam  |
sarvaṃ pūgaphalasyāntaḥ kṣiptvā veṣṭyaṃ trilohakaiḥ  || 2,7.23 ||
jihvopari sthite tasminnaro vīryaṃ na muñcati  |
śleṣmātasya kuraṇṭasya bījaṃ phañjyāḥ samāharet || 2,7.24 ||
ajākṣīreṇa taṃ piṣṭvā karṣaṃ bhuktvā tu vīryadhṛk |
sūraṇaṃ tulasīmūlaṃ tāmbūlaiḥ saha bhakṣayet || 2,7.25 ||
na muñcati naro vīryaṃ karṣaikena pṛthakpṛthak |
nakhāsthīni samādāya mārjārasya sitasya vai  || 2,7.26 ||
śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ nīlīmūlaṃ śmaśānajam  |
sarvaṃ baddhvā kaṭau vīryaṃ cirakālaṃ na muñcati  || 2,7.27 ||
bhūlatā sikthakaṃ tulyaṃ limpettailaiḥ kusumbhajaiḥ  |
pādau vīryadharo bhūyātpadbhyāṃ śayyāṃ na saṃspṛśet || 2,7.28 ||
navanītena vā lepyaṃ caṭakāṇḍaṃ ca pūrvavat |
indravāruṇīmūlamunmattājasya mūtrataḥ  || 2,7.29 ||
bhāvayettena lepena naro vīryaṃ na muñcati  |
dāḍimasya tvacaścūrṇaṃ phalaṃ bhallātakākṣayoḥ  || 2,7.30 ||
piṣṭvā tatkaṭutailena lepaḥ syātpūrvavatphalam  |
karpūraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ sūtaṃ munipuṣparasaṃ madhu  || 2,7.31 ||
mardayitvā limpettena liṅgaṃ yāvatsamantataḥ  |
jalaiḥ prakṣālayelliṅgaṃ bhajedrāmāṃ yathocitām  || 2,7.32 ||
vīryaṃ stambhayate puṃsāṃ yāmamātraṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
kṛṣṇadhattūratailena pāradaṃ gharṣayeddinam  || 2,7.33 ||
trilohairveṣṭitaṃ baddhaṃ tatkaṭyāṃ vīryadhārakam  |
svarṇaṃ vyomasattvaṃ tāraṃ tāmraṃ ca rocanam  || 2,7.34 ||
bījaṃ vai śarapuṅkhāyāḥ kṛṣṇadhattūrabījakam  |
sarvaṃ mardyaṃ vaṭakṣīraiḥ kuberākṣasya bījake  || 2,7.35 ||
tatkṣiptvā dhārayedvaktre vīryastambhakaraṃ ciram  |
kṛkalāsasya pucchāgraṃ mudrikākāratāṃ kṛtam  || 2,7.36 ||
ūrṇanābhasya jālena veṣṭayitvātha dhārayet |
vāmahaste kaniṣṭhāyāṃ naro vīryaṃ na muñcati  || 2,7.37 ||
sthalamīnaṃ samādāya śuṣkaṃ cūrṇena lepayet |
ulliptaṃ rakṣayetkiṃcidvaktre dhāryaśca vīryadhṛk || 2,7.38 ||
paścimasamudrasya taṭe amaracaṇḍeśvaro nāma devatāyatanaṃ tasyāgre vālukāmadhye sthalamīnāstiṣṭhanti te ca vālukāmīnāḥ kathyante  |
śuddhasūte vinikṣipya svarṇaṃ vā nāgameva vā  |
aṣṭamāṃśena tatsarvaṃ mardayettaptakhalvake  || 2,7.39 ||
śālmalyāścaiva pañcāṅgarasaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
śleṣmāntasya phalaṃ pakvaṃ kokilākṣasya bījakam  || 2,7.40 ||
tilapiṇyākacūrṇaṃ tu dattvā tāvadvimardayet |
jalaukā jāyate yāvattataḥ karpūraṭaṅkaṇam  || 2,7.41 ||
kapikacchukaromāṇi vākucītailakaṃ paṭu  |
māgadhīṃ ca jalaiḥ piṣṭvā tatsarvaṃ taptakhalvake  || 2,7.42 ||
kṣipetpūrvajalaukāṃ ca trisaptāhaṃ vimardayet |
sā yojyā kāmakāle tu nārīṇāṃ yonigahvare  || 2,7.43 ||
madadarpaharā tāsāṃ madavihvalakārakā  |
bālye ṣaḍaṅgulā yojyā yauvane sā navāṅgulā  || 2,7.44 ||
dvādaśāṅgulikā yojyā pragalbhānāṃ jalaukikā  |
yo vā tāṃ dhārayenmūrdhni vīryaṃ tasya sthiraṃ bhavet || 2,7.45 ||
pāradādaṣṭamāṃśena suvarṇaṃ nāgameva vā  |
yojayettaptakhalve tu śālmalītvaṅnijadravaiḥ  || 2,7.46 ||
munipattrarasairnīlīmūladrāvaiśca mardayet |
śleṣmātakaphalaṃ pakvaṃ kokilākṣasya bījakam  || 2,7.47 ||
majjā supakvā bilvasya kṣipettatraiva mardayet |
jalaukā jāyate yāvattatastasmātsamuddharet || 2,7.48 ||
trisaptāhaṃ taptakhalve karpūrādyaiśca pūrvavat |
mardayecca phalaṃ tadvajjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,7.49 ||
rasādaṣṭamabhāgaṃ tu suvarṇaṃ nāgameva vā  |
yojyaṃ ca triphalā bhṛṅgī śuṇṭhī chāgapayo ghṛtam  || 2,7.50 ||
kṣaudraṃ gomūtrakaṃ caiva sarvaṃ saptadināvadhi  |
mardayettadavacchinnaṃ jalaukā yāvatā bhavet || 2,7.51 ||
karpūrādyaiḥ punarmardyaṃ taptakhalve tu pūrvavat |
pūrvavajjāyate siddhistadvadyoge na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,7.52 ||
tridinaṃ mardayetkhalve sūtaṃ niṣkacatuṣṭayam  |
triphalāyāstu niryāsaṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ vinikṣipet || 2,7.53 ||
payaścaiva mahāśṛṅgyā dātavyaṃ mardanakṣamam  |
jalaukā mardanākhyeyaṃ jāyate sukhadā nṛṇām  || 2,7.54 ||
rāmāṇāṃ madamattānāṃ drāvikāgnau ghṛtaṃ yathā  |
pūrvavatkramayogena vīryastambhakarī bhavet || 2,7.55 ||
pāradaṃ maricaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ tagaraṃ kaṇṭakārikā  |
aśvagandhātilakṣaudrasaindhavaśvetasarṣapāḥ  || 2,7.56 ||
apāmārgo yavā māṣāḥ pippalī ca samaṃ jalaiḥ  |
piṣṭvā vimardayettena liṅgaṃ māsamaharniśam  || 2,7.57 ||
vardhate hastamātraṃ tatsthaulyena musalopamam  |
varāhavasayā kṣaudrairliṅgaṃ māsaṃ vilepayet || 2,7.58 ||
atidīrghaṃ dṛḍhaṃ sthūlaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
aśvagandhā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ bṛhatī ca śatāvarī  || 2,7.59 ||
pācayettilatailena mardayettena pūrvavat |
liṅgaṃ sthūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ dīrghaṃ māsamātrātprajāyate  || 2,7.60 ||
jambūsūkarajaṃ tailaṃ mahārāṣṭrī ca ṭaṅkaṇam  |
madhunā saha lepo'yaṃ māsālliṅgasya vṛddhikṛt || 2,7.61 ||
miśritaṃ musalīcūrṇaṃ māhiṣairnavanītakaiḥ  |
tadbhāṇḍaṃ dhānyarāśau ca sthitaṃ saptadinairharet || 2,7.62 ||
tena pralepayelliṅgaṃ vardhate māsamātrataḥ  |
pippalī maricaṃ kṣīraṃ sitā tulyaṃ vimardayet || 2,7.63 ||
māsaikaṃ vṛddhikṛlliṅge nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  |
māhiṣaṃ goghṛtaṃ tulyaṃ saindhavaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 2,7.64 ||
anena lepayelliṅgaṃ sthūlaṃ syānmāsamātrataḥ  |
aśvagandhāpamārgau ca sārivākṣaphalaṃ tilāḥ  || 2,7.65 ||
sarṣapendrayavaṃ tulyamajākṣīreṇa peṣayet |
tena liṅgaṃ tu māsaikaṃ mardanādvṛddhimāpnuyāt || 2,7.66 ||
māṃsīmakṣaphalaṃ kuṣṭhamaśvagandhāṃ śatāvarīm  |
taile paktvā pralepo'yaṃ māsālliṅgasya vṛddhikṛt || 2,7.67 ||
rohītamatsyajaṃ pittaṃ jalaukā lāṅgalī samam  |
anena lepayelliṅgaṃ syānmāsānmusalopamam  |
niśā sitāśvagandhā ca pāradaṃ mardayetsamam  |
anena mardayelliṅgaṃ yonikarṇastanāṃstathā  |
vardhante māsamātreṇa nātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 2,7.68 ||
oṃ namo bhagavate uḍḍāmareśvarāya sara prasara 2 kuru ṭha ṭhaḥ  |
anena mantreṇa sarve vardhanayogāḥ saptābhimantritāḥ siddhā bhavanti  |
jāmbūmārjārayoḥ pittaṃ yavāgūmarditaṃ dihet |
māsaikādvardhate liṅgaṃ stanau karṇau ca mardanāt || 2,7.69 ||
oṃ namo bhagavate uḍḍāmareśvarāya sara prasara prasara nikala nikala nikālaya nikālaya svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ  || 2,7.70 ||
gṛhagodhā śuno jihvā strījarāyuḥ samaṃ samam  |
piṣṭvā dhāryaṃ tāmrapātre saptāhāttaṃ punaḥ pacet || 2,7.71 ||
tilatailena tattailamardanādvardhate khalu  |
liṅgaṃ stanau ca karṇau ca hastau pādau na saṃśayaḥ  |
oṃ namo bhagavate uḍḍāmareśvarāya sara sara hili hili svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ  |
atitarasukhasādhyairyogarājaiḥ prasiddhaiḥ satatasuratayogyaṃ stambhanaṃ vardhanaṃ ca  |
nipuṇarasikarāmārañjakaṃ mohakaṃ syādgaditamiha samastaṃ bhogināṃ saukhyahetuḥ  || 2,7.72 ||


 2, 8
śrīśaile dehasiddhiḥ prabhavati sahasā vṛkṣamṛtkandatoyaistacchāstraṃ śambhunoktaṃ pragahanamakhilaṃ vīkṣitaṃ yattu sāram  |
vyāhṛtyānekayuktyā sakalasukhakaraṃ dṛṣṭasiddhaṃ tu yadyattadvakṣye sādhakānāmanubhavapathagaṃ bhuktaye muktaye ca  || 2,8.1 ||
{Felsmitguggulu bei ṃllikārjuna}
mallikārjunadevasya purato gajasaṃnibhā  |
śilā tiṣṭhati yā rātrau sravate gugguluṃ sadā  || 2,8.2 ||
pālāśacāṭunā grāhyaṃ kṣipedalābupātrake  |
tadguggulusamaṃ gandhaṃ niṣkaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  || 2,8.3 ||
māsānmṛtyujarāṃ hanti jīvedācandratārakam  |
tadgugguluṃ drute tāmre koṭibhāgena yojayet || 2,8.4 ||
tadbhavetkanakaṃ divyaṃ jāmbūnadasamaprabham  |
{ṅlocke bei ṅhaṇṭāsiddheśvara}
vāmato mallināthasya ghaṇṭāsiddheśvaraḥ sthitaḥ  || 2,8.5 ||
taddvāre vidyate kuṇḍaṃ tatra ghaṇṭā vilambate  |
rātrau kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ sopavāsaistribhirjanaiḥ  || 2,8.6 ||
kartavyaṃ sādhanaṃ tatra nirvikalpairanidritaiḥ  |
ekastu snāpayeddevaṃ jalamekastu vāhayet || 2,8.7 ||
ekastu vādayedghaṇṭāmavicchinnaṃ niśāvadhi  |
tatastuṣṭo bhavecchambhuḥ khecaratvaṃ prayacchati  || 2,8.8 ||
ghaṇṭāsiddheśvarasyaiva dakṣiṇe krośamātrake  |
nikhaneddṛśyate tatra mṛdgorocanasaṃnibhā  || 2,8.9 ||
sitākṣīraiḥ pibetkarṣaṃ saptāhādamaro bhavet |
{candrodaka im ḹ von ṃllinātha}
candrodakaṃ prasiddhaṃ vai mallināthasya paścime  || 2,8.10 ||
sthitaṃ vaiśākhamāse tu pūrṇamāsyāṃ susādhayet |
sādhako nirvikalpena sthitvā tasya samīpataḥ  || 2,8.11 ||
rātrau japaṃ prakurvāṇastattoyaṃ cārdharātrake  |
spraṣṭuṃ candro yadā gacchettadā kṣipraṃ karāñjalim  || 2,8.12 ||
kṛtvā tiṣṭhedyadā yāti tajjalaṃ tatkṣaṇātpibet |
vajrakāyo bhavedvīro jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,8.13 ||
{ṭamarindenbaumim Osten von śrīśaila}
śrīśailapūrvadvāre tu devo'sti tripurāntakaḥ  |
devasyottaradigbhāge ciñcāvṛkṣaḥ samīpataḥ  || 2,8.14 ||
vidyate tasya mūle tu bhairavo dṛśyate svayam  |
tasyāgre nikhanedbhūmiṃ puruṣasya pramāṇataḥ  || 2,8.15 ||
dṛśyate taptakuṇḍaṃ tu nīlavarṇajalānvitam  |
ciñcāvṛkṣasya pattrāṇi samyagvastreṇa bandhayet || 2,8.16 ||
tasmin kuṇḍe vinikṣipya tāni mīnā bhavanti vai  |
saṃgṛhya tasya kāṣṭhena pacetteṣāṃ vivarjayet || 2,8.17 ||
kaṇṭakāni śiraḥ pucchaṃ śeṣaṃ bhakṣeta sādhakaḥ  |
kṣaṇaṃ mūrchā bhavettena prabuddho jāyate svayam  || 2,8.18 ||
chidraṃ paśyati medinyāṃ jīvedvarṣāyutaṃ naraḥ  |
tripurāntakadevasya paścime gavyūtidvaye  || 2,8.19 ||
maṇipalliriti grāmastasya paścimato giriḥ  |
tasya paścimadigbhāge kapāṭaṃ dṛśyate śubham  || 2,8.20 ||
pūrvābhimukhaḥ praviśettato gacchecca dakṣiṇam  |
daśadhanvantaraṃ yāvaddṛśyante jvalanaprabhāḥ  || 2,8.21 ||
āmrākārāḥ supāṣāṇā grāhyā vastreṇa bandhayet |
raktavarṇaṃ bhavedvastraṃ kṣīramadhye kṣipettataḥ  || 2,8.22 ||
tatkṣīraṃ jāyate raktaṃ tatkṣīraṃ sādhakaḥ pibet |
saptāhādvajrakāyaḥ syājjīveccandrārkatārakam  || 2,8.23 ||
tripurāntakadevasya uttare kokilābilam  |
vikhyātaṃ sarvalokeṣu kṛtvā vamanarecanam  || 2,8.24 ||
sādhakaḥ praviśettatra daśadhanvantarāvadhi  |
pāṣāṇāḥ kokilākārāstiṣṭhanti tāṃstu cāharet || 2,8.25 ||
teṣāṃ pṛṣṭhe tilāḥ kṣiptāḥ sphuṭantyeva hi tatkṣaṇāt |
ityevaṃ pratyayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃśca ghṛṣṭvā parasparam  || 2,8.26 ||
kṣīramadhye kṣipettān vai kṣīraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ prajāyate  |
ākaṇṭhāntaṃ pibettattu divyakāvyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,8.27 ||
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  |
chidraṃ paśyati medinyāṃ vāyuvego mahābalaḥ  || 2,8.28 ||
tripurāntakadevasya pūrvadigyojanāntare  |
devaḥ svargapurī nāma vidyate tatra vai khanet || 2,8.29 ||
devāgre bhūmikāṃ jānumātrāṃ tatra phalā iva  |
nirgacchanti tu pāṣāṇāḥ kharasparśā bhavanti vai  || 2,8.30 ||
tripurāntakadevasya paścime yojanārdhake  |
vidyate hi biladvāraṃ tanmadhye dhanuṣāṃ trayam  || 2,8.31 ||
gacchetkharjūravṛkṣo vai dṛśyate kṛṣṇavarṇakaḥ  |
tatphalānāṃ rasaṃ pītvā mūrchā saṃjāyate kṣaṇam  || 2,8.32 ||
tataḥ saṃjāyate siddho jīveccandrārkatārakam  |
śrīśaile dakṣiṇe dvāre jāleśvarasureśvarau  || 2,8.33 ||
devau prasiddhau vidyete nikhanettatra bhūmikām  |
pāṣāṇāḥ śrīphalākārā niryānti sparśabhedakāḥ  || 2,8.34 ||
devo rāmeśvarastatra vidyate tasya saṃnidhau  |
rudrākṣākārapāṣāṇāḥ kharasparśā bhavanti te  || 2,8.35 ||
jyotiḥsiddhavaṭe vṛkṣe ekapādena tiṣṭhati  |
āvartadevako nāma tatsamīpe tu paścime  || 2,8.36 ||
vidyate parvatastatra pāṣāṇāstālakopamāḥ  |
ādāya tān dhamettīvraṃ divyaṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 2,8.37 ||
tatraiva dakṣiṇadvāre devaḥ syātkuṇḍaleśvaraḥ  |
tatsamīpe khanedbhūmiṃ jānumātrāṃ tato haret || 2,8.38 ||
pāṣāṇāḥ śrīphalākārā raktāśca sparśavedhakāḥ  |
puruṣeśvaradevasya kuṇḍamasti samīpataḥ  || 2,8.39 ||
guñjā ca riṭhṭhakaścaiva dvau vṛkṣau tatra tiṣṭhataḥ  |
tayoḥ pattrāṇi saṃbhakṣya tatkṣaṇādamaro bhavet || 2,8.40 ||
tathā hastiśilā tatra tasyā dakṣiṇataḥ khanet |
hastamātraṃ tataḥ paśyejjambūphalasamākṛtiḥ  || 2,8.41 ||
śilā tatra samākhyātā sparśagrāhyā prayatnataḥ  |
nāmnā hastiśiraḥ khyātaṃ saiva hastiśilā bhavet || 2,8.42 ||
tasyā dakṣiṇadigbhāge yojanaikena tiṣṭhati  |
chāyāchattraṃ prasiddhaṃ tacchivarūpaṃ śivoditam  || 2,8.43 ||
śatahastapramāṇaṃ tu tatkṣetraṃ parivartulam  |
yo'sau gacchati tasyādho nāsau kenāpi dṛśyate  || 2,8.44 ||
rudratulyo bhavetsiddhaḥ krīḍate bhuvanatraye  |
athavā sādhayeddūrātkhyātaṃ siddhyaṣṭakaṃ tu yat || 2,8.45 ||
kāntābhraṃ kāñcanaṃ sūtaṃ mardyaṃ kramaguṇottaram  |
tadgolaṃ veṣṭayellohaistribhiryatnātkrameṇa vai  || 2,8.46 ||
vastre baddhvā tu tadvastraṃ vaṃśāgre bandhayeddṛḍham  |
mantrayetkālīmantreṇa chāyāchattre niveśayet || 2,8.47 ||
tadvaṃśāgraṃ kṣaṇaikena jāyate guṭikā tu tām  |
sādhako dhārayedvaktre khecaratvapradāyikām  || 2,8.48 ||
tālakaṃ kunaṭīṃ tadvadvaṃśe baddhvā niveśayet |
tābhyāmañjitanetro yo nidhiṃ paśyati bhūgatam  || 2,8.49 ||
vaṃśe baddhvā kṣipetkhaḍgaṃ pūrvamantreṇa mantritam  |
tatkhaḍgaṃ dhārayeddhaste trailokyavijayī bhavet || 2,8.50 ||
vastre gorocanaṃ baddhvā vaṃśasthaṃ tatra veśayet |
tenaiva tilakaṃ kuryāddṛṣṭvā mohayate jagat || 2,8.51 ||
srotoñjanaṃ kajjalaṃ ca kṣiptvā tatraiva pūrvavat |
tena cāñjitanetro yo devairapi na dṛśyate  || 2,8.52 ||
pādukāṃ tena yogena kṣiptvā padbhyāṃ tu dhārayet |
yatrecchā tatra tatraiva gantā bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 2,8.53 ||
tadvadraktapaṭaṃ tatra kṣiptvā saṃveṣṭya sādhakaḥ  |
adṛśyo jāyate samyakpaṭe mukte tu dṛśyate  || 2,8.54 ||
kāntābhraṃ kāñcanaṃ sūtaṃ kṛtvā golaṃ tu veśayet |
vaṃśāgrasthaṃ mantrayuktaṃ sparśavedhī bhavettu tat || 2,8.55 ||
śrīśaile paścime dvāre nāmnā brahmeśvareśvaraḥ  |
talliṅgaṃ sparśamityāhurdurgā tatraiva tādṛśī  || 2,8.56 ||
navamaṃ yattu sopānaṃ nadyāṃ tattādṛśaṃ sthitam  |
devasya tiṣṭhati dvāre mudgavarṇaṃ vilokayet || 2,8.57 ||
brahmeśvarasya nairṛtye saṃsthitaṃ tintiṇīvanam  |
tasmin vane sthitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tatra tiṣṭhati caṇḍikā  || 2,8.58 ||
ekapādena ciñcādhastadvṛkṣātpattramāharet |
baddhvā vastre kṣipetkuṇḍe snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 2,8.59 ||
snānānte poṭalī grāhyā sarve matsyā bhavanti te  |
tatkāṣṭhaiḥ pācayettāni kaṇṭhaṃ pucchaṃ śirastyajet || 2,8.60 ||
bhāgaṃ devāya saṃkalpya dvitīyamatitherbhavet |
tṛtīyaṃ sādhako bhuktvā kṣaṇaṃ bhavati mūrchitaḥ  || 2,8.61 ||
tatkṣaṇājjāyate siddhaśchidrāṃ paśyati medinīm  |
ajarāmaratāṃ yāti na bādhyastridaśairapi  || 2,8.62 ||
alampurottare bhāge grāmaḥ syādbhīmapādukaḥ  |
tasya grāmasya pārśve tu hastamātraṃ khanedbhuvam  || 2,8.63 ||
pāṣāṇāḥ sarpavadvakrā grāhyāḥ sparśā bhavanti te  |
yogeśvarīti vikhyātā devatā yā tvalampure  || 2,8.64 ||
devyā hyagre guhā ramyā tanmadhye nikhanedbhuvam  |
pāṣāṇā bhekasaṃkāśā grāhyā mārjāraviṣṭhayā  || 2,8.65 ||
miśrīkṛtya kṣipedvaṅge tadvaṅgaṃ tāratāṃ vrajet |
madhvājyasahitān bhakṣetpāṣāṇāṃstānvicakṣaṇaḥ  || 2,8.66 ||
śrīśailasyottaradvāre maheśo nāma devatā  |
bhramarāmraḥ sthitastatra tasya pakvaphalāni vai  || 2,8.67 ||
sphoṭayettatra niryānti bhramarā jīvasaṃyutāḥ  |
ityevaṃ pratyayaṃ jñātvā bhramarāṃstān parityajet || 2,8.68 ||
phalāni pācayetkṣīraiḥ pibetkṣīraṃ yatheṣṭakam  |
kiṃcinmūrchā bhavettena tato bodhe pibetpunaḥ  || 2,8.69 ||
evaṃ kuryāttrisaptāhaṃ vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ  |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedācandratārakam  || 2,8.70 ||
sarvavāṅmayavettā ca vāyuvegī bhavennaraḥ  |
tenaivāmraphalaikena varjitabhramareṇa ca  |
sahasrapalavaṅgaṃ tu drāvitaṃ stambhayeddhruvam  || 2,8.71 ||
kṣepāttāramavāpnoti satyamīśvarabhāṣitam  |
tatphalaṃ chidritaṃ kṛtvā utpātya bhramaraṃ tataḥ  || 2,8.72 ||
śuddhapāradakarṣaikaṃ tattulyaṃ kṛṣṇamabhrakam  |
kṣiptvā tasminmukhaṃ ruddhvā limpenmṛdgomayena ca  || 2,8.73 ||
chāyāśuṣkaṃ tadāmrotthaiḥ kvāthairyāmaṃ vipācayet |
svabhāvaśītalā grāhyā guṭikā phalamadhyagā  || 2,8.74 ||
mukhasthā khecaraṃ datte adṛśyatvaṃ mahābalā  |
kvāthe vākpatitulyaḥ syājjīvedācandratārakam  || 2,8.75 ||
tāraṃ tāmraṃ bhujaṃgaṃ vā koṭibhāgena vedhayet |
kṛṣṇābhraṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca pūrvavattatphale kṣipet || 2,8.76 ||
limpenmṛdgomayaistadvadāraṇyotpalakaiḥ puṭet |
svabhāvaśītalaṃ grāhyaṃ tadrasaṃ madhusarpiṣā  || 2,8.77 ||
guñjāmātraṃ sadā khādenmāsādbālo bhavennaraḥ  |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ vīro navanāgabalānvitaḥ  || 2,8.78 ||
vidyate lokavikhyātaḥ pūjyaḥ pañcopacārakaiḥ  |
oṃ huṃ phaṭkāramantreṇa nirvikalpena sādhakaiḥ  || 2,8.79 ||
vīkṣya paścimadigbhāge hyantarikṣe ca tatkṣaṇāt |
paśyeddivyavimānāni jāyate pratyayo mahān  || 2,8.80 ||
tataḥ paścimadigbhāge gacchennadīṃ taretsudhīḥ  |
gahvaraṃ dṛśyate tatra praviśetpaścimamukham  || 2,8.81 ||
yojanatritayaṃ gacchedekākī nirvikalpakaḥ  |
pañcayojanavistīrṇaṃ dṛśyate kadalīvanam  || 2,8.82 ||
tasya madhye'tivistīrṇaṃ jalapūrṇaṃ sarovaram  |
siṃhāsanaṃ tu tanmadhye śuddhasphaṭikasaṃnibham  || 2,8.83 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā daṇḍavadbhūmau nipatenmantramuccaret |
oṃ hrīṃ vidyāvāgīśvarādhipataye hrīmoṃ svāhā  |
snānaṃ kṛtvā prayatnena lakṣamekaṃ japedanu  |
yatheṣṭaṃ bhojanaṃ kṛtvā kandamūlaphalādikam  || 2,8.84 ||
tataḥ siṃhāsanasyordhvaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasaṃnibhaḥ  |
caturbhujastrinetraśca dṛśyate parameśvaraḥ  || 2,8.85 ||
stotramantrairnamaskāraiḥ praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ  |
varaṃ datte yatheṣṭaṃ vai sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ  || 2,8.86 ||
śatamāyatanaṃ tatra kūpānāṃ ca śataṃ nava  |
tāvatsaṃkhyāstathārāmā nandanākhyavanāni ca  || 2,8.87 ||
tathā navaśataṃ vāpyo vidyante kadalīvane  |
biladvārāṇi tāvanti kalpavṛkṣāstathaiva ca  || 2,8.88 ||
tatraiva maukalī nāma yakṣiṇī prakaṭā sthitā  |
sā vakti bhojanaṃ dehi yadīcchasi samīhitam  || 2,8.89 ||
tatastasya pravaktavyaṃ dāsyāmi parameśvari  |
kṣīrānnaṃ vā phalāhāraṃ tadagre dāpayetsudhīḥ  || 2,8.90 ||
sā vakti mama putro'yaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vakṣasi dhāraya  |
yadā na muñcase bhūmau tadā siddhyaṣṭakaṃ tava  || 2,8.91 ||
dāsye'haṃ nātra saṃdeho bhojanānte'thavā punaḥ  |
tyakte karomi saṃhāraṃ tasmādyatnena rakṣaya  || 2,8.92 ||
ityevaṃ sādhako vīraḥ kuryātsiddhimavāpnuyāt |
gaccheduttaradigbhāge tatsaro yojanārdhakam  || 2,8.93 ||
tatrāsti puṣpasampūrṇaṃ divyākhyaṃ nandanaṃ vanam  |
puṣpāṇāṃ ghrāṇamātreṇa kṣutpipāsā na vidyate  || 2,8.94 ||
athavā bhakṣayettasmātphalamekaṃ yathācitam  |
tena bhakṣitamātreṇa vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,8.95 ||
saro dakṣiṇadigbhāgaṃ gacchedyojanapādakam  |
tatra dāḍimasampūrṇaṃ dṛśyate nandanaṃ vanam  || 2,8.96 ||
tatphalaṃ bhakṣayetsiddho jīvedyugasahasrakam  |
tatsaraḥpūrvadigbhāge gacchetkrośārdhamātrakam  || 2,8.97 ||
tatra dhātrīphalaiḥ pūrṇaṃ śyāmalaṃ nandanaṃ vanam  |
phalāni bhakṣayettāni jīvetkalpaśatatrayam  || 2,8.98 ||
tatsaraḥpaścimabhāge gacchedyojanamātrakam  |
tatra bilvaphalaiḥ pūrṇaṃ dṛśyate nandanaṃ vanam  || 2,8.99 ||
tatphalaṃ bhakṣayedvīro jīveccandrārkatārakam  |
tannandanavane ramye liṅgaṃ syānnīlavarṇakam  || 2,8.100 ||
praviśeduttaradvāre tatra nāgo mahābalaḥ  |
ugraḥ saptaphaṇākāro dṛśyate'tibhayaṃkaraḥ  || 2,8.101 ||
tasya kuryānnamaskāraṃ hruṃ hruṃ kuryātsamuddharet |
tadāsau vadate vāṇīmadṛśyatvaṃ dadāmi te  || 2,8.102 ||
gaccha tvaṃ paścimadvāre tatrāsti divyakanyakā  |
gacchettatra mahāvīraḥ sādhako mantramuccaran  || 2,8.103 ||
hāraṃ dāsyati sā tuṣṭā na praveśaṃ prayacchati  |
taṃ hāraṃ dhārayetkaṇṭhe sākṣādvāgīśvaro bhavet || 2,8.104 ||
tatastu dakṣiṇaṃ dvāraṃ gacchettatra bhayaṃkaram  |
muktakeśaṃ vakranetraṃ gadāhastaṃ digambaram  || 2,8.105 ||
nīlavarṇaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ dṛṣṭvā mantraṃ samuccaret |
ha hā he he haṃ hruṃkāraṃ phaṭhuṃ svāhāntameva ca  || 2,8.106 ||
anena mantrapāṭhena kṣetrapālaḥ prasīdati  |
dadāti khecarīṃ siddhiṃ na praveśaṃ kadācana  || 2,8.107 ||
tatastu pūrvadigbhāge dvāraṃ tatra gaṇeśvaraḥ  |
dṛśyate pūjayettaṃ vai sādhakaḥ praviśettataḥ  || 2,8.108 ||
dṛśyate divyacāpaṃ tu tathā liṅgaṃ manoharam  |
pūjayecchivamantreṇa rakāreṇaiva nānyathā  || 2,8.109 ||
tatastatra japaṃ kuryādahorātramupoṣitaḥ  |
pratyakṣo jāyate rudro varaṃ datte yathepsitam  || 2,8.110 ||
ityevamādayaḥ santi siddhayaḥ kadalīvane  |
akṣarairlikhitaṃ dvāre tatra padmāvatībilam  || 2,8.111 ||
sarasaḥ pūrvadigbhāge yojanaikena tiṣṭhati  |
vamanaṃ recanaṃ kṛtvā praviśettatra sādhakaḥ  || 2,8.112 ||
dhanvantaraṃ śataṃ yāvadgacchettatra mṛdaṅgam  |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ vādayedvīraḥ samyakpadmāvatī tataḥ  || 2,8.113 ||
āgatya cāmṛtaṃ datte yatpānādamaro bhavet |
tatastaṃ prārthayatyeva āgaccha mama mandiram  || 2,8.114 ||
divyaṃ kanyāpañcaśataṃ parivāreṇa svīkuru  |
ahaṃ patnī bhaviṣyāmi yāvadbrahmā ca jīvati  || 2,8.115 ||
tadante paramaṃ sthānaṃ gacchāmo nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
kadambeśvaradevastu āgneyyāṃ diśi vidyate  || 2,8.116 ||
tasya pūrvataṭākāgre sthito vṛkṣaḥ kadambakaḥ  |
tasya pattrāṇi saṃgṛhya kaṭutailena lepayet || 2,8.117 ||
tadvṛkṣabījatailairvā tatkāṣṭhaiḥ pācayetsudhīḥ  |
matsyā bhavanti te sarve tāmrapātre vinikṣipet || 2,8.118 ||
kaṇṭhapucchaśirovarjaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ tu bhakṣayet |
tatkṣaṇājjāyate siddho rudratulyo mahābalaḥ  || 2,8.119 ||
nadīsthāne ca yo vṛkṣo vidyate kuṇḍaleśvare  |
pūrvavatkramayogena siddhiḥ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,8.120 ||
kapoteśvaradevasyottare puṣpagiriḥ sthitaḥ  |
tasya kuryātprayatnena trivāraṃ tu pradakṣiṇam  || 2,8.121 ||
tataḥ śiraḥ samāruhya khecaratvaṃ labhennaraḥ  |
tatpūrvottaradigbhāge chelikādvārasthitam  || 2,8.122 ||
uttarābhimukhaṃ tatra praviśeddhanuṣāṃ trayam  |
mūṣikākārapāṣāṇāstiṣṭhanti tān samāharet || 2,8.123 ||
takraiḥ piṣṭvā tadekaṃ tu pibenmūrchāmavāpnuyāt |
kṣaṇāduttiṣṭhate siddho jīvedbrahmadinatrayam  || 2,8.124 ||
sa eva sarvalohānāṃ sparśavedhakaro bhavet |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā devāgre siddhimāpnuyāt || 2,8.125 ||
kapoteśvaradevasya dakṣiṇe devatādvayam  |
tanmadhye kaṭimātraṃ tu khanedgorocanopamāḥ  || 2,8.126 ||
pāṣāṇāstān samādāya madhvājyābhyāṃ prapeṣayet |
tatpānājjāyate martyaḥ kalpāyurnātra saṃśayaḥ  || 2,8.127 ||
kapoteśvaradevasya vāyavye hastamātrakam  |
khanetpārāvataprakhyāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśabhedakāḥ  || 2,8.128 ||
tiṣṭhanti grāhayedekaṃ devatārādhane kṛte  |
mallikārjunadevasya aiśānyāṃ bhṛgupātanam  || 2,8.129 ||
vidyate tatsamīpasthaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tasmānmṛdāharet |
pañcagavyena sammiśrāṃ khadirāṅgārakairdhamet || 2,8.130 ||
nīlotpalasamaṃ lohaṃ patatyevātha secayet |
madhvājyābhyāṃ sutaptaṃ tatsaptavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 2,8.131 ||
tadgolaṃ dhārayedvaktre viṣṇutulyo bhavennaraḥ  |
bhṛgupātātpūrvabhāge krośodadhikapāṭake  || 2,8.132 ||
tasyāgre acchatailākhyaḥ parvato nāma viśrutaḥ  |
tatpaścime biladvāraṃ tanmadhye dhanvapañcakam  || 2,8.133 ||
gate tu dṛśyate kuṇḍaṃ raso lākṣārasaprabhaḥ  |
alābupātre saṃgṛhya koṭivedhī bhavettu saḥ  || 2,8.134 ||
{sparśavedha:: rockneara tempul}
mallikārjunadevasya pūrvato lokaviśrutaḥ  |
prākāraścandraguptasya vidyate tatra mandiram  || 2,8.135 ||
tasya pūrve sthitaścaityaścaityapūrve mahāśilā  |
mudgavarṇā ca sā khyātā sparśavedhakarā tu sā  || 2,8.136 ||
gajastatraiva vikhyātastamāruhya samāhitaḥ  |
tasya pṛṣṭhāttṛṇaṃ grāhyaṃ tatsarvaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet || 2,8.137 ||
gajasya cottare pārśve jānumātraṃ khanedbhuvam  |
jambūphalasamākārāḥ santi te sparśavedhakāḥ  || 2,8.138 ||
gajasyādhaḥ khanedvātha jānumātraṃ labhettataḥ  |
trikoṇaguṭikāṃ siddhāṃ lakṣavedhakarāṃ parām  || 2,8.139 ||
śrīśailasya tu vāyavye tīrthaṃ devahradaḥ sthitam  |
tatra kuṇḍe mudgavarṇāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśavedhakāḥ  || 2,8.140 ||
tatra kūrmopamaṃ liṅgaṃ sparśavedhakaraṃ param  |
kṛttikāyāṃ supūrṇāyāṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ samāharet || 2,8.141 ||
gavyūtidvayatastasmānnāmnā nīlavanaṃ smṛtam  |
tatra liṅgaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ jalaṃ caiva tu tādṛśam  || 2,8.142 ||
aśvābhrakākasadṛśāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśavedhakāḥ  |
santi devagṛhasyāntaḥ khanejjānvantarāddharet || 2,8.143 ||
śrīśailasya tu nairṛtye nāmnā guṇḍiprabhā sthitā  |
tasyāgrādgartamṛdgrāhyā pītavarṇā puṭairdahet || 2,8.144 ||
tasmānniḥsarate hema sphuradrūpaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
tatraiva bhramarāḥ syuśca pūrvavatphaladāyakāḥ  || 2,8.145 ||
tathā tambīpure nāmni tīrthe ca vipule śubham  |
sadāphalaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ pūrvavatsādhayetsudhīḥ  || 2,8.146 ||
