mahānandeśvaraṃ nāma śrīśailasya tu nairṛte  |
tasya pūrvottare pārśve gacchedrājapathena tu  || 2,8.147 ||
kālavarṇeśvaro nāma devastasyāgrakuṇḍakam  |
indragopakasaṃkāśastanmadhye vidyate rasaḥ  || 2,8.148 ||
taptāni saptalohāni tatsekātkāñcanaṃ bhavet |
śrīśailasya tu īśāne devaḥ syātturaleśvaraḥ  || 2,8.149 ||
tasyottare suvikhyāta umāparvata uttamaḥ  |
tasya mūrdhni triśūlābhā darbhāstiṣṭhanti śobhanāḥ  || 2,8.150 ||
tasyādhaḥ kaṭimātraṃ tu khātvā nīlāṃ mṛdaṃ haret |
tāṃ pacedakṣajaiḥ kāṣṭhairbhāgaṃ devāya kalpayet || 2,8.151 ||
atitheśca tathāgneśca bhāgaṃ bhāgaṃ prakalpayet |
bhāgamātmani bhuñjīta jīvetkalpāyutaṃ naraḥ  || 2,8.152 ||
tatra koṭīśvaraṃ khyātamileśvarasamīpataḥ  |
tasyāgre puṣpamātraṃ tu sparśavedhakaraṃ bhavet || 2,8.153 ||
acaleśaśca tatraiva sparśavedhakaraḥ paraḥ  |
dakṣiṇe leśasya yojanaikena vidyate  || 2,8.154 ||
amareśvaradevākhyastatra svarṇaśilā śubhā  |
tasya pṛṣṭhe ca gomāṃsaṃ mahāmāṃsaṃ ca vā kṣipet || 2,8.155 ||
prajvālya badarīkāṣṭhaiḥ prātaḥ svarṇopamā tu sā  |
dṛśyate rajasā strīṇāṃ bhāvitaṃ vastrakhaṇḍakam  || 2,8.156 ||
tacchilāloḍitaṃ kuryādrakṣedalābupātrake  |
tena saṃsparśamātreṇa lohaṃ bhavati hāṭakam  || 2,8.157 ||
śrīśaile tatra tatraiva pāṣāṇāḥ piṇḍabhūsthitāḥ  |
taccūrṇaṃ triphalāsārdhaṃ karṣaikaṃ bhakṣayetsadā  || 2,8.158 ||
māsārdhaṃ tu jarāṃ hanti jīvedācandratārakam  |
nairṛtye śailarājasya paṭāhakarṇa īśvaraḥ  || 2,8.159 ||
tasya ceśānadigbhāge pañcaviṃśatidhanvake  |
dvihastamātrordhvaśilā tatra hastadvayaṃ khanet || 2,8.160 ||
śrāvasampuṭasaṃkāśān pāṣāṇāṃstriṃśadāharet |
kṛtvā tānagnivarṇāṃśca siñcetkūṣmāṇḍajairdravaiḥ  || 2,8.161 ||
tanmadhyānnavanītaṃ tu gṛhṇīyāddevāya bhāgakam  |
atitherbhāgaikaṃ tu bhāgaikaṃ bhakṣayedbudhaḥ  || 2,8.162 ||
mūrchā bhaveddivārātraṃ prabuddho jāyate naraḥ  |
jīvedvarṣāyutaṃ vīro valīpalitavarjitaḥ  || 2,8.163 ||
tasya devasya sopānaṃ dvitīyaṃ sparśavedhakam  |
tasya devasya pārśve tu pāṣāṇāḥ śvetapītakāḥ  || 2,8.164 ||
sarvasparśā na saṃdeha ekameva samāharet |
tasya devasya pūrve tu kūpaḥ syānnātidūrataḥ  || 2,8.165 ||
tanmadhye bhekasaṃkāśāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśavedhāḥ  |
uttare tasya devasya vikhyāto liṅgaparvataḥ  || 2,8.166 ||
tasya cottarapārśve tu nadī syātpūrvavāhinī  |
nadyāḥ paścimadigbhāge liṅgaṃ piṅgalavarṇakam  || 2,8.167 ||
tatra śailodakaiḥ kuṇḍaṃ pūrṇaṃ syātkṣaṇavedhakam  |
maheśāddakṣiṇe bhāge caṇḍikā yojanadvaye  || 2,8.168 ||
piṇḍādevīti vikhyātā tasyā vāyavyakoṇataḥ  |
kūpastiṣṭhati tanmadhye pāṣāṇā mudgavarṇakāḥ  || 2,8.169 ||
tathā pathyānibhāścaiva sarve te sparśavedhakāḥ  |
sparśanādatra sarveṣāṃ vedhayuktirvidhīyate  || 2,8.170 ||
sthūlaścetpeṣayecchlakṣṇaṃ tena mūṣāṃ tu kārayet |
aṣṭalohāni tanmadhye samāvartyāni kārayet || 2,8.171 ||
tatsarvaṃ jāyate svarṇamevaṃ kuryādyathepsitam  |
sūkṣmaścedaṣṭalohānāṃ drutānāṃ yatra kutracit || 2,8.172 ||
taṃ sarvaṃ nikṣipenmadhye sarvaṃ tatkāñcanaṃ bhavet |
mallikārjunavāyavye tīrthaṃ sarveśvaraṃ sthitam  || 2,8.173 ||
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge jalamārge'rdhayojane  |
gate ḍāṅgarikaṃ tatra dṛśyate tasya mūrdhani  || 2,8.174 ||
dhātrīphalāni kṛṣṇāni vidyante tāni bhakṣayet |
yatheṣṭāni tu saptāhaṃ vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ  || 2,8.175 ||
valīpalitanirmukto jīvetkalpaśatatrayam  |
tasmācca dakṣiṇe bhāge kākalerīmahānavam  || 2,8.176 ||
tatrāsti stambhakadalī praviśettatra sādhakaḥ  |
gacchetkrośārdhamātraṃ tu dṛśyate rasakuṇḍakam  || 2,8.177 ||
gṛhītvālābupātre tu koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  |
tasmin vane mayūraḥ syānnīlavarṇaśilāmayaḥ  || 2,8.178 ||
mukhāgre tasya kuṇḍaṃ tu dvādaśāṅgulanīlakam  |
tanmadhye śuṣkavaṃśaṃ tu kṣiptvā syānnūtanaḥ kṣaṇāt || 2,8.179 ||
dine pattraṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ jāyate tasya nānyathā  |
tatra pāradaprasthaikaṃ kṣiptvā tāvajjalaṃ haret || 2,8.180 ||
kṣaudratulyaṃ pibettaṃ vai tatkṣaṇānmūrchito bhavet |
muhūrtāllabdharājyaḥsyājjīvedyugasahasrakam  || 2,8.181 ||
śailasyāgneyabhāge tu guṭikāsiddhakeśvaraḥ  |
vidyate tasya purataḥ pañcahastāṃ khanedbhuvam  || 2,8.182 ||
pāṣāṇā badarākārāḥ santi khegatidāyakāḥ  |
ekameva samāhṛtya vaktre dhāryaḥ khagāmibhiḥ  || 2,8.183 ||
śrīśaile sarvayogānāmuktānāṃ vidhirucyate  |
mantranyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā paścāllakṣaṃ japedanu  || 2,8.184 ||
aghoraṃ tena vai śīghraṃ tattatsiddhimavāpnuyāt |
kṣetradevatāmāvāhayet |
tatra svapne devatā pratyakṣībhūya varaṃ dadāti  |
evaṃ mantraparaiḥ suniścalatarairbhaktaiśca tatsādhakaiḥ śambhoḥ pūjanatatparaiḥ pratidinaṃ pūjāvidheḥ pālakaiḥ  |
kāryaṃ śrīgirisādhanaṃ japaparairāmnāyapāraṃgatairno cetkleśakaraṃ parākṛtamaho vyarthaṃ bhavenniścitam  || 2,8.185 ||


 3, 1
yena sṛṣṭaṃ vidā cidātmasvamaruttejojalorvīgaṇāḥ satsaṃvicchivaśaktibhairavakalāḥ śrīkaṇṭhapañcānanaḥ  |
īśo rudramurāridhātṛvibudhāścandrārkatārāgaṇāḥ so'yaṃ pātu carācaraṃ jagadidaṃ nirnāmanāmādhipaḥ  || 3,1.1 ||
sūte sūtavaro varaṃ ca kanakaṃ śabdātparaṃ sparśanāddhūmādvidhyati tatkṣaṇādaghaharaṃ saṃkhyāṃ sakharvāṃśataḥ  |
saṃkhyāmarbudakoṭilakṣamayutaṃ yuktyā sahasraṃ śataṃ datte khegatimakṣayaṃ śivapadaṃ tasmai parasmai namaḥ  || 3,1.2 ||
natvā śrīpārvatīṃ devīṃ bhairavaṃ siddhasaṃtatim  |
rasaratnākaraṃ vakṣye dehe lohe śivaṃkaram  || 3,1.3 ||
{rasanāmāni}
śivabījaḥ sūtarājaḥ pāradaśca rasendrakaḥ  |
etāni rasanāmāni tathānyāni śive yathā  || 3,1.4 ||
datte śivapadaṃ siddhiṃ sādhakānāṃ mahottamām  |
śivabījaṃ tadākhyātaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam  || 3,1.5 ||
{mercury:: nirukti}
yataḥ paraśivātsūtastena sūtaḥ sa coditaḥ  |
saṃsārasya paraṃ pāraṃ datte'sau pāradaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 3,1.6 ||
rasībhavanti lohāni dehā api susevanāt |
rasendrastena vikhyāto dravatvācca rasaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 3,1.7 ||
rasaśāstreṣu sarveṣu śambhunā sūcitaṃ purā  |
raso rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ sūcanānnaiva budhyate  || 3,1.8 ||
siddhaiḥ śivamukhātprāptaṃ teṣāṃ siddhistu sādhanāt |
sampradāyakramo yuktistaiḥ svaśāstreṣu gopitā  || 3,1.9 ||
mayāpi tanmukhātprāptaṃ sādhitaṃ bahudhā tataḥ  |
rasaśāstrāṇi sarvāṇi samālokya yathākramam  || 3,1.10 ||
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya prakaṭīkriyate'dhunā  |
na krameṇa vinā śāstraṃ na śāstreṇa vinā kramaḥ  || 3,1.11 ||
śāstraṃ kramayutaṃ jñātvā yaḥ karoti sa siddhibhāk |
{ācārya}
ācāryo jñānavāndakṣo rasaśāstraviśāradaḥ  || 3,1.12 ||
mantrasiddho mahāvīro niścalaḥ śivavatsalaḥ  |
devībhaktaḥ sadā dhīro devatāyāgatatparaḥ  || 3,1.13 ||
sarvāmnāyaviśeṣajñaḥ kuśalo rasakarmaṇi  |
evaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyukto rasavidyāgururbhavet || 3,1.14 ||
{śiṣya}
gurubhaktāḥ sadācārāḥ satyavanto dṛḍhavratāḥ  |
nirālasāḥ svadharmajñāḥ sadājñāparipālakāḥ  || 3,1.15 ||
dambhamātsaryanirmuktāḥ kulācāreṣu dīkṣitāḥ  |
atyantasādhakāḥ śāntā mantrārādhanatatparāḥ  || 3,1.16 ||
ityevaṃ lakṣaṇairyuktāḥ śiṣyāḥ syū rasasiddhaye  |
{anucarāḥ}
sahāyāḥ sodyamāḥ sarve yathā śiṣyāstato'dhikāḥ  || 3,1.17 ||
kulīnāḥ svāmibhaktāśca kartavyā rasakarmaṇi  |
{badalchemists}
nāstikā ye durācārāścumbakā gurutalpagāḥ  || 3,1.18 ||
vidyāṃ gṛhītumicchanti cauryeṇa ca balācchalāt |
na teṣāṃ sidhyate kiṃcinmaṇimantrauṣadhādikam  || 3,1.19 ||
kurvanti yadi mohena nāśayanti svakaṃ dhanam  |
iha loke sukhaṃ nāsti paraloke tathaiva ca  || 3,1.20 ||
tasmādbhaktibalādeva saṃtuṣyati yathā guruḥ  |
tathā śiṣyeṇa sā grāhyā rasavidyātmasiddhaye  || 3,1.21 ||
hastamastakayogena varaṃ labdhvā susādhayet |
{rasaśālā}
ātaṅkarahite deśe dharmarājye manorame  || 3,1.22 ||
umāmaheśvaropete samṛddhe nagare śubhe  |
kartavyaṃ sādhanaṃ tatra rasarājasya dhīmatā  || 3,1.23 ||
atyantopavane ramye caturdvāropaśobhite  |
tatra śālā prakartavyā suvistīrṇā manoramā  || 3,1.24 ||
samyagvātāyanopetā divyacitrairvicitritā  |
{rasamaṇḍapa}
tatsamīpe same dīrghe kartavyaṃ rasamaṇḍapam  || 3,1.25 ||
atiguptaṃ suvistīrṇakapāṭārgalabhūṣitam  |
dhvajachattravitānāḍhyaṃ puṣpamālāvilambitam  || 3,1.26 ||
bherīkākalaghaṇṭādiśṛṅginādavināditam  |
bhūḥ samā tatra kartavyā sudṛḍhā darpaṇopamā  || 3,1.27 ||
tanmadhye vedikā ramyā kartavyā lakṣaṇānvitā  |
{rasaliṅga}
niṣkatrayaṃ hemapattraṃ rasendro navaniṣkakam  || 3,1.28 ||
amlena mardayedyāmaṃ tena liṅgaṃ tu kārayet |
dolāyantre sāranāle jambīrasthaṃ dinaṃ pacet || 3,1.29 ||
{rasaliṅga:: worship}
talliṅgaṃ pūjayettatra suśubhairupacārakaiḥ  |
liṅgakoṭisahasrasya yatphalaṃ samyagarcanāt || 3,1.30 ||
tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ rasaliṅgārcanādbhavet |
brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi gohatyāprayutānyapi  || 3,1.31 ||
tatkṣaṇādvilayaṃ yānti rasaliṅgasya darśanāt |
sparśanātprāpyate muktiriti satyaṃ śivoditam  |
vāṅmāyāṃ śrīmadghoreṇa mantrarājena vārcayet || 3,1.32 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ śubhraṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanam  || 3,1.33 ||
pretārūḍhaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ rasaliṅgaṃ vicintayet |
tasyotsaṅge mahādevīmekavaktrāṃ caturbhujām  || 3,1.34 ||
akṣamālāṅkuśaṃ dakṣe vāme pāśābhayaṃ śubham  |
dadhantīṃ taptahemābhāṃ pītavastrāṃ vicintayet || 3,1.35 ||
vāṅmāyā śrī kāmarājaśaktirbījarasāṅkuśā  |
yai namo dvādaśaiteṣāṃ kāmavidyā rasāṅkuśā  || 3,1.36 ||
anayā pūjayeddevīṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ  |
nandibhṛṅgimahākālānpūjayetpūrvadikkramāt || 3,1.37 ||
pūjayennāmamantraistu praṇavādinamo'ntakaiḥ  |
evaṃ nityārcanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ rasasiddhaye  || 3,1.38 ||
{rasadīkṣā}
rasadīkṣā śivenoktā dātavyā sādhakāya vai  |
yathoktena vidhānena guruṇā muditātmanā  || 3,1.39 ||
sumuhūrte sunakṣatre candratārābalānvite  |
kalaśaṃ toyasampūrṇaṃ hemaratnaphalairyutam  || 3,1.40 ||
sthāpayedrasaliṅgāgre divyavastreṇa veṣṭitam  |
gandhapuṣpākṣatadhūpadīpairnaivedyato'rcayet || 3,1.41 ||
pūjānte havanaṃ kuryādyonikuṇḍe sulakṣaṇe  |
tilājyaiḥ pāyasaiḥ puṣpairaṣṭādhikaśataiḥ pṛthak || 3,1.42 ||
aghoreṇa rasāṅkuśyā homānte śiṣyamāvahet |
kākinīśaktisaṃyuktaṃ rasasiddhiparāyaṇam  || 3,1.43 ||
{kākinī (def.)}
yasyāḥ saṃkucitāḥ keśāḥ śyāmā yā padmalocanā  |
surūpā taruṇī citrā vistīrṇajaghanā śubhā  || 3,1.44 ||
saṃkīrṇaradanā pīnastanabhāreṇa cānatā  |
cumbanāliṅganasparśakomalā mṛdubhāṣiṇī  || 3,1.45 ||
aśvatthapattrasadṛśayonideśena śobhitā  |
kṛṣṇapakṣe puṣpavatī sā nārī kākinī smṛtā  || 3,1.46 ||
rasabandhe prayoge ca uttamā rasasādhane  |
{kākiṇī:: substitute}
tadabhāve surūpā tu yā kācittaruṇāṅganā  || 3,1.47 ||
tasyā deyaṃ trisaptāhaṃ gandhakaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam  |
karṣaikaikaṃ prabhāte tu sā bhavetkākinīsamā  || 3,1.48 ||
{rasadīkṣā (cont.)}
evaṃ śaktiyuto yo'sau dīkṣayettaṃ gurūttamaḥ  |
susnātamabhiṣiñceta vimalaiḥ kalaśodakaiḥ  || 3,1.49 ||
aghoramaṅkuśīṃ vidyāṃ dadyācchiṣyāya sadguruḥ  |
yathāśaktyātha śiṣyeṇa dātavyā gurudakṣiṇā  || 3,1.50 ||
athājñayā gurormantraṃ lakṣaṃ lakṣaṃ pṛthagjapet |
daśāṃśena hunetkuṇḍe trikoṇe hastamātrake  || 3,1.51 ||
kamalaṃ caturasraṃ ca caturdvāraiḥ suśobhitam  |
jātipuṣpaṃ trimadhvaktaṃ pūrṇānte kanyakārcanam  |
kṛtvātha praviśecchālāṃ śubhāṃ liptāṃ suvedikām  || 3,1.52 ||
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tatra sindūreṇa dvihastakam  |
vedikāyāṃ likhetsamyaktadbahiścāṣṭapattrakam  || 3,1.53 ||
kamalaṃ caturasraṃ ca caturdvāreṣu śobhitam  |
karṇikāyāṃ nyasetkhalvaṃ lohajaṃ svarṇarekhitam  || 3,1.54 ||
tanmadhye rasarājaṃ tu palānāṃ śatamātrakam  |
pañcāśatpañcaviṃśaṃ vā pūjayedrasaliṅgavat || 3,1.55 ||
vajravaikrāntavajrābhrakāntapāṣāṇaṭaṅkaṇam  |
bhūnāgaṃ śaktayaścaitāḥ ṣaṭsu pattreṣu pūjayet || 3,1.56 ||
{uparasāḥ}
gandhatālakakāsīsaśilākaṅkuṣṭhabhūkhagam  |
rājāvarto gairikaṃ ca khyātā uparasā amī  || 3,1.57 ||
{uparasa:: worship}
pūjyā aṣṭadaleṣvete pūrvādīśāntagāḥ kramāt |
{mahārasāḥ}
rasakaṃ vimalā tāpyaṃ capalā tutthamañjanam  || 3,1.58 ||
hiṅgulaṃ sasyakaṃ caiva khyātā ete mahārasāḥ  |
pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ pattrāgreṣu prapūjayet || 3,1.59 ||
pūrvadvāre svarṇaraupye dakṣiṇe tāmrasīsake  |
paścime vaṅgakāntau ca uttare tīkṣṇamuṇḍake  || 3,1.60 ||
sarvametamaghoreṇa pūjayedaṅkuśānvitam  |
{āusstattungdesālchemisten}
viḍakāñjikayantrāṇi kṣāramṛllavaṇāni ca  || 3,1.61 ||
koṣṭhī mūṣā vaṅkanālī tuṣāṅgāravanopalāḥ  |
bhastrikā daṃśakānekā śilā khalvo'pyudūkhalam  || 3,1.62 ||
svarṇakāropakaraṇaṃ samastatulanāni ca  |
mṛtkāṣṭhatāmralohādyapātrāṇi vividhāni ca  || 3,1.63 ||
divyauṣadhāni vargāśca rañjakaṃ snehanāni ca  |
etāni dvārabāhye tu mūlamantreṇa pūjayet || 3,1.64 ||
vāṅmāyāṃ heṃ tataḥ kṣmeṃ ca kṣmaśca pañcākṣaro manuḥ  |
anena mūlamantreṇa bhairavaṃ tatra pūjayet || 3,1.65 ||
{rasasiddhas}
sarveṣāṃ rasasiddhānāṃ nāmāni kīrtayettadā  |
vyālācāryaścandrasenaḥ subuddhirnaravāhanaḥ  || 3,1.66 ||
nāgārjuno ratnaghoṣaḥ surānando yaśodharaḥ  |
indradyumnaśca māṇḍavyaścarpaṭiḥ śūrasenakaḥ  || 3,1.67 ||
vāḍabo nāgabuddhiśca khaṇḍaḥ kāpāliko haraḥ  |
kāmalī tāttvikaḥ śambhurloko lampaṭaśāradau  || 3,1.68 ||
bāṇāsuro muniśreṣṭho govindaḥ kapilo baliḥ  |
ete sarve tu bhūpendrā rasasiddhā mahābalāḥ  || 3,1.69 ||
caranti sarvalokeṣu nirjarāmaraṇāḥ sadā  |
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyākā rasasiddhipradāyakāḥ  || 3,1.70 ||
vandyāḥ pūjyāḥ prayatnena tataḥ kuryādrasāyanam  |
harṣayeddvijadevāṃśca tarpayediṣṭadevatām  || 3,1.71 ||
kumārīyoginīyogimunimāyikasādhakān  |
tarpayetpūjayedbhaktyā nijaśaktyanusārataḥ  || 3,1.72 ||
ityevaṃ sarvasambhārayuktaṃ kuryādrasotsavam  |
sarvavighnapraśāntyarthaṃ sarvepsitaphalapradam  || 3,1.73 ||
anyathā cedvimūḍhātmā mantradīkṣākramaṃ vinā  |
kartumicchati sūtasya sādhanaṃ guruvarjitaḥ  || 3,1.74 ||
nāsau siddhimavāpnoti yatnakoṭiśatairapi  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena śāstroktāṃ kārayetkriyām  || 3,1.75 ||
samyaksādhanasodyamā guruyutā rājājñayālaṃkṛtā nānākarmaṇi kovidā rasaparāstvāḍhyā janaiścārthitāḥ  |
mātrāyantrasupākakarmakuśalāḥ sarvauṣadhīkovidāsteṣāṃ sidhyati nānyathā vidhibalātśrī pāradaḥ pāradaḥ  || 3,1.76 ||


 3, 2
bhaktyā śāstravicāraṇādanudinaṃ pūjāvidheḥ pālanātsvātmānandanimajjanātparahitātkāryakriyāgopanāt |
nityaṃ sadgurusevanādanubhavātsūtasya sadbhāvanāddāsyante nijaraśmayo varabalātsatsampradāyātsphuṭam  || 3,2.1 ||
rasādilohaparyantaṃ śodhane māraṇe hitam  |
bhāvanāyāṃ kvaciccaiva nānāvargo nigadyate  || 3,2.2 ||
{defaultvalues}
aṅge nokte bhavenmūlaṃ dravaḥ sarvāṅgato bhavet |
amātrāyāṃ samā mātrā vijñeyā rasakarmaṇi  || 3,2.3 ||
{kṣāravarga}
tilāpāmārgakadalīcitrakārdrakamūlakam  |
śigrumokṣapalāśaṃ ca sarvamantaḥpuṭe dahet || 3,2.4 ||
samāloḍya jalairvastrairbaddhvā grāhyamadhojalam  |
śodhayetpācayedagnau mṛdbhāṇḍena tu tajjalam  || 3,2.5 ||
grāhyaṃ kṣārāvaśeṣaṃ tadvṛkṣakṣāramidaṃ smṛtam  |
{śvetavarga, śuklavarga}
cunnaṃ kūrpaṃ śaṅkhaśuktivarāṭaiḥ śuklavargakaḥ  || 3,2.6 ||
{viḍvarga}
kapotacāṣagṛdhrāṇāṃ śikhikukkuṭayośca viṭ |
{amlavarga}
cāṅgerī caṇakāmlaṃ tu mātuluṅgāmlavetasam  || 3,2.7 ||
ciñcānāraṅgajambīramamlavarga iti smṛtaḥ  |
{lavaṇapañcaka}
sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ kācaṃ cullikā ca suvarcalam  || 3,2.8 ||
cūlikānavasāraḥ syādetallavaṇapañcakam  |
{trikṣāra}
sajjīkṣāraṃ yavakṣāraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca tṛtīyakam  || 3,2.9 ||
{mūtravarga}
kṣāratrayamidaṃ khyātamajāśvamahiṣīgavām  |
nārīmeṣīkharoṣṭrāṇāṃ mūtravargo gajasya ca  || 3,2.10 ||
{mitrapañcaka}
madhvājyaṭaṅkaṇaṃ guñjā guḍaḥ syānmitrapañcakam  |
{pittavarga}
narāśvaśikhigomatsyapittāni pittavargake  || 3,2.11 ||
{vasāvarga}
matsyāhinarameṣīṇāṃ śikhināṃ ca vasā matā  |
{raktavarga}
mañjiṣṭhā kuṅkumaṃ lākṣā dāḍimaṃ raktacandanam  || 3,2.12 ||
bandhūkaṃ karavīraṃ ca raktavargo hyayaṃ bhavet |
{pītavarga}
kusumbhaṃ kiṃśukaṃ rātriḥ pataṃgaṃ madayantikā  || 3,2.13 ||
pītavargo hyayaṃ khyāto divyauṣadhigaṇaṃ śṛṇu  |
{divyauṣadhigaṇa}
ajakarṇī śaṅkhapuṣpī rudantī kākatuṇḍikā  || 3,2.14 ||
haṃsapādī vyāghranakhī cāṇḍālī kṣīrakandakaḥ  |
vandhyākarkoṭakī rambhā gojihvā kokilākṣakaḥ  || 3,2.15 ||
śākavṛkṣo hemavallī pātālagaruḍī śamī  |
kaṭutumbī vajralatā sūraṇaṃ vanasūraṇam  || 3,2.16 ||
meṣaśṛṅgī cakramardo jalakumbhī śatāvarī  |
guñjā kośātakī nīlī ākhukarṇī triparṇikā  || 3,2.17 ||
kukkuṭī kṛṣṇatulasī puṅkhā śvetāparājitā  |
garuḍī lāṅgalī brāhmī cāṅgerī padmacāriṇī  || 3,2.18 ||
{vajraśodhanam (1)}
gṛhītvātha śubhaṃ vajraṃ vyāghrīkandodare kṣipet |
mahiṣīviṣṭhayā liptvā tulāgnau ca puṭe pacet || 3,2.19 ||
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya hayamūtrairniṣecayet |
vyāghrīkande punaḥ kṣiptvā vajrakaṃdodare kṣipet |
hayamūtrairniṣiñcyācca puṭetsiñcyācca pūrvavat || 3,2.20 ||
evaṃ saptadinaiḥ śuddhaṃ vajraṃ syānnātra saṃśayaḥ  |
{vajraśodhanam (2)}
kulatthakodravakvāthahayamūtrasnuhīpayaḥ  || 3,2.21 ||
kṣiptvā bhāṇḍe kṣipettasmin vyāghrīkandagataṃ pavim  |
dolāyaṃtre divārātrau samuddhṛtya punaḥ kṣipet || 3,2.22 ||
vyāghrīkandaṃ mahākande kṣiptvā gajapuṭe pacet |
tatpakvaṃ kāñcanīdrāvaiḥ secayecchuddhimāpnuyāt |
{vajraśodhanam (3)}
ākhukarṇī meghanādaḥ priyaṅgurmeṣaśṛṅgikā  |
amlavetasanirguṇḍīkulatthakodravāḥ śamī  || 3,2.23 ||
muniśca hayamūtreṇa kaṣāyaṃ kārayecchubham  |
jambīre sūraṇe vātha kṣiptvā vajraṃ dinaṃ pacet || 3,2.24 ||
pūrvakvāthena dolāyāṃ śuddhimāpnoti nānyathā  |
{vajra:: māraṇa}
śuddhaṃ vajraṃ matkuṇānāṃ raktairliptvā dhamettu tat || 3,2.25 ||
agnivarṇaṃ kṣipenmūtre gardabhotthe punaḥ punaḥ  |
lepitaṃ dhāmitaṃ tadvadevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā  || 3,2.26 ||
{vajra:: māraṇa}
tālakaṃ matkuṇaiḥ piṣṭvā gole tasminkṣipettu tat |
ruddhvā mūṣāṃ dhameddārḍhyāthayamūtre vinikṣipet || 3,2.27 ||
samuddhṛtya punastadvatsaptavārānmṛto bhavet |
{vajramāraṇam (3)}
meṣaśṛṃgī bhujagāsthi kūrmapṛṣṭhaṃ śilājatu  || 3,2.28 ||
gandhakaṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ munipuṣpaṃ satālakam  |
trikṣāraṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ meṣaśṛṅgīndravāruṇī  || 3,2.29 ||
vajravallī mūṣakarṇī badarīkuḍmalāni ca  |
mūṣakasya malaṃ stanyaṃ snuhyarkakṣīramatkuṇāḥ  || 3,2.30 ||
pañcāṅgāṃ śarapuṅkhāṃ ca hastinīraṃ nṛchāgayoḥ  |
peṭārībījaṃ strīpuṣpaṃ pārāvatamalaṃ śilām  || 3,2.31 ||
puṣpāṇi caiva vākucyāḥ pañcāṅgaṃ nimbakasya ca  |
dhātrīvṛkṣasya pañcāṅgaṃ gorambhā cājamūtrakam  || 3,2.32 ||
haṃsapādī vajrakandaṃ bṛhatīphalasūraṇe  |
gojihvā karkaṭaṃ māṃsaṃ mūtravargaṃ ca miśrayet || 3,2.33 ||
etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ supiṇḍitam  |
tatpiṇḍe nikṣipedvajramandhamūṣāgataṃ puṭet || 3,2.34 ||
kulatthaṃ kodravaṃ piṣṭvā hayamūtrairviloḍayet |
tanmadhye secayettaptāṃ mūṣāṃ puṭavinirgatām  || 3,2.35 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam  |
ādāya pūrvajaṃ vajratāle matkuṇapeṣite  || 3,2.36 ||
golake nikṣipedruddhvā mūṣāṃ tīvrānale dhamet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā dhāmyaṃ hayamūtrairniṣecayet || 3,2.37 ||
anena kramayogena mṛtaṃ bhavati niścitam  |
taṃ mṛtaṃ cūrṇayetkhalve siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ  || 3,2.38 ||
{mercury:: śodhana}
bhrāmakasya mukhaṃ tāpyaṃ peṭārībījaṭaṅkaṇe  |
kṣāraṃ cottaravāruṇyā gandhakaṃ tālakaṃ śamī  || 3,2.39 ||
bhūdharasthaṃ puṭaikena samuddhṛtya vimardayet |
pūrvavatpūrvajairdrāvaistadvadruddhvā ca pācayet || 3,2.40 ||
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ daśavāraṃ ca pūrvavat |
pātayetpātanāyaṃtre samyakśuddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,2.41 ||
{mercury:: śodhana}
rasasya daśamāṃśaṃ tu gandhaṃ dattvā vimardayet |
jambīrotthairdravairyāmaṃ pātyaṃ pātanayantrake  || 3,2.42 ||
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pātyaṃ saptavāraṃ viśuddhaye  |
{mercury:: śodhana}
athavā pāradaṃ mardyaṃ taptakhalve dināvadhi  |
kumārīrajanīcunnaiḥ pātyaṃ pātanayantrake  || 3,2.43 ||
kanyāgnitriphalābhiśca punarmardyaṃ ca pātayet |
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pātyaṃ saptavāraṃ viśuddhaye  |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryātsamyakśuddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,2.44 ||
{mercury:: mardana:: application}
yuktaṃ sarvasya sūtasya taptakhalve vimardanam  |
śodhane cāraṇe caiva jāraṇe ca viśeṣataḥ  |
mūrchane māraṇe caiva bandhane ca praśasyate  || 3,2.45 ||
{taptakhalva}
ajāśakṛttuṣāgniṃ ca bhūgarte tritayaṃ kṣipet |
tasyopari sthitaṃ khalvaṃ taptakhalvamidaṃ bhavet || 3,2.46 ||
khalvaṃ lohamayaṃ śastaṃ mardakaṃ caiva lohajam  |
tadabhāve śilotthaṃ vā yogyaṃ khalvaṃ ca mardakam  || 3,2.47 ||
{mercury:: hiṅgulākṛṣṭa}
athavā hiṃgulātsūtaṃ grāhayettannigadyate  |
gomūtrairmāhiṣairmūtraistilatailasurāmlakaiḥ  || 3,2.48 ||
saptāhaṃ hiṃgulaṃ pācyaṃ lohapātre kramāgninā  |
cālayellohadaṇḍena drāvaṃ dattvā punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,2.49 ||
sadravaṃ taṃ samādāya śikhipittena bhāvayet |
dinānte pātanāyantre pātayeccaṇḍavahninā  || 3,2.50 ||
saptakañcukanirmuktaḥ khyāto'yaṃ śuddhasūtakaḥ  || 3,2.51 ||
kanyābhistriphalābhiśca punarmardyaṃ ca pātayet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryātsamyakśuddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,2.52 ||
{mercury:: hiṅgulākṛṣṭa}
dinaikaṃ hiṅgulaṃ khalve mardyamamlena kenacit |
pātayetpātanāyaṃtre dinānte tatsamuddharet |
vinā karmāṣṭakenaiva sūto'yaṃ sarvakāryakṛt || 3,2.53 ||
sarvasiddhamatametadīritaṃ sūtaśuddhikaramadbhutaṃ param  |
alpakarmavidhibhūrisiddhidaṃ dehalohakaraṇe hi śasyate  || 3,2.54 ||


 3, 3
ṣaṭkāṣṭakaṃ hyaṣṭakamaṣṭakaṃ ca śodhyaṃ vimardyaṃ ca yathoditaṃ tat |
vajrādilohāntakamuktapūrvaṃ tadvakṣyate sūtavarasya siddhyai  || 3,3.1 ||
{vajra:: subtypes}
śvetā raktāḥ pītakṛṣṇā dvijādyā vajrajātayaḥ  |
puṃstrīnapuṃsakāśceti lakṣaṇena tu lakṣayet || 3,3.2 ||
{puṃvajra:: parīkṣā}
vṛttāḥ phalakasampūrṇāstejasvanto bṛhattarāḥ  |
puruṣāste samākhyātā rekhābinduvivarjitāḥ  || 3,3.3 ||
{strīvajra:: parīkṣā}
rekhābindusamāyuktāḥ ṣaḍasrāstāḥ striyaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
{napuṃsaka:: parīkṣā}
trikoṇaṃ pattradehaṃ yaddīrghaṃ yatsyānnapuṃsakam  || 3,3.4 ||
sarveṣāṃ puruṣāḥ śreṣṭhā vedhakā rasabandhakāḥ  |
strīvajrā dehasiddhyarthaṃ krāmakaṃ syānnapuṃsakam  || 3,3.5 ||
{divyauṣadhi}
ajāmārī kākamācī devadālīndravāruṇī  |
kākajaṅghā śikhiśikhā sarpākṣī nāgavallikā  || 3,3.6 ||
mīnākṣī kṛṣṇadhattūro balā nāgavallī jayā  |
muṇḍī mahābalā pūgī trividhaṃ citrakaṃ niśā  || 3,3.7 ||
mūrvā kāñcānanaṃ kanyā peṭārī sūryavartakaḥ  |
viṣṇukrāntā kāravallī vākucī sinduvārikā  || 3,3.8 ||
svarṇapuṣpī khaṇḍajārī mañjiṣṭhā pīlukaṃ vacā  |
snuhī raktasnuhī bilvaṃ kārpāsaḥ kaṃguṇī ghanā  || 3,3.9 ||
palāśāṅkolavijayā meghanādārkasarṣapāḥ  |
brahmadaṇḍī mahārāṣṭrī śvetā raktā punarnavā  || 3,3.10 ||
udumbarasomalatā kumbhī puṣkaramūlakam  |
tilaparṇī kṛṣṇajīrā vṛścikālī ca kālikā  || 3,3.11 ||
karavīro'gnidamanī bṛhatī bhūmipāṭalī  |
yavaciñcī candralatā markaṭī vanarājakam  || 3,3.12 ||
badarī lajjarī lākṣā caṇā vartulapatrakā  |
apāmārgo bhūkadambo viṣamuṣṭyekavīrakaḥ  || 3,3.13 ||
gorambhā kadalī jātī musalī sahadevikā  |
eraṇḍaḥ saindhavaṃ pathyā śuṃṭhī maṇḍūkaparṇikā  || 3,3.14 ||
marūvako hiṃgu vālo lakṣmaṇā hastimūlikā  |
kṣīravṛkṣāśca ye sarve tathā nānāvidhaṃ viṣam  || 3,3.15 ||
divyauṣadhigaṇaḥ khyāto rasarājasya sādhane  |
vyastaṃ vātha samastaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ niyojayet || 3,3.16 ||
{viṣavargaḥ}
tīvragandharasasparśairvividhaistu vanodbhavaiḥ  |
mardanātsvedanātsūto mriyate badhyate'pi ca  || 3,3.17 ||
raktaśṛṅgī kālakūṭaṃ hāridraṃ saktūkaṃ tathā  |
maustikaṃ viṣavargaḥ syātmūṣākaraṇamucyate  |
{vajramūṣā:: production}
tuṣaṃ vastraṃ samaṃ dagdhaṃ tatpādāṃśā ca mṛttikā  |
kūpīpāṣāṇapādaṃ ca vajravallyā dravairdinam  || 3,3.18 ||
mardayetkārayenmūṣāṃ vajrākhyāṃ rasabandhakām  |
{vajramūṣā (2)}
valmīkamṛttikāṅgārāḥ purāṇaṃ lohakiṭṭakam  || 3,3.19 ||
śvetapāṣāṇakaṃ caitatsarvaṃ cūrṇyaṃ samaṃ samam  |
sarvatulyaṃ tuṣaṃ dagdhaṃ sarvaṃ toyaiśca mardayet || 3,3.20 ||
mūṣāsaṃpuṭakaṃ kuryātsandhiṃ lipyācca tena vai  |
sarvakāryakarā eṣā vajramūṣā mahābalā  || 3,3.21 ||
{vajramūṣā (3)}
gārā dagdhāstuṣā dagdhā dagdhā valmīkamṛttikā  |
gajāśvānāṃ malaṃ dagdhaṃ yāvattatkṛṣṇatāṃ gatam  || 3,3.22 ||
pāṣāṇabhedīpatrāṇi kṛṣṇā mṛcca samaṃ samam  |
vajravallyā dravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ vā śoṣayeddṛḍham  || 3,3.23 ||
tena koṣṭhaṃ vaṅkanālaṃ vajramūṣāṃ ca kārayet |
vartulā gostanākārā vajramūṣā prakīrtitā  || 3,3.24 ||
mūrchane māraṇe bandhe dvaṃdvamelāpake hitā  |
saiva chidrānvitā madhyagambhīrā sāraṇocitā  || 3,3.25 ||
prakaṭā śarāvakākārā bījanirvāpaṇe hitā  |
{vajra:: māraṇa}
sūtaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ tulyaṃ dinaṃ punarnavādravaiḥ  |
marditaṃ taptakhalve tu vajramūṣāndhitaṃ pacet || 3,3.26 ||
ciñcābījaṃ meṣaśṛṃgī strīpuṣpaṃ cāmlavetasam  |
pañcāṅgaṃ śarapuṅkhāyāḥ śaśadantāḥ śilājatu  || 3,3.27 ||
etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ sucūrṇayet |
snuhyarkonmattavāruṇyāḥ kṣīraiḥ stanyairvimardayet || 3,3.28 ||
tadgolake kṣipedvajraṃ ruddhvā mūṣāṃ dhameddṛḍham  |
guḍūcīṃ saindhavaṃ hiṃgu samustottaravāruṇīm  || 3,3.29 ||
kvāthaiḥ kaulatthakaiḥ piṣṭvā tasmindrāve niṣecayet |
tadvajraṃ pūrvavadgole kṣiptvā ruddhvā dhamettathā  || 3,3.30 ||
secanāntaṃ punaḥ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam  |
tālamatkuṇayogena saptavāraṃ punardhamet || 3,3.31 ||
secayedaśvamūtreṇa tadvajraṃ mriyate dhruvam  |
{vajra:: māraṇa}
trivarṣarūḍhakārpāsamūlamādāya peṣayet || 3,3.32 ||
trivarṣanāgavallyā vā nijadrāvaiḥ prapeṣayet |
tadgolake kṣipedvajraṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 3,3.33 ||
evaṃ saptapuṭaiḥ pakva ekaikena mṛto bhavet |
{vajramāraṇam (6)}
tālakāsīsasaurāṣṭrayorhyapāmārgasya bhasma ca  || 3,3.34 ||
piṣṭvā kaulatthakaiḥ kvāthaistasminvajraṃ sutāpitam  |
kṣiptvā trisaptavārāṇi mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,3.35 ||
{vajramāraṇam (7)}
vaikrāntabhasmanā sārdhaṃ peṣayedamlavetasam  |
tadgolake kṣipedvajramandhamūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 3,3.36 ||
secayedaśvamūtreṇa pūrvagole punaḥ kṣipet |
ruddhvā dhmātaṃ punaḥ secyamevaṃ kuryāttrisaptakam  || 3,3.37 ||
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ sarvakarmasu yojayet |
{vajramāraṇam (8)}
uttarāvāruṇīkṣīraiḥ kāntapāṣāṇajaṃ mukham  || 3,3.38 ||
kṣaṇaṃ piṣṭvā tu tadgole vajraṃ kṣiptvā pacedanu  |
nṛtaile gandhataile vā mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,3.39 ||
{vajramāraṇam (9)}
bhūnāgaṃ gandhakaṃ vātha nārīstanyena peṣayet |
tadgolasya pacedvajraṃ pūrvataile mṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,3.40 ||
{vajramāraṇam (10)}
snuhīkṣīreṇa vimalāṃ piṣṭvā tadgolake kṣipet |
vajraṃ nirudhya mūṣāṃ tāṃ śuṣkāṃ tīvrāgninā dhamet || 3,3.41 ||
taptamaśvasya mūtre tu kṣiptvā vajraṃ samāharet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kāryaṃ tatastālakamatkuṇāḥ  || 3,3.42 ||
kṛtvā golaṃ kṣipettasminvajramūṣāṃ nirudhya ca  |
dhāmitaṃ pūrvavatsecyaṃ saptavārairmṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,3.43 ||
{vajramāraṇam (11)}
vajraṃ matkuṇarakteṣu kṣiptvā liptvātape kṣipet |
śuṣkaṃ lepyaṃ punaḥ śoṣyaṃ yāvatsaptadināvadhi  || 3,3.44 ||
viṣṇukrāntāpeṭakāryordravaiḥ siñcetpunaḥ punaḥ  |
taptaṃ taptaṃ tu tadvajraṃ śatavārānmṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,3.45 ||
{vajramāraṇam (12)}
gandhakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ bhāvyaṃ strīpuṣpeṇa tu saptadhā  |
punaḥ strīrajasāloḍya tasminvajraṃ sutāpitam  || 3,3.46 ||
secayettāpayedevaṃ mṛtaṃ syāttu trisaptadhā  |
{vajra:: mṛdūkaraṇa}
mātuluṅgagataṃ vajraṃ ruddhvā bāhye mṛdā lipet |
pacetpuṭe samuddhṛtya tadvacchatapuṭaiḥ pacet || 3,3.47 ||
nāgavallyā dravairliptaṃ tatpatreṇaiva veṣṭitam  |
jānumadhyasthitaṃ yāmaṃ tadvajraṃ mṛdutāṃ vrajet || 3,3.48 ||
{vajraudanaṃ vajramṛdūkaraṇam (2)}
mātṛvāhakajīvasya madhye vajraṃ vinikṣipet |
jambīrodaragaṃ vātha dolāyantre dinaṃ pacet || 3,3.49 ||
kulatthakodravakvāthaistraiphale vā kaṣāyake  |
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya jambīre tu punaḥ kṣipet || 3,3.50 ||
mātṛvāhakajīve vā kṣiptvā paktvā ca pūrvavat |
punaḥ kṣepyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ tridinānte samuddharet || 3,3.51 ||
badarīvaṭanimbānāmaṅkurāṇi samāharet |
piṣṭvā tadgolake vajraṃ pūrvapakvaṃ vinikṣipet || 3,3.52 ||
aśvatthapatrakairveṣṭyaṃ tadgolaṃ jānumadhyagam  |
dinaṃ vā dhārayetkakṣe mṛdurbhavati niścitam  || 3,3.53 ||
{vajramṛdūkaraṇam (3)}
pāradaṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca dinamamlena mardayet |
tadgole nikṣipedvajraṃ sūtreṇāveṣṭayedbahiḥ  || 3,3.54 ||
nāgavallīdravaiścaiva veṣṭitaṃ dhānyarāśigam  |
māsānte tatsamuddhṛtya nāgavallyā dravairlipet |
taddalairveṣṭitaṃ jānumadhyasthaṃ mṛdutāṃ vrajet || 3,3.55 ||
{vajramṛdūkaraṇam (4)}
kāntapāṣāṇavajraṃ vā cūrṇaṃ vā kāntalohajam  || 3,3.56 ||
sasūtamamlayogena dinamekaṃ vimardayet |
tadgole nikṣipedvajraṃ nimbakārpāsakolajaiḥ  || 3,3.57 ||
patraiḥ piṣṭaistu saṃveṣṭya nāgavallīdalaistataḥ  |
veṣṭyaṃ tajjānumadhyasthaṃ dinānte mṛdutāṃ vrajet || 3,3.58 ||
{varjamṛdūkaraṇam (5)}
eraṇḍavṛkṣamadhye tu tatphale vā kṣipetpavim  |
māsamātrātsamuddhṛtya jānumadhye tu pūrvavat || 3,3.59 ||
komalaṃ jāyate vajraṃ dinānte nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
{vajramṛdūkaraṇam (6)}
vajraṃ tittiramāṃsena veṣṭayennikṣipenmukhe  || 3,3.60 ||
atisthūlasya bhekasya mukhaṃ sūtreṇa veṣṭayet |
nikhaneddhastamātrāyāṃ bhūmau māsātsamuddharet || 3,3.61 ||
maṇḍūkasaṃpuṭe ruddhvā samyaggajapuṭe pacet |
tadvajraṃ pūrvagolasthaṃ jānumadhye gataṃ dinam  || 3,3.62 ||
bhavedvajraudanaṃ sākṣānmāryaṃ paścācca yojayet |
sarvavajraudanānāṃ tu māraṇaṃ pūrvavadbhavet || 3,3.63 ||
{vaikrāntaśodhanam}
trikṣāraiḥ pañcalavaṇairvasāmūtrāmlakodravaiḥ  |
matsyapittaistailaghṛtaiḥ kulatthaiḥ kāñjikānvitaiḥ  || 3,3.64 ||
saptāhaṃ dolakāyantre vyāghrīkandagataṃ pacet |
saptavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāntaṃ śuddhimāyāti niścitam  || 3,3.65 ||
{uparasānāṃ śodhanam}
jambīrāṇāṃ drave magnamātape dhārayeddinam  |
śudhyanti ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śaṃkho varāṭāñjanagairikam  |
kāsīsaṃ bhūkhagaṃ caiva śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 3,3.66 ||
{gandhakaśuddhiḥ (1)}
yāmaikaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ dravairnimbājagandhayoḥ  |
śṛṅgīdhattūrayorvātha tilaparṇyāśca vā dravaiḥ  |
tadādāya ghṛtaistulyaṃ lohapātre kṣaṇaṃ pacet || 3,3.67 ||
laghvagninā drutaṃ tadvai ajākṣīre vinikṣipet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryācchuddhimāyāti gandhakam  || 3,3.68 ||
{gandhakaśodhanam (2)}
karañjairaṇḍatailaṃ ca chāgīdugdhaṃ ca bhāṇḍake  || 3,3.69 ||
kṣiptvā tasya mukhaṃ ruddhvā svacchavastreṇa buddhimān  |
gandhakaṃ dhūrtajairdrāvairdinaṃ bhāvyaṃ viśoṣayet || 3,3.70 ||
taccūrṇaṃ pūrvabhāṇḍasya vastropari nidhārayet |
ācchādya ca śarāveṇa pṛṣṭhe deyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu  || 3,3.71 ||
drutaṃ gandhaṃ samādāya bhāvyaṃ dhattūrajairdravaiḥ  |
tadvaddrāvyaṃ punarbhāvyaṃ drāvayecca punastataḥ  || 3,3.72 ||
ādāya matsyapittena saptadhā bhāvyamātape  |
tataḥ kośātakībījacūrṇena saha peṣayet || 3,3.73 ||
bhāvayedbhṛṅgajairdrāvaiḥ saptāhamātape khare  |
toyena kṣālitaṃ śoṣyaṃ tato mṛdvagninā kṣaṇam  || 3,3.74 ||
ghṛtākte lohapātre tu drāvitaṃ ḍhālayettataḥ  |
bhṛṅgarājadravāntasthaṃ samyakśuddhaṃ bhavettu tat || 3,3.75 ||
{gandhataila}
atha śuddhasya gandhasya tailapātanamucyate  |
amlaparṇī devadālī dāḍimaṃ mātuluṅgakam  || 3,3.76 ||
nāraṅgaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ dravamekasya cāharet |
gandhakasya tu pādāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dravasaṃyutam  || 3,3.77 ||
etābhyāṃ gandhakaṃ bhāvyaṃ gharme vāratrayaṃ punaḥ  |
dhattūrastulasī kṛṣṇā laśunaṃ devadālikā  || 3,3.78 ||
śigrumūlaṃ kākamācī karpūraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpikā  |
kṛṣṇāguru ca kastūrī vandhyākarkoṭakī samam  || 3,3.79 ||
mātuluṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭvā kṣipederaṇḍatailake  |
anena lohapātrasthaṃ bhāvayetpūrvagandhakam  || 3,3.80 ||
trivāraṃ kṣaudratulyaṃ tajjāyate gandhavarjitam  |
idaṃ gandhakatailaṃ syāttattadyogeṣu yojayet || 3,3.81 ||
{haritālaśuddhiḥ (1)}
sacūrṇeṇāranālena dolāyantreṇa tālakam  |
dinaṃ pakvaṃ vicūrṇyātha bhāvyaṃ kūṣmāṇḍajairdravaiḥ  || 3,3.82 ||
śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punarbhāvyaṃ śatavāraṃ viśuddhaye  |
{haritālaśuddhiḥ (2)}
tālakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā tālātpādāṃśaṭaṅkaṇam  || 3,3.83 ||
dvayaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaiḥ kṣālayetkāñjikaistathā  |
tacchuṣkaṃ poṭalībaddhaṃ sacūrṇe kāñjike pacet || 3,3.84 ||
dvidinaṃ dolakāyantre tadvatkūṣmāṇḍajairdravaiḥ  |
pācitaṃ śuddhimāyāti tālaṃ sarvatra yojayet || 3,3.85 ||
{vimalaśuddhiḥ}
suvarṇavarṇaṃ vimalaṃ tāpyaṃ vā kaṇaśaḥ kṛtam  |
punarnavāyāḥ kalkasthaṃ kaulatthe svedayejjale  || 3,3.86 ||
saindhavairbījapūrāktairyuktaṃ vā poṭalīkṛtam  |
dolāyantre dinaṃ svedyaṃ śuddhimāyāti niścitam  || 3,3.87 ||
{rasakaśuddhiḥ}
rajasvalārajomūtrai rasakaṃ bhāvayeddinam  |
taireva dinamekaṃ tu mardayecchuddhimāpnuyāt || 3,3.88 ||
{uparasa:: śodhana}
punarnavāmeghanādakapijambīratindukaiḥ  |
agastipuṣpakumudayavaciñcāmlavetasaiḥ  || 3,3.89 ||
yavasūraṇabhūdhātrīmāṇḍūkīkaravīrakaiḥ  |
kāravallīkṣīrakandaraktotpalaśamīvanaiḥ  || 3,3.90 ||
meṣaśṛṅgyā coṣṭravasāśakravāruṇiṭaṅkaṇaiḥ  |
tailamatsyavasāvyoṣairdravairetaiḥ sakāñjikaiḥ  || 3,3.91 ||
etaiḥ samastairvyastairvā dolāyantre dinatrayam  |
abhrapatrādyuparasān śuddhihetostu pācayet || 3,3.92 ||
{uparasa:: śodhana}
sūryāvarto vajrakandaḥ kadalī devadālikā  |
śigruḥ kośātakī vandhyā kākamācī ca vālukam  || 3,3.93 ||
āsāmekarasenaiva trikṣārapaṭupañcakam  |
bhāvayedamlavargeṇa tīvragharme dināvadhi  || 3,3.94 ||
etatkalkena saṃlepyamabhrakaṃ vajramākṣikam  |
vaikrāṃtaṃ sasyakaṃ tālaṃ kāntapāṣāṇamañjanam  || 3,3.95 ||
vimalāṃ ca śilāṃ tutthamanyānuparasāṃstathā  |
dolāyantre sāranāle pūrvakalkayute pacet |
tīvrānale dinaikena śuddhimāyānti tāni vai  || 3,3.96 ||
{dhānyābhrakaraṇam}
śuddhamabhraṃ bhinnapatraṃ kṛtvā vrīhiyute dṛḍhe  |
vastre baddhvā sāranāle bhāṇḍamadhye vimardayet || 3,3.97 ||
hastābhyāṃ svayamāyāti yāvadamlāntare tu tat |
dravaṃ tyaktvā tu tacchoṣyaṃ dinaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ bhavet || 3,3.98 ||
{abhra:: māraṇa:: niścandra}
etaddhānyābhrakaṃ mardyaṃ bhānudugdhairdināvadhi  |
kṛtvā pūpaṃ bhānupatrairveṣṭitaṃ pācayetpuṭe  || 3,3.99 ||
ityevaṃ daśadhā pācyaṃ dugdhairbhāvyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
tato vaṭajaṭākvāthairmardyaṃ daśapuṭaiḥ pacet || 3,3.100 ||
saghṛtairmahiṣīkṣīrairmardyaṃ deyaṃ puṭatrayam  |
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ yojanīyaṃ rasāyane  || 3,3.101 ||
{abhrakabhasma (2)}
punarnavādyauṣadhāni khyātāni hyabhraśodhane  || 3,3.102 ||
dhānyābhrakaṃ tu taireva tridinaṃ tu puṭe pacet |
pūrvavatkramayogena mriyate pañcabhiḥ puṭaiḥ  || 3,3.103 ||
{metals:: śodhana}
taile takre gavāṃ mūtre kāñjike ravidugdhake  |
kulatthānāṃ kaṣāye ca jambīrāṇāṃ drave tathā  || 3,3.104 ||
taptvā taptvā niṣiñcedekaikasmiṃstu saptadhā  |
svarṇādilohapatrāṇi śuddhimāyānti niścitam  || 3,3.105 ||
{lead, tin:: śodhana}
drāvite nāgavaṅge ca pacettadvadviśuddhaye  |
{bronśe, copper:: śodhana}
trikṣāraṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ jambīrāmlena saptadhā  || 3,3.106 ||
bhāvayedātape tīvre tatkalkena vilepya ca  |
ghoṣāratāmrapatrāṇāṃ śuddhyai gajapuṭe pacet || 3,3.107 ||
{lead:: māraṇa}
lohapātre pacennāgaṃ tulyaṃ yāvaddrutaṃ bhavet |
cālyaṃ palāśadaṇḍena bhasmībhūtaṃ samuddharet || 3,3.108 ||
bhasmatulyāṃ śilāṃ tasminkṣiptvā cāmlena kenacit |
peṣayedyāmamātraṃ tu ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 3,3.109 ||
svāṅgaśītaṃ punaḥ piṣṭvā viṃśatyaṃśaśilāyutam  |
amlena yāmamātraṃ ca ruddhvā pacecca pūrvavat |
evaṃ ṣaṣṭipuṭaiḥ pakvo mṛto bhavati pannagaḥ  || 3,3.110 ||
{iron (gen.):: māraṇa}
nārīstanyena sampiṣṭaṃ hiṅgūlaṃ palapañcakam  || 3,3.111 ||
tena lohasya patrāṇi lepayetpalapañcakam  |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyātkaṣāyaistraiphalaiḥ punaḥ  || 3,3.112 ||
jambīrairāranālairvā viṃśāṃśadaradena ca  |
piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭellohaṃ tadvatpācyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,3.113 ||
catvāriṃśatpuṭaireva tīkṣṇaṃ kāntaṃ ca muṇḍakam  |
mriyate nātra saṃdeho dattvā dattvaiva hiṅgulam  || 3,3.114 ||
{vaṅgabhasma}
lohapātre drute vaṅge pādāṃśaṃ tālakaṃ kṣipet |
cālyamaśvatthadaṇḍena jātaṃ bhasma samuddharet || 3,3.115 ||
tadbhasma haritālaṃ tu tulyamamlena mardayet |
palāśakadravairvātha yāmānte coddhṛtaṃ puṭet || 3,3.116 ||
uddhṛtya daśamāṃśena tālena saha mardayet |
pūrvadrāvaistu yāmaikaṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
catvāriṃśatpuṭairevaṃ pakvaṃ syānmṛtavaṅgakam  || 3,3.117 ||
{copper:: māraṇa}
kaṇṭavedhīkṛtaṃ tāmrapatraṃ tulyāṃśagandhakaiḥ  || 3,3.118 ||
amlapiṣṭaiḥ pralipyātha ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
uddhṛtya cūrṇayettasmin pādāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet || 3,3.119 ||
jambīrairāranālairvā piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭetpunaḥ  |
evaṃ catuḥpuṭaiḥ pacyādgandho deyaḥ puṭe puṭe  || 3,3.120 ||
mātuluṅgadravairevaṃ puṭamekaṃ pradāpayet |
sitaśarkarayā pañcapuṭaṃ deyaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,3.121 ||
{silver:: māraṇa}
mākṣikenāmlapiṣṭena tattulyaṃ tārapatrakam  |
liptvā ruddhvā puṭe paktvā samuddhṛtya vicūrṇayet || 3,3.122 ||
kaṇāmākṣikasindhūtthabhūdhātryaśca samaṃ samam  |
pūrvacūrṇena tulyāṃśamidamamlena mardayet || 3,3.123 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyāttaireva mardayetpuṭet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ tāraṃ bhasmatvamāpnuyāt || 3,3.124 ||
{gold:: māraṇa}
mṛtaṃ nāgaṃ snuhīkṣīrairathavāmlena kenacit |
piṣṭvā tena samāṃśena svarṇapatrāṇi lepayet || 3,3.125 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe paktvā samuddhṛtya vicūrṇayet |
tasminnevaṃ mṛtaṃ nāgamaṣṭamāṃśena lepayet || 3,3.126 ||
yāmaikaṃ mardayedamlai ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
evamaṣṭapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavati hāṭakam  |
{brass, bronśe:: māraṇa}
āre ghoṣe prakartavyaṃ tāmravanmāraṇaṃ param  || 3,3.127 ||
vividhaparamayogairyuktiryuktaiḥ prasiddhairanubhavapathadṛṣṭaiḥ śodhanaṃ māraṇaṃ ca  |
pavibaligaganānāṃ sarvalohe viśeṣādgaditamiha hitārthaṃ vārtikānāṃ vibhūtyai  || 3,3.128 ||


 3, 4
samyaksiddhamatāntaraiḥ samucitaiḥ satsaṃpradāyaiḥ śubhaiḥ khyātairgandhakajāraṇādivividhairyogaiḥ susiddhaiḥ kramāt |
yaddṛṣṭaṃ sulabhaṃ suvarṇakaraṇaṃ tārasya saṃraṃjanāt |
satyaṃ sajjanarakṣaṇāturatayā saṃtanyate tattvataḥ  || 3,4.1 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi}
daśaniṣkaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ niṣkaikaṃ śuddhagandhakam  |
stokaṃ stokaṃ kṣipetkhalve mardakena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 3,4.2 ||
kuṭṭanājjāyate piṣṭiḥ seyaṃ gandhakapiṣṭikā  |
{svarṇapiṣṭi}
bhāgatrayaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ bhāgaikaṃ śuddhahāṭakam  || 3,4.3 ||
amlena mardayedyāmaṃ khyāteyaṃ svarṇapiṣṭikā  |
{sulfur:: ṣaḍguṇajāraṇa}
gandhapiṣṭiṃ hemapiṣṭyā samayā veṣṭayedbahiḥ  || 3,4.4 ||
vastreṇa veṣṭayedgāḍhaṃ sūtākhye lohasaṃpuṭe  |
nidhāya poṭalīmadhye sarvatulyaṃ ca gandhakam  || 3,4.5 ||
kṣiptvā nirodhayetsaṃdhiṃ mṛlloṇena ca rodhayet |
bhūdharākhye puṭe paktvā jīrṇe gandhaṃ punaḥ kṣipet || 3,4.6 ||
ṣaḍguṇe gandhake jīrṇe śanairvastraṃ nivārayet |
punaḥ punaḥ samaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā jāryaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 3,4.7 ||
niḥśeṣaṃ naiva kartavyaṃ pramādādyāti sūtakaḥ  |
evaṃ śataguṇe jīrṇe yantrāduddhṛtya piṣṭikām  || 3,4.8 ||
{vedha:: silver=> gold}
tattulye saṃpuṭe hemni ruddhvā bāhye ca veṣṭayet |
kumārīdravapiṣṭena kācenāṅgulamātrakam  || 3,4.9 ||
tadbahiṣṭaṅkaṇenaiva loṇamṛttikayā tataḥ  |
veṣṭyamaṅgulitailena sūryatāpena śoṣitam  || 3,4.10 ||
koṣṭhīyantre gataṃ dhāmyaṃ vaṅkanāle dṛḍhāgninā  |
tatkhoṭaṃ jāyate divyaṃ sindūrāruṇasaṃnibham  || 3,4.11 ||
tena vedhastu tārasya drutasya śatabhāgataḥ  |
deyaḥ saṃjāyate svarṇaṃ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ  || 3,4.12 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi}
gandhakaṃ śodhitaṃ cūrṇyaṃ saptadhā kanakadravaiḥ  |
bhāvayedātape tadvannārīṇāṃ rajasā punaḥ  || 3,4.13 ||
tadgandhaṃ karṣamekaṃ tu narapittena lolitam  |
palaikaṃ pāradaṃ śuddhamātape kharpare kṣipet || 3,4.14 ||
tatpṛṣṭhe lolitaṃ gandhaṃ kṣiptvāṅguṣṭhena mardayet |
piṣṭikā jāyate divyā sarvakāmaphalapradā  || 3,4.15 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi}
tilaparṇīrasenaiva saptadhā śuddhagaṃdhakam  |
bhāvayetpeṣayettacca chāyāśuṣkaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,4.16 ||
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ palaikaṃ ca mūṣāyāṃ hi nidhāpayet |
śuddhasvarṇasya gulikāṃ niṣkamātrāṃ vinikṣipet || 3,4.17 ||
tāṃ mūṣāṃ vālukāyantre sthāpayetpūrvagandhakam  |
truṭiśastruṭiśo dattvā cullyāṃ mandāgninā pacet || 3,4.18 ||
pādāṃśe gandhake jīrṇe jāyate gandhapiṣṭikā  |
gandhapiṣṭiḥ pṛthaggrāhyā svarṇasya gulikāṃ vinā  || 3,4.19 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi}
chāyāyāṃ gandhakaṃ bhāvyaṃ śatadhā markaṭīdravaiḥ  |
gharme mṛtkharpare sūtaṃ kṣipetkiṃcicca gandhakam  || 3,4.20 ||
markaṭīdravasaṃyuktaṃ jīrṇe gandhe dravaṃ punaḥ  |
deyaṃ pādāṃśakaṃ yāvadgandhakaṃ sadravaṃ kramāt || 3,4.21 ||
jāyate piṣṭikā divyā sarvakāmaphalapradā  |
{gandhapiṣṭi}
gandhakaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayettridhā  || 3,4.22 ||
tridhā jambīrajairdrāvairhaṃsapādyāśca saptadhā  |
karpūraṃ ca pṛthagbhāvyaṃ śvetādrikarṇikādravaiḥ  || 3,4.23 ||
ātape trīṇi vārāṇi tato jāraṇamārabhet |
dviguñjāmātrakarpūrairyantragarbhaṃ pralepayet || 3,4.24 ||
tadgarbhe bhāvitaṃ gandhaṃ sārdhaniṣkaṃ pradāpayet |
śuddhasūtaṃ palaṃ cārdhaṃ karpūraṃ pūrvatulyakam  || 3,4.25 ||
pūrvatulyaṃ tato gandhaṃ krameṇātha pradāpayet |
śvetādrikarṇikāmūlaṃ gomūtreṇa prapeṣayet || 3,4.26 ||
ācchādya tena kalkena śarāveṇa nirudhya ca  |
pācayennalikāyantre dinānte taṃ samuddharet || 3,4.27 ||
karpūrādi punardeyaṃ tadvajjāryaṃ dināvadhi  |
ityevaṃ tu punaḥ kuryājjāyate gandhapiṣṭikā  || 3,4.28 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi}
pāradasya palaikaṃ tu karṣaikaṃ śuddhagandhakam  |
snigdhakhalve karāṅgulyā devadālīdrave plutam  || 3,4.29 ||
mardayedātape tīvre jāyate gandhapiṣṭikā  |
{gandhapiṣṭi}
śuddhasūtaṃ palaikaṃ tu karṣārdhaṃ śuddhagandhakam  || 3,4.30 ||
nidhāya tāmrakhalve tu tadadho'gniṃ mṛduṃ kṣipet |
karāṅguṣṭhena saṃmardya yāmādbhavati piṣṭikā  || 3,4.31 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi:: stambhana}
tiktakośātakībījaṃ cāṇḍālīkanda eva ca  |
nārīstanyena sampiṣya lepayedgandhapiṣṭikām  || 3,4.32 ||
nikṣipetsūraṇe kande kṣīrakandodare tathā  |
vandhyākarkoṭakīvajrakande vā kuḍuhuñjike  || 3,4.33 ||
mukhaṃ kandasya majjābhī ruddhvā kandaṃ mṛdā lipet |
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre karīṣāgnau dināvadhi  || 3,4.34 ||
ūrdhvādhaḥ parivartena yathā kando na dahyate  |
tataḥ piṣṭīṃ samuddhṛtya stambhitā jāyate dhruvam  || 3,4.35 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi:: stambhita:: jāraṇa}
athāsyā jāraṇā kāryā stanākhye lohasaṃpuṭe  |
nidhāya jārayedgandhaṃ tulyaṃ tulyaṃ tu bhūdhare  || 3,4.36 ||
jīrṇe gandhe punardeyaṃ yantre jāryaṃ ca pūrvavat |
evaṃ śataguṇaṃ jāryaṃ jīrṇe yantrātsamuddharet || 3,4.37 ||
sarvāsāṃ gandhapiṣṭīnāṃ rañjanaṃ syāttu jāraṇāt |
{gandhapiṣṭi:: māraṇa}
divyauṣadhagaṇadrāvaiḥ piṣṭiḥ khalve vimardayet |
dinānte golakaṃ kṛtvā bījairdivyagaṇodbhavaiḥ  || 3,4.38 ||
lepayedvajramūṣāṃ tu golaṃ tatra nirodhayet |
dinaikaṃ bhūdhare pacyādūrdhvādhaḥ parivartayet || 3,4.39 ||
samuddhṛtya punarmardyaṃ pūrvadrāvaistu pūrvavat |
puṭāntaṃ kārayedevaṃ daśavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,4.40 ||
{gandhapiṣṭi:: vedha:: silver=> gold}
mriyate nātra saṃdeho gandhapiṣṭyāstataḥ punaḥ  |
mṛtapiṣṭipalaikaṃ tu peṣayedvāsakadravaiḥ  || 3,4.41 ||
tatkalkairnāgapatraṃ tu lepayitvā palāṣṭakam  |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ nyasetpiṇḍe vāsāpatrasamudbhave  || 3,4.42 ||
taṃ piṇḍaṃ saṃpuṭe ruddhvā puṭedāraṇyakotpalaiḥ  |
samuddhṛtya punardeyā palaikā mṛtapiṣṭikā  || 3,4.43 ||
vāsādrāvairdinaṃ mardyaṃ taṃ golaṃ veṣṭayetpunaḥ  |
vāsāpatrotthapiṇḍena ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 3,4.44 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryādraktavarṇaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet |
tannāgaṃ palamātraṃ tu yāmaikaṃ vāsakadravaiḥ  || 3,4.45 ||
marditaṃ lepayettena tāmrapatraṃ palāṣṭakam  |
dinaikaṃ pācanāyantre pācayenmriyate dhruvam  || 3,4.46 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena tenaiva tāre vedhaṃ pradāpayet |
tattāraṃ jāyate svarṇaṃ jāmbūnadasamaprabham  || 3,4.47 ||
yayā kayā gandhapiṣṭyā stambhanaṃ jāraṇaṃ vinā  |
pūrvavatkramayogena divyaṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 3,4.48 ||
{vedha:: silver=> gold}
arjunasya tvaco bhasma vāsābhasma samaṃ samam  |
gṛhakanyādravairmardyaṃ dinaikaṃ tena lepayet || 3,4.49 ||
kaṇṭavedhyaṃ nāgapatraṃ ruddhvā laghupuṭe pacet |
uddhṛtya mardayedyāmaṃ pūrvadrāvaiḥ sabhasmakaiḥ  || 3,4.50 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe paktvā svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet |
evaṃ śatapuṭaiḥ pācyaṃ bhasma drāvaiśca mardayet || 3,4.51 ||
tenaiva tārapatrāṇi madhuliptāni lepayet |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe paktvā punarliptvā puṭe pacet || 3,4.52 ||
ityevaṃ tu tridhā kuryāttāramāyāti kāñcanam  |
{vedha:: silver=> gold}
lohapātre drute nāge cūrṇitaṃ rasakaṃ samam  || 3,4.53 ||
kṣiptvā cullyāṃ pacedyāmaṃ cālyaṃ pāṣāṇamuṣṭinā  |
yāmānte hiṅgulaṃ kṣepyaṃ cūrṇitaṃ nāgatulyakam  || 3,4.54 ||
susūkṣmaṃ mardayettāvatdṛḍhaṃ pāṣāṇamuṣṭinā  |
paceccaṇḍāgninā tāvaddinānāmekaviṃśatim  || 3,4.55 ||
jāyate kuṅkumābhaṃ tu tāraṃ tenaiva vedhayet |
catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśena divyaṃ bhavati kāñcanam  || 3,4.56 ||
{vedha:: silver=> gold}
drāvayetkharpare nāgaṃ pādāṃśaṃ tatra nikṣipet |
ciñcāśvatthatvacaḥ kṣāraṃ lohadarvyā vimardayet || 3,4.57 ||
yāmānte tatsamuddhṛtya vajrīkṣīrairdināvadhi  |
mardyaṃ khalve samuddhṛtya mṛdbhāṇḍāntarnirodhayet || 3,4.58 ||

 3, 7
dhātusattvayutapiṣṭikākramastambhanaṃ ca nigaḍena lepanam  |
khoṭabandhakṛtavidrutiṃ tathā vedhayuktirakhilā nigadyate  || 3,7.1 ||
{piṣṭigolam}
divyauṣadhadravairmardyaṃ taptakhalve dinatrayam  |
śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ tato gharme śoṣyaṃ svacchaṃ samāharet || 3,7.2 ||
svarṇanāgārkakāntaṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca rūpyakam  |
yatheṣṭaikaṃ vicūrṇyādau vyomasattvamathāpi vā  || 3,7.3 ||
pūrvasūtena saṃtulyaṃ yāmamamlena mardayet |
mahārasāṣṭakādekaṃ sūtatulyaṃ vinikṣipet || 3,7.4 ||
divyauṣadhīdravaireva yāmātsvinnātape khare  |
mardayetkhalvake gāḍhaṃ tadgolaṃ vastraveṣṭitam  || 3,7.5 ||
gandhataile dinaṃ pacyāttato vastrātsamuddharet |
piṣṭigolamidaṃ khyātaṃ tathānyamatamucyate  || 3,7.6 ||
{piṣṭigola}
pūrvaṃ yanmarditaṃ sūtaṃ tasya bhāgatrayaṃ bhavet |
nāgadvayaṃ yatheṣṭaikaṃ tasminnāgaṃ vinikṣipet || 3,7.7 ||
vaikrāntaṃ kuṇḍagolaṃ ca divyauṣadhidravaṃ tathā  |
mardyaṃ yāmatrayaṃ khalve chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu golakam  || 3,7.8 ||
kṛtvātha bandhayedvastre gandhataile dinaṃ pacet |
vastramadhyātsamuddhṛtya piṣṭigolamidaṃ bhavet || 3,7.9 ||
{piṣṭigola:: nigaḍa}
athāsya piṣṭigolasya nigaḍaḥ procyate śubhaḥ  |
gugguluṃ brahmabījāni taistulyaṃ caiva saindhavam  || 3,7.10 ||
snuhyarkapayasā mardyaṃ nigaḍo'yaṃ mahottamaḥ  |
{piṣṭigola:: nigaḍa}
pālāśaṃ kokilākṣasya bījāni saindhavaṃ tathā  || 3,7.11 ||
unmattanīlijairdrāvairmardyaḥ syānnigaḍottamaḥ  |
{piṣṭigola:: nigaḍa}
abhrakaṃ saindhavaṃ tāpyaṃ vālūmṛllohakiṭṭakam  |
snuhyarkapayasā piṣṭaṃ yāmānte nigaḍo bhavet || 3,7.12 ||
{piṣṭigola:: nigaḍa}
vākucībrahmadhattūrabījāni cāmlavetasam  || 3,7.13 ||
kākaviṭkadalīkandatālagandhamanaḥśilā  |
gugguluṃ pañcaloṇāni gojihvā kokilākṣakam  || 3,7.14 ||
tiktakośātakī nīlī viṣamuṣṭīndravāruṇī  |
jīradvayaṃ karkaṭāsthi strīrajomūtramiśritam  || 3,7.15 ||
snuhyarkakṣīratailaiśca mardyaṃ yāmadvayaṃ dṛḍham  |
vyastaṃ vātha samastaṃ vā nigaḍo'yaṃ mahottamaḥ  |
{mercury:: khoṭa}
eteṣvekena tadgolaṃ lepyamaṅgulamātrakam  || 3,7.16 ||
mūṣāyāṃ bilvamātrāyāṃ loṇaṃ kiṃcidadhaḥ kṣipet || 3,7.17 ||
liptaṃ golaṃ kṣipettasyāmūrdhvaṃ loṇaṃ ca dāpayet |
ruddhvā mṛllavaṇaiḥ sandhiṃ sarvato dagdhaśaṅkhakaiḥ  || 3,7.18 ||
mūṣālepaḥ prakartavyaḥ chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
karīṣāgnau divārātrau trirātraṃ vā tuṣāgninā  || 3,7.19 ||
mṛdunā svedayetpaścātsamuddhṛtyātha lepayet |
samyaṅnigaḍakalkena pūrvamūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 3,7.20 ||
ūrdhvādho lavaṇaṃ dattvā ruddhvā lepyā ca pūrvavat |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ raso bhavati khoṭatām  || 3,7.21 ||
sauvīraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kācaṃ dattvā dattvā dhameddṛḍham  |
tacchuddhaṃ jāyate khoṭamabhīkṣṇaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,7.22 ||
ityevaṃ sarvasattvaiśca piṣṭikāṃ kārayetpṛthak |
pūrvavatkramayogena khoṭo bhavati tadrasaḥ  || 3,7.23 ||
{mercury:: khoṭa}
bhāgadvayaṃ suvarṇasya tribhāgaṃ pāradasya ca  |
pūrvavatkārayetpiṣṭīṃ tadvatkhoṭaṃ ca śodhayet || 3,7.24 ||
ityevaṃ piṣṭikhoṭāni kṛtvā sarvatra yojayet |
{viḍavaṭī}
tatkhoṭaṃ svarṇasaṃtulyaṃ samāvartaṃ tu kārayet |
mākṣikaṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ śilāgandhaṃ samaṃ samam  || 3,7.25 ||
bhūnāgairmardayedyāmaṃ caṇamātravaṭīkṛtam  |
eṣā viḍavaṭī khyātā yojyā sarvatra jāraṇe  || 3,7.26 ||
ekaikaṃ vāhayetsūte dhāmyamāne punaḥ punaḥ  |
evaṃ daśaguṇaṃ hema jārayettatkrameṇa tu  || 3,7.27 ||
prakāśamūṣāgarbhe tu grasate vaḍavānalaḥ  |
{mercury:: rañjana, => śatavedhin}
mākṣikaṃ daradaṃ gandhaṃ rājāvartaṃ pravālakam  |
śilā tutthaṃ ca kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ prakalpayet || 3,7.28 ||
vargābhyāṃ pītaraktābhyāṃ kaṅguṇītailakaiḥ saha  |
bhāvayeddivasānpañca sūryatāpe punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,7.29 ||
jāritaṃ sūtakhoṭaṃ tu kalkenānena saṃyutam  |
vālukāhaṇḍimadhyasthaṃ śrāvasaṃpuṭamadhyagam  || 3,7.30 ||
tridinaṃ pācayeccullyāṃ kalkaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
rañjito jāyate sūtaḥ śatavedhī na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,7.31 ||
tāre tāmre bhujaṅge vā candrārke vātha yojayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ jāmbūnadasamaprabham  || 3,7.32 ||
{drutasūta}
piṣṭikhoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ strīpuṣpeṇa tu bhāvayet |
dinatrayaṃ khare gharme śuktau vā nālikeraje  || 3,7.33 ||
mīnākṣī kadalīkandaṃ śvetā raktā punarnavā  |
śilājatu sasindhūtthaṃ nārīpuṣpeṇa mardayet || 3,7.34 ||
pūrvavadbhāvitaṃ khoṭaṃ tasminpiṇḍe vinikṣipet |
pūrvavadbhāvitaṃ khoṭaṃ tasminpiṇḍaṃ vinikṣipet || 3,7.35 ||
mukhaṃ tatpiṇḍakalkena ruddhvā piṇḍaṃ ca bandhayet |

{tin:: śodhana}
kārpāsārkakarañjadhūrtamunijairbhallātaguñjāgnijaiḥ snugvajrīpayasā ca sūraṇabhavairdrāvaiśca mūlaiḥ phalaiḥ  |
takrāktairbahutaptakharparagataṃ vaṅgaṃ niṣiñcyānmuhuryāvatpañcadinaṃ tadeva vimalaṃ vāde sadā yojayet || 3,8.1 ||
{tin:: śodhana}

 3, 10
lohairmahārasavarairviḍapakvabījaṃ kṛtvātha pāradavare vidhivacca jāryam  |
yojyaṃ tathā sakalasāraṇakarmayoge tadvakṣyate vividhabījaviḍādhikāram  || 3,10.1 ||
{pakvabīja (1)}
tāmraṃ samaṃ śuddhaṃ drāvitaṃ lepayetpunaḥ  |
sāmlena tāpyakalkena dhametsvarṇāvaśeṣitam  || 3,10.2 ||
evaṃ daśaguṇaṃ vāhyaṃ tāpyaṃ vā tutthasattvakam  |
pakvabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ svarṇaśeṣaṃ samāharet || 3,10.3 ||
{pakvabīja}
nāgābhraṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ tulyaṃ svarṇe vāhyaṃ dviṣaḍguṇam  |
pūrvavatsvarṇaśeṣaṃ tu grāhyaṃ syātpakvabījakam  || 3,10.4 ||
{pakvabīja (3)}
svarṇaṃ pītābhrasattvaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ dhamet |
tāpyaṃ tālakavāpena svarṇaśeṣaṃ samāharet |
evaṃ daśaguṇaṃ sattvaṃ vāhyaṃ syātpakvabījakam  || 3,10.5 ||
{pakvabīja (4)}
rasakābhrakayoḥ sattvaṃ tāmraṃ nāgaṃ kramottaram  || 3,10.6 ||
cūrṇitaṃ dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
tatkhoṭaṃ mākṣikaṃ tutthamamlaiḥ piṣṭvā puṭe pacet || 3,10.7 ||
samuddhṛtya punaścāmlairmardyaṃ ruddhvā puṭe pacet |
evaṃ pañcapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ drute svarṇe tu vāhayet |
dhameddaśaguṇaṃ yāvattāvatsyātpakvabījakam  || 3,10.8 ||
{bīja:: froṃ lead}
lohasya kuṭyamānasya sutaptasya dalāni vai  |

lohaparpaṭamākṣīkaṃ kaṃkuṣṭhaṃ vimalābhrakam  |
mṛtaśulbaṃ śilāsūtaṃ daradārkasnuhīpayaḥ  || 3,10.10 ||
etaiḥ samaṃ nāgacūrṇaṃ mardyaṃ ruddhvā puṭe pacet |
punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ yāvadvārāṃścaturdaśa  || 3,10.11 ||
evaṃ śataguṇaṃ vāhyaṃ śuddhahemni dhaman dhaman  |
tatsvarṇaṃ nāgabījaṃ syādgorocananibhaṃ bhavet || 3,10.12 ||
{bīja:: froṃ lead:: pakva:: rañjana}
kharparasthe drute nāge brahmabījadalāni hi  |
kṣiptvāgniṃ jvālayeccaṇḍaṃ brahmadaṇḍena cālayet || 3,10.13 ||
caturyāmāttu tadbhasma jātaṃ pātrātsamuddharet |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyātpādāṃśaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ punaḥ  || 3,10.14 ||
dattvāmlamarditaṃ pacyādevaṃ vārāṃścaturdaśa  |
raktavarṇaṃ bhavedbhasma tadbhāgaṃ kharpare kṣipet || 3,10.15 ||
bhāgatrayaṃ śilācūrṇaṃ pṛthakpātre vinikṣipet |
pañcāṅgaṃ vāsakācūrṇaṃ tvakcūrṇaṃ cārjunasya vai  || 3,10.16 ||
śākakiṃśukakoraṇṭaśigrūṇāṃ puṣpamāharet |
nāginī nāgakanyā ca kumārī cāhimārakam  || 3,10.17 ||
sarveṣāṃ pratibhāgaikaṃ śilāmadhye vinikṣipet |
sarvaṃ caturguṇairmūtraiśchāgajaiḥ kvāthamācaret || 3,10.18 ||
pūrvoktanāgabhūtaiśca kharparasthasya saṃkṣipet |
cālayetpācayeccullyāṃ yāvatsaptadināvadhi  || 3,10.19 ||
yatnena mṛtanāgena vāpo deyo drutasya ca  |
pakvabījasya vārāṃstrīn rañjitaṃ jāyate śubham  || 3,10.20 ||

dalānāṃ caiva bījānāṃ piṣṭīstambhe viśeṣataḥ  |

{bīja:: pakva:: rañjana}

sarvaṃ lākṣārasaiḥ piṣṭvā kṣiptvā tailaṃ caturguṇam  |
