etān samūlānādāya nātiśuṣkān vikhaṇḍayet |
pañcāṅgaṃ mūlakaṃ dagdhvā tilakāṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam  || 3,10.72 ||
etatkṣāraiḥ pūrvakalkaṃ mūtravargeṇa bhāvayet |
vastrapūtaṃ dravaṃ pacyātmṛdvagnau lohapātrake  || 3,10.73 ||
bāṣpāṇāṃ budbudānāṃ ca bahūnāmudgamo yadā  |
tadā kāsīsaṃ saurāṣṭrī kṣāratrayaṃ kaṭutrayam  || 3,10.74 ||
gaṃdhakaṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ navasāraṃ ca hiṃgulam  |
eteṣāṃ nikṣipeccūrṇaṃ tasminpātre'tha cālayet || 3,10.75 ||
guḍapākaṃ samuttārya lohasaṃpuṭake kṣipet |
saptāhaṃ bhūmigarbhe'tha dhānyarāśau tathā punaḥ  |
saptāhaṃ saṃsthitaḥ siddho viḍo'yaṃ vaḍavānalaḥ  || 3,10.76 ||
{viḍa:: forsattvajāraṇa}
bhāvayenniculakṣāraṃ devadālīdaladravaiḥ  |
ekaviṃśativāraṃ tu biḍo'yaṃ sattvajāraṇe  || 3,10.77 ||
{viḍa:: tīvrānala}
devadālīśiphābījaṃ guṃjāsaiṃdhavaṭaṃkaṇam  |
samāṃśaṃ niculakṣāramamlavargeṇa saptadhā  || 3,10.78 ||
kośātakīdalarasairbhāvayeddinasaptakam  |
tīvrānalo nāma biḍo vihito hemajāraṇe  || 3,10.79 ||
{viḍa:: forjāraṇa of gold}
mūlakārdrakavahnīnāṃ kṣāraṃ gomūtralolitam  |
vastrapūtaṃ dravaṃ grāhyaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ tena bhāvayet || 3,10.80 ||
śatavāraṃ khare gharme viḍo'yaṃ hemajāraṇe  || 3,10.81 ||
{viḍa:: forjāraṇa of gold}
trikṣāraṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tālaṃ manaḥśilā  |
dinaikamamlavargeṇa pakvaṃ syāddhemajāraṇe  || 3,10.82 ||
{viḍa:: forjāraṇa of gold}
gaṃdhakaṃ navasāraṃ ca jambīrāmlena mardayet |
śatavāraṃ khare gharme biḍo'yaṃ hemajāraṇe  || 3,10.83 ||
{viḍa:: forjāraṇa}
kanyāhayāridhattūradravairbhāvyaṃ tu gaṃdhakam  |
śatadhā taṃ khare gharme viḍo'yaṃ sarvajāraṇe  || 3,10.84 ||
anena grasate śīghraṃ śuktisaṃbhavasaṃpuṭe  || 3,10.85 ||
{viḍa:: forimprovementof grāsana}
saiṃdhavaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ tulyaṃ tāmravallīdravaiḥ plutam  |
anena biḍayogena gaganaṃ grasate rasaḥ  || 3,10.86 ||
{siddhaviḍa}
śilāgaṃdhakasindhūtthaṃ pratyekaṃ ca palaṃ palam  |
palatrayaṃ ca bhūnāgaṃ sarvamekatra mardayet || 3,10.87 ||
śoṣayecca punardeyaṃ bhūnāgānāṃ palatrayam  |
tadvanmardyaṃ punaḥ śoṣyaṃ pācayenmandavahninā  || 3,10.88 ||
evamaṣṭaguṇaṃ dattvā mardyaṃ pācyaṃ vicūrṇayet |
ayaṃ siddhaviḍo yojyo jāraṇe hemakarmaṇi  || 3,10.89 ||
samyaksaṃskṛtagaṃdhakādyuparasaṃ sattvaṃ tato vyomajaṃ paścānmākṣikasattvahāṭakavaraṃ garbhadrutau drāvitam  |
rāgai rañjitabījajālamakhilaṃ bāhyāṃ drutiṃ dvaṃdvitāṃ sūte sarvamidaṃ krameṇa vidhinā siddhairbiḍairjārayet || 3,10.90 ||


 3, 11
{rasasya 18 saṃskārāḥ}
siddhaiḥ sūtavarasya karma vividhaṃ khyātaṃ vicitraiḥ kramaiḥ sādhyāsādhyavivekato hyanubhavan dṛṣṭvā samastaṃ mayā  |
yuktyāṣṭādaśadhā viśeṣavidhinā svedādivedhāntakaṃ dakṣāṇāṃ sukhasādhyameva sukhadaṃ saṃtanyate sāmpratam  || 3,11.1 ||
{18 saṃskāras}
svedanaṃ mardanaṃ mūrchotthāpanaṃ pātanaṃ tridhā  |
nirodhanaṃ niyāmaśca dīpanaṃ cānuvāsanam  || 3,11.2 ||
jāraṇaṃ cāraṇaṃ caiva garbhabāhyadrutistathā  |
rañjanaṃ sāraṇaṃ cānusāraṇā pratisāraṇā krāmaṇaṃ dehaloheṣu  || 3,11.3 ||
{kāñjika:: production}
nānādhānyairyathāprāptaistuṣavarjairjalānvitaiḥ  |
mṛdbhāṇḍaṃ pūritaṃ rakṣedyāvadamlatvamāpnuyāt || 3,11.4 ||
tanmadhye bhṛṅgarāṅmuṇḍī viṣṇukrāntā punarnavā  |
mīnākṣī caiva sarpākṣī sahadevī śatāvarī  || 3,11.5 ||
triphalā girikarṇī ca haṃsapādī ca citrakam  |
samūlaṃ khaṇḍayitvā tu yathālābhaṃ niveśayet || 3,11.6 ||
pūrvāmlabhāṇḍamadhye tu dhānyāmlakamidaṃ bhavet |
{kāñjka:: use}
svedanādiṣu sarvatra rasarājasya yojayet |
{kāñjika:: substitutedbyāranāla}
atyamlamāranālaṃ vā tadabhāve niyojayet || 3,11.7 ||
{svedanam}
tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ rājī citrakaṃ triphalārdrakam  |
mahābalā nāgabalā meghanādaḥ punarnavā  || 3,11.8 ||
meṣaśṛṅgī citrakaṃ ca navasāraṃ samaṃ samam  |
etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā pūrvāmlenaiva mardayet || 3,11.9 ||
tatkalkena limpedvastre yāvadaṅgulamātrakam  |
tanmadhye nikṣipetsūtaṃ baddhvā pacyāddinatrayam  |
dolāyantre'mlasaṃyukte svedito jāyate rasaḥ  || 3,11.10 ||
{mardanam}
prakṣālya kāñjikaiḥ soṣṇaistamādāya vimardayet |
atyamlamāranālaṃ tattadabhāve niyojayet || 3,11.11 ||
gṛhadhūmeṣṭikācūrṇaṃ dagdhorṇā lavaṇaṃ guḍam  |
rājikā triphalā kanyā citrakaṃ bṛhatī kaṇā  || 3,11.12 ||
vandhyākarkoṭakī cātha vyastaṃ vātha samastakam  |
kvāthayedāranālena tena mardyaṃ tryahaṃ rasam  |
prakṣālya kāñjikenaiva samādāya vimūrchayet || 3,11.13 ||
{mūrchanam}
meṣaśṛṅgī kṛṣṇadhūrto balā śvetāparājitā  || 3,11.14 ||
kanyāgnitriphalā caiva sarpākṣī sūraṇaṃ vacā  |
gojihvā cāṅgulī nīlī muruṇḍī kṣīrakandakam  || 3,11.15 ||
rājikā kākamācī ca ravikṣīraṃ ca kāñcanam  |
vyastānāṃ vā samastānāṃ drāvaiścaiṣāṃ vimardayet || 3,11.16 ||
yāmaikaṃ rasarājaṃ ca mūṣāyāṃ saṃnirodhayet |
puṭaikena pacettaṃ tu bhūdhare vātha mardayet || 3,11.17 ||
sarvadrāvairyathāpūrvaṃ ruddhvā ruddhvā vipācayet |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryājjāyate mūrchito rasaḥ  || 3,11.18 ||
{utthāpanam}
jalaiḥ soṣṇāranālairvā lolanādutthito bhavet |
athavā pātanāyantre pācanādutthito bhavet |
evamutthāpitaḥ sūtastridhā pātyaḥ krameṇa tu  || 3,11.19 ||
{ūrdhvapātanam}
kākamācī jayā brāhmī cāṅgerī raktacitrakam  |
aṅkolī rājavṛkṣaśca tilaparṇī kumārikā  || 3,11.20 ||
maṇḍūkī citrakaṃ pāṭhā kākajaṅghā śatāvarī  |
bhūpāṭalī devadālī nirguṇḍī girikarṇikā  || 3,11.21 ||
śaṇapuṣpyārdrakaṃ śṛṅgī gojihvā kṣīrakandakam  |
nīlī caiṣāṃ samastānāṃ vyastānāṃ ca dravairdinam  || 3,11.22 ||
tāmrapādayutaṃ sūtaṃ mardayedamlakaiḥ saha  |
tatpiṣṭaḥ pātayedyantre cordhvapātanake punaḥ  || 3,11.23 ||
ādāya mardayettadvattāmracūrṇena saṃyutam  |
pātayenmardayeccaiva tāmraṃ dattvā punaḥ punaḥ  |
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryānmardanaṃ pātanaṃ kramāt || 3,11.24 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ samādāya adhaḥpātena pātayet || 3,11.25 ||
{adhaḥpātanam}
triphalā rājikā śigrustryūṣaṃ lavaṇacitrakam  |
sūtatulyaṃ tu tatsarvaṃ kāñjikairmardayeddinam  || 3,11.26 ||
tena limpedūrdhvabhāṇḍaṃ pṛṣṭhe deyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu  |
adhaḥpātanayantre tu pātitaṃ tu samuddharet || 3,11.27 ||
{tiryakpātanam}
triphalā rājikā śigrustryūṣaṃ lavaṇacitrakam  |
dhānyābhrakaṃ rasaṃ sarvaṃ mardayedāranālakaiḥ  || 3,11.28 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ tu tatpātyaṃ tiryagyantre dṛḍhāgninā  || 3,11.29 ||
{nirodhanam}
lavaṇenāmbupiṣṭena haṇḍikāntargataṃ rasam  |
ācchādyātha jalaṃ kiṃcitkṣiptvā śrāveṇa rodhayet |
ūrdhvaṃ laghupuṭaṃ deyaṃ labdhvāpyadho bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,11.30 ||
{dīpanam}
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ bhūkhagaṃ śigrumūlakam  |
svarṇapuṣpī ca kāsīsaṃ maricaṃ rājikā madhu  || 3,11.31 ||
kṣīrakando jayā kanyā vijayā girikarṇikā  |
kākajaṅghā śoṇapuṣpī pātālagaruḍī kaṇā  || 3,11.32 ||
vandhyākarkoṭakī vahnirvyastaṃ vātha samastakam  |
peṣayedamlavargeṇa taddravairmardayedrasam  || 3,11.33 ||
dinānte bandhayedvastre dolāyantre tryahaṃ pacet |
pūrvadrāvairghaṭe pūrṇe grāsārthī jāyate rasaḥ  || 3,11.34 ||
{anuvāsanam}
dīpitaṃ rasarājaṃ tu jambīrarasasaṃyutam  |
dinaikaṃ dhārayedgharme mṛtpātre vāsito bhavet || 3,11.35 ||
svedanādiśubhakarmasaṃskṛtaḥ saptakañcukavivarjito bhavet |
aṣṭamāṃśamavaśiṣyate tadā śuddhasūta iti kathyate budhaiḥ  || 3,11.36 ||


 3, 12
samukhavimukhasūte jāraṇārthaṃ pravakṣye vividhasulabhayogairbhojanaṃ gaṃdhakasya  |
tadanu ca ghanacūrṇairbhojanaṃ pācanaṃ syātnikhilasūtavibhūtyai vārtikānāṃ sukhāya  || 3,12.1 ||
{mercur:: jāraṇa of sulfur}
gaṃdhakaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu saptadhā bṛhatīdravaiḥ  |
bhāvayedvātha vṛntākarasenaiva tu saptadhā  || 3,12.2 ||
palaikaṃ pāradaṃ śuddhaṃ kācakūpyantare kṣipet |
karṣakaṃ bhāvitaṃ gaṃdhaṃ karpūraṃ māṣamātrakam  || 3,12.3 ||
kṣiptvā tatra mukhaṃ ruddhvā mṛdā kūpīṃ ca lepayet |
dīpāgninā dinaṃ pacyānmukhamudghāṭayetpunaḥ  || 3,12.4 ||
jīrṇe gaṃdhaṃ ca karpūraṃ dattvā tadvacca jārayet |
evaṃ śataguṇe jīrṇe gaṃdhakaṃ jārayedrase  || 3,12.5 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of sulfur}
kāsīsaṃ caiva saurāṣṭrī sajjīkṣāreṇa modakam  |
śigrutoyena saṃyuktaṃ kṛtvā bhāvyamanena vai  || 3,12.6 ||
saptāhaṃ cūrṇitaṃ gaṃdhaṃ taṃ gaṃdhaṃ jārayetpunaḥ  |
iṣṭikāgartamadhye tu samyakśuddharasaṃ kṣipet || 3,12.7 ||
mukhaṃ svacchena vastreṇa chādayettasya pṛṣṭhataḥ  |
daśāṃśaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ dattvā śrāvakeṇa nirodhayet || 3,12.8 ||
pṛṣṭhe laghupuṭaṃ deyaṃ jīrṇe gaṃdhaṃ punaḥ kṣipet |
evaṃ śataguṇaṃ jāryaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ pārade śanaiḥ  || 3,12.9 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of pakvabīja}
taṃ rasaṃ taptakhalve tu kṣipedvastreṇa gālitam  |
pādāṃśakaṃ pakvabījaṃ dattvāmlairmardayeddinam  || 3,12.10 ||
taṃ kṣipeccāraṇāyantre jaṃbīrarasasaṃyutam  |
taṃ yaṃtraṃ dhārayedgharme jārito jāyate rasaḥ  || 3,12.11 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of pakvabīja}
saviḍaṃ kalayedyaṃtre dinaikaṃ tu puṭe pacet |
jāritaṃ syātpunarbījaṃ dattvā jāryaṃ ca pūrvavat |
jārayecca punastadvadevaṃ jāryaṃ samaṃ kramāt || 3,12.12 ||
{mercury:: sāraṇā withpakvabīja}
jāritaṃ sāraṇāyantre kṣipettailaṃ vasānvitam  |
drāvitaṃ nālamūṣāyāṃ pakvabījarasānvitam  || 3,12.13 ||
tadyaṃtre dhārayedevaṃ sārito jārayedrasaḥ  |
sāritaṃ tatpunarmardyaṃ pūrvavadbiḍasaṃyutam  || 3,12.14 ||
jārayetkacchape yaṃtre jīrṇe bīje tu sārayet |
pūrvavatsāraṇāyantre bījena dviguṇena vai  || 3,12.15 ||
punastaṃ jārayettadvattathaiva pratisārayet |
triguṇena tu bījena pūrvavajjārayetpunaḥ  || 3,12.16 ||
{mercury:: mukha:: ~bandhana}
tadrasaṃ tālakaṃ tulyaṃ tailaṃ dhattūrasaṃbhavam  |
divyauṣadhīgaṇadrāvaṃ sarvaṃ mardyaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,12.17 ||
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ pacyātkarīṣāgnau dināvadhi  |
punardivyauṣadhīdrāvairmardyaṃ pācyaṃ dināvadhi  |
ityevaṃ ca punaḥ kuryātsūto baddhamukho bhavet || 3,12.18 ||
{rasabandhanam}
taṃ rasaṃ dhautamākṣīkaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śulbaṃ rasaḥ śaśī  |
samāṃśaṃ devadālyutthadravairmardyaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,12.19 ||
tridinaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ marditaṃ golakīkṛtam  |
vajramūṣāgataṃ ruddhvā śoṣyaṃ tīvrāgninā dhamet || 3,12.20 ||
khadirāṅgārayogena khoṭabaddho bhavedrasaḥ  |
tatkhoṭaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaiḥ kācaiḥ śodhayedvai dhamandhaman  || 3,12.21 ||
tejaḥpuñjo raso baddho bālārkasadṛśo bhavet || 3,12.22 ||
{silver=> gold}
tadrasaṃ sikthakenaiva veṣṭayitvā prapūjayet |
śatāṃśaṃ tu drute tāre krāmaṇenaiva saṃyutam  || 3,12.23 ||
tattāraṃ jāyate svarṇaṃ jāṃbūnadasamaprabham  |
aṣṭānavatibhāgaṃ syādityevaṃ vedhako mataḥ  || 3,12.24 ||
{mercury:: mukhakaraṇa}
atha vakṣye rasendrasya vāsitasya mukhaṃ kramāt |
yena vyomādivaikrāntaṃ caratyāśvabhiṣecitam  || 3,12.25 ||
amlavetasajaṃbīrabījapūrakabhūkhagaiḥ  |
tridinaṃ mardayetsūtaṃ bhūnāgaiśca dinatrayam  |
taptakhalve dinaṃ mardyaṃ sūtasyetthaṃ mukhaṃ bhavet || 3,12.26 ||
{mercury:: mukhakaraṇa}
madhyagartasamāyuktaṃ kārayediṣṭikādvayam  |
dhānyābhraṃ gaṃdhakaṃ śuddhaṃ pratyekaṃ daśaniṣkakam  || 3,12.27 ||
yāmaṃ jambīrajairdrāvairmardyaṃ tenaiva lepayet |
gartadvayaṃ samāṃśena hyadhogartaṃ suśodhitam  || 3,12.28 ||
viṃśaniṣkaṃ kṣipetsūtamūrdhvaṃ deyāpareṣṭikā  |
liptvā mṛllavaṇaiḥ saṃdhiṃ dīptāgniṃ jvālayedadhaḥ  || 3,12.29 ||
avicchinnaṃ divārātrau yāvatsaptadināvadhi  |
svāṃgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya rasaṃ kiṭṭavivarjitam  |
ityevaṃ tu tridhā kuryādrasasya tu mukhaṃ bhavet || 3,12.30 ||
{mercury:: mukhakaraṇa}
vaṃśanālāndhitaṃ sūtaṃ bhāṇḍe gomūtrapūrite  |
trisaptāhaṃ paceccullyāṃ sūtasyetthaṃ mukhaṃ bhavet || 3,12.31 ||
{parallelbetween saṃskārasandritual}
samukhe nirmukhe sūte vakṣyate jāraṇaṃ kramāt |
khalvapītaṃ raso liṅgaṃ mardanaṃ mārjanaṃ smṛtam  || 3,12.32 ||
nānauṣadhīrasaiḥ snānaṃ sattvairbījaiḥ prapūjanam  |
svarṇādiratnajātaiśca upahāraṃ prakalpayet || 3,12.33 ||
naivedyaṃ rañjanaṃ divyaṃ sāraṇā syādvisarjanam  |
vedhanaṃ dehaloheṣu samyakpūjāvidheḥ phalam  || 3,12.34 ||
yāvaddināni vahnau tu dhāraṇe jāryate rasaḥ  |
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate  || 3,12.35 ||
yatkiṃcidrasarājasya sādhanārthe vyayo bhavet |
tatsarvaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ datte śrī bhairavī dhruvam  || 3,12.36 ||
sādhakānāṃ sudhīrāṇāmiha loke paratra ca  |
ato bhūpairvārtikendraiḥ sādhyaḥ syādbhuktimuktidaḥ  || 3,12.37 ||
{abhra:: preparation forcāraṇa}
arkakṣīreṇa dhānyābhraṃ dinaṃ mardyaṃ nirudhya ca  |
kapotākhye puṭe pacyātkṣīrairyāmaṃ vimardayet || 3,12.38 ||
ruddhvādhaḥ pūrvavatpacyādevaṃ vāracatuṣṭayam  |
tridhā ca mūlakadrāvai rambhākandadravaistridhā  || 3,12.39 ||
apāmārgakākamācīmīnākṣībhṛṅgarāṇmuniḥ  |
punarnavā meghanādo vidāriścitrakaṃ tathā  || 3,12.40 ||
kramādeṣāṃ dravaireva mardanaṃ puṭapācanam  |
ekaikenaikavāraṃ ca dattvā tadbhāvayetpunaḥ  || 3,12.41 ||
śatāvarī tālamūlī kadalī taṇḍulīyakam  |
arkaḥ punarnavā śigruryavaciñcā hyanukramāt || 3,12.42 ||
pratidravairdinaikaṃ tu bhāvitaṃ cāraṇe hitam  || 3,12.43 ||
{abhra:: Vorbereitungauf cāraṇa}
arkakṣīraistu dhānyābhraṃ yāmaṃ mardyaṃ nirudhya ca  |
kapotākhye puṭe pacyādevaṃ vārāṃścaturdaśa  || 3,12.44 ||
kadalī musalī śigrurvandhyāṅkollārkapīlukam  |
nāgavallī kuberākṣī bhūmyapāmārgatumbaruḥ  || 3,12.45 ||
eṣāmekadravaṃ grāhyaṃ kāṃjike vyomasaṃyutam  |
sarvametatkṣipedbhāṇḍe tanmadhye pācayettryaham  || 3,12.46 ||
pūrvābhraṃ dolikāyantre samuddhṛtyātha śoṣayet |
kāsīsaṃ tuvarī sindhuṣṭaṃkaṇaṃ ca samaṃ samam  || 3,12.47 ||
sarvametaddaśāṃśaṃ tu kṣiptvā tasminvimardayet |
dinaikaṃ taptakhalve tu kṣiptvā tasminvimardayet || 3,12.48 ||
dinaikaṃ taptakhalve tu tadabhraṃ cāraṇe hitam  || 3,12.49 ||
{abhra:: preparation forcāraṇa}
śatavāraṃ drutaṃ nāgaṃ muṇḍīdrāve vinikṣipet |
tena drāveṇa dhānyābhraṃ marditaṃ saptadhā puṭet |
mardyaṃ mardyaṃ nirudhyātha kapotākhye puṭe pacet || 3,12.50 ||
tattulyaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ dattvā pūrvoktairmuṇḍikādravaiḥ  || 3,12.51 ||
sastanyairbījapūrotthairdrāvairbhāvyaṃ dināvadhi  |
etadabhraṃ tu sūtasya cāraṇe paramaṃ hitam  || 3,12.52 ||
{siddhamūlī}
vyāghrapādī haṃsapādī kadalyagnikumārikāḥ  |
bṛhatī lāṅgalī vajrī khaṇḍajārīndravāruṇī  || 3,12.53 ||
vandhyākarkoṭakī mūṣā sarpākṣī śaṅkhapuṣpikā  |
maṇḍūkī agnimathano vikhyātā siddhamūlikā  || 3,12.54 ||
etāḥ samastā vyastā vā coktasthāne niyojayet || 3,12.55 ||
{mercury:: samukha:: cāraṇa of abhra}
athātaḥ samukhe sūte pūrvābhraṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśakam  |
dattvā mardyaṃ taptakhalve siddhamūlīdravairdinam  || 3,12.56 ||
tatastaṃ cāraṇāyaṃtre jaṃbīrarasasaṃyutam  |
gharme dhāryaṃ dinaikaṃ tu caratyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,12.57 ||
{mercury:: lakṣa-|koṭivedhin}
cāritaṃ bandhayedvastre dolāyaṃtre dināvadhi  |
siddhamūlīdravairyuktaiḥ pātanājjīryate hyalam  || 3,12.58 ||
ajīrṇaṃ cetpacedyaṃtre kacchapākhye dināvadhi  |
aṣṭamāṃśaṃ viḍaṃ dattvā caratyeva na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,12.59 ||
anena kramayogena cāryaṃ jāryaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
jīrṇe śataguṇe samyaksahasrāṃśena vidhyati  || 3,12.60 ||
sahasraguṇite jīrṇe lakṣavedhī bhavedrasaḥ  |
tadvallakṣaguṇe jīrṇe koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,12.61 ||
kṛṣṇābhraṃ vā suvarṇaṃ vā yathāśaktyā tu jārayet |
pūrvavatkramayogena phalaṃ syādubhayoḥ samam  || 3,12.62 ||
{mercury:: forproductionof silver}
anenaiva krameṇaiva tāraṃ vā śvetamabhrakam  |
jārayettu yathāśaktyā tārakarmaṇi śasyate  || 3,12.63 ||
{mercury:: mukhabandhana, koṭivedhin}
pūrvavatpakvabījena sāraṇādi yathākramam  |
kartavyaṃ rasarājasya vedhanaṃ krāmaṇaṃ tathā  || 3,12.64 ||
caṃdrārkaṃ jārayetsarvaṃ tāmraṃ vā tārakarmaṇi  |
evaṃ śataguṇe jīrṇe sahasrāṃśena vedhayet || 3,12.65 ||
sahasraguṇite jīrṇe pūrvavatsāraṇātrayam  |
kṛtvā jāryaṃ punastadvaccārayecca tridhā punaḥ  || 3,12.66 ||
ityevaṃ ca punaḥ sāryaṃ punaḥ sāryaṃ ca jārayet |
mukhabandhādivedhāntaṃ kārayetpūrvavadrase  || 3,12.67 ||
tenaiva lakṣabhāgena divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  |
yadā lakṣaguṇe jīrṇe tridhā sāryaṃ ca jārayet || 3,12.68 ||
ityevaṃ saptadhā kāryaṃ bandhayecca tato mukham  |
pūrvavatkrāmaṇāntaṃ ca kṛto'sau jāyate rasaḥ  || 3,12.69 ||
koṭivedhī tu caṃdrārke satyaṃ śaṃkarabhāṣitam  || 3,12.70 ||
{mercury:: nirmukha:: cāraṇa}
atha nirmukhasūtasya vakṣye cāraṇajāraṇe  |
śuddhasūte tu vaikrāṃtaṃ māritaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśakam  || 3,12.71 ||
kṣiptvā mardyaṃ taptakhalve siddhamūlīrasaistryaham  |
saṃskāreṇa hyanenaiva nirmukhaścarati dhruvam  || 3,12.72 ||
{mercury:: cāraṇa:: nirmukha}
athavā taptakhalve tu bhūlatāsaṃyutaṃ rasam  |
mardayettridinaṃ paścātpātyaṃ pātanayantrake  || 3,12.73 ||
saṃskāreṇa hyanenāpi nirmukhaṃ carati kṣaṇāt || 3,12.74 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa:: nirmukha}
asyaiva jāraṇāyogyo vyomasaṃskāra ucyate  |
dhānyābhramamlavargeṇa dolāyaṃtre tryahaṃ pacet |
snuhīkṣīraistato mardyaṃ yāmaikaṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet || 3,12.75 ||
kapotākhyapuṭaikena tamādāyātha mardayet |
mūlakaṃ kadalīkandaṃ mīnākṣī kākamācikā  || 3,12.76 ||
munirārdrakavarṣābhūmeghanādāpāmārgakam  |
eraṇḍaśca dravaireṣāṃ pṛthagdeyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu  || 3,12.77 ||
dolāyaṃtre tataḥ pacyādvajrīkṣīrairdināvadhi  |
vacā nimbaṃ dhūmasāraṃ kāsīsaṃ ca sucūrṇitam  || 3,12.78 ||
abhrasya ṣoḍaśāṃśena pratyekaṃ miśrayettataḥ  |
mardayettāmrakhalve tu caṇakāmlairdināvadhi  || 3,12.79 ||
navasārairayaḥpātraṃ lepayettatra nikṣipet |
pāradaṃ sādhitaṃ sābhraṃ caṇakāmlaṃ ca kāṃjikam  || 3,12.80 ||
mṛdvagninā paceccullyāṃ rasaścarati tatkṣaṇāt |
jārayetpūrvayogena tataścāryaṃ ca jārayet || 3,12.81 ||
mātrā yuktiryathāpūrvaṃ seyaṃ nirmukhajāraṇā  |
kṛṣṇābhrakaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca jāryaṃ syāddhemakarmaṇi  || 3,12.82 ||
tāraṃ vā śvetamabhraṃ vā jāryaṃ syāttārakarmaṇi  |
sāraṇādikrāmaṇāntaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tu jārayet || 3,12.83 ||
kartavyaṃ sūtarāje tu tadvadbhavati kāṃcanam  |
śvetena jārayetśvetaṃ yathābījaṃ tathāṅkuram  || 3,12.84 ||
proktaṃ yathā sugamasādhitapītagaṃdhaṃ kṛṣṇābhrahemarajataṃ sitamabhrakaṃ ca  |
saṃsārya tadrasavare varavārtikendraḥ kuryānmahākanakabhārasahasrasaṃkhyam  || 3,12.85 ||


 3, 13
dṛṣṭayogaphalasiddhidaṃ dhruvaṃ pāradasya varajāraṇe hitam  |
sattvapātanamanekayogato dvaṃdvamelamabhiṣekamāryakam  || 3,13.1 ||
{abhra:: śodhana}
mustākvāthena saptāhaṃ kuryāddhānyābhrakaṃ plutam  |
śigrusūraṇarambhānāṃ kaṃdasyaikasya ca dravaiḥ  || 3,13.2 ||
peṭārīmūlajaṃbīradrāvairvāpyaṃ pariplutam  |
itthaṃ plutasyābhrakasya pādāṃśaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ kṣipet || 3,13.3 ||
dinaikaṃ mardayetkhalve yuktamamlena kenacit || 3,13.4 ||
{kāntaloha, mākṣika, uparasa:: sattva:: pātana}
guñjāguggululākṣorṇāsarjīsarjarasaṃ guḍam  |
kṣudramīnaṃ yavakṣāraṃ kācapiṇyākasūraṇam  || 3,13.5 ||
bhūlatā triphalā vahniḥ kṣīrakandaṃ punarnavā  |
dhattūro lāṅgalī pāṭhā balā gaṃdhakatiktakam  || 3,13.6 ||
gokṣīraṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ sarpavṛścikaviṣaṃ madhu  |
ṣaḍbinduḥ kṣudraśambūkamasthīni śaśakasya ca  || 3,13.7 ||
pārāvatamalaṃ tryūṣamiṃdragopaṃ ca śigrukam  |
godhūmaṃ sarṣapaṃ tulyaṃ chāgīdugdhena mardayet || 3,13.8 ||
etadvyastaṃ samastaṃ vā yāmamātreṇa piṇḍitam  |
asya piṇḍasya bhāgaikaṃ dvibhāgaṃ śodhitābhrakam  || 3,13.9 ||
pañcamāhiṣabhāgaikaṃ sarvamekatra lolayet |
karṣāṃśā vaṭikāḥ kāryāḥ kiṃcicchāyāviśoṣitāḥ  || 3,13.10 ||
khadirāṅgārasaṃtapte koṣṭhīyantre kṣipan kṣipan  |
vaṭikāḥ pañcapañcaiva vaṅkanālena saṃdhaman  || 3,13.11 ||
samāptau kiṭṭamādāya sphoṭayetsvāṅgaśītalam  |
vartulaṃ sattvamādāya śeṣakiṭṭaṃ vicūrṇayet || 3,13.12 ||
cūrṇādardhaṃ pūrvapiṇḍaṃ tadvanmāhiṣapañcakam  |
ekīkṛtya dhamettadvatsattvaṃ tattatsamāharet || 3,13.13 ||
ityevaṃ ca punaḥ kuryāttridhā sattvaṃ vimuñcati  |
anena kramayogena kāntasattvaṃ ca mākṣikam  || 3,13.14 ||
kaṭhinoparasāścānye śuddhā bhūnāgamṛttikā  |
muñcanti sattvasaṃghātaṃ grāhayettatpṛthakpṛthak || 3,13.15 ||
{abhra:: sattva:: mṛdūkaraṇa}
abhrasattvaṃ samādāya samāṃśaṃ kācaṭaṃkaṇam  |
dattvā dattvā trivāraṃ tu vajramūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 3,13.16 ||
amlavargaṃ snuhīpatraṃ ciñcābījaṃ savalkalam  |
kalkayettatra tatsattvaṃ saptavāraṃ niṣecayet || 3,13.17 ||
mṛduśubhraṃ bhavetsattvaṃ saptavāraṃ niṣecayet || 3,13.18 ||
{abhra:: sattva:: pātana}
dhānyābhraṃ daśabhāgaṃ syātśuddhanāgaṃ tribhāgakam  |
ṭaṃkaṇaṃ mākṣikaṃ sūtaṃ bhāgaikaikaṃ suśodhitam  || 3,13.19 ||
ūrṇā sarjī yavakṣāraṃ bhāgaṃ bhāgaṃ vimiśrayet |
mardyaṃ mūtrāmlavargābhyāṃ yathāprāptaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,13.20 ||
ajāpañcāṅgasaṃyuktaṃ pūrvavatsattvapātanam  |
kṛtvādāya mṛdu sākṣānnirmalaṃ yojayedadhaḥ  || 3,13.21 ||
{mākṣika:: sattvam:: pātana}
dolāyaṃtre sāranāle mākṣikaṃ svedayeddinam  |
cūrṇitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ lohapātre dinaṃ pacet || 3,13.22 ||
ādāya bhāvayedgharme vajrīkṣīrairdināvadhi  |
gṛhadhūmairghṛtaiḥ kṣaudraiḥ saṃyuktaṃ mardayeddinam  || 3,13.23 ||
aṃdhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ sattvaṃ guṃjānibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.24 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana}
kadalīkaṃdatoyena mākṣikaṃ śatadhātape  |
bhāvitaṃ pācayedyāmaṃ sājyairvātāritailakaiḥ  |
pūrvavaddhamanātsattvamiṃdragopanibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.25 ||
{mākṣika:: sattvam:: pātana}
snuhyarkapayasā stanyairmākṣikaṃ mardayeddinam  |
kaṃkuṣṭhaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ caiva pratipādāṃśamiśritam  || 3,13.26 ||
mūkamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ sattvaṃ maṇinibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.27 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana}
kadalīkaṃdatulasījaṃbīrāṇāṃ dravaiḥ kramāt |
bhāvayenmākṣikaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ pratidrāveṇa saptadhā  |
ruddhvā dhmāte patetsattvaṃ śukasaṃnibhaṃ śubham  || 3,13.28 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana}
mūtravargāmlavargaiśca dvisaptāhaṃ vibhāvayet |
mākṣikaṃ tīvragharmeṇa dinairamlaiśca mardayet || 3,13.29 ||
mitrapañcakasaṃyuktairvaṭī kṛtvā dhameddṛḍham  |
vyomavadvaṃkanālena sattvaṃ śulbanibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.30 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana}
stanyaiḥ kaṅkuṣṭhakaiścaiva kadalītoyasaṃyutaiḥ  |
mitrapaṃcakasaṃyuktairmākṣikaṃ dinasaptakam  || 3,13.31 ||
bhāvitaṃ mardayedyāmaṃ dinaṃ vātāritailakaiḥ  |
mṛdvagninā pacedyāmaṃ yāvadbhavati golakam  |
sattvaṃ kiṃśukapuṣpābhaṃ vyomavaddhamanādbhavet || 3,13.32 ||
{vimala:: sattva:: pātana}
vimalānāṃ ca śuddhānāṃ samyaksyāttāpyavadvidhiḥ  || 3,13.33 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: dhautasattva}
suśuddhaṃ mākṣikaṃ cūrṇaṃ mardyamamlena kenacit |
kṣālayedāranālaistu hyadhasthaṃ svarṇacūrṇavat || 3,13.34 ||
jāyate tatsamuddhṛtya dhautasattvamidaṃ bhavet |
yojayedvāpane caiva bījānāṃ yatra yatra vai  || 3,13.35 ||
{mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana}
mākṣikaṃ pañcamitrāktaṃ saptāhānte vaṭīkṛtam  |
pūrvavaddhamanenaiva sattvaṃ lākṣānibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.36 ||
{manaḥśilā:: sattva:: pātana}
agastyaśigrujaistoyaistryahaṃ bhāvyā manaḥśilā  |
tālakauṣadhayogena sattvaṃ hemanibhaṃ bhavet |
{manaḥśilā:: sattva:: pātana}
gomūtrairmātuluṅgāmlairdinaṃ bhāvyā manaḥśilā  |
tāṃ raktapītapuṣpāṇāṃ rasaiḥ pittaiśca bhāvayet || 3,13.37 ||
dinānte mardayedyāmaṃ mitrapaṃcakasaṃyutam  |
guṭikāṃ kācakūpyantaḥ kṣiptvā tāṃ kācakūpikām  |
sarvato'ṅgulamānena vastramṛttikayā limpet || 3,13.38 ||
śuṣkāṃ tāṃ vālukāyaṃtre śanairmṛdvagninā pacet |
śuṣke drave nirudhyātha samyakmṛllavaṇairmukham  || 3,13.39 ||
caṇḍāgninā pacettāvadyāvaddvādaśayāmakam  |
svāṃgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya bhittvā kūpīṃ samāharet || 3,13.40 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ śilāsattvaṃ bālārkakiraṇopamam  || 3,13.41 ||
{haritāla:: sattva:: pātana}
bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa tālasya viṣaṃ pāradaṭaṃkaṇam  |
śvetābhrabaṃgayoścūrṇaṃ pratibhāgaṃ vimiśrayet || 3,13.42 ||
sarvaṃ snuhyarkapayasā mardayeddivasatrayam  |
śilāvadgrāhayetsattvaṃ tālakātsphaṭikopamam  || 3,13.43 ||
{haritāla:: sattva:: pātana}
snukkṣīrakaṭutumbyutthaistālaṃ bhāvyaṃ dvisaptadhā  || 3,13.44 ||
tilasarṣapaśigrūṇi lavaṇaṃ mitrapaṃcakam  |
ebhistulyaṃ pūrvatālaṃ dinamekaṃ vimardayet || 3,13.45 ||
chidramūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ bhūdhare sattvamāharet || 3,13.46 ||
{haritāla:: sattva:: pātana}
lākṣā rājī guḍaṃ śigruṣṭaṃkaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ tilāḥ  |
ebhistulyaṃ śuddhatālaṃ mardayedravidugdhakaiḥ  || 3,13.47 ||
dinaṃ vā vajriṇīdugdhaiḥ kuṣmāṇḍasya dravaistathā  |
tena kalkena liptāṃtaśchidramūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 3,13.48 ||
puṭādvā dhamanādgrāhyaṃ sattvaṃ pātālayantrake  || 3,13.49 ||
{haritāla:: sattva:: pātana}
tālakādaṣṭamāṃśena deyaṃ sūtaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇam  |
kuṣmāṇḍasya rasaiḥ snuhyāḥ kṣīrairmardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
tadgolaṃ chidramūṣāṃtargrāhyaṃ sattvaṃ ca pūrvavat || 3,13.50 ||
{tuttha:: sattva:: pātana}
lākṣābhayā ca bhūnāgaṃ gṛhadhūmaṃ jaṭākaṇā  |
niśāṭaṃkaṇamadhvājyānyebhistulyaṃ ca tutthakam  || 3,13.51 ||
sarvaṃ mardyamajākṣīrairandhamūṣāgataṃ dhamet |
sattvaṃ kiṃśukapuṣpābhaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,13.52 ||
{tuttha:: sattva:: pātana}
haridrāsūraṇāṅkollaṃ taṇḍulī gaṃdhakaṃ guḍam  |
madhvājyaṭaṃkaṇaṃ tulyaṃ tutthamebhiḥ samaṃ bhavet || 3,13.53 ||
stanyena mardayetsarvaṃ chidramūṣāṃ vilepayet |
ruddhvā pātālayaṃtreṇa dhmāte sattvaṃ vimuñcati  || 3,13.54 ||
{tuttha:: sattva:: pātana}
tutthasya ṭaṃkaṇaṃ pādaṃ cūrṇayenmadhusarpiṣā  |
tutthena miśritaṃ dhmātaṃ koṣṭhīyantre dṛḍhāgninā  |
dhāmite muñcate sattvaṃ kīratuṇḍasamaprabham  || 3,13.55 ||
{varanāgasattva}
sauvīraṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca mūṣāyāmandhayetsamam  |
haṭhāddhmāte bhavetsattvaṃ varanāgaṃ taducyate  || 3,13.56 ||
{rasaka:: sattva:: pātana}
kṣārāmlaṃ snehapittaiśca kramādbhāvyaṃ dinaṃ dinam  |
puṣpāṇāṃ raktapītānāṃ rasairbhāvyaṃ dinadvayam  || 3,13.57 ||
rasakaṃ cūrṇayetpaścādūrṇā lākṣā niśābhayā  |
ṭaṃkaṇaṃ śigrudhūmaṃ ca bhūnāgaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet || 3,13.58 ||
ebhiḥ samaṃ tu taccūrṇamajākṣīreṇa mardayet |
yāmametena kalkena lepyā vārtākamūṣikā  || 3,13.59 ||
śuṣkā cārdhamukhāṅgārairdhmāte sattvaṃ samāharet || 3,13.60 ||
{rasaka:: sattva:: pātana}
rasakasyaikabhāgaṃ tu triphalāmitrapaṃcakam  |
rajanīgṛhadhūmaṃ ca daśānāmekabhāgakam  || 3,13.61 ||
ajākṣīrairdinaṃ mardyamathavāmlena kenacit |
pūrvavadgrāhayetsattvaṃ rasakātkuṭilaprabham  || 3,13.62 ||
{vaikrānta:: sattva:: pātana}
vandhyācūrṇaṃ ca vaikrāṃtaṃ chāyāyā mardayetsamam  |
ajāmūtrairdinaikaṃ tu sattvaṃ rajatavadbhavet || 3,13.63 ||
{vaikrānta:: sattva:: pātana}
mokṣamoraṭapālāśakṣāraṃ gomūtragālitam  |
gṛhītamātape śuṣkaṃ vajrakandaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇam  || 3,13.64 ||
lākṣā śikhiśikhātulyaṃ vaikrāṃtaṃ sarvatulyakam  |
meṣaśṛṃgīdravairmardyametatsarvaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,13.65 ||
piṇḍitaṃ mūkamūṣāṃtaḥ kṛtvā dhāmyaṃ haṭhāgninā  |
tatraiva muñcate sattvaṃ vaikrāṃtaṃ rasabandhakam  || 3,13.66 ||
{vaikrānta:: sattva:: pātana}
vaikrāṃtānāṃ palaikaṃ tu karṣaikaṃ ṭaṃkaṇasya ca  |
ravikṣīrairdinaṃ bhāvyamatha śigrudravairdinam  || 3,13.67 ||
guṃjāpiṇyākavahnīnāṃ pratikarṣaṃ niyojayet |
etena gulikāṃ kṛtvā koṣṭhīyantre dhameddṛḍham  || 3,13.68 ||
śaṃkhakundendusaṃkāśaṃ sattvaṃ vaikrāṃtajaṃ bhavet || 3,13.69 ||
{vaikrānta:: sattva:: pātana}
vaikrāṃtaṃ vajrakaṃdaṃ ca samaṃ snukpayasā samam  |
mahiṣīnavanītaṃ ca sakṣaudraṃ mardayeddinam  |
pūrvavaddhamanātsattvamiṃdragopanibhaṃ bhavet || 3,13.70 ||
{gairika:: sattva:: pātana}
gairikaṃ raktavargeṇa pītavargeṇa bhāvitam  |
saptāhaṃ mardayedyāmaṃ mitrapaṃcakasaṃyutam  |
tadvaṭī koṣṭhikāyaṃtre sattvaṃ muñcati nirmalam  || 3,13.71 ||
{saurāṣṭrī:: sattva:: pātana}
sitā sitā ca saurāṣṭrī gopittairbhāvayettu tām  |
śatavāraṃ prayatnena mitrapaṃcakasaṃyutam  |
dhamanānmuñcate sattvaṃ krāmakaṃ koṣṭhiyaṃtrake  || 3,13.72 ||
{sasyaka:: sattva:: pātana}
sasyakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ bhāvyaṃ dinaṃ śaśakaraktakaiḥ  |
strīmūtrairvātha gomūtraistatpādāṃśāṃ niśāṃ kṣipet |
mardyaṃ karaṃjatailena yāmaikaṃ golakaṃ ca tat || 3,13.73 ||
aṃdhamūṣāgataṃ dhāmyaṃ ghaṭikārdhaṃ dṛḍhāgninā  |
iṃdragopakasaṃkāśaṃ sattvaṃ muñcati śobhanam  || 3,13.74 ||
{kāsīsa:: sattva:: pātana}
caturvidhaṃ tu kāsīsaṃ raktaṃ pītaṃ sitāsitam  |
mardayetpittavargeṇa tiktakośātakīdravaiḥ  || 3,13.75 ||
kāsamardadravaiścaiva mitrapaṃcakasaṃyutaiḥ  |
tadvaṭīḥ koṣṭhikāyaṃtre dhmāte sattvaṃ vimuñcati  || 3,13.76 ||
{rājāvarta:: sattva:: pātana}
rājāvartamayaḥ pātre pācayenmāhiṣairghṛtaiḥ  |
payobhiśca dinaṃ pacyānmitrapaṃcakasaṃyutam  || 3,13.77 ||
rajanyāḥ pañcagavyena piṇḍībaddhaṃ tu kārayet |
khadirāṃgārasaṃyogātkoṣṭhyāṃ sattvaṃ vimuñcati  || 3,13.78 ||
{srotoñjana:: parīkṣā}
valmīkaśikharākāraṃ bhaṅge nīlotpaladyuti  |
gharṣaṇe gairikacchāyaṃ srotoṃjanamidaṃ bhavet || 3,13.79 ||
{srotoñjana:: sattva:: pātana}
rājāvartakavatsattvaṃ grāhyaṃ srotoṃjanādapi  || 3,13.80 ||
{dvandvam. of vyomasattva andminerals}
vyomasattvasya cūrṇaṃ tu yatkiṃciddhātucūrṇakam  |
dvandvamelāpaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ taddvayaṃ samam  || 3,13.81 ||
dhmātavyamarivargeṇa kṣipte milati tatkṣaṇam  || 3,13.82 ||
{sattva:: melāpana}
viṣaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaguṃjāśca khuraṃ śṛṅgaṃ ca bheṣajam  |
maṇḍūkavasayā piṣṭvā mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 3,13.83 ||
tasyāṃ milati sattvāni cūrṇāni vividhāni ca  |
{dvandvamel.:: lepa for~}
trikṣāraṃ dhātakīpuṣpaṃ gugguluṃ ca samaṃ samam  |
strīstanyaiḥ peṣito lepo dvaṃdvamelāpane hitaḥ  || 3,13.84 ||
{sarvasattvamelāpana (2)}
varṣābhūḥ kadalīkaṃdaḥ kākamācī punarnavā  |
guṃjā narakapālaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇaṃ peṣayetsamam  |
anena pūrvavallepāddhemābhraṃ milati kṣaṇāt || 3,13.85 ||
{melāpana:: metals, sattva anormahārasas}
tāraṃ ca vyomasattvaṃ ca anenaiva tu melayet |
lāṅgalī camarīkeśānkuśakārpāsayoḥ phalam  || 3,13.86 ||
bhūlatāmiṃdragopaṃ ca pāṣāṇabhedikā samam  |
nārīstanyena saṃpeṣya mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 3,13.87 ||
sarvalohāni sattvāni tathā caiva mahārasāḥ  |
milanti nātra saṃdehastīvradhmānānalena tu  || 3,13.88 ||
{dvandvamelāpaka}
viṣaṃ chuchundarīmāṃsaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ samapeṣitam  |
mūṣālepamanenaiva kṛtvā tatra vinikṣipet |
yatkiṃciddvaṃdvayogaṃ tu dhamanena milatyalam  || 3,13.89 ||
{dvandvamelāpaka}
kaṅguṇītailasampiṣṭamapāmārgasya bhasmakam  |
tena praliptāyāṃ dvaṃdvaṃ kṣiptvā dhameddhaṭhāt |
milatyeva na saṃdehastattanmārakavāpanāt || 3,13.90 ||
{abhra:: productionof abhra-metal-compounds}
hemābhraṃ nāgatāpyābhraṃ śulbābhraṃ gaṃdhakena ca  |
sindhūtthahiṃgulābhyāṃ tu tīkṣṇābhraṃ dhamanāddṛḍham  || 3,13.91 ||
nāgābhraṃ śilayā yuktaṃ vaṅgābhraṃ tālakena ca  |
tārābhraṃ baṃgatālābhyāṃ tālavatsarvasattvakam  || 3,13.92 ||
{abhra:: vaṅgābhra}
milatyeva na saṃdehaḥ pūrvamūṣāgataṃ kṣaṇāt |
śvetābhrakasya sattvaṃ tu vaṅgacūrṇaṃ samaṃ samam  || 3,13.93 ||
kadalīkaṃdatoyena mardayeṭṭaṃkaṇaiḥ saha  |
aṃdhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ vaṅgābhraṃ milati kṣaṇāt || 3,13.94 ||
{abhra:: vaṅgābhra}
guṃjā narakapālaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇaṃ vanaśigrukam  |
strīstanyena samaṃ piṣṭvā mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 3,13.95 ||
baṃgaṃ śvetābhrasattvaṃ ca cūrṇitaṃ tatra nikṣipet |
ruddhvā dhmāte milatyeva tārakarmaṇi jārayet || 3,13.96 ||
{abhra:: vaṅgābhra}
abhrasattvaṃ vicūrṇyādau tatpādāṃśaṃ tu ṭaṃkaṇam  |
pāradaṃ ṭaṃkaṇāṃśaṃ ca kākamācīdravairdinam  || 3,13.97 ||
piṣṭvā tadgolakaṃ peṣyamabhrāṃśairvaṅgapatrakaiḥ  |
pūrvamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ vaṅgābhraṃ milati kṣaṇāt || 3,13.98 ||
{abhiṣeka (1)}
samyagāvartitaṃ nāgaṃ palaikaṃ kāṃjike kṣipet |
palānāṃ śatamātre tu śatavāraṃ drutaṃ drutam  || 3,13.99 ||
anena kāṃjikenaiva śatavāraṃ vibhāvayet |
yatkiṃciccāraṇāvastu tatastaṃ jārayedrase  |
abhiṣeko hyayaṃ khyātaḥ kathyate tu matāntaram  || 3,13.100 ||
{abhiṣeka (2)}
trikṣāraṃ paṃcalavaṇaṃ kāṅkṣīkāsīsagaṃdhakam  |
māhiṣaṃ kāṃjikairyuktaṃ tāmrapātre dinatrayam  || 3,13.101 ||
sthitaṃ gharme punastasmin drutaṃ nāgaṃ vinikṣipet |
tārakarmaṇi baṃgaṃ vā śatavārāṇi secayet || 3,13.102 ||
taddravaṃ tāmrapātrasthamabhiṣekaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
anena cāraṇāvastu śatavārāṇi bhāvayet || 3,13.103 ||
{sāraṇāyāṃ saṃketaḥ}
dvaṃdvitaṃ vyomasattvaṃ ca bījāni vividhāni ca  |
dvaṃdvitaṃ vajrabījaṃ ca bhāvitaṃ sārayetsadā  || 3,13.104 ||
svarṇādi lohamakhilaṃ kṛtaśuddhacūrṇaṃ yojyaṃ pṛthaggaganasattvavare samāṃśam  |
taddvaṃdvitaṃ carati sūtavaro'bhiṣiktaṃ vipro yathā madhurapāyasamājyayuktam  || 3,13.105 ||


 3, 14
sūtena sattvaracitena ca jāritena pakvākhyabījaguṇasaṃkhyasusāritena  |
vijñāya yastu matimān sa tu vārtikendraścaṃdrārkavedhavidhinā kanakaṃ karoti  || 3,14.1 ||
{mercury:: cāraṇa}
svarṇe nāgaṃ samāvartya māṣamātraṃ tu gharṣayet |
taptakhalve tatastasminpalamekaṃ rasaṃ kṣipet || 3,14.2 ||
siddhamūlīdravaṃ dattvā mardayetkāṃjikairdinam  |
gharme vā taptakhalve vā tato grāsaṃ tu dāpayet || 3,14.3 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ pūrvaṃ dvaṃdvaṃ sattvaṃ vibhāvitam  |
dattvā mardyaṃ dinaikaṃ tu cāraṇāyaṃtrake kṣipet || 3,14.4 ||
sajambīrairdinaṃ gharme dhāritaṃ carati dhruvam  || 3,14.5 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa}
jāraṇaṃ trikṣāraṃ paṃcalavaṇamamlavarge snuhīpayaḥ  |
gomūtrairlolayetsarvaṃ tena vastraṃ ghanaṃ lipet || 3,14.6 ||
tanmadhye jāritaṃ sūtaṃ baddhvā bhūrjena veṣṭayet |
siddhamūlyamlasaṃyuktaṃ dolāyaṃtre tryahaṃ pacet || 3,14.7 ||
uddhṛtyoṣṇāranālena kṣālayellohapātrake  |
vastrapūtaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā soṣṇapātre vimardayet || 3,14.8 ||
hastenaiva bhavedyāvatsudhautaṃ pāradaṃ punaḥ  |
caturguṇena vastreṇa kṣālayennirmalo bhavet || 3,14.9 ||
ajīrṇe ca punarmardyamamlaṃ dattvā dināvadhi  |
dolāyāṃ svedayettadvadbhavejjīrṇaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,14.10 ||
{mercury:: preparation forgrāsana}
iṣṭikā guḍadagdhorṇā gṛhadhūmaṃ ca sarjikā  |
saiṃdhavena yutaṃ sarvaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ rasasya tu  || 3,14.11 ||
dattvā tato'mlavargeṇa gharme mardyaṃ dināvadhi  |
iṣṭikādamlavargeṇa dolāyaṃtre dinaṃ pacet |
jīrṇe jīrṇe tvidaṃ kuryādgrāsagrāhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,14.12 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of bījas}
dvātriṃśāṃśaṃ tato grāsaṃ dattvā cāryaṃ ca jārayet |
pūrvavatsvedanāntaṃ ca kṛtvā grāsaṃ tṛtīyakam  || 3,14.13 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ pradātavyaṃ tajjīrṇe cāṣṭamāṃśakam  |
jārayetpūrvayogena hyevaṃ grāsacatuṣṭayam  || 3,14.14 ||
tataḥ kacchapayantreṇa jārayettannigadyate  |
ūrdhvādhaścāṣṭamāṃśena grāse grāse biḍaṃ kṣipet || 3,14.15 ||
caturthāṃśaṃ tato grāsaṃ grāsaṃ deyaṃ samaṃ punaḥ  |
jīrṇe jīrṇe samaṃ deyamevaṃ jāryaṃ ca ṣaḍguṇam  || 3,14.16 ||
rāgāṇāṃ grahaṇārthaṃ ca grāse grāse tu pūrvavat |
ityevaṃ dvaṃdvayogānāṃ sattvānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  || 3,14.17 ||
svarṇādisarvalohānāṃ bījānāṃ jāraṇāhitam  |
kartavyaṃ vakṣyate tatra mātrāyuktiśca pūrvavat || 3,14.18 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of dvandvas}
abhāve vyomasattvasya kāntapāṣāṇasattvakam  |
tīkṣṇapāṣāṇasattvaṃ vā dvaṃdvitaṃ vyomasattvavat || 3,14.19 ||
jārayetpūrvayogena sarveṣāṃ syātphalaṃ samam  |
ityevaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ dvaṃdvaṃ yatkiṃcijjārayedrase  || 3,14.20 ||
{siddhabīja:: preparation}
jāritaṃ siddhabījena sārayettannigadyate  |
tāpyasattvaṃ ghoṣatāmraṃ śuddhahema samaṃ samam  || 3,14.21 ||
āvartya dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāmandhitaṃ punaḥ  |
samuddhṛtya punardhāmyaṃ mūṣāyāṃ prakaṭaṃ dhamet || 3,14.22 ||
mākṣikāddhautasattvaṃ ca stokaṃ stokaṃ vinikṣipet |
svarṇaśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatpācyaṃ ca tatpunaḥ  || 3,14.23 ||
pūrvavacca dhamettāvadyāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣitam  |
tāpyasattvena tāmreṇa dvaṃdvamevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,14.24 ||
ṣaḍvāraṃ dhamanenaiva grāhyaṃ svarṇāvaśeṣitam  |
siddhabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ dāḍimīpuṣpavadbhavet || 3,14.25 ||
anena siddhabījena pūrvavatsāraṇātrayam  |
kṛtvātha jārayettadvajjīrṇe baddhvā mukhaṃ tathā  || 3,14.26 ||
bandhanaṃ śodhanaṃ caiva krāmaṇaṃ caiva pūrvavat |
caṃdrārke drāvite yojyaṃ sahasrāṃśena kāṃcanam  || 3,14.27 ||
{mercury:: jāraṇa of sulfur}
athātaḥ śuddhasūtasya jāraṇe pūrvabhāvitam  |
atha śuddhasya sattvasya jārayetpūrvabhāṣitam  |

mukhanālānvitā ūrdhvavaktrā syāddvādaśāṅgulā  |
dṛḍhā lohamayī kuryādanayā sadṛśī parā  || 3,14.29 ||
ekasyāṃ nikṣipetsūtamanyasyāṃ gaṃdhakaṃ samam  |
ekasyā mukhamadhye tu hyaparasyā mukhaṃ kṣipet || 3,14.30 ||
liptvā mṛllavaṇaiḥ saṃdhiṃ gaṃdhakādhaḥ puṭaṃ laghu  |
rasasyādho jalaṃ sthāpyaṃ gaṃdhadhūmaṃ pibatyalam  || 3,14.31 ||
jīrṇe gaṃdhe samudghāṭya tulyaṃ gaṃdhaṃ ca dāpayet |
ityevaṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ gaṃdhaṃ jāryaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,14.32 ||
jāritaḥ sūtarājo'yaṃ vāsanāmukhito bhavet || 3,14.33 ||
{mercury:: vāsanāmukhita:: ?}
vyomasattvaṃ tāpyasattvaṃ śulbaṃ śuddhaṃ samaṃ samam  |
āvartya dvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāmatha cūrṇayet || 3,14.34 ||
bhāvayedabhiṣekeṇa pūrvavatśatavārakam  |
pūrvavaccārayedetadvāsanāmukhite rase  || 3,14.35 ||
tadvajjāryaṃ prayatnena yāvadbhavati ṣaḍguṇam  |
tatsūte sāritaṃ jāryaṃ siddhabījaṃ tu pūrvavat || 3,14.36 ||
mukhaṃ ca baṃdhanaṃ kṛtvā vedhāyāntaṃ pradāpayet |
krāmaṇena samāyuktaṃ caṃdrārkaṃ kāṃcanaṃ bhavet |
sahasrāṃśena tatsatyaṃ raso'yaṃ kāmarūpakaḥ  || 3,14.37 ||

tiktakośātakīdrāvaṃ lāṃgalīdrāvasaṃyutam  |

pādāṃśaṃ pakvabījaṃ ca cārayitvātha jārayet |

jārayecca punaḥ sūte kacchapākhye viḍānvite  |

unmattamunipatrāṇi rajanī kākamācikā  |

saptāhaṃ taptakhalve tu kacchape tu dinaṃ pacet |

taṃ ruddhvā vajramūṣāyāṃ tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  |

kukkuṭāṇḍanibhaṃ baddhaṃ jāyate cūrṇayetpunaḥ  |

pacedgajapuṭe'pyevaṃ deyaṃ puṭacatuṣṭayam  |

sahasrāṃśena tenaiva taṃ śulbaṃ tu vedhayet |

 3, 16
yāsāṃ chedena raktaṃ pravahati satataṃ prāyaśo raktabhūmau  |
saṃgrāhyā bhūlatāstā viṣaharadhanadaṃ pātayetteṣu sattvam  |
tadyuktyā pāradendre carati yadi samaṃ sāraṇākarmayogairbaddho'yaṃ koṭivedhī samaravirajate yojayedbhāskare vā  || 3,16.1 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (1)}
bhūlatā kāṃtapāṣāṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā samaṃ samam  |
tatsatvamabhravadgrāhyaṃ tatratyairauṣadhaiḥ saha  || 3,16.2 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (2)}
sauvīraṃ kāṃtapāṣāṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ pāṣāṇacūrṇakam  |
eteṣāṃ tulyabhūnāgacūrṇamekatra kalpayet || 3,16.3 ||
abhravadgrāhayetsatvaṃ tatratyairauṣadhairdhaman  || 3,16.4 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (3)}
kāṃtapāṣāṇasauvīracūrṇaṃ syādbhūlatāsamam  |
abhravadgrāhayetsatvaṃ rasarājasya bandhakam  || 3,16.5 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (4)}
sauvīrakāṃtatīkṣṇānāṃ cūrṇaṃ bhūnāgamṛtsamam  |
dhāryaṃ bhāṇḍe kṣipettasmin sajīvā bhūlatā punaḥ  || 3,16.6 ||
udakaiḥ secayennityaṃ yāvattadbhakṣayanti vai  |
tatsattvamabhravatpiṇḍaṃ baddhvā satvaṃ samāharet || 3,16.7 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (5)}
kāṃtapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ tu bhūlatācūrṇasaṃyutam  |
ajāmūtraistrisaptāhaṃ bhāvayedātape khare  |
tatpiṇḍaṃ dhārayetkoṣṭhyāṃ satvaṃ grāhyaṃ suśobhanam  || 3,16.8 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (6)}
gomūtraṃ rajanī rājī lavaṇaṃ kalkayetsamam  |
tena siñcyāttu bhūnāgaṃ khare gharme dravatyalam  || 3,16.9 ||
kaṃkuṣṭhaṃ taddravaṃ tulyaṃ kṛtvā satvaṃ samāharet |
vyomavatkramayogena rasabandhakaraṃ bhavet || 3,16.10 ||
{bhūnāgasattva (7)}
gaṃdhakaṃ rasakaṃ cūrṇya bhūlatācūrṇatulyakam  |
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ dhmātaṃ satvaṃ bhavati śobhanam  || 3,16.11 ||
etatsatvaṃ vicūrṇyātha pūrvavaccābhiṣekitam  || 3,16.12 ||
{bhūnāgataila}
dinaṃ bhūnāgasaṃtulyaṃ mardyaṃ sauvīramañjanam  |
pañcamāhiṣasaṃmiśraṃ kṛtvātha vaṭakīkṛtam  |
tasmātpātālayaṃtreṇa tailaṃ grāhyaṃ puṭena vai  || 3,16.13 ||
{bhūnāgataila}
bhūlatāstu gavāṃ mūtraiḥ kṣālayettābhirāharet |
tailaṃ pātālayaṃtreṇa tattailaṃ jāraṇe hitam  || 3,16.14 ||
{guhyasūta}
taptakhalve śuddhasūtaṃ jīvadbhūnāgasaṃyutam  |
tridinaṃ mardayedgāḍhaṃ tatsamastaṃ samuddhṛtam  || 3,16.15 ||
bhūnāgacūrṇayuktāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ saṃniveśayet |
tadūrdhvaṃ bhūlatācūrṇaṃ dattvā ruddhvātha śoṣayet || 3,16.16 ||
gartāntargomayaṃ sārdhaṃ kṣiptvā mūṣāṃ niveśayet |
pādamātraṃ tu tāṃ garbhe karīṣaṃ tuṣavahninā  || 3,16.17 ||
puṭe pacyāddinaikaṃ tu samuddhṛtyātha dāpayet |
ūrdhvādho bhūlatācūrṇaṃ dattvā tadvatpuṭe pacet || 3,16.18 ||
māsamātramidaṃ kuryādbhavedagnisaho rasaḥ  |
jāyate mūrtibaddhasya rākṣaso vaḍavāmukham  || 3,16.19 ||
grasate sarvalohāni satvāni vividhāni ca  |
vajrādisarvalohāni dattāni ca mṛtāni ca  |
guhyasūtamidaṃ khyātaṃ vakṣyate cāsya jāraṇam  || 3,16.20 ||
{candrārka => gold}
asyaiva ṣoḍaśāṃśena dattvā bhūnāgasattvakam  |

evaṃ satvaṃ samaṃ jāryaṃ pūrvavatkacchapena vā  |
garbhadrāveṇa bījaṃ ca pūrvavatṣaḍguṇaṃ śanaiḥ  || 3,16.24 ||
jārayeddrāvitaṃ garte mūṣāyantre tu pūrvavat |

caṃdrārkaṃ vā drutaṃ tāmraṃ nāgaṃ vā kāṃcanaṃ bhavet |
