mārayetpakvabījāni tridhā taṃ jārayetkramāt |
pūrvavadbaṃdhanatvaṃ ca kṛtvā taṃ krāmaṇena vai  || 3,16.53 ||
yojayellakṣabhāgena caṃdrārke drāvite tu tam  |
svarṇaṃ bhavati rūpāḍhyaṃ śaṃbhunā parikīrtitam  || 3,16.54 ||
{mercury:: koṭivedhin}
pītavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāṃtaṃ śuddhaṃ bhāvyaṃ dināvadhi  |
vyāghrīdravāśvamūtrābhyāṃ vyāghrīkaṃdagataṃ pacet || 3,16.55 ||
dolāyaṃtre divārātraṃ samuddhṛtyātha cūrṇayet |
etaccūrṇaṃ palaikaṃ tu sūte daśaguṇe kṣipet || 3,16.56 ||
vyāghrīkaṃdadravaiścāśvamūtrairmardyaṃ dināvadhi  |
anena lepayetsvarṇapatraṃ śatapalaṃ punaḥ  || 3,16.57 ||
vajramūṣāgataṃ ruddhvā tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  |
svedayedvā divārātrau kārīṣāgnāvathoddharet || 3,16.58 ||
vyāghrīkaṃdāśvamūtrābhyāṃ mardyaṃ tadvatpuṭe pacet |
evaṃ śatapuṭaiḥ pakvamabhiṣiktaṃ ca kārayet || 3,16.59 ||
samukhe rasarājendre cāryametacca jārayet |
vyomasatvakrameṇaiva yāvatśataguṇaṃ śanaiḥ  || 3,16.60 ||
mūṣāyantre'thavā jāryaṃ yathā pūrvaṃ krameṇa vai  |
tatrasthasya rasendrasya garbhadrāvaṇabījakam  || 3,16.61 ||
pūrvavaddrāvitaṃ garte kramājjāryaṃ caturguṇam  |
tatrasthaṃ pakvabījena jārayetsaptaśṛṅkhalaiḥ  || 3,16.62 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,16.63 ||
{copper=> silver}
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāṃtaṃ vyāghrīkaṃdodare kṣipet |
aśvamūtrairdinaṃ svedyaṃ tadbhāgaikaṃ vicūrṇayet || 3,16.64 ||
śuddhasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ taptakhalve dināvadhi  |
vyāghrīkaṃdadravaiścāśvamūtraiścaiva tu mardayet || 3,16.65 ||
tāraṃ dattvā ṣaḍaṃśena punastadvacca mardayet |
sarvatulyaṃ punaḥ sūtaṃ dattvā tatraiva mardayet || 3,16.66 ||
jātaṃ golaṃ samuddhṛtya nigalena tu lepayet |
vajramūṣāgataṃ ruddhvā tridinaṃ tuṣavahninā  || 3,16.67 ||
svedayedvā divārātrau nirvāte kariṣāgninā  |
uddhṛtya mardayeccātha bījairdivyauṣadhīdravaiḥ  || 3,16.68 ||
tridinaṃ taptakhalve tu hayamūtreṇa saṃyutam  |
pūrvavallepitaṃ ruddhvā tadvatpācyaṃ puṭena vai  || 3,16.69 ||
anena kramayogena saptadhā pācayetpuṭaiḥ  |
anena vedhayettāmraṃ drāvitaṃ śatamāṃśataḥ  |
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ dattvā tāraṃ bhavati śobhanam  || 3,16.70 ||
{copper=> silver}
śvetavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāṃtaṃ suśuddhaṃ pūrvavatkramāt |
raktavaikrāṃtayogena tāraṃ tenaiva mārayet || 3,16.71 ||
tattāraṃ jārayetsūte tadvacchataguṇaiḥ śanaiḥ  |
tadvanmāryaṃ puṭenaiva bhavedayutavedhakaḥ  || 3,16.72 ||
tadvatvai tārabījena sāritaṃ jārayetkramāt |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā lakṣavedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,16.73 ||
drute tāmre pradātavyaṃ tattāraṃ jāyate śubham  || 3,16.74 ||
{silver=> gold}
tāmravarṇaṃ tu vaikrāṃtaṃ śuddhahiṃgulasaṃyutam  |
mardayedamlavargeṇa taptakhalve dinatrayam  || 3,16.75 ||
anena svarṇapatrāṇi praliptāni puṭe pacet |
samuddhṛtya punarmardyamamlavargeṇa saṃyutam  || 3,16.76 ||
pacetsaptapuṭairevaṃ tadbhasma palamātrakam  |
śuddhasūtapalaikaṃ tu divyauṣadhīdravaistryaham  || 3,16.77 ||
marditaṃ kārayedgolaṃ nirmalena ca lepayet |
ruddhvā dinatrayaṃ svedyaṃ karīṣatuṣavahninā  || 3,16.78 ||
tato divyauṣadhīdrāvairmarditaṃ nigalena ca  |
ruddhvā liptvā dhamedgāḍhaṃ baṃdhamāyāti niścitam  || 3,16.79 ||
anena śatamāṃśena tāraṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,16.80 ||
{silver=> gold}
raktavaikrāṃtasatvaṃ tu saha hemnā tu cūrṇayet |
dvaṃdvamelāpaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet || 3,16.81 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ vābhiṣiktaṃ tu samukhe jārayedrase  |
abhrasatvaprakāreṇa jārayetpāradaṃ samam  || 3,16.82 ||
tadrasaṃ pakvabījena sārayetpūrvavattridhā  |
tadvacca jāraṇā kāryā mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet || 3,16.83 ||
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva tāraṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,16.84 ||
{silver, copper, lead=> gold}
raktavaikrāṃtasatvaṃ ca śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ samam  |
mardyaṃ divyauṣadhīdrāvaistaptakhalve dinatrayam  || 3,16.85 ||
tato nigalaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ puṭet |
karīṣāgnau divārātrau tridinaṃ ca tuṣāgninā  || 3,16.86 ||
samuddhṛtya punarlepyaṃ tadgolaṃ nigalena ca  |
mūṣānte lavaṇaṃ dattvā ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ viśoṣayet || 3,16.87 ||
punaśca lavaṇaṃ dattvā ruddhvā saṃdhiṃ viśoṣayet |
lepayetśaṃkhacūrṇena tāṃ mūṣāṃ sarvato'ṅgulam  |
koṣṭhīyantre haṭhāddhāmyaṃ baddho bhavati tadrasaḥ  || 3,16.88 ||
tāre tāmre bhujaṃge vā sahasrāṃśena vedhayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharaṇamuttamam  || 3,16.89 ||
{capalābhedāḥ}
raktā pītā sitā kṛṣṇā capalā tu caturvidhā  |
vaikrāṃtasya prakāreṇa śodhyāḥ syū rasabandhakāḥ  || 3,16.90 ||
{capalā:: vedha with~}
lāṃgalī karavīrāgnigirikarṇī ca ṭaṃkaṇam  |
sauvīrāñjanatulyāṃśaṃ nārīstanyena peṣayet || 3,16.91 ||
vajramūṣodaraṃ tena lepayetsarvato'ṅgulam  |
capalā raktapītā vā bhāgamekaṃ vicūrṇayet || 3,16.92 ||
suvarṇabhāgāścatvāro dvibhāgaṃ śuddhapāradam  |
divyauṣadhīdravairmardyaṃ sarvametaddinatrayam  || 3,16.93 ||
pūrvamūṣāṃ nirudhyātha chāyāśuṣkaṃ dhameddhaṭhāt |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate khoṭaṃ śatāṃśena tu tena vai  || 3,16.94 ||
suśuddhaṃ vedhayennāgaṃ bhavedguṃjānibhaṃ tu tat |
nāgenānena śulbaṃ tu śatāṃśenaiva vedhayet || 3,16.95 ||
aruṇābhaṃ bhavecchulbaṃ tena śulbena vedhayet |
śuddhatāraṃ śatāṃśena tattāraṃ kāṃcanaṃ bhavet || 3,16.96 ||
tadvacca jāraṇā kāryā mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva tāraṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,16.97 ||
suśuddhaṃ vedhayennāgaṃ bhavedguṃjānibhaṃ ca tat |
nāgenānena śulbaṃ tu śatāṃśenaiva vedhayet || 3,16.98 ||
{lead=> copper=> silver=> gold}
ṣaḍbhāgaṃ capalācūrṇaṃ tāraṃ syātsaptabhāgakam  |
bhāgāṣṭakaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca navabhāgaṃ ca pāradam  || 3,16.99 ||
sarvaṃ divyauṣadhīdrāvairmardayeddivasatrayam  |
tadgolaṃ nigalenaiva sarvato lepayedghanam  || 3,16.100 ||
lāṃgalī karavīrāgnigṛdhraviṣṭhā samaṃ samam  |
peṣayenmātuliṃgāmlaistena mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 3,16.101 ||
tadruddhvā pūrvavadgolaṃ dhametkhoṭaṃ bhavettu tat |
tenaiva tu śatāṃśena nāge vedhaṃ pradāpayet || 3,16.102 ||
tena nāgena śulbaṃ ca śatāṃśenaiva vedhayet |
tāmreṇa vedhayettāraṃ pūrvavatkāṃcanaṃ bhavet || 3,16.103 ||
{silver=> gold}
palaikaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca kācapātre vinikṣipet |
pūrvavajjāritaṃ gaṃdhaṃ kṣipettasminpalatrayam  || 3,16.104 ||
jaṃbīrāṇāṃ dravaṃ dattvā gaṃdhatulyaṃ śanaiḥ pacet |
vālukābhāṇḍamadhye tu yāvajjīryati gaṃdhakam  || 3,16.105 ||
pūrvasaṃskṛtadhānyābhraṃ palamekaṃ ca tatra vai  |
kṣiptvā jaṃbīranīraṃ ca biḍaṃ dattvātha pācayet || 3,16.106 ||
jīrṇe yāvadbhavettattu hyamlaṃ tāvatkṣipanpacet |
tasminsvarṇaṃ palaikaṃ tu cūrṇitaṃ cābhiṣekitam  || 3,16.107 ||
kṣipettasminviḍaṃ cātha deyaṃ jaṃbīrasaṃyutam  |
pacejjīrṇaṃ bhavedyāvattatraiva mṛduvahninā  || 3,16.108 ||
biḍamamlaṃ kṣipanneva jīrṇe coddhṛtya mardayet |
tridinaṃ taptakhalve tu divyauṣadhīdravairyutam  || 3,16.109 ||
mūlamīśvaraliṅgyutthaṃ śigrumūlaṃ ca peṣayet |
vajramūṣāmanenaiva liptvā pūrvarasaṃ kṣipet || 3,16.110 ||
ruddhvā svedyaṃ karīṣāgnāvahorātrātsamuddharet |
pūrvavanmarditaṃ ruddhvā dhamedbaddho bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,16.111 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśena datte tāramanena vai  |
vedhayejjārayeddivyaṃ kāṃcanaṃ siddhasaṃmatam  || 3,16.112 ||
{silver=> gold}
śataniṣkaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ tu gaṃdhakam  |
kṣaṇaṃ kanyādravairmardyaṃ pātanāyaṃtragaṃ pacet || 3,16.113 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ samādāya gaṃdhakaṃ daśaniṣkakam  |
dattvā mardyaṃ punastadvadyaṃtre pacyātsamuddharet || 3,16.114 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam  |
gaurīyantre tu tatsūtaṃ kṣiptvā deyaṃ tu gaṃdhakam  || 3,16.115 ||
bhāvitaṃ pūrvayogena viṃśatyaṃśena cūrṇitam  |
ruddhvā laghupuṭe pacyājjīrṇe gaṃdhaṃ pradāpayet || 3,16.116 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punarjāryaṃ yathāśakti krameṇa vai  |
jīrṇe śataguṇe gandhe śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,16.117 ||
sahasraguṇite jīrṇe sahasrāṃśena vedhayet |
lakṣajīrṇe lakṣavedhī koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,16.118 ||
jīrṇe koṭiguṇe gandhe'pyevaṃ syāduttarottaram  |
sārayetpakvabījena pūrvavajjārayetkramāt || 3,16.119 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā tāre vedhaṃ pradāpayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharaṇabhūṣaṇam  || 3,16.120 ||
bhūsattvakaiḥ paramaguhyatamaiḥ sasūtairvaikrāntakaiḥ sacapalai rasagaṃdhakaiśca  |
dṛṣṭvānubhūya sakalaṃ sukhasādhyayogaiḥ samyaksuvarṇakaraṇaṃ gaditaṃ sudhīnām  || 3,16.121 ||


 3, 17
vajrābhrasatvavarahāṭakalohajālaṃ kuryāddrutaṃ dravabhavaṃ kila baṃdhayogyam  |
nānāvidhaiḥ sugamasaṃskṛtayogarājaistadvakṣyate paramasiddhikaraṃ narāṇām  || 3,17.1 ||
{abhrakadruti (1)}
śuddhakṛṣṇābhrapatrāṇi pīlutailena lepayet |
gharme śoṣyāṇi saptāhaṃ liptvā liptvā punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,17.2 ||
tridinaṃ cāmlavargeṇa tadvacchoṣyāṇi cātha vai  |
snuhyarkārjunavajrīṇāṃ kaṭutuṃbyā samāharet || 3,17.3 ||
kṣāraṃ kṣāratrayaṃ caitadaṣṭakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ samam  |
vajrakaṃdaṃ kṣīrakandaṃ bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā  || 3,17.4 ||
vanavṛntāka eteṣāṃ dravairbhāvyaṃ dinatrayam  |
anena kṣārakalkena pūrvapatrāṇi lepayet || 3,17.5 ||
ātape kāṃsyapātre tu sthāpyaṃ lepyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
evaṃ dinatrayaṃ kuryāddrutirbhavati nirmalā  || 3,17.6 ||
{abhrakadruti (2)}
amlavargeṇa patrāṇi kṣipedgharme dinatrayam  |
tathānyānyabhrapatrāṇi kṣālayetkṣīrakaṃdakaiḥ  || 3,17.7 ||
kṣārairyāvadbhavetkalkastatkalkaiḥ pūrvapatrakam  |
liptvā liptvā kṣipedgharme kāṃsyapātre viśoṣayet || 3,17.8 ||
saptāhānnātra saṃdeho rasarūpā drutirbhavet || 3,17.9 ||
{abhrakadruti (3)}
kākāṇḍāphalacūrṇaṃ tu mitrapaṃcakasaṃyutam  |
etattulyaṃ ca dhānyābhramamlairmardyaṃ dināvadhi  |
aṃdhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ drutirbhavati nirmalā  || 3,17.10 ||
{abhrakadruti (4)}
dhānyābhrakaṃ sagomāṃsamabhrapādaṃ ca saiṃdhavam  |
snuhyarkapayasā drāvairmunibhirmardayettryaham  || 3,17.11 ||
tadgolaṃ kadalīkaṃde kṣiptvā bāhye mṛdā lipet |
karīṣāgnau tryahaṃ pacyāddrutirbhavati nirmalā  || 3,17.12 ||
{abhrakadruti (5)}
agastyapatraniryāsairmardyaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ dinam  |
tadgolaṃ nikṣipetkaṃde sūraṇotthe nirudhya ca  || 3,17.13 ||
tatkaṃdaṃ nikhanedgoṣṭhabhūmau māsātsamuddharet |
kaṃdodarāddrutirgrāhyā sūtatulyā tu nirmalā  || 3,17.14 ||
{abhrakadruti (6)}
dhānyābhrakaṃ dinaṃ mardyamajamāryā dravairdinam  |
sthāpayenmṛṇmaye pātre tryahādgharme bhavatyalam  || 3,17.15 ||
{abhrakadruti (7)}
saptāhaṃ munitoyena dhānyābhraṃ saiṃdhavaṃ śilā  |
mardayedbhāvayedgharme tato dārvī suvarcalam  || 3,17.16 ||
maricamabhrapādāṃśaṃ mūrvāpatrarasairdinam  |
mardayedvajravallyutthairdravairbhāvyaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,17.17 ||
śarāvasaṃpuṭe taṃ tu ruddhvā dhmāte drutirbhavet || 3,17.18 ||
{abhrakadruti (8)}
dhānyābhrakaṃ prayoktavyaṃ kākinībījatulyakam  |
snuhīkṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ gharme tāpyaṃ drutirbhavet || 3,17.19 ||
{abhrakadruti (9)}
vajravallīdravairmardyaṃ dhānyābhraṃ sasuvarcalam  |
tulyaṃ tridinaparyantaṃ tatastaṃ śarāvasaṃpuṭe  || 3,17.20 ||
ruddhvā dhmāte dravatyeva rasarūpaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
ityevaṃ rasarūpaṃ ca jāyate naiva saṃśayaḥ  || 3,17.21 ||
{abhrakadruti (10)}
uduṃbarodbhavaiḥ kṣīrairabhrapatrāṇi pācayet |
sthālyāṃ vā pācayedetān bhavanti navanītavat || 3,17.22 ||
tatastaṃ vaṭakaṃ kṛtvā chidramūṣāṃ nirudhya ca  |
yāmatrayaṃ dhamedgāḍhamadhobhāṇḍe drutiḥ patet || 3,17.23 ||
{abhrakadruti (11)}
kākoduṃbarijaiḥ kṣīrairmardyaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ dinam  |
vanamūṣakabījāni tvagvarjyānyabhrakaiḥ samam  || 3,17.24 ||
mardayitvārdhayāmaṃ taṃ drāvyaṃ pātālayaṃtrakaiḥ  |
ahorātraṃ puṭaṃ deyaṃ drutirbhavati nirmalā  || 3,17.25 ||
{abhrakadruti (12)}
kapitiṃdujātaphalaiḥ samaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ dṛḍham  |
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu kācakūpyāṃ niveśayet || 3,17.26 ||
narakeśairmukhaṃ ruddhvā kūpikāṃ lepayenmṛdā  |
puṭetpātālayaṃtreṇa dinānte drutimāpnuyāt || 3,17.27 ||
{abhrakadruti (13)}
dhānyābhrakasamāṃśena cūrṇaṃ guṃjāphalasya tu  |
snuhīkṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ bhāvitaṃ dhamanādbhavet || 3,17.28 ||
{abhrakadruti (14)}
raktotpalasya nīlotthadravairmardyaṃ dinatrayam  |
dhānyābhrakaṃ dhamedruddhvā vajramūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 3,17.29 ||
vegīphalasya cūrṇena tulyaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ tryaham  |
bhāvyaṃ gharme snuhīkṣīrairdhmātaṃ saṃpuṭagaṃ dravet || 3,17.30 ||
{abhrakadruti (15)}
athavā chāgamūtreṇa bhāvayetkapitiṃdujam  |
phalacūrṇaṃ tu tacchuṣkaṃ drute satve pravāpayet || 3,17.31 ||
dvitrivāraprayogeṇa drutirbhavati nirmalā  || 3,17.32 ||
{abhrakasattvadruti (2)}
narakeśodbhavaistailaiḥ secayedabhrasattvakam  |
tadgolaṃ gomayairliptvā vajramūṣāntare kṣipet |
haṭhāddhmāte dravatyeva tiṣṭhate rasarājavat || 3,17.33 ||
{abhrakasattvadruti (3)}
bhāvayennaramūtreṇa kṣīrakaṃdasya cūrṇakam  |
daśavāraṃ prayatnena śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,17.34 ||
tenāvāpaṃ drute satve dattvā dattvā ca saṃdhamet |
yāvattaddravatāṃ yāti tāvaddeyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
lohaṃ ca dravate tena haṭhāddhmāte na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,17.35 ||
{abhrakasattvadruti (4)}
paṃcāṃgaṃ devadālyutthaṃ cūrṇaṃ bhāvyaṃ ca taddravaiḥ  |
śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punarbhāvyaṃ śatavāraṃ prayatnataḥ  || 3,17.36 ||
taccūrṇaṃ daśamāṃśena drute satve pratāpayet |
tatpunarjāyate baddho vāpo deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,17.37 ||
{abhrakasattvadruti (5)}
kṣīrakaṃdadravairbhāvyaṃ śatadhā kṣīrakaṃdakam  |
tadvāpena dravetsattvaṃ lohāni sakalāni ca  || 3,17.38 ||
{abhrakasattvadruti (6)}
sarvaṃ dhānyāmlasaṃdhānairbhāvyamabhrakasatvakam  |
niculakṣārasaṃyuktaṃ dhmātaṃ tiṣṭhati sūtavat || 3,17.39 ||
{suvarṇadruti (1)}
iṃdragopakacūrṇaṃ tu devadālīphaladravaiḥ  |
bhāvitaṃ caikaviṃśāhāddrute hemni pravāpayet || 3,17.40 ||
kiṃcitkiṃcitsamaṃ yāvattāvattiṣṭhati sūtavat || 3,17.41 ||
{suvarṇaraupyadruti (2)}
śatadhā naramūtreṇa bhāvayeddevadālikām  |
taccūrṇaṃ vāpamātreṇa drutiḥ syātsvarṇatārayoḥ  || 3,17.42 ||
{tīkṣṇalohadruti (1)}
suradālībhavaṃ bhasma naramūtreṇa bhāvitam  |
trisaptavāraṃ taṃ kṣāraṃ vāpe tīkṣṇadrutirbhavet || 3,17.43 ||
{tīkṣṇalohadruti (2)}
meṣaśṛṃgī sakūrmāsthiśilājatuni vāpayet |
sāraṃ drutirbhavetsatyamāvartyādau pradāpayet || 3,17.44 ||
{sarvadhātudruti (1)}
gaṃdhakaṃ kāṃtapāṣāṇaṃ cūrṇayitvā samaṃ samam  |
drute lohe pratīvāpo deyo lohāṣṭakaṃ dravet || 3,17.45 ||
{sarvadhātudruti (2)}
devadālyā dravairbhāvyaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ dinasaptakam  |
tena pravāpamātreṇa lohaṃ tiṣṭhati sūtavat || 3,17.46 ||
{suvarṇadruti (3)}
atisthūlasya bhekasya nivāryāntrāṇi nikṣipet |
udare ṭaṃkaṇaṃ pūrṇaṃ tadrakṣedbhāṃḍamadhyagam  || 3,17.47 ||
aṣṭāhādgrāhayettasmāttailaṃ pātālayaṃtrake  |
tattailaṃ drāvite svarṇe kṣipeddrutimavāpnuyāt || 3,17.48 ||
{suvarṇadruti (4)}
iṃdragopo'śvalālā ca śaśamaṇḍūkayorvasā  |
asthīni ca samaṃ piṣṭvā drute hemni pravāpayet || 3,17.49 ||
jāyate rasarūpaṃ taccirakālaṃ ca tiṣṭhati  || 3,17.50 ||
{suvarṇadruti (5)}
iṃdragopaṃ kulīrāsthi devadālyāśca bījakam  |
cūrṇitaṃ bhāvayeddrāvairdevadālyudbhavairdinam  || 3,17.51 ||
anena drāvite hemni vāpo deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
tiṣṭhate rasarūpaṃ taccirakālaṃ śivoditam  || 3,17.52 ||
{tīkṣṇalohadruti (3)}
tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca saptāhaṃ pakvadhātrīphaladravaiḥ  |
lolitaṃ bhāvayedgharme kṣīrakandadravaiḥ punaḥ  || 3,17.53 ||
saptāhaṃ bhāvayetsamyaksrāvasaṃpuṭake tathā  |
dhāmitaṃ dravamāyāti ciraṃ tiṣṭhati sūtavat || 3,17.54 ||
{kāntalohadruti}
śṛgālameṣakūrmāhiśalyāni ca śilājatu  |
etatsarvaṃ cūrṇayitvā sutapte kāṃtacūrṇake  |
vāpayeddravatāṃ yāti yathā sūtaṃ suniścitam  || 3,17.55 ||
{sarvalohadruti (3)}
lohacūrṇaṃ yatheṣṭaikaṃ panasasya phaladravaiḥ  |
saptāhaṃ bhāvayedgharme hyamlavargeṇa mardayet |
dravate dhamanenaiva lipiyogyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,17.56 ||
{sarvalohadruti (4)}
gaṃdhakaṃ raktalavaṇaṃ tulyaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
drutānāṃ taptacūrṇānāṃ sarveṣāṃ drāvaṇaṃ param  || 3,17.57 ||
{sarvalohadruti (5)}
pītamaṇḍūkagarbhe tu cūrṇitaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ kṣipet |
ruddhvā bhāṃḍe kṣipedbhūmau trisaptāhātsamuddharet || 3,17.58 ||
tatsamastaṃ vicūrṇyātha drute lohe pravāpayet |
tiṣṭhanti rasarūpāṇi sarvalohāni nānyathā  || 3,17.59 ||
{mākṣikasattvadruti (2)}
eraṃḍotthena tailena guṃjākṣaudraṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇam  |
marditaṃ tasya vāpena satvaṃ mākṣikajaṃ dravet || 3,17.60 ||
{sarvaratnānāṃ sarvalohānāṃ drutiḥ}
kṣāratrayaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ ca caṇakāmlāmlavetasam  || 3,17.61 ||
jvālāmukhī cekṣurakaṃ sthalakumbhīphalāni ca  |
snuhyarkapayasā ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā tadgolakaṃ kṣipet || 3,17.62 ||
vajramukhyāni ratnāni vastre baddhvā paceddhaṭhāt |
dolāyaṃtreṇa dhānyāmle bhavedyāmāṣṭakaṃ drutam  || 3,17.63 ||
vajrābhrakaṃ nīlapuṣpaṃ muktāvidrumamākṣikam  |
pauṇḍraṃ vaiḍūryamāṇikyaṃ rājāvartendranīlakam  || 3,17.64 ||
vaikrāṃtaṃ sphāṭikaṃ caiva dravanti rasasannibhāḥ  |
etairevauṣadhairlohajātaṃ dravati vāpanāt || 3,17.65 ||
{vajradruti (1)}
vajravallyantarasthaṃ ca kṛtvā vajraṃ nirundhitam  |
jalabhāṃḍagataṃ svedyaṃ saptāhāddravatāṃ vrajet || 3,17.66 ||
{vajradruti (2)}
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu saptāhaṃ vetasāmle vinikṣipet |
saptāhāduddhṛtaṃ taṃ vai puṭe ruddhvā drutirbhavet || 3,17.67 ||
{vaikrāntadruti (1)}
śvetavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāṃtamamlavetasabhāvitam  |
saptāhānnātra saṃdehaḥ khare gharme dravatyalam  || 3,17.68 ||
{vaikrāntadruti (2)}
ketakīsvarasaṃ grāhyaṃ saiṃdhavaṃ svarṇapuṣpikā  |
iṃdragopakasaṃyuktaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṃḍe vinikṣipet || 3,17.69 ||
saptāhaṃ svedayettasminvaikrāṃtaṃ dravatāṃ vrajet |
lohāṣṭakaṃ ca ratnāni yogasyāsya prabhāvataḥ  || 3,17.70 ||
kurute yogarājo'yaṃ ratnānāṃ drāvaṇaṃ param  || 3,17.71 ||
{sarvadrutīnāṃ sthāpanādhāraḥ}
kusumbhatailamadhye tu saṃsthāpyā drutayaḥ pṛthak |
tiṣṭhanti cirakālaṃ tu prāpte kārye niyojayet || 3,17.72 ||
ityevaṃ drutisaṃcayaṃ samucitaiḥ sārātisārairmataiḥ kṛtvā vārtikapuṃgavo'tra satataṃ śrīpārade melayet |
tenaivādbhutabhakṣaṇaṃ sukanakaṃ kṛtvātha vidvadvare deyaṃ dīnajane ca duḥkhavimukhaṃ kuryātsamastaṃ jagat || 3,17.73 ||


 3, 18
drutiriha paripācyā jārayetpāradendre munigaṇitamathāsau sāritaḥ koṭivedhī  |
atha pavikṛtabījaṃ ratnagarbhaṃ drutaṃ vā carati yadi rasendraḥ syāttadā śabdavedhī  || 3,18.1 ||
{drutīnāṃ rasena saha melāpanam (1)}
pāṭhā vaṃdhyā tālamūlī nīlīsindūracitrakā  |
padmakandaṃ kṣīrakandaṃ samaṃ nāgabalā tathā  || 3,18.2 ||
eteṣāṃ grāhayetsvacchaṃ rasaṃ vastreṇa gālitam  |
drutiṃ samukhasūtaṃ ca auṣadhīnāṃ tathā dravam  || 3,18.3 ||
sarvaṃ kṣiptvā ghoṣapātre śoṣayedātape khare  |
dravaḥ punaḥ punardeyo yāvadyāmatrayaṃ bhavet || 3,18.4 ||
milanti drutayaḥ sarvāḥ pārade nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,18.5 ||
{sarvadrutimelāpana (2)}
vajrakaṃdāmṛtā guṃjā dravairmardyaṃ ca pūrvavat |
milanti drutayaḥ sarvā rasarāje na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,18.6 ||
{sarvadrutimelāpana (3)}
kṛṣṇāguru sitā hiṅgu kastūrībrahmabījakam  |
tulyaṃ cūrṇaṃ daśāṃśena sūte drutiyute kṣipet || 3,18.7 ||
milanti drutayaḥ sarvā anenaiva na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,18.8 ||
{sarvadrutimelāpana (4)}
kṛṣṇāguru śvetahiṃgu sitā laśunanābhayaḥ  |
pūrvavanmardanenaiva milanti drutayo rase  || 3,18.9 ||
{sarvadrutimelāpana (5)}
aśvalālārdrakaṃ nimbapatrāṇi laśunaṃ samam  |
ṭaṃkaṇena samāyuktaṃ pūrvavaddrutimelakam  || 3,18.10 ||
{sarvadrutimelāpana (6)}
mākṣikaṃ saviṣaṃ guṃjā ṭaṃkaṇaṃ strīrajaḥ samam  |
strīstanyaṃ saṃyutaṃ piṣṭvā tena mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 3,18.11 ||
drutiyuktaṃ rasaṃ tatra kṣiptvā ruddhvā dināvadhi  |
svedayetkarīṣāgnisthaṃ tridinaṃ vā tuṣāgninā  |
milanti drutayaḥ sarvā mīlitā jārayettataḥ  || 3,18.12 ||
drutayo mīlitā yena mūṣāṃ tenaiva lepayet |
tathā ca jīvayogena khyāte'yaṃ liptamūṣikā  || 3,18.57 ||
{copper=> gold}
hemakāṃtadrutiṃ tulyāṃ melayetsamukhe rase  |
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ rasātsarvaṃ liptamūṣāndhitaṃ puṭet || 3,18.58 ||
satuṣe'tha karīṣāgnau yāvatsūtāvaśeṣitam  |
punaśca melayettadvatsarvavajjārayettataḥ  || 3,18.59 ||
evaṃ samāṃ drutiṃ sūte jārayetkramayogataḥ  |
tatastaṃ pakvabījena sārayejjāraṇātrayam  || 3,18.60 ||
mūṣāyantre tu tajjāryaṃ mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
tārāre tāmrasaṃyukte śatāṃśena niyojayet || 3,18.61 ||
krāmaṇena samāyuktaṃ divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,18.62 ||
{copper=> gold}
hemābhraśulbadrutayo dviguṇaṃ jārayedrase  |
pūrvavatkramayogena tato raṃjakabījakam  || 3,18.63 ||
mūṣāyantre samaṃ jāryaṃ sārayetsāraṇātrayam  |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā sahasrāṃśena vedhayet |
tārāraṃ tāmrasaṃyuktaṃ divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,18.64 ||
{=> gold}
kāṃtaśulbasuvarṇānāṃ drutayaḥ samukhe rase  |
jārayetpūrvayogena pratyekaṃ dviguṇaṃ kramāt || 3,18.65 ||
tato raṃjakabījāni dviguṇaṃ tasya jārayet |
atha bījaistridhā sāryaṃ jārayetsārayetpunaḥ  || 3,18.66 ||
jārito'tha mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvātha vedhayet |
ayutāṃśena tenaiva pūrvavatkāṃcanaṃ bhavet || 3,18.67 ||
{lakṣavedhī rasaḥ (drutijāraṇena)}
kāṃtahemābhradrutayo yāvatpañcaguṇaṃ kramāt |
jārayetpūrvayogena tato raṃjakabījakam  || 3,18.68 ||
jāryaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ tasminmūṣāyantre prayatnataḥ  |
sārayetpakvabījena tridhā taṃ jārayetpunaḥ  || 3,18.69 ||
punaḥ sāryaṃ punarjāryamevaṃ vāratraye kṛte  |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā lakṣavedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,18.70 ||
{daśalakṣavedhī rasaḥ}
ā ratnahemadrutayaḥ ṣaḍguṇaṃ jāryate rase  |
ṣaḍguṇaṃ raṃjakaṃ bījaṃ tatastasyaiva jārayet || 3,18.71 ||
tridhā sāryaṃ punarjāryamevaṃ vāracatuṣṭayam  |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā nāgatailena vedhayet |
daśalakṣāṃśayogena divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,18.72 ||
{mercury:: vedhin:: 100 to koṭi}
pratyekaṃ sūtatulyāṃśamabhrahemadrutidvayam  |
melitaṃ pūrvayogena jārayettatkrameṇa vai  || 3,18.73 ||
śatavedhī bhavetsūto dvidhā sahasravedhakaḥ  |
triguṇe'yutavedhī syāllakṣavedhī caturguṇe  || 3,18.74 ||
samyakpañcaguṇe jīrṇe daśalakṣāṇi vidhyati  |
evaṃ rasaguṇe jīrṇe koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,18.75 ||
tataḥ saptaguṇaṃ tasya jāryaṃ raṃjakabījakam  |
tridhātha pakvabījena sārayetpūrvavatkramāt || 3,18.76 ||
jāraṇā sāraṇā kāryā punaḥ sāraṇajāraṇe  |
anena kramayogena saptaśṛṅkhalikākramāt || 3,18.77 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā koṭivedhī bhavettu saḥ  |
tāre ca tāmrasaṃyukte krāmaṇāntaṃ niyojayet || 3,18.78 ||
{mercury:: bandhana (?)}
śvetābhratāraghoṣāradrutayaḥ samukhe rase  |
jāryāḥ samā yathāpūrvaṃ tārabījena sārayet |
tridhā taṃ pūrvavajjāryaṃ mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet || 3,18.79 ||
{tin => silver}
kāṃtatārāradrutayo dviguṇāḥ samukhe rase  |
jārayettriguṇā yāvatpakvabījena cāthavā  || 3,18.80 ||
sāritaṃ jāritaṃ kuryātpūrvavacchṛṅkhalātrayam  |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā ayutāṃśena vedhayet || 3,18.81 ||
drute baṃge tu tattāraṃ bhavetkuṃdendusannibham  || 3,18.82 ||
{tin:: stambhana}
tāratīkṣṇaghoṣajātā drutayaḥ samukhe rase  |
kuryātcaturguṇā yāvattārabījena sārayet || 3,18.83 ||
catasraḥ śṛṅkhalā yāvanmukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
anena lakṣabhāgena baṃgastambho bhaveddṛḍhaḥ  || 3,18.84 ||
{mercury:: koṭivedhī}
tārā kāṃtadrutayo jāryā saptaguṇā rase  |
tatsāryaṃ tārabījena saptaśṛṃkhalikā kramāt || 3,18.85 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,18.86 ||
{saptaśṛṅkhalā}
samukhasya rasendrasya dhānyābhraṃ pūrvasaṃskṛtam  |
cārayejjārayettadvatsamāṃśaṃ cātha tasya vai  || 3,18.87 ||
ṣaḍguṇaṃ dvaṃdvite vyomni sarvaṃ jāryaṃ ca pūrvavat |
tato mākṣikasatvaṃ ca pādāṃśaṃ tasya garbhataḥ  || 3,18.88 ||
drāvayejjārayettadvattāvadrasakasatvakam  |
pūrvavaddrāvitaṃ jāryaṃ mūṣāyantre tu tatkramāt || 3,18.89 ||
garbhadrāvaṇakaṃ bījaṃ drāvitaṃ jārayetpunaḥ  |
bhaveccaturguṇaṃ yāvatpaścādabhrasuvarṇayoḥ  || 3,18.90 ||
drutiṃ samasamāṃ sūte dvaṃdvayitvātha jārayet |
pūrvavatkramayogena kāṃtahemno drutiḥ punaḥ  || 3,18.91 ||
pratyekaṃ jārayettulyaṃ svarṇatīkṣṇadrutistathā  |
dvaṃdvitāṃ jārayettulyāṃ tato raṃjakabījakam  || 3,18.92 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena jārye tasmin caturguṇam  |
tatastaṃ pakvabījena sārayetsāraṇātrayam  || 3,18.93 ||
tadeva jāritaṃ kuryānmūṣāyantre tu pūrvavat |
ityevaṃ saptavārāṇi sāritaṃ tattridhā tridhā  || 3,18.94 ||
pūrvavajjāraṇā kāryā khyāteyaṃ saptaśṛṅkhalā  |
sāraṇā yatra yatroktā vijñeyā vārtikaiḥ punaḥ  || 3,18.95 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ  |
krāmaṇena samāyuktaṃ caṃdrārkaṃ kāṃcanaṃ bhavet || 3,18.96 ||
karmāṣṭādaśakenaiva kramādvedhaḥ prakāśitaḥ  |
samukhaṃ nirmukhaṃ baṃdhaṃ rasabaṃdhaṃ tatheritam  || 3,18.97 ||
gopitaṃ śaṃbhunā siddhaiḥ sūcitaṃ na prakāśitam  |
vārtikānāṃ hitārthāya mayā tatprakaṭīkṛtam  || 3,18.98 ||
{vajrabīja}
vajrabhasma śuddhahema vyomasatvamayorajaḥ  |
catvāri samabhāgāni nāgacūrṇaṃ catuḥsamam  || 3,18.99 ||
dvaṃdvamelāpaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
ekībhūte samuddhṛtya mūṣāyāṃ prakaṭaṃ dhamet || 3,18.100 ||
mākṣikāddhautasattvaṃ ca stokaṃ stokaṃ vinikṣipet |
hemavajrāvaśeṣaṃ tu yāvatsyāduddharettataḥ  || 3,18.101 ||
tasminnāgaṃ vyomasattvamayaścūrṇaṃ ca pūrvavat |
nikṣipeddvaṃdvaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet || 3,18.102 ||
miśrībhūtaṃ samuddhṛtya mūṣāyāṃ prakaṭaṃ dhamet |
svarṇavajrāvaśeṣaṃ tadyāvajjātaṃ samuddharet || 3,18.103 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punarjāryaṃ vyomasatvāyasaṃ phaṇī  |
pūrvavatkramayogena ṣaḍguṇaṃ jārayetpunaḥ  || 3,18.104 ||
mākṣikāddhautasattvakam [... au6 Zeichenjh]  |
kṣipan kṣipandhamettaṃ tu bāhyamevaṃ tu ṣaḍguṇam  || 3,18.105 ||
vajrabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ jāraṇe paramaṃ hitam  || 3,18.106 ||
{vajrabījajāraṇena sparśavedhī śabdavedhī rasaḥ}
vāsanāmukhite sūte dvaṃdvitaṃ vyomasatvakam  |
pūrvavatkramayogena ṣaḍguṇaṃ jārayetpunaḥ  || 3,18.107 ||
tasmin jāryaṃ vajrabījaṃ vyomasattvakrameṇa vai  |
grasate kacchape yaṃtre yathājīrṇaṃ tathā phalam  || 3,18.108 ||
dviguṇe'yutavedhī syāttriguṇe lakṣavedhakaḥ  |
yadā caturguṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ daśalakṣāṇi vidhyati  || 3,18.109 ||
koṭivedhī pañcaguṇe daśakoṭyastu ṣaḍguṇe  |
arbudāṃśātsaptaguṇe śaṅkhavedhyaṣṭame guṇe  || 3,18.110 ||
navame kharvavedhī syāddaśame padmavedhakaḥ  |
trayodaśaguṇe jīrṇe sparśavedhī bhavedrasaḥ  || 3,18.111 ||
caturdaśaguṇe jīrṇe bhavetpāṣāṇavedhakaḥ  |
tripañcaguṇite jīrṇe saśailavanakānanām  || 3,18.112 ||
vedhayenmedinīṃ sarvāṃ sa bhavedbhūcaro rasaḥ  |
evaṃ kalāguṇe jīrṇe trailokyavyāpako bhavet || 3,18.113 ||
khecaro rasarājendro mukhasthaḥ khegatipradaḥ  |
jāyate ca yathāśaktyā tataḥ sāryaṃ krameṇa vai  || 3,18.114 ||
vajrabījena tulyena prathamā sāraṇā bhavet |
pūrvavajjāraṇā kāryā dviguṇenānusārayet || 3,18.115 ||
tathaiva jārayedbhūyaḥ kartavyā pratisāraṇā  |
triguṇena tu tenaiva mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet || 3,18.116 ||
dvisahasrādilakṣāntaṃ vedhakasyāpyayaṃ vidhiḥ  |
ityevaṃ ca punaḥ kuryātsāraṇāṃ koṭivedhake  || 3,18.117 ||
daśakoṭyādyarbudānte ca jārite vedhake rase  |
triprakārā prakartavyā sāraṇā tu tridhā tridhā  || 3,18.118 ||
caturguṇā śaṅkhavedhe tadūrdhvaṃ pañcadhā bhavet |
ṣaḍguṇā padmavedhe tu mūlavedhe tu saptadhā  || 3,18.119 ||
aṣṭadhā sparśavedhe tu daśadhā śabdavedhake  |
tatastrayodaśaguṇāḥ kalāguṇe kalāguṇāḥ  || 3,18.120 ||
kramaśaḥ sāraṇā kāryā yathāśaktyānusārataḥ  |
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā paścādvedhaṃ prakalpayet || 3,18.121 ||
caṃdrārke vā bhujaṃge vā krāmaṇena samāyutam  |
ityevaṃ padmaparyantaṃ saṃkhyāvedhāttu yo rasaḥ  || 3,18.122 ||
tadveṣṭitaṃ madhūcchiṣṭaiḥ kuṃtavedhe tu yojayet |
tatsarvaṃ kanakaṃ divyaṃ jāyate śaṃbhubhāṣitam  || 3,18.123 ||
{dhūmavedhavidhi}
dhūmavedhe rasaṃ piṣṭvā tena vastraṃ pralepayet |
tato jyotiṣmatītaile dhṛtvā vartiṃ kalpayet || 3,18.124 ||
jvalitāṃ tāṃ tāmrakūṭe yojayetpattratāṃ gate  |
taddhūmagaṃdhamātreṇa sarvaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,18.125 ||
{sparśavedhavidhi}
sparśavedhī raso yo'sau guṭikāṃ tena kārayet |
drutānāmaṣṭalohānāṃ kṣiptvā madhye samuddharet |
tadbhavetkāṃcanaṃ divyamasaṃkhyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,18.126 ||
{śabdavedhavidhi}
śabdavedhī raso yo'sau guṭikāṃ tena kārayet |
dhārayedvaktramadhye tu tato lohāni vedhayet |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate svarṇaṃ śrute śabde na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,18.127 ||
{pāṣāṇavedhavidhi}
pāṣāṇavedhako yo'sau parvatāni tu tena vai  |
vedhayedagninā taptān sarvaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,18.128 ||
{medinīvedhavidhi}
medinīvedhako yo'sau rājikārdhārdhamātrakaḥ  |
tenaiva vedhayetsarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām  |
medinī sā svarṇamayī bhavetsatyaṃ śivoditam  || 3,18.129 ||
{trailokyavyāpakavidhi}
trailokyavyāpako yo'sau taṃ kare dhārayettu yaḥ  |
sa bhavetkhecaro divyo mahākāyo mahābalaḥ  || 3,18.130 ||
svecchācārī mahāvīraḥ śivatulyo bhavettu saḥ  |
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ sarvalohāni kāṃcanam  || 3,18.131 ||
jāyante nātra saṃdehastatsvedasparśanādapi  |
rasakāyo mahāsiddhaḥ sarvalokeṣu pūjyate  || 3,18.132 ||
avadhyo devadaityānāṃ yāvaccandrārkamedinī  |
bhuñjāno divyabhogāṃśca krīḍate bhairavo yathā  || 3,18.133 ||
{rasabījaṃ śatavedhī}
bhāgatrayaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ bhāgaikaṃ mṛtavajrakam  |
kākinīrajasā mardyaṃ taptakhalve dināvadhi  || 3,18.134 ||
tenaiva pādabhāgena hemapatrāṇi lepayet |
vyomavallīrasaiḥ piṣṭaṃ kāṃtaṭaṃkaṇatālakam  || 3,18.135 ||
anena cāṣṭamāṃśena pūrvaliptāni lepayet |
ruddhvā svedyaṃ divārātrau karīṣāgnau tataḥ punaḥ  || 3,18.136 ||
kadalīkaṃdasauvīraṭaṃkaṇaṃ ca samaṃ samam  |
kaṇṭakāryā dravaiḥ piṣṭvā mūṣā lepyā tvanena vai  || 3,18.137 ||
tanmadhye pūrvapakvaṃ yadruddhvā dhāmyaṃ dṛḍhāgninā  |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate khoṭaṃ sauvīraṃ kācaṭaṃkaṇam  || 3,18.138 ||
dattvā dattvā dhametkhoṭaṃ jāyate bhāskaropamam  |
rasabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ vedhake jāraṇe hitam  |
caṃdrārke śatavedhī syātkāṃcanaṃ kurute śubham  || 3,18.139 ||
{śabdavedhī rasaḥ}
atha vakṣye rasendrasya samāṃśasya ca bhakṣaṇam  |
pūrvoktaṃ rasabījaṃ tu samukhe cārayedrase  || 3,18.140 ||
abhrasatvaprakāreṇa jārayettatkrameṇa vai  |
pañcapañcāṃśaguṇitaṃ yadā grasati pāradaḥ  || 3,18.141 ||
tatastenaiva bījena sāraṇākrāmaṇātrayam  |
tataśca jāritaṃ kuryānmukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
śabdavedhī bhavetso hi rasaḥ śaṃkarabhāṣitam  || 3,18.142 ||
{rasabījaṃ śatavedhi}
samukhasya rasendrasya pakvabījaṃ samāṃśakam  |
jārayeccābhiṣiktaṃ tadabhrasattvakrameṇa vai  || 3,18.143 ||
mṛtavajraṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ tasminsūte vinikṣipet |
tālakaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ kāṃtaṃ tṛtīyaṃ cāṣṭamāṃśakam  || 3,18.144 ||
dattvā tasmiṃstadā khalve vyomavallīdravairdinam  |
tatsarvaṃ marditaṃ kṛtvā chāyāśuṣkaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 3,18.145 ||
dvaṃdvamelāpaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhitaṃ dhamet |
karīṣāgnau divārātrau dhmāte khoṭaṃ bhavettu tat || 3,18.146 ||
kācaṭaṃkaṇasauvīraiḥ śodhayettaṃ dhaman dhaman  |
rasabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ pūrvavatśatavedhakam  |
jāyate rasarājo'yaṃ kurute kanakaṃ śubham  || 3,18.147 ||
{mercury:: rañjana:: red}
athavā samukhe sūte pūrvavajjārayeddinam  |
pañcapañcāṃśaguṇitaṃ yadā grasati pāradaḥ  || 3,18.148 ||
rasabījena cānyena tridhā sāryaṃ krameṇa vai  |
sārite jāraṇā kāryā mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
śabdavedhī bhavetsākṣāttāmraṃ svarṇaṃ karoti vai  || 3,18.149 ||
{rasabījam}
pakvabījasya cūrṇaṃ tu pūrvavaccābhiṣekitam  |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena sūtasya samukhasya tu cārayet || 3,18.150 ||
dvyaṅgulyāṃ mardanenaiva gharme carati tatkṣaṇāt |
tadbījaṃ jārayettasya svedanaiścābhrasatvavat || 3,18.151 ||
anena kramayogena samaṃ bījaṃ tu sārayet |
tadvaddvādaśabhāgena pakvabījaṃ tu tasya vai  || 3,18.152 ||
cārayenmardayanneva kacchapākhye'tha jārayet |
abhrasatvaprakāreṇa samaṃ yāvacca jārayet || 3,18.153 ||
tatastasyāṣṭamāṃśena pakvabījaṃ tu dāpayet |
mardayettaptakhalve tatcaratyeva hi tatkṣaṇāt || 3,18.154 ||
taṃ sūtaṃ sūraṇe kaṃde garbhe kṣiptvā nirudhya ca  |
liptvā kaṃdaṃ puṭe pacyādyathā kaṃdo na dahyate  || 3,18.155 ||
tatraiva grasate sūto jīrṇe grāsaṃ tu dāpayet |
anena kramayogena samabījaṃ samaṃ punaḥ  || 3,18.156 ||
pādāṃśaṃ pakvabījaṃ tu dattvā cāryaṃ ca mardayet |
mūṣāyantre tato jāryaṃ svedanena punaḥ punaḥ  || 3,18.157 ||
anena kramayogena samabījaṃ ca jārayet |
evaṃ caturguṇe jīrṇe pakvabīje tu pārade  |
jāyate kuṃkumābhastu rasendro balavattaraḥ  || 3,18.158 ||
{dhūmavedhī śabdavedhī pāṣāṇavedhī rasaḥ}
abhrakaṃ bhrāmakaṃ brāhmī mṛtalohāṣṭakaṃ tathā  |
mahārasāścoparasāḥ kaṭutumbyāśca bījakam  || 3,18.159 ||
śaṅkhanābhirmeṣaśṛṅgī vajrakaṃdaṃ samaṃ samam  |
mayūrasya tu raktena sarvaṃ pācyaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,18.160 ||
tatastaṃ mardayetkhoṭaṃ śikhirakte dinadvayam  |
anena mṛtavajraṃ tu lepitaṃ kārayettataḥ  || 3,18.161 ||
mūṣāmāmraphalākārāṃ dvidviliptāṃ tu kārayet |
tanmadhye pūrvasūtaṃ tu pādāṃśaṃ liptavajrakam  || 3,18.162 ||
athavā vajrabījaṃ ca pūrvakalkena lepitam  |
athavā dvaṃdvitaṃ vajraṃ samaṃ svarṇena yatkṛtam  || 3,18.163 ||
talliptaṃ pūrvakalkena pādāṃśaṃ tatra nikṣipet |
ācchāditaṃ dhamenmandaṃ mūṣādhomukhavāyunā  || 3,18.164 ||
kiṃcitkiṃcidbiḍaṃ dattvā jīrṇe tasmātsamuddharet |
punastalliptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā vajreṇa saṃyutam  |
pūrvavatkramayogena jīrṇe vajre samuddharet |
anena kramayogena vajraṃ vā vajrabījakam  || 3,18.165 ||
svarṇadvaṃdvitavajraṃ vā jārayettatpunaḥ punaḥ  |
ekādaśaguṇaṃ yāvattāvajjāryaṃ rasendrake  || 3,18.166 ||
sudagdhāṃ śaṅkhanābhiṃ tu mātuliṃgarasairdinam  |
mardayellolayettena muktācūrṇaṃ suśobhanam  || 3,18.167 ||
drāvitaṃ mauktikaṃ vātha pūrvavajjārayeddhaman  |
mūṣāyāṃ biḍaliptāyāṃ pādaṃ pādaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 3,18.168 ||
ekādaśaguṇaṃ yāvattajjāryaṃ kacchapena tat || 3,18.169 ||
nīlīniryāsasaṃtulyaṃ śikhipittaṃ vimardayet |
indranīlaṃ ca nīlaṃ ca tena liptvātha jārayet || 3,18.170 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena dhamanātsvedanena vā  |
viḍalepitamūṣāyāmekādaśaguṇaṃ kramāt || 3,18.171 ||
drāvitaṃ cendranīlaṃ vā nīlaṃ ca drāvitaṃ kramāt |
dvaṃdvitaṃ rasarājasya jāryamabhradrutiryathā  |
ityevaṃ jārayennīlaṃ drāvitaṃ kaṭhinaṃ tu vā  || 3,18.172 ||
śikhipittanṛraktābhyāṃ lepitaṃ padmarāgakam  |
jārayedrasarājasya tvekādaśaguṇaṃ kramāt |
jāryaṃ vā drāvitaṃ tattu yathā cābhradrutiḥ purā  || 3,18.173 ||
rajanī tulyakaṃkuṣṭhaṃ brahmapuṣpadravairdinam  |
bhāvitaṃ tena liptaṃ tu puṣparāgaṃ tu jārayet || 3,18.174 ||
kaṭhinaṃ drāvitaṃ vātha rudrasaṃkhyākrameṇa vai  |
evaṃ ratnairbhavettṛpto rasarājo mahābalaḥ  || 3,18.175 ||
anenaiva śatāṃśena madhūcchiṣṭena lepayet |
śuddhahāṭakapatrāṇi ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 3,18.176 ||
iṃdragopasamākāraṃ tatsvarṇaṃ jāyate śubham  |
anenaiva suvarṇena sārayetsāraṇātrayam  || 3,18.177 ||
ratnatṛptaṃ sūtarājaṃ mūṣāyantre vinikṣipet |
śanaiḥ śanairdhamettāvadyāvatsūtāvaśeṣitam  || 3,18.178 ||
mukhaṃ baddhvā rasaṃ baddhvā dhūmavedhī bhavettu tat |
anena kramayogena punaḥ sāraṇajāraṇā  || 3,18.179 ||
kartavyāstriprakārā vai mukhaṃ baddhvātha bandhayet |
śabdavedhī rasendro'yaṃ jāyate khegatipradaḥ  || 3,18.180 ||
punaśca trividhā kāryā sāraṇājjāraṇā kramāt |
tasyaiva tu rasendrasya mukhabandhaṃ ca kārayet || 3,18.181 ||
tenaiva vedhayetsarvaṃ giripāṣāṇabhūtalam  |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ jāmbūnadasamaprabham  || 3,18.182 ||
siddhairbhūcarakhecarā śivamukhātprāptā mahājāraṇā kṛtvā tāṃ ca rase rasātalamidaṃ svarṇena pūrṇaṃ kṛtam  |
teṣāṃ karma vicārya sāramakhilaṃ spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ tanmayā yaḥ kaścidgurutantramantraniratastasyaiva siddhaṃ bhavet || 3,18.183 ||


 3, 19
saṃsāre sārabhūtaṃ sakalasukhakaraṃ suprabhūtaṃ dhanaṃ vai tatsādhyaṃ sādhakendrairgurumukhavidhinā vakṣyate tasya siddhyai  |
ratnādīnāṃ viśeṣātkaraṇamiha śubhaṃ gaṃdhavādaṃ samagraṃ jñātvā tattatsusiddhaṃ hyanubhavapathagaṃ pāvanaṃ paṇḍitānām  || 3,19.1 ||
{padmarāgakaraṇam}
caturguṇena toyena lākṣāṃ piṣṭvā tu taddravaiḥ  |
vastrapūtaṃ śatapalaṃ gṛhya mṛdbhāṇḍagaṃ pacet || 3,19.2 ||
mṛdvagninā pādaśeṣaṃ jātaṃ yāvacca tasya vai  |
kṣipetpalaṃ palaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarjiṭaṃkaṇalodhrakam  || 3,19.3 ||
kiṃcitpacyāttataḥ śītaṃ kācakūpyāṃ surakṣayet |
sthūlamatsyatvacaṃ pacyāddivārātraṃ jalena tat || 3,19.4 ||
ghanībhūtaṃ samuttārya khyāto'yaṃ matsyakajjalam  |
etatkarṣadvayaṃ tasyāḥ kācakūpyāṃ vinikṣipet || 3,19.5 ||
varṣopalāstu tenaiva lālayitvā supācite  |
madhūkatailamadhye tu kṣaṇaṃ paktvā samuddharet |
jāyante padmarāgāṇi divyatejomayāni ca  || 3,19.6 ||
{indranīlakaraṇam}
nīlīcūrṇaṃ palaikaṃ tu pūrvakūpyāṃ tu taddravam  |
taddravaṃ dvipalaṃ cūrṇe kṣiptvā sarvaṃ vilolayet || 3,19.7 ||
kṣiptvā varṣotpalāṃstena pūrvatailagatānpacet |
indranīlāni tānyeva jāyante nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.8 ||
{marakatamaṇikaraṇam}
mañjiṣṭhāṃ tālakaṃ nīlī samacūrṇaṃ prakalpayet |
kācakūpyāṃ sthitairdrāvaiḥ sarvametatsulolayet || 3,19.9 ||
varṣotpalāṃstu tenaiva siktvā pacyācca pūrvavat |
sarve marakatāstena samīcīnā bhavanti vai  || 3,19.10 ||
{gomedamaṇikaraṇam}
mañjiṣṭhāyāḥ kaṣāyeṇa peṣayenmatsyakajjalam  |
varṣotpalāṃstu tenaiva siktvā pacyācca pūrvavat || 3,19.11 ||
gomedāni tu tānyeva pravartante na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.12 ||
{puṣparāgakaraṇam}
piṣṭvā tālakatulyaṃ tu jalaike rasakuṅkumam  |
tanmadhye cāṣṭamāṃśaṃ tu kṣipenmatsyotthakajjalam  || 3,19.13 ||
tatsarvaṃ pācayedyāmamavatārya surakṣayet |
varṣopalāṃstu tenaiva siktānpacyācca pūrvavat |
bhavanti puṣparāgāste yathā khanyutthitāni ca  || 3,19.14 ||
{nīlamāṇikyakaraṇam}
nīlīcūrṇasya tulyāṃśaṃ kṣipenmatsyotthakajjalam  |
bījakāṣṭhaṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ jale sthāpyaṃ dināvadhi  || 3,19.15 ||
tatsarvaṃ pācayedyāmamavatārya surakṣayet |
varṣopalāṃstu tenaiva siktāḥ pacyācca pūrvavat |
nīlamāṇikyasadṛśāste bhavanti na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.16 ||
{muktākaraṇam (1)}
proktāni raṅgadravyāṇi kācakūpyāṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
rakṣayitvā prayatnena prāpte kārye niyojayet || 3,19.17 ||
sūryakāṃtasya madhye tu bilaṃ kuryātsuvartulam  |
tathānyaṃ sūryakāntaṃ ca kuryādācchādane hitam  || 3,19.18 ||
sūkṣmamuktāphalānyādau drāvayetpūrvayogataḥ  |
taddrutaṃ sūryakāṃtasya bile pūryaṃ prayatnataḥ  || 3,19.19 ||
sūryakāntenāpareṇa chāditaṃ gharmadhāritam  |
yāmamātrādbhavedbaddhaṃ mauktikaṃ cātiśobhanam  || 3,19.20 ||
chidraṃ kṛtvā nibadhyātha suśubhre vastrakhaṇḍake  |
suśubhraistaṇḍulaiḥ sārdhaṃ kaṇḍayettadulūkhale  || 3,19.21 ||
laghuhastena yāmaikaṃ tata uddhṛtya kṣālayet |
tvacāriṣṭaphalānāṃ tu jalena saha peṣayet |
tenaiva kṣālite muktāphalaṃ bhavati śobhanam  || 3,19.22 ||
{muktākaraṇam (2)}
mauktikāni susūkṣmāṇi cūrṇitāni vinikṣipet |
prasūtāyā iḍāyāstu sadyaḥ kṣīraiḥ kṣaṇāvadhi  || 3,19.23 ||
tenaiva vartulākārā guṭikāḥ kārayettataḥ  |
kācapātre sthitāḥ śoṣyāḥ chāyāyāṃ dinamātrakam  || 3,19.24 ||
protayedaśvavālena mālāṃ kṛtvātha śoṣayet |
chāyāyāṃ kaṭhinā yāvattāvatsthāpyā vilambitāḥ  || 3,19.25 ||
sthūlasya kṛṣṇamatsyasya ekakaṇṭasya codarāt |
nivāryāntrāṇi tatraiva pūrvamālāṃ niveśayet || 3,19.26 ||
udaraṃ sīvayetsūtreṇaiva bhāṇḍe nirudhya tat |
māsamātrātsamuddhṛtya chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetpunaḥ  || 3,19.27 ||
kaṇḍanaṃ kṣālanaṃ caiva pūrvavatkārayecchanaiḥ  |
bhavanti tāni śubhrāṇi samyaṅmuktāphalāni vai  || 3,19.28 ||
{muktākaraṇam (3)}
muktāśuktiṃ samādāya jalaśuktimathāpi vā  |
gharṣayetpṛṣṭhabhāgaṃ tu tasya kārṣṇyāpanuttaye  || 3,19.29 ||
tāḥ śubhrāścūrṇayecchlakṣṇamīḍākṣīrādipūrvavat |
kārayetkṣālanāntaṃ ca mauktikāni bhavanti vai  || 3,19.30 ||
{muktākaraṇam (4)}
sadya uddhṛtya matsyasya sthūlasya cakṣuṣī haret |
ekaikaṃ bandhayedvastre īḍākṣīrairdinaṃ pacet || 3,19.31 ||
chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetpaścātkaṇḍanaṃ kṣālanaṃ tataḥ  |
kārayetpūrvavattāni mauktikāni bhavanti vai  || 3,19.32 ||
{pravālakaraṇam (1)}
dagdhaśaṃkhaṃ ca daradaṃ samaṃ cūrṇaṃ prakalpayet |
prasūtāyā mahiṣyāstu pañcame divase haret || 3,19.33 ||
kṣīraṃ tenaiva tanmardyaṃ yāmaikaṃ pūrvacūrṇakam  |
vartulāṃ guṭikāṃ kṛtvā protayettāmrasūtrake  || 3,19.34 ||
rambhāgarbhadalenaiva madhyamāṃguṣṭhatarjanī  |
veṣṭayitvā tu tairgrāhyā gulikāstāḥ pṛthakpṛthak || 3,19.35 ||
āvartyāvartya saṃsthāpyā raṃbhāpatraiḥ prayatnataḥ  |
chāyāśuṣkāḥ śubhāḥ protyāstāmrasūtreṇa vai punaḥ  || 3,19.36 ||
madhukaṃ taptatailāktaṃ dhūmena svedayecchanaiḥ  |
jāyate padmarāgābhaṃ pravālaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.37 ||
{pravālakaraṇam (2)}
dagdhaḥ śaṃkhaḥ sasindūraṃ samāṃśaṃ cūrṇayettataḥ  |
kṣīraiḥ sadyaḥprasūtāyā eḍāyā mardayeddṛḍham  || 3,19.38 ||
pūrayecca tṛṇotthe vā nāle vaṃśādisaṃbhave  |
supakve cānnabhāṇḍe tu yavāgūvarjite kṣipet || 3,19.39 ||
ācchādya pacyānmandāgnau ghaṭikānte samuddharet |
pravālā nalikāgarbhe jāyante padmarāgavat || 3,19.40 ||
{hiṅgulakaraṇam}
aśuddhaṃ pāradaṃ bhāgaṃ caturbhāgaṃ ca ṭaṃkaṇam  |
ubhau kṣiptvā lohapātre kṣaṇaṃ mṛdvagninā pacet || 3,19.41 ||
tasminmanaḥśilācūrṇaṃ pāradāddaśamāṃśataḥ  |
kṣiptvā cālyamayodarvyā hyavatārya suśītalam  || 3,19.42 ||
kṛtvātha khaṇḍaśaḥ kṣiptvā kācakūpyāṃ nirudhya ca  |
vastramṛttikayā samyakkācakūpīṃ pralepayet || 3,19.43 ||
sarvato'ṅgulamānena chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
vālukāyaṃtragarbhe tu dvidinaṃ mṛdunāgninā  || 3,19.44 ||
kramavṛddhāgninā paścātpaceddivasapañcakam  |
saptāhāttatsamuddhṛtya hiṃgulaṃ syānmanoharam  || 3,19.45 ||
{sindūrakaraṇam (1)}
ciṃcātvagbhasmapādāṃśaṃ drute nāge vinikṣipet |
pācayellohaje pātre lohadarvyā nigharṣayet |
caṇḍāgninā dinaikaṃ tu sindūraṃ jāyate śubham  || 3,19.46 ||
{sindūrakaraṇam (2)}
raktaśākhinyapāmārgakuṭajasya tu bhasmakam  |
caturthāṃśaṃ drute nāge dattvā mardyaṃ dinadvayam  || 3,19.47 ||
pūrvavallohapātre tu sindūraṃ jāyate śubham  || 3,19.48 ||
{sindūrakaraṇam (3)}
bhasmanā pūrvavannāgaṃ śākasya vārijasya vā  |
sindūraṃ jāyate divyaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.49 ||
{sindūrakaraṇam (4)}
palānāṃ dviśataṃ nāgaṃ drāvayellohabhājane  |
samūlavāsakābhasma pādāṃśaṃ tatra nikṣipet || 3,19.50 ||
pītavarṇaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatpacyātpracālayet |
tataḥ suśītalaṃ kṛtvā jalena cālayetpunaḥ  || 3,19.51 ||
palamātrā vaṭī kṛtvā vāsābhasmopari kṣipet |
chāyāśuṣkā samāhṛtya mṛdbhāṇḍe nūtane kṣipet || 3,19.52 ||
kṣiptvā ruddhvā paceccullyāṃ nirvāte tīvravahninā  |
chidraṃ kuryādbhāṇḍavaktre śalākāṃ lohajāṃ kṣipet || 3,19.53 ||
raktavarṇā yadā syātsā tāvatpacyātparīkṣayet |
sindūraṃ jāyate divyaṃ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ  || 3,19.54 ||
{saindhavakaraṇam}
navabhāṇḍe palaśataṃ sāmudralavaṇaṃ kṣipet |
niṣkaṃ niṣkaṃ sūtagaṃdhau kṣiptvā caṇḍāgninā pacet || 3,19.55 ||
dviyāmānte kṣipettasmiṃllohanārācakaṃ yadi  |
raktavarṇaṃ bhavettadvai tadā vahniṃ nivārayet || 3,19.56 ||
svabhāvaśītalaṃ grāhyaṃ sindhūtthaṃ lavaṇaṃ bhavet || 3,19.57 ||
{suvarcalakaraṇam}
āranālaṃ palaikaṃ tu dviniṣkaṃ ca suvarcalam  |
māṣaikaṃ gaṃdhakaṃ piṣṭvā sarvaṃ pātre tu dhārayet || 3,19.58 ||
palaikaṃ saiṃdhavaṃ taptaṃ kṛtvā tatra niṣecayet |
punastāpyaṃ punaḥ secyaṃ dravo yāvadviśuṣyati  |
suvarcalaṃ bhavettāvannātra kāryā vicāraṇā  || 3,19.59 ||
{hiṅgukaraṇam (1)}
hiṅgunāgaramekaikaṃ laśunasya paladvayam  |
catuṣpalaṃ nimbabījaṃ māṣacūrṇaṃ palāṣṭakam  || 3,19.60 ||
sarvatulyāṃ bilvamajjāmajākṣīreṇa peṣayet |
tatsarvaṃ bandhayedgāḍhaṃ sārdragocarmagarbhataḥ  |
pakṣatrayaṃ dhānyarāśau kṣipeddhiṃgu bhavettataḥ  || 3,19.61 ||
{hiṅgukaraṇam (2)}
babbūlavṛkṣaniryāsaṃ sāmudralavaṇaṃ tathā  |
tvagvarjyaṃ ca kaṇā tulyaṃ meṣīkṣīreṇa peṣayet || 3,19.62 ||
asya piṇḍasya pādāṃśaṃ śuddhahiṃgu niyojayet |
tatsarvaṃ pūrvavadbaddhaṃ carmaṇā divasatrayam  || 3,19.63 ||
nirvāte lambitaṃ rakṣethiṃgu syācchuddhahiṃguvat || 3,19.64 ||
{hiṅgukaraṇam (3)}
palaikaikaṃ guḍaṃ śuṇṭhī dvikaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaguggulum  |
eraṇḍabījamajjā ca tuṣavarjyaṃ paladvayam  || 3,19.65 ||
nistvaṅmāṣā paladvaṃdvamekīkṛtya prapeṣayet |
trikarṣaṃ hiṅgu tanmadhye kṣiptvā toyena lolayet || 3,19.66 ||
tatsarvaṃ pūrvavadbaddhvā saptāhāddhiṅgutāṃ vrajet || 3,19.67 ||
{hiṅgukaraṇam (4)}
dvipale śuddhahiṃgu syādeḍākṣīraṃ ca viṃśatiḥ  |
godhūmamāṣayoścūrṇaṃ pratyekaṃ tu catuṣpalam  || 3,19.68 ||
alābupātramadhyasthaṃ tatsarvaṃ lolitaṃ kṣipet |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ bhavettāvadyāvaddhiṃgu śubhaṃ bhavet || 3,19.69 ||
{vaṅgakaraṇam (1)}
dhattūrabījacūrṇaṃ tu vajrīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet |
śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punarbhāvyamevaṃ gharme trisaptadhā  || 3,19.70 ||
tadvāpyaṃ drutanāgasya daśamāṃśena dāpayet |
ḍhālayetsnukpayomadhye tadvaṅgaṃ jāyate śubham  |
bhāvayedrajanīmadhye tadbaṃgaṃ jāyate śubham  || 3,19.71 ||
{vaṅgakaraṇam (2)}
bhāvayedrajanīcūrṇaṃ vajrīdugdhena saptadhā  |
tadvāpaṃ daśamāṃśena drute nāge pradāpayet || 3,19.72 ||
tadvāpaṃ drutanāgasya daśamāṃśena dāpayet |
tatḍhālyaṃ triphalākvāthe punastadvacca vāpayet || 3,19.73 ||
ṭaṃkaṇaṃ navasāraṃ ca dattvā secyaṃ nṛmūtrake  |
drāvitaṃ ca punarḍhālyaṃ nṛmūtre vaṅgatāṃ vrajet || 3,19.74 ||
{amlavetasakaraṇam}
tvagbījarahitaṃ ciṃcāphalaṃ kāṃjikasaṃyutam  |
paktvā kuryādvastrapūtaṃ jambīrāmlaṃ tu tatsamam  || 3,19.75 ||
cāṅgerīmātuliṃgāmlairyathāprāptaṃ samāharet |
vastrapūtaṃ tu tatsarvaṃ pacetpādāvaśeṣitam  || 3,19.76 ||
saurāṣṭrī tutthakāsīsaṃ trikṣāraṃ paṭupañcakam  |
mūlasāraṃ ca tulyāṃśaṃ sarvaṃ cūrṇaṃ vinikṣipet || 3,19.77 ||
pūrvapakve tu pādāṃśaṃ punarmṛdvagninā pacet |
ghanībhūtaṃ bhavedyāvaccaṭṭakenaiva cālayet |
amlavetasamityetajjāyate śobhanaṃ param  || 3,19.78 ||
{sāhīkaraṇam}
triphalā bhṛṅgakoraṇṭabhallātakaravīrakam  |
bījāmrasamameteṣāṃ samāṃśaṃ bolakajjale  || 3,19.79 ||
kṣiptvā mardyaṃ tāmrapātre pañcāhājjāyate maṣī  |
tālapattreṣu bhūrjeṣu likhyate paramaṃ dṛḍham  || 3,19.80 ||
{ghṛtakaraṇam}
nārikelātphalarasaṃ grāhyaṃ bhāgacatuṣṭayam  |
tanmadhye ghṛtamekaṃ tu kṣiptvā bhāṇḍe vilolayet || 3,19.81 ||
śatāṃśena kṣipettasmin raktaśākinimūlakam  |
mṛdvagninā pacetkiṃcittatsarvaṃ jāyate ghṛtam  || 3,19.82 ||
{ghṛtakaraṇam (2)}
ghṛtaṃ toyaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā viṃśatyaṃśena cunnakam  |
kṣiptvā sarvaṃ tu mṛdbhāṃḍe kṣaṇaṃ hastena mardayet |
ghṛtaṃ tajjāyate sarvaṃ na cāgniṃ sahate kvacit || 3,19.83 ||
{ghṛtakaraṇam (3)}
meṣīmedaḥ pañcapalaṃ tilatailaṃ ca tatsamam  |
pacenmṛdvagninā tāvadyāvatphenaṃ nivartate  || 3,19.84 ||
dviniṣkaṃ kāṃjikaṃ tasmin kṣiptvā vastreṇa cālayet |
pādāṃśaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ tasmin dadyātsarvaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,19.85 ||
{ghṛtakaraṇam (4)}
tilatailaṃ vipacyādau yāvatphenaṃ nivartate  |
gugguluṃ nikṣipettasmin kiṃcidgaṃdhanivṛttaye  || 3,19.86 ||
viṃśatyaṃśena toyasya kṣiptvā cunnaṃ vilolayet |
jalatulyaṃ pūrvatailaṃ miśrayettatsuśītalam  || 3,19.87 ||
mardayenmṛṇmaye pātre hastena kṣaṇamātrakam  |
ghanībhūte ghṛtaṃ cārdhaṃ kṣiptvā sarvaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhavet || 3,19.88 ||
{candanakaraṇam}
saṃchedya nimbavṛkṣaṃ tu hastaikaṃ rakṣayedadhaḥ  |
tasya mūrdhni bilaṃ kuryāttatraiva navaguggulum  || 3,19.89 ||
pūrayettena kāṣṭhena bilaṃ ruddhvātha lepayet |
saṃdhiṃ mṛllavaṇenaiva śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭe pacet || 3,19.90 ||
svabhāvaśītalaṃ grāhyaṃ tanmūlaṃ candanaṃ bhavet || 3,19.91 ||
{karpūrakaraṇam}
palatrayaṃ pacedbhaktaṃ samyagrājānnataṇḍulam  |
tadbhaktaṃ śītalaṃ kṛtvā gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ prayatnataḥ  || 3,19.92 ||
niṣkamātraṃ ca karpūraṃ kṣiptvā tasmiṃśca peṣayet |
śuṣkasya vaṃśanālasya sthūlasya tena codaram  || 3,19.93 ||
lepyamaṅgulamānena chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca kārayet |
chittvātha kadalīpuṣpaṃ tanniryāsena pūrayet || 3,19.94 ||
vaṃśanālaṃ punarvastrakhaṇḍe ruddhvā ca tanmukham  |
ātape tridinaṃ śoṣyaṃ bhūgarte nikhanettataḥ  || 3,19.95 ||
trisaptāhātsamuddhṛtya śoṣayitvā samāharet |
karpūraṃ tasya garbhasthaṃ rakṣetkarpūrabhājane  |
karpūraṃ jāyate divyaṃ yathā bījaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  || 3,19.96 ||
{javādīyāṅkastūrīkaraṇam}
panasasyārdhaṃ pakvasya bījānyekasya khaṇḍayet |
navabhāṇḍe vinikṣipya niṣkaṃ śuṇṭhī palaṃ tathā  || 3,19.97 ||
cūrṇayitvā kṣipettasmin tatsarvaṃ dravatāṃ vrajet |
tena ghṛṣṭvā kṣipettasmin caturniṣkaṃ ca candanam  || 3,19.98 ||
mṛdvagnau pācayettāvadyāvadāraktatāṃ gatam  |
tacchītalaṃ kācapātre kṣiptvā tasyopari kṣipet || 3,19.99 ||
campakaṃ ketakīmallījātīpuṣpāṇi tatpunaḥ  |
dinaṃ śubhrapaṭe baddhvā mukhaṃ tasyaiva rakṣayet || 3,19.100 ||
tataḥ puṣpāṇi saṃtyaktvā kastūrīṃ māṣamātrakām  |
māṣaikaṃ śuddhakarpūre tasminneva vinikṣipet || 3,19.101 ||
nikṣipedviṃśadaṃśena samyagjāvādikāmapi  |
tatsarvaṃ mathitaṃ pūrvaṃ samyagjāvādibhājane  || 3,19.102 ||
veṣṭayenmallikāpuṣpaistadbhāṃḍaṃ divasatrayam  |
samyagbhavati jāvādi varṇaiḥ parimalairapi  || 3,19.103 ||
{kastūrīkaraṇam}
madhūkatailaṃ tailaṃ vā tilotthaṃ palapañcakam  |
muṇḍīdrāvaṃ daśapalaṃ sarvamekatra yojayet || 3,19.104 ||
mallikā mālatī jātī ketakī śatapattrikā  |
anyāni ca sugandhīni puṣpāṇi tatra nikṣipet || 3,19.105 ||
dinaikaṃ mudritaṃ rakṣetpuṣpaṃ niṣpīḍya saṃtyajet |
sikthakaṃ viṃśatirniṣkān kṣiptvā tasminpacecchanaiḥ  || 3,19.106 ||
yāvattailāvaśeṣaṃ syātkarpūraṃ cārdhaniṣkakam  |
niṣkaṃ mārjārajāvādiṃ kṣiptvā tadavatārayet || 3,19.107 ||
anyapātre vinikṣipya śītalaṃ tatpunaḥ pacet |
kṣaṇamātrāttaduttārya kṣipejjāvādi bhājane  || 3,19.108 ||
sāndraṃ bhavati tatsarvaṃ yathā bījaṃ na saṃśayaḥ  |
puṣpāṇi bakulasyaiva ratnamālāṃ samaṃ samam  || 3,19.109 ||
taccūrṇamikṣudaṇḍasya kṛtanālasya codare  |
kṣiptvā tasya mukhaṃ ruddhvā tanmajjābhirmṛdā punaḥ  || 3,19.110 ||
puṭettṛṇāgninā tāvadyāvadgaṃdho na dahyate  |
dravanti tāni puṣpāṇi mukhaṃ bhittvā dravaṃ haret || 3,19.111 ||
kastūrīcarma nirlomaṃ mustātulyaṃ vicūrṇayet |
cūrṇasya daśamāṃśena samyakkastūrikāṃ kṣipet || 3,19.112 ||
pūrvadrāveṇa tatsarvaṃ peṣitaṃ golakīkṛtam  |
kastūrīmadanākārā kiṃcitkāryā prayatnataḥ  || 3,19.113 ||
tatsarvaṃ chāyayā śoṣyaṃ madanā rakṣayetpṛthak |
guṭikāḥ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā madanaiḥ saha miśrayet |
kastūrīcarmaṇā baddhvā samyaṅmṛgamado bhavet || 3,19.114 ||
{kuṇkumakaraṇam (1)}
nārikelakapālaṃ vā ghṛṣṭaṃ vā nimbakāṣṭhakam  |
yatkiṃcicchubhrakāṣṭhaṃ vā toyena saha kārayet || 3,19.115 ||
tatpādaṃ rajanī cātha tasminmadhye vinikṣipet |
gairikaṃ vā rajanyardhaṃ tatsarvaṃ kuṃkumaṃ bhavet || 3,19.116 ||
{kuṅkumakaraṇam (2)}
pālāśapuṣpajaṃ kvāthaṃ gharme dhāryaṃ tu kharpare  |
viṃśatyaṃśaṃ kṣipettasmin peṣitaṃ śubhrataṇḍulam  || 3,19.117 ||
taṇḍulārdhaṃ tathā cunnaṃ sarvaṃ kāṣṭhena lolayet |
ghanībhūtaṃ bhavedyāvattāvadgharme pracālayet |
tatastenaiva vaṭikāḥ kṛtvā syuḥ kuṅkumopamāḥ  || 3,19.118 ||
{kuṅkumakaraṇam (3)}
pālāśapuṣpapādāṃśaṃ samyakśubhraṃ ca taṇḍulam  |
piṣṭvātha vaṭikāḥ kāryā śoṣyāḥ syuḥ kuṃkumopamāḥ  || 3,19.119 ||
{divyadhūpa (1)}
kramāttaraguṇaṃ kuryātkastūrī śaśikuṅkumam  |
nakhamāṃsī sarjarasamustā kṛṣṇāguruḥ sitā  || 3,19.120 ||
candanaṃ ca daśaitāni cūrṇitāni vimiśrayet |
cūrṇatulyairguggulubhiḥ sarvamekatra kuṭṭayet || 3,19.121 ||
stokaṃ stokaṃ kṣipettailaṃ śilāyāṃ lohamuṣṭinā  |
dinamekaṃ prayatnena vartikāṃ tena kārayet || 3,19.122 ||
tadagrajvalitaṃ kuryājjvālāṃ nivārya tatkṣaṇāt |
devānāṃ divyadhūpo'yaṃ mantrāṇāṃ sādhane hitaḥ  || 3,19.123 ||
{divyadhūpa (2)}
pāṣāṇabhedacūrṇaṃ tu gugguluṃ ca palaṃ palam  |
māṃsī mustā nakhaṃ bolacandanāguruvālakam  || 3,19.124 ||
lākṣāguḍaṃ sarjarasaṃ sitākarpūrasaṃyutam  |
prati niṣkadvayaṃ cūrṇya kastūrī kuṃkumaṃ tathā  || 3,19.125 ||
māṣaikaikaṃ kṣipettasmin sarvaṃ kuṭyādulūkhale  |
tilatailaṃ kṣipetkiṃcillohadaṇḍena taddṛḍham  || 3,19.126 ||
yāmaikaṃ kuṭṭayetsiddho divyo dhūpaḥ śivoditaḥ  |
devādevākaro deyaḥ pūrvavadvartakīkṛtaḥ  |
sarvasaubhāgyajanakaḥ sarvamantro'ghanāyakaḥ  || 3,19.127 ||
{puṣpadruti}
vajrīkṣīreṇa saṃyuktaṃ śuddhaṃ vastraṃ punaḥ punaḥ  |
ātape śoṣitaṃ kuryādityevaṃ dinasaptakam  || 3,19.128 ||
jātīpuṣpapalaikaṃ tu niṣkaṃ cūrṇitaṭaṃkaṇam  |
kṣaudraṃ niṣkatrayaṃ yojyaṃ sarvamekatra lolayet || 3,19.129 ||
mṛtpātre dhārayedgharme ramye vā kācabhājane  |
ācchādayettu vastreṇa jalasiktena tatkṣaṇāt || 3,19.130 ||
dravanti tāni puṣpāṇi yuñjyādyogeṣu taddravam  |
anenaiva prakāreṇa puṣpāṇāṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak |
drutiḥ kāryā sugandhānāṃ gaṃdhavādeṣu yojayet || 3,19.131 ||
{dhānyavṛddhikaraṇam (1)}
mandāramūlamārdrāyāṃ bharaṇyāṃ vā kuśodbhavam  |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃgrāhya yatnena dhavamālye vinikṣipet || 3,19.132 ||
pravātātimukhaṃ yattu tatkāṣṭhaṃ tu samāharet |
dhānyasya rāśigaṃ kuryāddhānyavṛddhikaraṃ param  || 3,19.133 ||
{dhānyavṛddhikaraṇam (2)}
kṛkalāsasya vāmākṣi hemnāveṣṭyābhimantritam  |
dhānyarāśau vinikṣipya dhānyavṛddhikaraṃ param  || 3,19.134 ||
{dhanadhānyavṛddhikaraṇam (3)}
tasyaiva dakṣiṇaṃ netraṃ hemnāveṣṭya tataḥ kṣipet |
yasminkasminbhave dravye dhānye vā vṛddhikārakam  || 3,19.135 ||
{dravyādivṛddhikaraṇam}
kṛṣṇacitrakamūlaṃ tu kṣiptaṃ yasmin suvastuni  |
tatsarvaṃ cākṣayaṃ nityaṃ vyayīkṛtya na kṣīyate  || 3,19.136 ||
dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ suvarṇaṃ navaratnakam  |
yatkiṃciddravyajātaṃ tadakṣayyaṃ tiṣṭhati dhruvam  || 3,19.137 ||
{dhānyavṛddhikaraṇam (4)}
mūlaṃ suśvetaguṃjāyā jalamadhye vinikṣipet |
tanmūlaṃ dhānyarāśau ca kṣiptvā mantravidhānataḥ  || 3,19.138 ||
taddhānyaṃ vardhate nityaṃ bhakṣyamāṇaṃ sahasraśaḥ  |
mantrakhaṇḍe yathā proktaṃ guñjāmūlasya sādhanam  |
tathaivātra prakartavyaṃ siddhirbhavati nānyathā  || 3,19.139 ||
ādau sarvadiśāntareṣu gamanaṃ kṛtvā guroḥ saṃmukhātprāptaṃ bhaktibalena yuktividhinā sārātisāraṃ mahat |
tatsarvaṃ dhanavardhanaṃ nigaditaṃ bhūyiṣṭhamadhvāṃ kvacidbhūpānāṃ viduṣāṃ mahāmatimatāṃ vidvān bhavetpālanaiḥ  || 3,19.140 ||


 3, 20
sāṅgopāṅgamanekayoganicayaṃ sāraṃ varaṃ coddhṛtaṃ yuktaṃ pāradabandhanaṃ mṛduhaṭhātdṛṣṭaṃ paraṃ yanmayā  |
tatsarvaṃ sugamaṃ pravacmi sahasā siddhānanādāgataṃ pratyakṣānubhavena vārtikagaṇaiḥ sāmrājyadaṃ vīkṣyatāt || 3,20.1 ||
{pāradabandhana (1)}
śuddhapāradabhāgaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇena samaṃ samam  |
mardayettriphalākvāthairnaramūtrairyutaistataḥ  || 3,20.2 ||
karṣāṃśā gulikāḥ kṛtvā māṣacūrṇairjalānvitaiḥ  |
sūtādṛṣṭaguṇairliptvā chāyāśuṣkāṃ dhameddṛḍham  || 3,20.3 ||
koṣṭhīyantre vaṃkanāle kiṭṭaṃ bhittvā samāharet |
raso'sau vartulākāraḥ ṣaṇḍabaddho bhavatyalam  || 3,20.4 ||
{pāradabandhanam (2)}
āraṇyamallikādrāvairmūṣāṃ kanyādravaiśca vā  |
dravairhariṇakhuryā vā naramūtrayutaṃ rasam  || 3,20.5 ||
tridinaṃ mardayetkhalve mūtraṃ dattvā punaḥ punaḥ  |
tadvaṭīṃ māṣapiṣṭena liptvā dhāmyaṃ ca pūrvavat || 3,20.6 ||
tadvatsūto bhavedbaddhastacchodhyaṃ kācaṭaṃkaṇaiḥ  || 3,20.7 ||
{pāradabandhanam (3)}
markaṭīmūlajadrāvaiḥ pāradaṃ mardayeddinam  |
markaṭīmūlaje piṇḍe kṣipettanmarditaṃ rasam  || 3,20.8 ||
tatpiṇḍe vajramūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet |
jāyate khoṭabaddho'yaṃ sarvakāryakarakṣamaḥ  || 3,20.9 ||
{pāradabandhanam (4)}
arkamūlaṃ ravikṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā mūṣāṃ ghanaṃ kṣipet |
tanmadhye jāritaṃ sūtaṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvātha rodhayet || 3,20.10 ||
mṛṇmaye saṃpuṭe taṃ ca nirundhyāllohasaṃpuṭe  |
tato gajapuṭe pacyātpārado bandhamāpnuyāt || 3,20.11 ||

bhuktaṃ tasminkṣipetsvarṇaṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ dhamandhaman  || 3,20.118 ||
grasate bhārasaṃkhyā tu mūṣā lepyā punaḥ punaḥ  |
muñcatyasau drute nāge guhyādguhyaṃ prakāśitam  || 3,20.119 ||
{guhyākhyasūtena vaṅgavedhaḥ}
bhūnāgaṃ ṭaṃkaṇaṃ tulyaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet |
taṃ vāhayeddrute baṃge yāvacchataguṇaṃ dhaman  || 3,20.120 ||
tataḥ śataguṇaṃ baṃgaṃ tasyaivopari vāhayet |
stokaṃ stokaṃ dhamanneva grasate nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 3,20.121 ||
{guhyavaṅgakaraṇaṃ tena vedhaḥ}
tālakaṃ saiṃdhavaṃ tulyaṃ bhūnāgadravapeṣitam  |
mūṣāgarbhe lipettena tadbaṃgaṃ tatra nikṣipet || 3,20.122 ||
stokaṃ stokaṃ kṣipettasmin biḍaṃ dattvā dhamandhaman  |
bhārasaṃkhyā grasatyevaṃ guhyavaṅgamiti smṛtam  || 3,20.123 ||
drute baṃge vinikṣiptaṃ yāvatsaṃkhyā na saṃśayaḥ  |
tāvaddrute na saṃdehaḥ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ  || 3,20.124 ||
kṣiptvātha māhiṣe śṛṅge mardayannagninā pacet |
niṣkamekaṃ bhavedyāvattāvanmardyaṃ kṣipan kṣipan  || 3,20.125 ||
tadbhavedrasatulyaṃ tu samādāyātha tatsamam  || 3,20.126 ||
{guhyayogaḥ}
pāradaṃ śuddhahemātha satvaṃ bhūnāgasaṃbhavam  |
catvāriṃśannāgabhāgā mardyaṃ jaṃbīrajadravaiḥ  || 3,20.127 ||
tadgolakaṃ viśoṣyātha kalke bhūnāgasaṃbhave  |
mūṣāgarbhe vilepyādau tasyāṃ golaṃ nirodhayet || 3,20.128 ||
dhamettīvrāgninā tāvadyāvannātrāvaśeṣitam  |
sarvavadgrasate datte guhyākhyaṃ yogamuttamam  || 3,20.129 ||
{kāmadhenuḥ (1)}
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi guṭikābaṃdhamuttamam  |
samajīrṇaṃ kṛtaṃ vyoma samato rasaṃ jārayet || 3,20.130 ||
ravisaṃkhyāṃśakaṃ śulbaṃ dattvā piṣṭiṃ ca kārayet |
dhānyābhrakasamaṃ gaṃdhaṃ śulbe kṣiptvā vimardayet || 3,20.131 ||
tayormūṣākṛtiṃ kṛtvā piṣṭīmadhye vimocayet |
naramāṃsena saṃveṣṭya māṣapiṣṭyā tathaiva ca  || 3,20.132 ||
pacedatasītailena māsamātraṃ tu sādhakaḥ  |
akṣayā kāmadhenuśca vaṅgastambhanakāriṇī  || 3,20.133 ||
{kāmadhenuḥ (2)}
pārade jāryaṃ kṛṣṇābhraṃ rukmamaṣṭaguṇaṃ yadi  |
rañjitaṃ gandharāgeṇa naramāṃsena veṣṭitam  || 3,20.134 ||
māṣapiṣṭyā pralipyāthātasītailena pācayet |
kāmadhenuriyaṃ khyātā nāgastambhanakāriṇī  || 3,20.135 ||
{kāmadhenuḥ (3)}
rasātpādāṃśakaṃ hemapiṣṭiṃ kuryācca sundarām  |
vilipya kāmadhenuṃ ca nāgadrāve niyojayet || 3,20.136 ||
taṃ nāgaṃ kurute rukmaṃ vāñchitārtheṣu siddhidam  |
guṭikāṃ kāmadhenuṃ tāṃ pratyahaṃ dhārayenmukhe  |
śastrāstrairna ca bhidyeta divyadehamavāpnuyāt || 3,20.137 ||
{silver=> gold}
śilayā mārito nāgaḥ sūtarājasamanvitaḥ  |
rañjito gandharāgeṇa samahemnā ca sārayet |
tāravedhaḥ pradātavyo divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,20.138 ||
{lead=> gold}
śilayā ravidugdhena nāgapatrāṇi lepayet |
mārayetpuṭayogena divyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,20.139 ||
{sitasvarṇa => gold}
meṣīkṣīrāmlavargābhyāṃ daradaṃ gharmabhāvitam  |
śatadhā tatprayogena śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ kharātape  || 3,20.140 ||
sitasvarṇasya patrāṇi liptvā liptvā puṭe pacet |
evaṃ trisaptadhā kuryāddivyaṃ bhavati kāṃcanam  || 3,20.141 ||
{gaganagrāsaḥ}
tṛṇajātīyamūlaṃ tu mātuliṅgarasena ca  |
tridinaṃ mardayetsūtaṃ gaganaṃ grasate kṣaṇāt || 3,20.142 ||
siddhairgaṇaiḥ suravarai rasasiddhikāmairbaddhaṃ haṭhātparamamantrabalena taiśca  |
tasmādviśiṣṭamanujaiḥ kṛtamantrajāpaiḥ kāryaṃ tato rasavare varabandhanaṃ ca  || 3,20.143 ||
